## Practice 332: Dietary Menu Request | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a hotel. The woman works at room service, and the man is a hotel guest.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello, is this room service? I'm calling from room 742.
WOMAN: Yes, this is room service. How may I help you today?
MAN: I have some dietary restrictions and was wondering if you could help me with a special meal request.
WOMAN: Of course, we're happy to accommodate dietary requirements. Could you tell me about your specific needs?
MAN: I'm gluten-intolerant and also allergic to dairy products. I was looking at your dinner menu, but I'm not sure what would be safe for me to eat.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. We actually have a separate menu for guests with dietary restrictions. Would you like me to send one up to your room?
MAN: That would be great, thank you. How long will that take?
WOMAN: I can have someone bring it up within the next 10 minutes. Is there anything else you need?
MAN: No, that's all for now. I'll wait for the menu and then call back to place my order.
WOMAN: Perfect. You'll have it shortly.

**Q1:** What is the primary reason for the man's call?
- 1. To complain about room service
- 2. To request information about dietary options
- 3. To make a dinner reservation
- 4. To ask about room cleaning
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To complain about room service: His initial call is not to complain but to inquire about options.
✅ 2. To request information about dietary options: The man says 'I have some dietary restrictions and was wondering if you could help me with a special meal request.'
❌ 3. To make a dinner reservation: He is calling room service to order food, not to make a reservation.
❌ 4. To ask about room cleaning: Room cleaning is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What solution does the woman offer?
- 1. To connect him with a dietary specialist
- 2. To send a special menu to his room
- 3. To prepare an immediate meal
- 4. To transfer him to the restaurant
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To connect him with a dietary specialist: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. To send a special menu to his room: The woman says 'We actually have a separate menu for guests with dietary restrictions. Would you like me to send one up to your room?'
❌ 3. To prepare an immediate meal: She first offers to send a special menu, not to prepare a meal immediately.
❌ 4. To transfer him to the restaurant: There is no mention of transferring him to the restaurant.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, it's room 742 again. I've looked through the special menu you sent up.
WOMAN: Yes, how can I assist you with your order?
MAN: I'd like to order the grilled salmon with vegetables, but I have a few questions about the preparation.
WOMAN: Of course, I'd be happy to help. What would you like to know?
MAN: First, can you ensure it's cooked in olive oil rather than butter? And for the vegetables, are they steamed without any dairy-based sauces?
WOMAN: Yes, we can definitely prepare the salmon with olive oil. The vegetables are normally steamed plain, and we can serve any sauces on the side.
MAN: That's perfect. And one more thing - for the rice side dish, is it plain rice or does it have any seasonings?
WOMAN: It's plain basmati rice, but we can add herbs if you'd like.
MAN: Plain is fine, thank you. I'll take the meal as described.

**Q3:** What dish does the man order?
- 1. Steamed vegetables with rice
- 2. Grilled salmon with rice and vegetables
- 3. Plain basmati rice with herbs
- 4. Salmon with dairy-based sauce
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Steamed vegetables with rice: This is only part of his complete order.
✅ 2. Grilled salmon with rice and vegetables: The man says 'I'd like to order the grilled salmon with vegetables' and also discusses the rice side dish.
❌ 3. Plain basmati rice with herbs: He specifically declines herbs, saying 'Plain is fine.'
❌ 4. Salmon with dairy-based sauce: He has a dairy allergy and wants to avoid dairy-based sauces.

**Q4:** What specific request does the man make about the meal preparation?
- 1. To cook everything in butter
- 2. To add special seasonings to the rice
- 3. To use olive oil instead of butter
- 4. To add extra vegetables
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cook everything in butter: The man specifically wants to avoid butter due to his dairy allergy.
❌ 2. To add special seasonings to the rice: He actually says 'Plain is fine' when offered herbs for the rice.
✅ 3. To use olive oil instead of butter: The man asks 'can you ensure it's cooked in olive oil rather than butter?' due to his dairy allergy.
❌ 4. To add extra vegetables: There is no mention of wanting extra vegetables.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the restaurant's service?
- 1. They are inflexible with special requests
- 2. They are willing to modify dishes
- 3. They only serve standard meals
- 4. They charge extra for modifications
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are inflexible with special requests: The conversation shows they are very accommodating to special requests.
✅ 2. They are willing to modify dishes: The woman confirms they can prepare the salmon with olive oil instead of butter and serve sauces on the side, showing flexibility.
❌ 3. They only serve standard meals: They have a special menu for dietary restrictions and are willing to modify dishes.
❌ 4. They charge extra for modifications: There is no mention of extra charges for modifications.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello, room 742 here again. I hate to be a bother, but there seems to be a small issue with my order.
WOMAN: I'm sorry to hear that. What seems to be the problem?
MAN: The meal looks wonderful, but I notice there's a cream sauce on the side of the plate. I mentioned earlier about my dairy allergy.
WOMAN: I sincerely apologize for this oversight. I'll have a new plate prepared immediately without any dairy products.
MAN: Thank you, I appreciate that. How long will it take?
WOMAN: We'll prioritize your order. It should take about 15 minutes. Would you like us to add something complimentary to your order for the inconvenience?
MAN: That's not necessary, but thank you for offering. I'll just wait for the corrected order.
WOMAN: We'll have it up to you right away, and I'll personally check it before it leaves the kitchen.

**Q6:** What is the problem with the delivered meal?
- 1. The salmon is undercooked
- 2. There is a dairy-based sauce included
- 3. The portion size is too small
- 4. The vegetables are overcooked
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The salmon is undercooked: There is no mention of the salmon being undercooked.
✅ 2. There is a dairy-based sauce included: The man specifically states 'I notice there's a cream sauce on the side of the plate. I mentioned earlier about my dairy allergy.'
❌ 3. The portion size is too small: The portion size is not mentioned as an issue.
❌ 4. The vegetables are overcooked: There is no mention of the vegetables being overcooked.

**Q7:** How does the woman respond to the problem?
- 1. She offers a refund
- 2. She suggests a different meal
- 3. She promises to remake the order and check it personally
- 4. She denies there is a problem
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She offers a refund: A refund is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. She suggests a different meal: She offers to remake the same meal correctly, not a different one.
✅ 3. She promises to remake the order and check it personally: She says 'I'll have a new plate prepared immediately' and 'I'll personally check it before it leaves the kitchen.'
❌ 4. She denies there is a problem: She acknowledges the problem, saying 'I sincerely apologize for this oversight.'

**Q8:** What is the man's attitude throughout the final conversation?
- 1. Angry and demanding
- 2. Polite despite the inconvenience
- 3. Frustrated and impatient
- 4. Excited about the free offer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Angry and demanding: The man remains calm and polite throughout the conversation, not showing anger.
❌ 2. Polite despite the inconvenience: The man says 'I hate to be a bother' and politely declines the complimentary offer, showing he remains courteous despite the mistake.
✅ 3. Frustrated and impatient: While he has an issue, he expresses it calmly without showing frustration.
❌ 4. Excited about the free offer: The man actually declines the complimentary offer, saying 'That's not necessary, but thank you for offering.'

## Practice 333: Fitness Centre Membership | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a fitness center. The woman is a gym membership consultant, and the man is interested in joining the gym.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm interested in getting a gym membership. Could you tell me about your different plans?
WOMAN: Of course! We have several options. Our basic plan gives you access to all equipment and facilities, while our premium plan includes group fitness classes and personal training sessions.
MAN: That sounds interesting. What's the price difference between the two?
WOMAN: The basic plan is $45 per month with a 12-month commitment, and the premium is $75. Both plans include locker room access and free parking.
MAN: I see. And what about a shorter commitment? I might be relocating in six months for work.
WOMAN: We do offer month-to-month plans, but they're about 20% more expensive. However, if you sign up today, I can waive the registration fee.
MAN: That's good to know. Could I get a tour of the facilities before deciding?
WOMAN: Absolutely! I'd be happy to show you around right now if you have time.

**Q1:** Why does the man want to know about shorter membership terms?
- 1. He is uncertain about the gym's quality
- 2. He may move away for work
- 3. He cannot afford a long-term membership
- 4. He wants to try different gyms
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is uncertain about the gym's quality: The man doesn't express any concerns about the gym's quality.
✅ 2. He may move away for work: The man specifically states "I might be relocating in six months for work" when asking about shorter commitment options.
❌ 3. He cannot afford a long-term membership: The man doesn't mention any financial concerns.
❌ 4. He wants to try different gyms: The man doesn't indicate any interest in trying multiple gyms.

**Q2:** What incentive does the woman offer for signing up immediately?
- 1. A 20% discount on monthly fees
- 2. Free personal training sessions
- 3. No registration fee
- 4. Extra months free
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A 20% discount on monthly fees: The conversation mentions month-to-month plans are 20% more expensive, not a discount.
❌ 2. Free personal training sessions: These are included in the premium plan but not offered as a sign-up incentive.
✅ 3. No registration fee: The woman specifically says "if you sign up today, I can waive the registration fee."
❌ 4. Extra months free: This is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: The facilities look great! I noticed the pool area is under renovation. When will that be completed?
WOMAN: The pool should reopen in about two weeks. We're installing new heating systems and updating the changing rooms.
MAN: Perfect timing then. I'm particularly interested in the swimming classes you mentioned. How does that work with the premium membership?
WOMAN: Swimming classes are actually separate from the premium plan. They're $15 per session, or you can buy a package of 10 classes for $120.
MAN: Oh, I thought they were included. That changes things a bit.
WOMAN: Well, we do have a special aqua-fitness package that combines gym membership with pool access and classes. Would you like to hear about that?
MAN: Yes, please. That might be more what I'm looking for.
WOMAN: Great! Let me pull up those details for you.

**Q3:** What is currently happening at the gym?
- 1. Equipment upgrades
- 2. Pool renovations
- 3. Locker room repairs
- 4. Classroom expansions
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Equipment upgrades: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Pool renovations: The woman states "The pool area is under renovation" and explains they're "installing new heating systems and updating the changing rooms."
❌ 3. Locker room repairs: Only the pool changing rooms are being updated, not the main locker rooms.
❌ 4. Classroom expansions: This is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What surprises the man during the conversation?
- 1. The pool closure duration
- 2. The cost of swimming classes
- 3. The premium membership price
- 4. The swimming classes are not included in membership
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The pool closure duration: The man doesn't express surprise about this.
❌ 2. The cost of swimming classes: He doesn't specifically react to the price.
❌ 3. The premium membership price: He doesn't show surprise about this price.
✅ 4. The swimming classes are not included in membership: The man says "Oh, I thought they were included. That changes things a bit," showing he was surprised by this information.

**Q5:** What does the woman do when the man shows interest in aqua-fitness?
- 1. Offers a discount
- 2. Suggests a different package
- 3. Checks the computer system
- 4. Schedules a class
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Offers a discount: No discount is offered at this point.
❌ 2. Suggests a different package: She's providing information about the aqua-fitness package, not suggesting an alternative.
✅ 3. Checks the computer system: The woman says "Let me pull up those details for you" indicating she's checking her computer system for information.
❌ 4. Schedules a class: No class scheduling occurs at this point in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I've been thinking about the aqua-fitness package. Can I start with a trial period?
WOMAN: Absolutely! We offer a two-week trial for $30. It gives you full access to everything, including two complimentary swimming classes.
MAN: That sounds perfect. What documents do I need to sign up?
WOMAN: Just a valid ID and a credit card for the payment. We'll also need you to fill out a health questionnaire. It takes about 10 minutes.
MAN: Okay, I have my driver's license with me. Oh, and do you offer any student discounts?
WOMAN: Yes, we do! With a valid student ID, you get 15% off any membership plan, including the trial period.
MAN: Great! In that case, I'd like to sign up for the trial. I have my student ID right here.
WOMAN: Perfect! Let's get you started with the paperwork.

**Q6:** What is included in the trial period?
- 1. Full facility access and two swim classes
- 2. Basic membership features only
- 3. Unlimited swimming classes
- 4. Personal training sessions
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Full facility access and two swim classes: The woman says the trial "gives you full access to everything, including two complimentary swimming classes."
❌ 2. Basic membership features only: The trial includes full access, not just basic features.
❌ 3. Unlimited swimming classes: Only two complimentary classes are included, not unlimited.
❌ 4. Personal training sessions: Personal training sessions are not specifically mentioned as part of the trial.

**Q7:** What is required for membership registration?
- 1. Only a credit card
- 2. ID, credit card, and medical certificate
- 3. ID, credit card, and health questionnaire
- 4. Student ID and health questionnaire
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only a credit card: This is incomplete based on the conversation.
❌ 2. ID, credit card, and medical certificate: A medical certificate is not mentioned.
✅ 3. ID, credit card, and health questionnaire: The woman states "Just a valid ID and a credit card for the payment. We'll also need you to fill out a health questionnaire."
❌ 4. Student ID and health questionnaire: A student ID is only needed for the discount, not as a requirement for registration.

**Q8:** What is the final decision made by the man?
- 1. To sign up for a full membership
- 2. To take the trial with student discount
- 3. To think about it more
- 4. To only take swimming classes
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To sign up for a full membership: He decides on the trial, not a full membership.
✅ 2. To take the trial with student discount: The man says "I'd like to sign up for the trial. I have my student ID right here" indicating he's taking the trial with the student discount.
❌ 3. To think about it more: He makes a decision rather than postponing it.
❌ 4. To only take swimming classes: He signs up for the trial, not just swimming classes.

## Practice 334: Dental Appointment Rescheduling | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a dental clinic. The woman is a receptionist, and the man is a patient trying to reschedule his appointment.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my dental appointment scheduled for tomorrow morning at 10 AM.
WOMAN: Yes, how can I help you?
MAN: I need to reschedule it. Something urgent came up at work, and I won't be able to make it.
WOMAN: I understand. Let me check Dr. Thompson's availability. Could you give me your name, please?
MAN: It's Michael Chen.
WOMAN: Thank you, Mr. Chen. I see your appointment here. Let me look at our schedule for this week. We have an opening on Thursday at 2 PM, or Friday morning at 9 AM.
MAN: Thursday afternoon won't work for me. I have meetings all afternoon. How about Friday?
WOMAN: Friday at 9 AM is available. Would you like me to reschedule you for that time?
MAN: Yes, that would be perfect. Thank you.
WOMAN: Alright, I've moved your appointment to Friday at 9 AM with Dr. Thompson.

**Q1:** Why is the man calling initially?
- 1. To cancel his appointment
- 2. To change his insurance information
- 3. To reschedule his appointment
- 4. To confirm his appointment time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel his appointment: He doesn't want to cancel completely, just reschedule.
❌ 2. To change his insurance information: This comes up later, but isn't his initial reason for calling.
✅ 3. To reschedule his appointment: He explicitly states "I need to reschedule it. Something urgent came up at work, and I won't be able to make it."
❌ 4. To confirm his appointment time: He's not calling to confirm, but to change his appointment.

**Q2:** What time is the man's new appointment?
- 1. 10 AM Thursday
- 2. 2 PM Thursday
- 3. 9 AM Friday
- 4. 10 AM Friday
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 10 AM Thursday: This time is not mentioned as an option.
❌ 2. 2 PM Thursday: This was offered but the man declined because he has meetings all afternoon.
✅ 3. 9 AM Friday: The woman confirms "I've moved your appointment to Friday at 9 AM with Dr. Thompson."
❌ 4. 10 AM Friday: This time is not mentioned as an option.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry, one more thing - I just remembered I need to update my insurance information.
WOMAN: No problem at all. What's changed?
MAN: I switched jobs recently, so I have a new insurance provider. It's BlueCross now.
WOMAN: Okay, I can help you with that. Do you have your new insurance card with you?
MAN: Actually, I don't have it yet. It's supposed to arrive in the mail this week.
WOMAN: That's fine. You can bring it with you to your appointment on Friday. Just make sure to arrive about 15 minutes early to fill out the new insurance forms.
MAN: Will do. And just to confirm - the cleaning and check-up are still covered under the new insurance, right?
WOMAN: Yes, most insurance plans cover routine check-ups and cleanings, but we can verify that when you bring in your new card.

**Q3:** What has changed in the man's situation?
- 1. His dental insurance provider
- 2. His dentist
- 3. His contact information
- 4. His employment status
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. His dental insurance provider: The man states "I switched jobs recently, so I have a new insurance provider. It's BlueCross now."
❌ 2. His dentist: There's no mention of changing dentists; he's still seeing Dr. Thompson.
❌ 3. His contact information: He doesn't mention changing contact information.
❌ 4. His employment status: While he mentions switching jobs, this isn't the direct change being discussed - the focus is on his resulting insurance change.

**Q4:** What does the woman suggest the man should do?
- 1. Call back when he gets his insurance card
- 2. Arrive early to handle paperwork
- 3. Contact his insurance company
- 4. Schedule another appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Call back when he gets his insurance card: She says he can bring the card when he gets it, not call back.
✅ 2. Arrive early to handle paperwork: The woman specifically tells him to "arrive about 15 minutes early to fill out the new insurance forms."
❌ 3. Contact his insurance company: She never suggests he contact his insurance company.
❌ 4. Schedule another appointment: She confirms his rescheduled appointment and doesn't suggest making another one.

**Q5:** What is implied about the man's new insurance?
- 1. It won't cover his appointment
- 2. It's more expensive than his previous insurance
- 3. It provides better coverage
- 4. It will likely cover routine services
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It won't cover his appointment: The receptionist suggests it will likely cover the appointment.
❌ 2. It's more expensive than his previous insurance: There's no mention of cost comparison between insurance plans.
❌ 3. It provides better coverage: There's no comparison of coverage quality between his old and new insurance.
✅ 4. It will likely cover routine services: The woman states "most insurance plans cover routine check-ups and cleanings," implying his new plan will likely cover these services too.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I hate to be a bother, but I need to call about my Friday appointment again.
WOMAN: No problem, Mr. Chen. What seems to be the issue?
MAN: I just got an email saying my insurance card won't arrive until next week. Should I reschedule again?
WOMAN: Actually, that won't be necessary. You can still come in for your appointment. We can put a note in your file and update the insurance information later when you receive your card.
MAN: Oh, that's a relief! I was worried I'd have to pay out of pocket.
WOMAN: No, most insurance companies will cover the visit retroactively. Just bring your card in when you get it, and we'll update your file then.
MAN: Perfect, thank you so much for your help.
WOMAN: You're welcome! We'll see you Friday at 9 AM.

**Q6:** Why does the man call for the third time?
- 1. To change his appointment again
- 2. To ask about insurance coverage
- 3. To confirm the appointment time
- 4. To update his contact information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To change his appointment again: The man doesn't try to change his appointment in the third call.
✅ 2. To ask about insurance coverage: The man calls because he received an email saying his insurance card won't arrive until next week, and he's concerned about what this means for his appointment.
❌ 3. To confirm the appointment time: He doesn't ask about confirming the time in the third call.
❌ 4. To update his contact information: The man doesn't mention updating contact information in the third call.

**Q7:** What solution does the receptionist offer?
- 1. To cancel the appointment
- 2. To process the insurance later
- 3. To give him a discount
- 4. To contact his insurance company
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel the appointment: She doesn't suggest canceling.
✅ 2. To process the insurance later: She tells him "We can put a note in your file and update the insurance information later when you receive your card."
❌ 3. To give him a discount: No discount is mentioned.
❌ 4. To contact his insurance company: She doesn't suggest contacting the insurance company.

**Q8:** What was the man's main concern in the final conversation?
- 1. Missing his appointment
- 2. Getting a different dentist
- 3. Having to pay the full cost
- 4. Being late for his appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Missing his appointment: He doesn't express concern about missing the appointment.
❌ 2. Getting a different dentist: There's no mention of changing dentists.
✅ 3. Having to pay the full cost: The man explicitly states "I was worried I'd have to pay out of pocket" when expressing relief at the receptionist's solution.
❌ 4. Being late for his appointment: He doesn't mention concerns about being late.

## Practice 335: Antivirus Conflict Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a computer technical support help desk. The woman is the technical support representative, and the man is a customer calling for assistance.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm having some issues with my laptop. It keeps freezing and running really slow.
WOMAN: I'll be happy to help you with that. Could you tell me what model of laptop you're using?
MAN: It's a Dell Inspiron, I bought it about two years ago.
WOMAN: Okay, and when did you first notice these performance issues?
MAN: It started about a week ago. At first, it was just occasional freezing, but now it's happening every few minutes.
WOMAN: I see. Before we proceed with troubleshooting, could you tell me if you've installed any new software recently?
MAN: Actually, yes. I installed a new antivirus program last week.
WOMAN: That might be the cause. Sometimes having multiple antivirus programs can cause conflicts. Did you uninstall your previous antivirus before installing the new one?
MAN: Oh, no, I didn't think about that. I just installed the new one on top of the old one.
WOMAN: That's likely our problem. Let me guide you through removing one of them.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the man is experiencing?
- 1. His laptop battery is dying
- 2. His laptop is freezing and slow
- 3. His antivirus isn't working
- 4. His laptop won't turn on
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His laptop battery is dying: Battery issues are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. His laptop is freezing and slow: The man's first statement is "I'm having some issues with my laptop. It keeps freezing and running really slow."
❌ 3. His antivirus isn't working: The issue with the antivirus needing updates is discovered later, not the initial problem.
❌ 4. His laptop won't turn on: The laptop is operational, just performing poorly.

**Q2:** What likely caused the problem?
- 1. The laptop is too old
- 2. A virus infection
- 3. Multiple antivirus programs running
- 4. Too many programs running
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The laptop is too old: The age of the laptop (two years) is not identified as the cause.
❌ 2. A virus infection: There's no mention of a virus infection in the conversation.
✅ 3. Multiple antivirus programs running: The woman says "Sometimes having multiple antivirus programs can cause conflicts" and identifies this as "likely our problem."
❌ 4. Too many programs running: Having too many programs running is not mentioned as a cause.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Okay, I'm back at my computer now. How do I uninstall the old antivirus?
WOMAN: First, go to your Control Panel. You can find it by clicking the Windows Start button.
MAN: Right, I'm there. Now what?
WOMAN: Look for 'Programs and Features' or 'Add or Remove Programs.' Do you see either of those?
MAN: Yes, I found Programs and Features. I see both antivirus programs listed here.
WOMAN: Perfect. Right-click on the older antivirus program and select 'Uninstall.'
MAN: It's asking if I'm sure I want to remove it. Should I click 'Yes'?
WOMAN: Yes, go ahead. The uninstallation wizard will guide you through the process.
MAN: Okay, it's working. It says it needs to restart my computer when it's done.
WOMAN: That's normal. Let it restart, and then we can check if that resolved the issue.

**Q3:** Where does the woman tell the man to go first?
- 1. Task Manager
- 2. System Settings
- 3. Control Panel
- 4. File Explorer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Task Manager: The woman never mentions Task Manager in the conversation.
❌ 2. System Settings: The woman does not direct the man to System Settings.
✅ 3. Control Panel: The woman specifically says "First, go to your Control Panel. You can find it by clicking the Windows Start button."
❌ 4. File Explorer: File Explorer is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What happens after uninstalling the program?
- 1. The computer needs updating
- 2. The computer needs restarting
- 3. The screen freezes
- 4. Nothing happens
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The computer needs updating: While updates are mentioned later, this isn't what happens immediately after uninstallation.
✅ 2. The computer needs restarting: The man says "It's asking if I'm sure I want to remove it" and then "Okay, it's working. It says it needs to restart my computer when it's done."
❌ 3. The screen freezes: There's no mention of the screen freezing after uninstallation.
❌ 4. Nothing happens: Something does happen - the computer needs to restart.

**Q5:** What action does the woman guide the man through?
- 1. Installing new software
- 2. Updating Windows
- 3. Removing an antivirus program
- 4. Backing up data
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Installing new software: The woman doesn't guide him through installing anything new.
❌ 2. Updating Windows: Windows updates are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Removing an antivirus program: The woman guides him through uninstalling the older antivirus program to resolve the conflict.
❌ 4. Backing up data: Data backup is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling back after restarting my computer.
WOMAN: Great! How is it running now?
MAN: Much better actually, but I noticed something else. My new antivirus is showing that I haven't updated it yet.
WOMAN: That's important to address. An outdated antivirus won't provide proper protection.
MAN: How do I update it? I see a notification, but when I click it, nothing happens.
WOMAN: Try right-clicking the antivirus icon in your taskbar and select 'Check for Updates.'
MAN: Okay, done. Now it's downloading something.
WOMAN: Perfect. Let it complete the update. Once it's finished, your computer should be running smoothly and securely.
MAN: Thank you so much for your help. I really appreciate it.
WOMAN: You're welcome! Don't hesitate to call back if you need anything else.

**Q6:** What new issue does the man discover?
- 1. The antivirus needs updating
- 2. The computer is still slow
- 3. The old program is still there
- 4. The updates won't install
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The antivirus needs updating: The man says "My new antivirus is showing that I haven't updated it yet."
❌ 2. The computer is still slow: The man actually says it's running "Much better actually."
❌ 3. The old program is still there: The man successfully uninstalled the old program.
❌ 4. The updates won't install: The man mentions a notification issue, but later successfully starts the update process.

**Q7:** How does the woman suggest fixing the new issue?
- 1. Reinstall the program
- 2. Contact technical support
- 3. Right-click and check for updates
- 4. Download updates manually
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Reinstall the program: Reinstallation is not suggested.
❌ 2. Contact technical support: The woman doesn't suggest contacting additional support.
✅ 3. Right-click and check for updates: The woman specifically says "Try right-clicking the antivirus icon in your taskbar and select 'Check for Updates.'"
❌ 4. Download updates manually: Manual downloading is not mentioned.

**Q8:** What can be concluded from the conversation?
- 1. The man needs a new laptop
- 2. The problem was successfully resolved
- 3. The woman couldn't help
- 4. The man needs professional help
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man needs a new laptop: There's no indication that the laptop needs to be replaced.
✅ 2. The problem was successfully resolved: The man says his computer is running "Much better actually" after removing one antivirus, and then successfully starts updating the remaining antivirus.
❌ 3. The woman couldn't help: The woman successfully helped resolve the issues.
❌ 4. The man needs professional help: The man was able to fix his issues with the woman's guidance over the phone.

## Practice 336: EV Charger Installation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at an electric vehicle charging station company. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a homeowner requesting installation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm interested in getting an EV charging station installed at my home. Can you help me with that?
WOMAN: Of course! We offer several charging station options. Do you already own an electric vehicle?
MAN: Yes, I just bought a Tesla Model 3, and the portable charger isn't fast enough for my needs.
WOMAN: I understand. For Tesla vehicles, we recommend our Level 2 home charging station. It can fully charge your car in about 8 hours overnight.
MAN: That sounds perfect. What's involved in the installation process?
WOMAN: First, we'll need to schedule a home assessment to check your electrical panel capacity and determine the best location for installation. The assessment fee is $150, which will be credited toward your installation if you proceed.
MAN: Okay, that seems reasonable. How long does the assessment usually take?
WOMAN: The assessment typically takes about an hour. We can schedule it for any weekday between 9 AM and 5 PM.

**Q1:** What is the main reason the man is contacting the company?
- 1. To upgrade his electrical panel
- 2. To purchase a Tesla vehicle
- 3. To install an EV charging station
- 4. To repair his portable charger
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To upgrade his electrical panel: This becomes necessary but isn't his initial reason for calling.
❌ 2. To purchase a Tesla vehicle: He already owns a Tesla Model 3.
✅ 3. To install an EV charging station: His opening statement is 'I'm interested in getting an EV charging station installed at my home.'
❌ 4. To repair his portable charger: He mentions it's not fast enough but doesn't want it repaired.

**Q2:** What does the assessment fee include?
- 1. Installation costs
- 2. Credit towards installation
- 3. Permit processing
- 4. Equipment costs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Installation costs: The fee doesn't include installation costs directly.
✅ 2. Credit towards installation: The woman states the $150 fee 'will be credited toward your installation if you proceed.'
❌ 3. Permit processing: Permits are handled separately and aren't part of the assessment fee.
❌ 4. Equipment costs: The assessment fee doesn't cover equipment costs.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I'm calling about the home assessment scheduled for tomorrow. I was wondering what I need to prepare?
WOMAN: Yes, I have your appointment here. The main thing is ensuring clear access to your electrical panel and the garage or driveway area where you'd like the charging station installed.
MAN: Great. Also, my neighbor mentioned something about permits. Will I need those?
WOMAN: Yes, most municipalities require electrical permits for EV charger installations. Don't worry though - we handle all the paperwork for you.
MAN: That's a relief. How long does the permit process usually take?
WOMAN: It varies by location, but typically 5-10 business days. Once approved, we can usually complete the installation within a week.
MAN: Perfect. And about the electrical panel - mine is pretty old. Could that be an issue?
WOMAN: We'll check during the assessment. If upgrades are needed, we'll include that in your quote.

**Q3:** Why is the man calling about his appointment?
- 1. To reschedule the assessment
- 2. To know what preparation is needed
- 3. To cancel the appointment
- 4. To change the installation location
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To reschedule the assessment: The man doesn't ask to change the appointment time.
✅ 2. To know what preparation is needed: The man specifically asks 'what I need to prepare' for the scheduled assessment.
❌ 3. To cancel the appointment: There's no indication he wants to cancel.
❌ 4. To change the installation location: He doesn't mention wanting to change the location.

**Q4:** What is the company's role regarding permits?
- 1. They advise customers how to apply
- 2. They manage the entire process
- 3. They share the responsibility
- 4. They provide permit documentation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They advise customers how to apply: The company doesn't just provide advice.
✅ 2. They manage the entire process: The woman says 'we handle all the paperwork for you,' indicating complete management.
❌ 3. They share the responsibility: There's no indication of shared responsibility.
❌ 4. They provide permit documentation: This is less comprehensive than what they actually do.

**Q5:** What timeline is suggested for the complete process?
- 1. About one week total
- 2. 5-10 days total
- 3. 2-3 weeks total
- 4. 1-2 months total
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. About one week total: This is only the installation time after permits are approved.
❌ 2. 5-10 days total: This is only the permit processing time.
✅ 3. 2-3 weeks total: Combining the 5-10 business days for permits plus about a week for installation gives roughly 2-3 weeks total.
❌ 4. 1-2 months total: This timeframe is longer than what's indicated in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I received the quote for my charging station installation, but I have a few questions about the pricing.
WOMAN: Of course! I have your quote right here. What would you like to know?
MAN: Well, I see there's a separate line item for a 'service upgrade.' Is that really necessary?
WOMAN: Based on the assessment, your current 100-amp panel needs to be upgraded to 200 amps to safely handle the charging station. This ensures your home's electrical system won't be overloaded.
MAN: I see. And what about this 'surge protection' add-on?
WOMAN: That's optional, but highly recommended. It protects both your charging station and vehicle from power surges.
MAN: Makes sense. One last thing - do you offer any financing options?
WOMAN: Yes, we partner with several banks offering 0% interest for 12 months or lower rates for longer terms. Would you like me to send you the financing information?

**Q6:** Why does the electrical panel need upgrading?
- 1. It's too old
- 2. To handle the charging load safely
- 3. It's damaged
- 4. To meet local regulations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too old: While the man mentions his panel is old, the woman specifically explains the upgrade is needed for capacity reasons, not age.
✅ 2. To handle the charging load safely: The woman clearly states the 100-amp panel needs upgrading to 200 amps to safely handle the charging station and prevent overloading.
❌ 3. It's damaged: There is no mention of any damage to the electrical panel in the conversation.
❌ 4. To meet local regulations: The woman explains it's for safety and capacity, not regulatory compliance.

**Q7:** How is the surge protection presented to the customer?
- 1. As a mandatory requirement
- 2. As a recommended addition
- 3. As a safety regulation
- 4. As a warranty requirement
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. As a mandatory requirement: The woman clearly states it's optional, not mandatory.
✅ 2. As a recommended addition: The woman says it's 'optional, but highly recommended' for protecting the charging station and vehicle.
❌ 3. As a safety regulation: It's presented as a protective measure, not a regulatory requirement.
❌ 4. As a warranty requirement: There's no mention of warranty conditions in relation to surge protection.

**Q8:** What financing option is mentioned first?
- 1. Low-interest long-term loan
- 2. 12-month interest-free financing
- 3. Bank-specific loans
- 4. Variable rate financing
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Low-interest long-term loan: This is mentioned second as 'lower rates for longer terms.'
✅ 2. 12-month interest-free financing: The woman first mentions '0% interest for 12 months' before other options.
❌ 3. Bank-specific loans: While banks are mentioned as partners, this isn't the first option described.
❌ 4. Variable rate financing: This type of financing isn't specifically mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 337: Community Centre Fitness Tiers | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a woman and a staff member at a community center about fitness class changes.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the woman's visit?
- 1. To cancel her current membership
- 2. To inquire about membership changes
- 3. To sign up for new fitness classes
- 4. To complain about crowded classes
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel her membership: The woman never mentions wanting to cancel her membership.
✅ 2. To inquire about membership changes: The woman begins by saying "I saw the notice about changes to the fitness programs starting next month. I'm a bit confused about what this means for my current membership," clearly indicating she's there to inquire about the changes.
❌ 3. To sign up for new fitness classes: While she discusses classes, she doesn't come specifically to sign up for new ones.
❌ 4. To complain about crowded classes: Although she mentions crowding, this isn't the reason for her visit.

**Q2:** What special offer is available to existing members?
- 1. Free access to all new classes
- 2. Priority booking for all tiers
- 3. A reduced rate for Elite membership
- 4. Unlimited locker room access
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Free access to all new classes: No free access is mentioned.
❌ 2. Priority booking for all tiers: Priority booking depends on membership tier, not existing member status.
✅ 3. A reduced rate for Elite membership: The man says, "since you're an existing member, we're offering a special grandfather rate if you switch to Elite within the first month of the change. Instead of the regular $85 monthly fee, you'll pay $70."
❌ 4. Unlimited locker room access: No special locker room access is offered to existing members.

**Q3:** What change is being made to spin classes?
- 1. They will be offered less frequently
- 2. They will have smaller class sizes
- 3. They will be available daily except Sunday
- 4. They will only be available in the evening
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They will be offered less frequently: The opposite is true - they're adding more classes.
❌ 2. They will have smaller class sizes: While class sizes are being capped at 15 for spin classes, there's no indication this is smaller than before.
✅ 3. They will be available daily except Sunday: The man specifically says, "As for spin classes, we're adding a second instructor and offering classes every day except Sunday."
❌ 4. They will only be available in the evening: There's no mention of restricting spin classes to evening times only.

**Q4:** What advantage do Elite members have over other tiers?
- 1. Exclusive access to certain classes
- 2. Earlier class booking privileges
- 3. Special locker room access
- 4. Discounted monthly rates
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Exclusive access to certain classes: The content doesn't mention any classes exclusive to Elite members.
✅ 2. Earlier class booking privileges: The man states, "Elite members will have priority booking rights - they can reserve spots up to a week in advance" compared to Premium (3 days) and Basic (same-day only).
❌ 3. Special locker room access: The man specifically says locker room access remains the same for all tiers.
❌ 4. Discounted monthly rates: While existing members get a special rate for Elite membership, this isn't an advantage of the Elite tier itself over other tiers.

**Q5:** What is NOT changing under the new system?
- 1. The membership structure
- 2. The class schedule
- 3. The locker room policy
- 4. The booking system
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The membership structure: This is definitely changing, as they're moving from the current system to Basic, Premium, and Elite tiers.
❌ 2. The class schedule: The man mentions they've "expanded our class offerings" with more yoga and spin classes available.
✅ 3. The locker room policy: The man specifically states, "No, that will remain the same for all membership tiers. You'll still have access to day-use lockers, and monthly locker rentals are available for an additional fee."
❌ 4. The booking system: They're implementing a new booking system with different advance booking privileges based on membership tier.

**Q6:** What does the woman decide to do?
- 1. Cancel her membership
- 2. Attend an information session
- 3. Keep her current membership
- 4. Switch to Basic membership
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cancel her membership: She shows no intention of canceling.
✅ 2. Attend an information session: At the end of the conversation, she says, "I'll definitely attend one of those sessions" and "I'll stop by this Saturday for the information session to learn more about the Elite membership."
❌ 3. Keep her current membership: She doesn't decide this during the conversation.
❌ 4. Switch to Basic membership: She seems interested in the Elite membership, not Basic, but doesn't make a final decision during this conversation.

$4c

✅ 3. The menu appears different from before: The man says 'I'm a bit confused. The menu looks completely different today.'

- 3. It is only available on special days.
- 4. It has a new cooking method.

❌ 3. It is only available on special days: There is no mention of the salmon being limited to special days.

❌ 3. Gluten-free options: The wheat symbol indicates gluten-free options, not the leaf symbol.

- 2. They need to order set menus.
- 3. They must book one day ahead.

✅ 3. They must book one day ahead: The manager states 'For parties of six or more, we now require reservations at least 24 hours in advance.'
❌ 4. They have a two-hour time limit: The dining time limit mentioned is 90 minutes, not two hours, and applies to all tables during peak hours.

**Q5:** When are wine pairings offered at the restaurant?
- 1. Every Wednesday
- 2. Every Thursday
- 3. Every Friday
- 4. Every weekend
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every Wednesday: Wednesday is International Cuisine Night with dishes from different countries, not wine pairings.
❌ 2. Every Thursday: Thursday is Farm-to-Table evening with locally sourced ingredients, not wine pairings.
✅ 3. Every Friday: The manager states 'Fridays we offer a special tasting menu with wine pairings.'
❌ 4. Every weekend: Only Friday is mentioned for wine pairings, not the entire weekend.

**Q6:** How many points are needed for a free main course?
- 1. 200 points
- 2. 300 points
- 3. 400 points
- 4. 500 points
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 200 points: At 200 points, you can get a free appetizer or dessert, not a main course.
❌ 2. 300 points: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. 400 points: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. 500 points: The manager clearly states 'At 500 points, you can get any main course for free.'

## Practice 340: Hospital Visiting Rules | Listening Practice Test

$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man at the hospital?
- 1. To register as a new patient
- 2. To learn about visitor policies
- 3. To meet with hospital staff
- 4. To collect medical records
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To register as a new patient: The man states he's there to visit his mother, not to become a patient.
✅ 2. To learn about visitor policies: The man explicitly states "I heard there are some new visiting policies. I wanted to make sure I understand them correctly."
❌ 3. To meet with hospital staff: While he does speak with staff, his primary purpose is to visit his mother and learn about policies.
❌ 4. To collect medical records: There is no mention of collecting any records.

**Q2:** What is one advantage of being a designated visitor?
- 1. Can bring any food items
- 2. Can visit at any time
- 3. Can stay longer than regular hours
- 4. Can skip the check-in process
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Can bring any food items: The conversation states that all food items must be approved by nursing staff, with no exception for designated visitors.
❌ 2. Can visit at any time: Designated visitors still must follow visiting hours, with only limited extensions.
✅ 3. Can stay longer than regular hours: The woman states "designated visitors can stay up to one hour past regular visiting hours if needed."
❌ 4. Can skip the check-in process: The woman mentions that "all visitors" must check in at the nursing station, with no exceptions noted.

**Q3:** Why are fresh flowers not allowed in patient rooms?
- 1. They are too expensive
- 2. They attract insects
- 3. They take up too much space
- 4. They pose health risks
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are too expensive: Cost is not mentioned as a reason for prohibiting fresh flowers.
❌ 2. They attract insects: The conversation doesn't mention insects as a concern.
❌ 3. They take up too much space: Space concerns are not mentioned in relation to flowers.
✅ 4. They pose health risks: The woman explains that fresh flowers are not permitted "due to allergies and infection control," which are health risks.

**Q4:** What must visitors do before entering patient rooms?
- 1. Change their clothes
- 2. Use hand sanitizer
- 3. Get temperature checked
- 4. Sign a waiver form
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Change their clothes: The conversation doesn't mention changing clothes as a requirement.
✅ 2. Use hand sanitizer: The woman clearly states "All visitors must use hand sanitizer at the entrance and when entering patient rooms."
❌ 3. Get temperature checked: Temperature checks are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Sign a waiver form: There is no mention of signing any forms before entering rooms.

**Q5:** What does a green visitor badge indicate?
- 1. Hospital staff member
- 2. Designated visitor
- 3. Emergency contact
- 4. Social worker
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Hospital staff member: The conversation doesn't mention green badges being for staff members.
❌ 2. Designated visitor: The woman specifically states "Green badges are for designated visitors like yourself."
✅ 3. Emergency contact: The conversation doesn't associate green badges with emergency contacts.
❌ 4. Social worker: The conversation mentions that red badges are for those with special access like social workers, not green badges.

**Q6:** What is required for after-hours visits?
- 1. Special permission from security
- 2. Written request from patient
- 3. Approval from charge nurse
- 4. Additional registration fee
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Special permission from security: Security is not mentioned in relation to after-hours visits.
❌ 2. Written request from patient: The conversation doesn't mention patients needing to make written requests.
✅ 3. Approval from charge nurse: The woman specifically states "You'll need to contact the charge nurse, who can authorize after-hours visits."
❌ 4. Additional registration fee: No fees are mentioned for after-hours visits.

## Practice 341: Daycare Schedule Changes | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a parent and a daycare center administrator about policy changes.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the man visit the daycare center?
- 1. To enroll his son in the daycare
- 2. To complain about the new changes
- 3. To discuss policy updates in person
- 4. To update emergency contact information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To enroll his son in the daycare: The man's son Tommy is already enrolled at the daycare.
❌ 2. To complain about the new changes: The man doesn't express complaints, he asks questions to understand the changes.
✅ 3. To discuss policy updates in person: The man clearly states he received an email about policy changes and wanted to discuss them in person.
❌ 4. To update emergency contact information: While this is mentioned as a requirement, it's not why he came to visit.

**Q2:** What is the main concern the man expresses about the new schedule?
- 1. The length of outdoor playtime
- 2. The timing of his son's nap
- 3. The new pickup time policy
- 4. The educational activities schedule
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The length of outdoor playtime: The man doesn't express concern about outdoor playtime duration.
✅ 2. The timing of his son's nap: The man specifically asks how the new schedule will affect Tommy's rest time since he usually naps right after lunch.
❌ 3. The new pickup time policy: While discussed, this isn't presented as a concern by the man.
❌ 4. The educational activities schedule: The man shows interest in these activities but doesn't express concern about them.

**Q3:** What change is being made to the meal program?
- 1. More variety in meal options
- 2. Reduced portion sizes
- 3. Switch to organic food
- 4. New meal schedule
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. More variety in meal options: The administrator doesn't mention adding more variety to meals.
❌ 2. Reduced portion sizes: There's no mention of changing portion sizes.
✅ 3. Switch to organic food: The administrator clearly states they're switching to all-organic meals and snacks starting next month.
❌ 4. New meal schedule: No changes to meal timing or schedule were mentioned.

**Q4:** What new security measure is NOT mentioned?
- 1. Installation of security cameras
- 2. New entry system with codes
- 3. Written authorization for pickups
- 4. Updated emergency contacts
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Installation of security cameras: This security measure was never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. New entry system with codes: The administrator clearly states they're installing a new entry system and all parents will receive unique access codes.
❌ 3. Written authorization for pickups: The administrator mentions they now require written authorization for any new pickup person.
❌ 4. Updated emergency contacts: The administrator specifically says they need updated emergency contact information from everyone.

**Q5:** What is the new policy regarding late pickup?
- 1. Parents must call if running late
- 2. $$10 charge per 15 minutes late
- 3. Children must be picked up by 6:00 PM
- 4. Three strikes policy for late pickup
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Parents must call if running late: This requirement was not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. $10 charge per 15 minutes late: The administrator clearly states there's a late fee of ten dollars for every fifteen minutes after 5:30 PM.
❌ 3. Children must be picked up by 6:00 PM: The pickup deadline is 5:30 PM, not 6:00 PM.
❌ 4. Three strikes policy for late pickup: No such policy was mentioned in the conversation.

**Q6:** When will the daycare implement these changes?
- 1. Next week
- 2. Immediately
- 3. Beginning of next month
- 4. Gradually over three months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Next week: The administrator mentions sending information next week, but not implementing changes then.
❌ 2. Immediately: The changes are not being implemented right away.
✅ 3. Beginning of next month: The administrator clearly states that everything will be implemented starting the first of next month.
❌ 4. Gradually over three months: There's no mention of a gradual implementation over multiple months.

**Q4:** What must staff do before taking orders?

- 2. Ask about allergies

❌ 3. Verify table numbers: This isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Review menu prices: This isn't mentioned as a requirement before taking orders.

**Q5:** What is required of staff regarding the new policy?
- 1. Weekly kitchen inspections
- 2. Monthly team meetings
- 3. Daily safety reports
- 4. Online training completion

❌ 3. Daily safety reports: This isn't mentioned as a requirement for staff.
✅ 4. Online training completion: The woman states "Yes, everyone will need to complete the new training program by the end of this month. It's a two-hour online course, and you'll get paid for your time."

**Q6:** What color indicates allergen-free preparation tools?

- 2. Purple

❌ 4. Green: Green isn't mentioned as a color code in the conversation.

## Practice 343: Laboratory Equipment Maintenance | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a laboratory technician and a safety coordinator discussing equipment maintenance.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the new maintenance procedures?
- 1. To reduce equipment costs
- 2. To prevent equipment failures
- 3. To improve lab efficiency
- 4. To meet new regulations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To reduce equipment costs: Cost reduction is not mentioned as a reason for the new procedures.
✅ 2. To prevent equipment failures: The safety coordinator confirms that the changes are due to "the incident in Building C last month" and states that "The equipment malfunction could have been prevented with more frequent maintenance."
❌ 3. To improve lab efficiency: While the digital system might improve efficiency, this is not given as the main reason for the changes.
❌ 4. To meet new regulations: There is no mention of external regulations driving these changes.

**Q2:** What change is being made to the maintenance schedule?
- 1. Monthly to quarterly checks
- 2. Weekly to monthly checks
- 3. Monthly to bi-weekly checks
- 4. Bi-weekly to daily checks
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Monthly to quarterly checks: This is incorrect as the change is increasing frequency, not decreasing it.
❌ 2. Weekly to monthly checks: This would be decreasing the frequency, which is opposite to what's being implemented.
✅ 3. Monthly to bi-weekly checks: The safety coordinator specifically states, "Instead of monthly inspections, we're now requiring bi-weekly checks."
❌ 4. Bi-weekly to daily checks: This change is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What is a key feature of the new digital logging system?
- 1. It requires less staff training
- 2. It provides automatic alerts
- 3. It reduces paperwork
- 4. It increases accuracy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It requires less staff training: The conversation mentions mandatory training is needed for the new system, contradicting this option.
✅ 2. It provides automatic alerts: The safety coordinator states that the digital system "sends automatic reminders and flags any overdue maintenance tasks."
❌ 3. It reduces paperwork: While this might be true, it's not specifically mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. It increases accuracy: While the system might improve accuracy, this specific benefit isn't mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What is required before using calibrated equipment?
- 1. Supervisor approval
- 2. Digital system entry
- 3. Second technician verification
- 4. Safety office clearance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Supervisor approval: This requirement is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Digital system entry: While a digital system is being implemented, it's not specifically required before using calibrated equipment.
✅ 3. Second technician verification: The safety coordinator states, "All calibrations must now be verified by a second technician before the equipment can be used."
❌ 4. Safety office clearance: This requirement is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What must staff do during a non-emergency shutdown?
- 1. Contact maintenance first
- 2. Wait for supervisor approval
- 3. Log the incident before leaving
- 4. Follow evacuation procedures
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Contact maintenance first: This step is not mentioned in the non-emergency shutdown procedure.
❌ 2. Wait for supervisor approval: This requirement is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Log the incident before leaving: The safety coordinator explains that "After hitting the emergency stop, you'll need to log the incident immediately in the digital system and contact the safety office before leaving the area."
❌ 4. Follow evacuation procedures: The conversation specifically distinguishes between emergency evacuations and non-emergency shutdowns, stating that evacuation procedures are only for immediate danger situations.

**Q6:** When will the safety audits begin?
- 1. In two weeks
- 2. After one month
- 3. Next week
- 4. Immediately
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. In two weeks: This is when the new protocols will be implemented, not when audits begin.
✅ 2. After one month: The safety coordinator states, "The first round of audits will begin one month after implementation."
❌ 3. Next week: Next week is when training and documentation will be distributed, not when audits begin.
❌ 4. Immediately: The conversation clearly indicates a timeline for implementation and audits, not an immediate start.

## Practice 344: Immigration Office Policy Changes | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a woman and a man at an immigration office about recent policy changes.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man visiting the immigration office?
- 1. To submit his permanent residency application
- 2. To learn about new immigration policies
- 3. To get his documents certified
- 4. To schedule a language test
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To submit his permanent residency application: The man states he is planning to apply next month, not submitting today.
✅ 2. To learn about new immigration policies: The man says "I'm here because I heard there were some major changes to the visa application procedures... I want to make sure I have the correct information."
❌ 3. To get his documents certified: The man doesn't mention needing document certification as his reason for visiting.
❌ 4. To schedule a language test: The man has already taken his language test four months ago, as mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What change has been made to document requirements?
- 1. Documents must be translated officially
- 2. Original documents are no longer needed
- 3. Police certificates are needed for 7 years
- 4. Digital copies are now accepted
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Documents must be translated officially: There is no mention of translation requirements in the conversation.
❌ 2. Original documents are no longer needed: The woman mentions that applicants still need to bring in original documents after online submission.
✅ 3. Police certificates are needed for 7 years: The woman specifically states "Previously, we required police clearance certificates from the past five years, but now we need them from the past seven years."
❌ 4. Digital copies are now accepted: While digital copies are mentioned as part of the new online submission process, this is not presented as a change to document requirements but rather to the submission process.

**Q3:** What is mentioned about processing times?
- 1. They have increased by 4 weeks
- 2. They have decreased by 4 weeks
- 3. They remain the same as before
- 4. They depend on application type
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They have increased by 4 weeks: The woman states that processing times have improved, not increased.
✅ 2. They have decreased by 4 weeks: The woman specifically says "Standard applications are now processed within 12 weeks instead of the previous 16 weeks," which means processing times have decreased by 4 weeks.
❌ 3. They remain the same as before: The conversation clearly indicates that processing times have changed.
❌ 4. They depend on application type: While the woman mentions that the improved processing time "only applies if all documentation is submitted correctly the first time," she doesn't state that different application types have different processing times.

**Q4:** What has changed about language test results?
- 1. They are valid for a shorter period
- 2. The minimum scores are lower
- 3. New test formats are required
- 4. Tests must be taken locally
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. They are valid for a shorter period: The woman states that "test results are now valid for only 18 months instead of two years," which means they are valid for a shorter period.
❌ 2. The minimum scores are lower: The woman mentions that "The minimum language score requirement has increased slightly," not decreased.
❌ 3. New test formats are required: There is no mention of new test formats in the conversation.
❌ 4. Tests must be taken locally: The conversation does not mention where tests must be taken.

**Q5:** What is new about the application submission process?
- 1. Applications must be mailed in
- 2. Online submission is required first
- 3. In-person applications only
- 4. Phone appointments are needed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Applications must be mailed in: The conversation doesn't mention mailing applications.
✅ 2. Online submission is required first: The woman states "We've introduced a new online portal where you must submit your application first, before bringing in the physical documents."
❌ 3. In-person applications only: This contradicts the information about the new online submission requirement.
❌ 4. Phone appointments are needed: There is no mention of phone appointments in the conversation.

**Q6:** What benefit is available for the next 30 days?
- 1. Faster processing times
- 2. Simplified documentation
- 3. Original fee rates
- 4. Free document certification
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Faster processing times: The conversation doesn't mention faster processing as a special benefit for the next 30 days.
❌ 2. Simplified documentation: There is no mention of simplified documentation as a temporary benefit.
✅ 3. Original fee rates: The woman states "if you submit your application within the next 30 days, you can still pay the old rate. We're offering this grace period to help people adjust to the new system."
❌ 4. Free document certification: The conversation doesn't mention free document certification as a benefit.

## Practice 345: Alpine Peak Ski Rentals | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a ski resort staff member about winter safety guidelines and equipment rental.

$4d

**Q1:** What is included in the basic rental package?

- 2. skis, boots, and poles only
- 3. skis and boots only
- 4. skis, boots, poles, and insurance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. skis, boots, poles, and helmets: The staff member clearly states that helmets rent separately at $12 each.
✅ 2. skis, boots, and poles only: The staff member says "We'll need to fit each person individually for skis, boots, and poles. The basic rental package is $45 per person for a full day."
❌ 3. skis and boots only: The basic package includes poles as well, not just skis and boots.
❌ 4. skis, boots, poles, and insurance: Insurance is mentioned as optional for $5 per person, not included in the basic package.

**Q2:** What is mandatory for all skiers at the resort?
- 1. purchasing winter clothing
- 2. taking group lessons
- 3. wearing helmets
- 4. buying damage insurance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. purchasing winter clothing: The staff recommends appropriate winter clothing but doesn't say it must be purchased at the resort.
❌ 2. taking group lessons: Lessons are offered but not mandatory. The staff says "I'd highly recommend them for first-timers."
✅ 3. wearing helmets: The staff member explicitly states "Helmets are mandatory for all skiers but rent separately at $12 each."
❌ 4. buying damage insurance: Insurance is described as "optional" for $5 per person.

**Q3:** What must first-time skiers do before skiing?
- 1. purchase special equipment
- 2. attend a safety briefing
- 3. sign up for lessons
- 4. get a trail map
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. purchase special equipment: There's no mention of first-time skiers needing to purchase special equipment.
✅ 2. attend a safety briefing: The staff member clearly states "all first-time skiers must attend a 30-minute safety briefing before hitting the slopes."
❌ 3. sign up for lessons: While lessons are recommended, they're not mandatory. The staff says "I'd highly recommend them for first-timers."
❌ 4. get a trail map: There's no mention of trail maps being required before skiing.

**Q4:** How can skiers get help in an emergency?
- 1. call the main office
- 2. use their mobile phones
- 3. wave a flag for attention
- 4. contact staff or use emergency phones
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. call the main office: There's no mention of calling the main office in an emergency.
❌ 2. use their mobile phones: Mobile phones aren't mentioned as an emergency contact method.
❌ 3. wave a flag for attention: No flag system is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. contact staff or use emergency phones: The staff member explains "In case of emergency, there are red emergency phones at every lift station. You can also alert any staff member wearing a yellow jacket, and they'll radio for assistance."

**Q5:** What does the optional insurance cover?
- 1. medical expenses
- 2. equipment damage
- 3. lesson cancellations
- 4. personal belongings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. medical expenses: Medical coverage isn't mentioned as part of the optional insurance.
✅ 2. equipment damage: The staff member clearly states "We also offer optional insurance for $5 per person that covers any potential equipment damage."
❌ 3. lesson cancellations: There's no mention of insurance covering lesson cancellations.
❌ 4. personal belongings: The insurance is specifically for equipment damage, not personal belongings.

**Q6:** Why does the staff member suggest arriving early?
- 1. to avoid long lift lines
- 2. to get better equipment
- 3. to complete pre-skiing preparations
- 4. to get discounted rates
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. to avoid long lift lines: The staff member doesn't mention avoiding lift lines as a reason to arrive early.
❌ 2. to get better equipment: There's no mention of getting better equipment by arriving early.
✅ 3. to complete pre-skiing preparations: The staff member specifically says "I'd recommend arriving at least an hour before you plan to start skiing. That gives you time for equipment fitting, the safety briefing, and getting oriented."
❌ 4. to get discounted rates: No discounted rates for early arrival are mentioned in the conversation.

✅ 2. She is interested in conservation efforts: The woman directly states, "I'm particularly interested in conservation. Which tour would cover that topic the most?"
❌ 3. She needs access to the plant database: Database access is included in the tour price but is not mentioned as her reason for interest.
❌ 4. She wants to join the weekend tours: The woman actually prefers weekday tours, stating "I'd actually prefer a weekday."

**Q2:** What recent change has been made to the tours?
- 1. They added new conservation information.
- 2. They reduced the tour duration.
- 3. They increased the group size.
- 4. They changed the starting times.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. They added new conservation information.: The man states, "We've actually just updated our tour program to include more information about our plant conservation efforts."
❌ 2. They reduced the tour duration.: There is no mention of reducing tour duration in the conversation.
❌ 3. They increased the group size.: The conversation indicates they're actually running at half capacity (6 people instead of 12).
❌ 4. They changed the starting times.: There is no mention of changing starting times in the conversation.

**Q3:** Why are tour groups currently limited to six people?
- 1. To maintain social distancing.
- 2. Due to new garden policies.
- 3. Because of guide availability.
- 4. To protect rare plants.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To maintain social distancing: Social distancing is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Due to new garden policies: The man states, "Currently, we're running at half capacity due to our new policies, so just six people per tour."
❌ 3. Because of guide availability: Guide availability is not mentioned as a reason for the reduced capacity.
❌ 4. To protect rare plants: While rare plants are discussed, they are not given as the reason for the reduced group size.

**Q4:** What is included in the tour price?
- 1. A plant identification guide only
- 2. Access to conservation programs
- 3. A guidebook and database access
- 4. Special access to the seed bank
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A plant identification guide only: This is incomplete as more is included in the price.
❌ 2. Access to conservation programs: The tour discusses conservation programs but this is not listed as something included in the price.
✅ 3. A guidebook and database access: The man states, "It's $25 per person, which includes a detailed guidebook and access to our digital plant database for three months."
❌ 4. Special access to the seed bank: The seed bank is mentioned as part of their conservation programs, but access to it is not included in the tour price.

**Q5:** What happens if someone cancels less than 24 hours before the tour?
- 1. They lose their payment completely.
- 2. They receive a partial refund.
- 3. They can reschedule for free.
- 4. They get a garden credit.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They lose their payment completely: This is not stated in the conversation.
❌ 2. They receive a partial refund: The man does not mention a partial refund option.
❌ 3. They can reschedule for free: Rescheduling is not mentioned as an option.
✅ 4. They get a garden credit: The man says, "Any later than that [24 hours], and we can only offer a garden credit valid for six months."

**Q6:** Which tour schedule does the woman prefer?
- 1. Weekend mornings
- 2. Weekday afternoons
- 3. Weekday mornings
- 4. Saturday evenings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Weekend mornings: The woman specifically says she would prefer a weekday.
❌ 2. Weekday afternoons: The tours are only offered at 10 AM, which is morning, not afternoon.
✅ 3. Weekday mornings: The woman says "I'd actually prefer a weekday" and books for next Thursday, which would be at 10 AM as mentioned by the man.
❌ 4. Saturday evenings: The tours are only offered at 10 AM, not in the evening, and the woman prefers weekdays.
