## Practice 348: Dog Grooming Appointment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a pet grooming salon. The woman works at the salon, and the man is a customer trying to book an appointment for his dog.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there! I was wondering if I could make an appointment to get my dog groomed.
WOMAN: Of course! I'd be happy to help. What kind of dog do you have?
MAN: He's a Golden Retriever, about two years old. His name is Max.
WOMAN: Lovely breed! When was the last time Max had a professional grooming session?
MAN: Actually, this would be his first time. We usually bathe him at home, but his coat is getting quite thick and tangled.
WOMAN: I see. For Golden Retrievers, we recommend our full-service package. It includes a bath, blow-dry, brush-out, nail trimming, and ear cleaning. The session usually takes about two hours.
MAN: That sounds perfect. How much does that cost?
WOMAN: For a Golden Retriever, it's $85. We also offer a first-time customer discount of 15%.
MAN: Great! When's your earliest available appointment?

**Q1:** What does the man initially request?
- 1. A consultation for his pet
- 2. A grooming appointment
- 3. Information about services
- 4. Vaccination records
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A consultation for his pet: The man doesn't ask for a consultation.
✅ 2. A grooming appointment: The man specifically says "I was wondering if I could make an appointment to get my dog groomed."
❌ 3. Information about services: He doesn't initially ask for information about services.
❌ 4. Vaccination records: He doesn't request vaccination records.

**Q2:** What special offer does the woman mention?
- 1. A package discount
- 2. A seasonal promotion
- 3. A first-time customer discount
- 4. A breed-specific discount
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A package discount: No package discount is mentioned.
❌ 2. A seasonal promotion: No seasonal promotion is mentioned.
✅ 3. A first-time customer discount: The woman specifically says "We also offer a first-time customer discount of 15%."
❌ 4. A breed-specific discount: No breed-specific discount is mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule. We have an opening this Thursday at 10 AM, or next Monday at 2 PM.
MAN: Thursday would be perfect. Oh, wait - I should mention that Max gets a bit nervous around other dogs.
WOMAN: Don't worry, we have separate areas for anxious pets. We'll make sure he's comfortable. Do you have any specific concerns about his grooming?
MAN: Well, he's not great with loud noises, especially the blow dryer.
WOMAN: We can use our quiet dryer for sensitive pets. It takes a bit longer, but it's much gentler.
MAN: That would be wonderful. Should I bring anything special?
WOMAN: Just his vaccination records and a collar. If he has a favorite treat, you can bring that too.
MAN: Perfect. And what time should I pick him up?
WOMAN: Given that we'll use the quiet dryer, plan for about three hours. So, around 1 PM.

**Q3:** What accommodation does the woman offer for the dog?
- 1. A private grooming room
- 2. Special handling techniques
- 3. A quieter dryer
- 4. Extra treat rewards
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A private grooming room: While separate areas are mentioned, the specific accommodation for noise sensitivity is the dryer.
❌ 2. Special handling techniques: No specific handling techniques are mentioned.
✅ 3. A quieter dryer: The woman specifically offers "our quiet dryer for sensitive pets" because the dog doesn't like loud noises.
❌ 4. Extra treat rewards: While treats are mentioned, they aren't offered as an accommodation.

**Q4:** What must the customer bring to the appointment?
- 1. Vaccination records and collar only
- 2. The dog's favorite toys
- 3. Special grooming tools
- 4. The dog's medical history
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Vaccination records and collar only: The woman says "Just his vaccination records and a collar."
❌ 2. The dog's favorite toys: Toys aren't mentioned as required items.
❌ 3. Special grooming tools: No special tools are required.
❌ 4. The dog's medical history: Only vaccination records are required, not the full medical history.

**Q5:** Why will the appointment take longer than usual?
- 1. The dog needs special handling
- 2. They're using different equipment
- 3. The dog requires extra services
- 4. It's a first-time appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The dog needs special handling: While the dog is nervous, this isn't why it takes longer.
✅ 2. They're using different equipment: The woman says "We can use our quiet dryer for sensitive pets. It takes a bit longer, but it's much gentler."
❌ 3. The dog requires extra services: The extra time is due to the equipment, not additional services.
❌ 4. It's a first-time appointment: Being a first-time appointment isn't mentioned as a reason for extra time.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I just realized I need to reschedule Max's appointment for Thursday.
WOMAN: Oh, I'm sorry to hear that. Is everything okay?
MAN: Yes, but I completely forgot about a vet appointment I scheduled for him that morning.
WOMAN: No problem at all. Let me check what else we have available. Would you prefer morning or afternoon?
MAN: Afternoon would be better. And actually, could you add a teeth cleaning service as well?
WOMAN: Of course! That's an additional $25. I can offer you Friday at 2 PM or Saturday at 3 PM.
MAN: Friday at 2 PM works perfectly. And with the teeth cleaning, how long should I expect it to take?
WOMAN: It'll be about three and a half hours total. So you can pick him up around 5:30 PM.
MAN: Great, thank you for being so accommodating!
WOMAN: No problem at all. We'll see you and Max on Friday!

**Q6:** Why does the man need to reschedule?
- 1. Due to a work conflict
- 2. Because of a vet appointment
- 3. Personal emergency
- 4. Transportation issues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Due to a work conflict: No work conflict is mentioned.
✅ 2. Because of a vet appointment: The man says "I completely forgot about a vet appointment I scheduled for him that morning."
❌ 3. Personal emergency: No personal emergency is mentioned.
❌ 4. Transportation issues: No transportation issues are mentioned.

**Q7:** What additional service does the man request?
- 1. Nail trimming
- 2. Extra brushing
- 3. Teeth cleaning
- 4. Ear cleaning
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Nail trimming: This is already included in the full-service package.
❌ 2. Extra brushing: The man doesn't request this.
✅ 3. Teeth cleaning: When rescheduling, the man specifically asks "could you add a teeth cleaning service as well?"
❌ 4. Ear cleaning: This is already included in the full-service package.

**Q8:** What is the new expected pickup time?
- 1. 3:30 PM
- 2. 4:30 PM
- 3. 5:00 PM
- 4. 5:30 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 3:30 PM: This time isn't mentioned.
❌ 2. 4:30 PM: This time isn't mentioned.
❌ 3. 5:00 PM: This time isn't mentioned.
✅ 4. 5:30 PM: The woman says "It'll be about three and a half hours total. So you can pick him up around 5:30 PM."

## Practice 349: Library Card Registration | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a public library. The woman is a librarian, and the man wants to apply for a library card.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'd like to apply for a library card. I just moved to the area.
WOMAN: Welcome to our library! I'd be happy to help you with that. Do you have any photo ID and proof of address with you?
MAN: Yes, I have my driver's license. Would that work?
WOMAN: A driver's license is perfect since it has both your photo and address. However, I notice this address is from another city.
MAN: Oh right, I haven't updated it yet. But I have a utility bill with my new address. Would that work?
WOMAN: Yes, that's exactly what we need. Let me just get the application form for you to fill out.
MAN: Great, thanks. By the way, how many books can I borrow at once with a new card?
WOMAN: New members can borrow up to five items initially. After three months of good standing, that limit increases to fifteen items.
MAN: That sounds reasonable. And how long can I keep them?
WOMAN: The standard loan period is three weeks, with the option to renew twice if no one else has requested the item.

**Q1:** What is the man's primary purpose for visiting the library?
- 1. To borrow books immediately
- 2. To get information about library services
- 3. To obtain a library card
- 4. To use the computer facilities
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To borrow books immediately: While he asks about borrowing books, his first statement is about applying for a library card.
❌ 2. To get information about library services: He does ask about services, but this is secondary to his main purpose.
✅ 3. To obtain a library card: The man's first statement is "I'd like to apply for a library card. I just moved to the area."
❌ 4. To use the computer facilities: He only asks about computers later in the conversation.

**Q2:** What is the borrowing limit for new members?
- 1. Three items
- 2. Five items
- 3. Ten items
- 4. Fifteen items
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Three items: This is incorrect according to the conversation.
✅ 2. Five items: The librarian specifically states, "New members can borrow up to five items initially."
❌ 3. Ten items: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Fifteen items: This is the increased limit after three months, not for new members.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I've filled out the form. Could you check if I've done everything correctly?
WOMAN: Of course. Let's see... you've completed your name, address, and contact information. Oh, we also need an emergency contact.
MAN: Oh, I wasn't sure about that part. Can I put my wife's information there?
WOMAN: Yes, that would be perfect. We just need someone we can contact if there are any issues with your account.
MAN: Okay, I'll add her details now. Also, I noticed there's a section about email notifications.
WOMAN: Yes, that's optional but recommended. If you sign up, you'll receive reminders three days before your books are due.
MAN: That would be really helpful. I tend to forget due dates.
WOMAN: Many people do! The email service also notifies you when items you've requested become available.
MAN: Perfect, I'll definitely sign up for that. Should I check both boxes then?
WOMAN: Yes, and just initial next to them to confirm your consent.

**Q3:** Why does the form require emergency contact information?
- 1. To verify the applicant's identity
- 2. To contact someone if account issues arise
- 3. To have a secondary card holder
- 4. To send additional notifications
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To verify the applicant's identity: The emergency contact is not used for identity verification. The ID and proof of address serve this purpose.
✅ 2. To contact someone if account issues arise: The librarian explicitly states, "We just need someone we can contact if there are any issues with your account."
❌ 3. To have a secondary card holder: There's no mention of the emergency contact becoming a card holder.
❌ 4. To send additional notifications: The conversation mentions email notifications separately from emergency contacts.

**Q4:** What service does the email notification provide?
- 1. Only overdue notices
- 2. Daily library updates
- 3. Account balance alerts
- 4. Due date reminders and hold notifications
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only overdue notices: The service provides more than just overdue notices.
❌ 2. Daily library updates: There's no mention of daily updates in the conversation.
❌ 3. Account balance alerts: The conversation doesn't mention account balance notifications.
✅ 4. Due date reminders and hold notifications: The librarian states, "If you sign up, you'll receive reminders three days before your books are due" and "The email service also notifies you when items you've requested become available."

**Q5:** What action does the woman suggest regarding the email service?
- 1. To leave it for later
- 2. To check boxes and initial
- 3. To provide multiple email addresses
- 4. To sign up for premium service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To leave it for later: She encourages signing up now, not later.
✅ 2. To check boxes and initial: The librarian says, "Yes, and just initial next to them to confirm your consent" when asked about checking the boxes for email notifications.
❌ 3. To provide multiple email addresses: There's no mention of multiple email addresses.
❌ 4. To sign up for premium service: No premium service is mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Before you finalize my card, could you explain the different sections of the library?
WOMAN: Of course! The main floor, where we are now, has fiction, non-fiction, and reference materials. The children's section is also on this level.
MAN: And I saw some computers upstairs?
WOMAN: Yes, the second floor has our computer lab, study rooms, and multimedia section. Your library card gives you two hours of computer access per day.
MAN: That's good to know. What about printing services?
WOMAN: We charge ten cents per black and white page, and fifty cents for color. You can load money onto your card at the kiosk near the printers.
MAN: Perfect. One last question - I noticed a sign about e-books?
WOMAN: Yes! Your card gives you access to our digital collection. I can show you how to set that up on your phone right now if you'd like.
MAN: That would be great, thanks!

**Q6:** What is located on the main floor of the library?
- 1. Computer lab and study rooms
- 2. Only fiction and non-fiction books
- 3. Books and children's section
- 4. Multimedia section and printers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Computer lab and study rooms: These are on the second floor according to the conversation.
❌ 2. Only fiction and non-fiction books: The main floor has more than just these book sections.
✅ 3. Books and children's section: The librarian states, "The main floor, where we are now, has fiction, non-fiction, and reference materials. The children's section is also on this level."
❌ 4. Multimedia section and printers: These are mentioned as being on the second floor.

**Q7:** What is the daily limit for computer use?
- 1. One hour
- 2. Two hours
- 3. Three hours
- 4. Unlimited
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One hour: This is incorrect according to the conversation.
✅ 2. Two hours: The librarian specifically states, "Your library card gives you two hours of computer access per day."
❌ 3. Three hours: This is incorrect according to the conversation.
❌ 4. Unlimited: The conversation clearly mentions a time limit.

**Q8:** What does the woman offer to do at the end of the conversation?
- 1. Show where the printers are
- 2. Give a tour of the library
- 3. Help set up digital access
- 4. Explain the printing costs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Show where the printers are: She mentions printers but doesn't offer to show their location.
❌ 2. Give a tour of the library: She explains the sections but doesn't offer a physical tour.
✅ 3. Help set up digital access: The librarian says, "I can show you how to set that up on your phone right now if you'd like" regarding e-book access.
❌ 4. Explain the printing costs: She already explained the costs but didn't offer further explanation.

## Practice 350: Cloudy Pool Water | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a swimming pool maintenance office. The woman works at the office, and the man is a customer requesting service.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Good morning. I need some help with my pool maintenance. The water's starting to look a bit cloudy.
WOMAN: I understand. How long has it been since your last maintenance service?
MAN: Actually, we just bought the house last month, and I'm not sure when it was last serviced.
WOMAN: I see. We'll need to do a complete water test then. Would you like to schedule a service visit?
MAN: Yes, definitely. How soon can someone come out?
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule. We have an opening tomorrow morning at 9 AM, or Friday afternoon at 2 PM.
MAN: Tomorrow morning would be perfect. Do I need to be home?
WOMAN: Yes, someone needs to be there to give our technician access to the pool area.
MAN: That's fine. I work from home anyway. How long will it take?
WOMAN: The initial inspection and water testing usually takes about an hour.

**Q1:** Why does the man contact the pool service?
- 1. To schedule regular maintenance
- 2. To address water quality issues
- 3. To get information about prices
- 4. To prepare for winter season
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To schedule regular maintenance: He's not setting up regular maintenance, but addressing a specific issue.
✅ 2. To address water quality issues: The man's first statement is "I need some help with my pool maintenance. The water's starting to look a bit cloudy."
❌ 3. To get information about prices: While prices are discussed, this wasn't his initial reason for contact.
❌ 4. To prepare for winter season: Winter preparation is only mentioned later in the conversation, not as his initial reason.

**Q2:** What is required for the service appointment?
- 1. Payment in advance
- 2. Previous maintenance records
- 3. Someone to be at home
- 4. Pool to be empty
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Payment in advance: There's no mention of payment timing in the conversation.
❌ 2. Previous maintenance records: While the woman asks about previous service, records aren't required.
✅ 3. Someone to be at home: The woman clearly states "Yes, someone needs to be there to give our technician access to the pool area."
❌ 4. Pool to be empty: There's no mention of the pool needing to be empty for service.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I forgot to ask earlier, what's included in the service?
WOMAN: Our basic service includes water testing, chemical balancing, filter cleaning, and skimming the surface.
MAN: What about the pool walls? They're starting to feel a bit slimy.
WOMAN: That would require our deep cleaning service. It includes brushing the walls and floor, vacuuming, and algae treatment if needed.
MAN: How much extra would that cost?
WOMAN: The deep cleaning service is an additional $150 on top of the basic service fee of $100.
MAN: That seems reasonable. Let's do the complete service then.
WOMAN: Perfect. I'll update the work order. Our technician will bring all necessary equipment and chemicals.
MAN: Great. Oh, and where do I need to leave the pool key?
WOMAN: You can just hand it directly to the technician when they arrive.

**Q3:** What is included in the complete service?
- 1. Basic cleaning and winterizing
- 2. Water testing and algae removal
- 3. Surface skimming and chemical balance
- 4. Wall brushing and water testing
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Basic cleaning and winterizing: Winterizing is mentioned as a separate service, not part of the complete service.
❌ 2. Water testing and algae removal: Algae treatment is only mentioned as part of the deep cleaning if needed, not as a guaranteed part of the service.
❌ 3. Surface skimming and chemical balance: These are only part of the basic service, not the complete picture.
✅ 4. Wall brushing and water testing: The complete service includes both the basic service (water testing) and the deep cleaning (wall brushing), making this the most comprehensive answer.

**Q4:** How much will the man pay in total?
- 1. 100 dollars
- 2. 150 dollars
- 3. 250 dollars
- 4. 300 dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 100 dollars: This is only the cost of the basic service fee.
❌ 2. 150 dollars: This is only the cost of the deep cleaning service.
✅ 3. 250 dollars: The woman states the basic service is $100 and the deep cleaning is an additional $150, totaling $250. The man agrees to "the complete service."
❌ 4. 300 dollars: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What arrangement is made about pool access?
- 1. A key will be left in a hidden spot
- 2. The key will be given to the technician
- 3. The gate code will be provided
- 4. A spare key will be made
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A key will be left in a hidden spot: This arrangement is not mentioned.
✅ 2. The key will be given to the technician: The man asks where to leave the pool key, and the woman responds "You can just hand it directly to the technician when they arrive."
❌ 3. The gate code will be provided: No gate code is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A spare key will be made: Making a spare key is not discussed.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about the pool service scheduled for tomorrow morning.
WOMAN: Yes, I have it right here. Is there a problem?
MAN: Well, we're expecting some rain tomorrow. Should we reschedule?
WOMAN: Actually, rain doesn't affect most of our maintenance work. We can still test the water and clean the filters.
MAN: Are you sure? I don't want your technician working in bad weather.
WOMAN: Don't worry, our team is used to working in various conditions. The only thing we postpone for is lightning.
MAN: Oh, that's good to know. One more thing - my neighbor mentioned something about winter preparation?
WOMAN: Yes, that's a separate service we offer in the fall. Would you like me to send you information about our winterizing packages?
MAN: Yes, please. You can email it to the address in my account.
WOMAN: I'll do that right away. See you tomorrow morning!

**Q6:** Why does the man call back?
- 1. To change the appointment time
- 2. To express weather concerns
- 3. To cancel the service
- 4. To request a different technician
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To change the appointment time: The man doesn't try to change the time of the appointment.
✅ 2. To express weather concerns: The man calls back because he's concerned about the rain expected tomorrow, asking "Well, we're expecting some rain tomorrow. Should we reschedule?"
❌ 3. To cancel the service: The man doesn't try to cancel the service, just inquires if they should reschedule due to rain.
❌ 4. To request a different technician: There's no mention of the man wanting a different technician.

**Q7:** What does the woman say about working in rain?
- 1. They only work in good weather
- 2. They can do most services
- 3. They offer a rain discount
- 4. They need special equipment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only work in good weather: The woman actually says they can work in rain.
✅ 2. They can do most services: The woman states "rain doesn't affect most of our maintenance work. We can still test the water and clean the filters."
❌ 3. They offer a rain discount: No discount is mentioned for rainy conditions.
❌ 4. They need special equipment: No special equipment for rain is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What additional service is mentioned?
- 1. Monthly maintenance
- 2. Filter replacement
- 3. Winter preparation
- 4. Chemical treatment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Monthly maintenance: Regular maintenance is not specifically discussed as an additional service.
❌ 2. Filter replacement: Filter cleaning is mentioned as part of the basic service, not replacement.
✅ 3. Winter preparation: The man asks about "winter preparation" that his neighbor mentioned, and the woman confirms it's "a separate service we offer in the fall."
❌ 4. Chemical treatment: Chemical balancing is part of the basic service, not an additional service.

## Practice 351: Delayed Baggage Claim | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at the airport lost luggage counter, and the man is a passenger who has lost his luggage.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I just arrived on flight AC302 from Vancouver, and my suitcase hasn't shown up on the baggage carousel.
WOMAN: I'm sorry to hear that, sir. May I see your baggage claim ticket, please?
MAN: Yes, here it is. It's a large black suitcase with a red ribbon tied to the handle.
WOMAN: Thank you. Let me check our system... According to our records, your flight had some baggage handling issues in Vancouver. Several bags were delayed.
MAN: Oh no, that's not good. I have important business meetings tomorrow morning.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. The good news is that your bag has been located. It's scheduled to arrive on the next flight from Vancouver, which lands at 11:30 PM tonight.
MAN: That's a relief. How will I get it once it arrives?
WOMAN: We can deliver it to your hotel or residence. Could you please fill out this form with your local address?

**Q1:** What is the immediate problem the man is facing?
- 1. His flight was delayed
- 2. His baggage has not arrived
- 3. He missed his connecting flight
- 4. His ticket was cancelled
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His flight was delayed: The flight has already arrived; it's not delayed.
✅ 2. His baggage has not arrived: The man begins by saying "my suitcase hasn't shown up on the baggage carousel."
❌ 3. He missed his connecting flight: There's no mention of a connecting flight.
❌ 4. His ticket was cancelled: There's no mention of ticket cancellation.

**Q2:** When is the man's luggage expected to arrive?
- 1. Early next morning
- 2. Late afternoon
- 3. At 11:30 PM
- 4. Within an hour
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Early next morning: This is not when the luggage is expected.
❌ 2. Late afternoon: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. At 11:30 PM: The woman states the bag "is scheduled to arrive on the next flight from Vancouver, which lands at 11:30 PM tonight."
❌ 4. Within an hour: This timeframe is not mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sure, I'm staying at the Marriott downtown. Should I write down my room number too?
WOMAN: Yes, please include all the details. Also, we'll need a contact number where we can reach you.
MAN: The thing is, I have some medication in that suitcase that I need tonight.
WOMAN: In that case, I'd recommend filing a claim for overnight essentials. Our airline will reimburse you up to $100 for necessary items.
MAN: That would be helpful. What kind of receipts do you need?
WOMAN: Just keep the original receipts for any essential items you purchase. You can submit them along with this claim form.
MAN: Perfect. And how long does the reimbursement usually take?
WOMAN: Once you submit the receipts and form, it typically takes 7-10 business days for processing.
MAN: Alright, I understand. Let me fill out these forms then.

**Q3:** What solution does the airline offer for immediate needs?
- 1. A temporary suitcase
- 2. A $100 reimbursement
- 3. Free hotel accommodation
- 4. Complimentary toiletries
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A temporary suitcase: This is not mentioned as an option.
✅ 2. A $100 reimbursement: The woman states "Our airline will reimburse you up to $100 for necessary items."
❌ 3. Free hotel accommodation: This is not offered in the conversation.
❌ 4. Complimentary toiletries: These are not mentioned as being provided.

**Q4:** How long will the reimbursement process take?
- 1. 2-3 days
- 2. 7-10 business days
- 3. 24 hours
- 4. One month
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 2-3 days: This timeframe is not mentioned.
✅ 2. 7-10 business days: The woman specifically states "it typically takes 7-10 business days for processing."
❌ 3. 24 hours: This is much faster than what was stated.
❌ 4. One month: This is much longer than what was stated.

**Q5:** What does the man need to submit for reimbursement?
- 1. Only the claim form
- 2. Original receipts and claim form
- 3. A copy of his passport
- 4. His flight ticket
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only the claim form: This is incomplete based on the conversation.
✅ 2. Original receipts and claim form: The woman clearly states "Just keep the original receipts for any essential items you purchase. You can submit them along with this claim form."
❌ 3. A copy of his passport: This is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. His flight ticket: This is not mentioned as a requirement for reimbursement.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry, one more question. What happens if my suitcase doesn't arrive on that flight tonight?
WOMAN: If there's any further delay, we'll contact you immediately. We can also track your bag's location in real-time through our system.
MAN: Could you give me a tracking number or something?
WOMAN: Yes, here's your file reference number. You can use it to check the status online or through our airline's app.
MAN: Great. And what's the latest time you think the bag will be delivered to my hotel?
WOMAN: Given the flight arrival time, delivery should be completed by 1:30 AM at the latest.
MAN: That works. I really appreciate your help with all this.
WOMAN: You're welcome. Please don't hesitate to call if you need any further assistance. The contact number is on your claim form.

**Q6:** What additional service does the airline provide?
- 1. 24-hour customer support
- 2. Online tracking system
- 3. Free breakfast at the hotel
- 4. Airport pickup service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 24-hour customer support: While assistance is offered, 24-hour support is not specifically mentioned.
✅ 2. Online tracking system: The woman mentions "We can also track your bag's location in real-time through our system" and provides a reference number to "check the status online or through our airline's app."
❌ 3. Free breakfast at the hotel: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Airport pickup service: This service is not mentioned.

**Q7:** What is the expected delivery time for the luggage?
- 1. By midnight
- 2. By 11:30 PM
- 3. By 1:30 AM
- 4. By morning
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By midnight: This time is not mentioned.
❌ 2. By 11:30 PM: This is when the flight with his luggage arrives, not when it will be delivered.
✅ 3. By 1:30 AM: The woman clearly states "delivery should be completed by 1:30 AM at the latest."
❌ 4. By morning: This is too vague and not what was stated.

**Q8:** What information is provided on the claim form?
- 1. Flight details
- 2. Baggage description
- 3. Contact number for assistance
- 4. Reimbursement amount
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Flight details: The conversation doesn't mention flight details being on the claim form.
❌ 2. Baggage description: While the man describes his luggage, this isn't mentioned as being on the claim form.
✅ 3. Contact number for assistance: The woman specifically states "The contact number is on your claim form" in her final response.
❌ 4. Reimbursement amount: While reimbursement is discussed, the form doesn't show the amount.

## Practice 352: Spa Package Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a luxury spa. The woman is a spa receptionist, and the man is a customer inquiring about spa treatments.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I received a spa gift certificate for my birthday, and I'd like to book some treatments.
WOMAN: Welcome to Serenity Spa! I'd be happy to help you with that. May I see your gift certificate?
MAN: Here you go. It's for the deluxe package, I believe.
WOMAN: Yes, this is our premium package. It includes a 90-minute massage, facial treatment, and access to our thermal suite.
MAN: That sounds wonderful. What's included in the thermal suite?
WOMAN: The thermal suite features a steam room, sauna, hydrotherapy pool, and relaxation area. You can enjoy these facilities before or after your treatments.
MAN: Perfect. I'd like to book for this Saturday if possible.
WOMAN: Let me check our availability... I'm sorry, but we're fully booked this Saturday. However, I have openings next Tuesday or Wednesday.
MAN: Hmm, I'll need to check my schedule. Can I call you back in an hour?
WOMAN: Of course! I'll hold those slots for you until then.

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the man's visit?
- 1. To purchase a spa package
- 2. To make a dinner reservation
- 3. To redeem a gift certificate
- 4. To inquire about membership
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To purchase a spa package: The man already has a gift certificate, not looking to purchase.
❌ 2. To make a dinner reservation: He's there for spa treatments, not dinner.
✅ 3. To redeem a gift certificate: The man clearly states "I received a spa gift certificate for my birthday, and I'd like to book some treatments."
❌ 4. To inquire about membership: There's no mention of membership in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why can't the man book his preferred appointment time?
- 1. The spa is closed on Saturdays
- 2. The spa is fully booked
- 3. His gift certificate is not valid
- 4. The technician is unavailable
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The spa is closed on Saturdays: The spa is open but fully booked.
✅ 2. The spa is fully booked: The woman specifically says "I'm sorry, but we're fully booked this Saturday."
❌ 3. His gift certificate is not valid: The gift certificate is confirmed as valid for the deluxe package.
❌ 4. The technician is unavailable: No specific technician availability is mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I called earlier about the deluxe package. I'd like to book for next Tuesday.
WOMAN: Yes, I remember. Let me pull up those available times... We can start your treatments at either 10 AM or 2 PM.
MAN: The morning slot would be better. What time should I arrive?
WOMAN: Please arrive 30 minutes before your appointment to complete some paperwork and change into your robe and slippers.
MAN: Got it. And how long will all the treatments take?
WOMAN: The full experience will take about three hours, including the thermal suite access.
MAN: Perfect. Oh, and is there a cafe or restaurant on-site?
WOMAN: Yes, we have a wellness café that serves healthy meals and refreshments. Would you like me to make a lunch reservation?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be great.
WOMAN: I'll book you a table for 1:30 PM after your treatments.

**Q3:** What time will the man's treatments begin?
- 1. 9:30 AM
- 2. 10:00 AM
- 3. 1:30 PM
- 4. 2:00 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 9:30 AM: This is when he should arrive (30 minutes before the appointment).
✅ 2. 10:00 AM: The woman says "We can start your treatments at either 10 AM or 2 PM" and the man chooses the morning slot.
❌ 3. 1:30 PM: This is when his lunch reservation is scheduled.
❌ 4. 2:00 PM: This was the alternative appointment time offered but not chosen.

**Q4:** How long is the man expected to spend at the spa?
- 1. 90 minutes
- 2. Two hours
- 3. Three hours
- 4. Four hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 90 minutes: This is only the length of the massage portion.
❌ 2. Two hours: This is shorter than the stated duration.
✅ 3. Three hours: The woman specifically states "The full experience will take about three hours, including the thermal suite access."
❌ 4. Four hours: This is longer than the stated duration.

**Q5:** What additional service does the woman arrange for the man?
- 1. A massage upgrade
- 2. A lunch reservation
- 3. Extra thermal suite time
- 4. A personal trainer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A massage upgrade: No massage upgrade is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. A lunch reservation: The woman says "I'll book you a table for 1:30 PM after your treatments."
❌ 3. Extra thermal suite time: No extra time is mentioned for the thermal suite.
❌ 4. A personal trainer: No personal trainer is mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry, one more question. I have some dietary restrictions I should mention.
WOMAN: No problem at all. Our café caters to various dietary needs. What should I note down?
MAN: I'm gluten-free and vegetarian. Will that be an issue?
WOMAN: Not at all. We have plenty of options that would suit you. I'll add these requirements to your lunch reservation.
MAN: That's great. And what should I bring with me on Tuesday?
WOMAN: Just yourself! We provide robes, slippers, towels, and all amenities. Though if you plan to use the pool, bring your swimsuit.
MAN: Perfect. Oh, and is there parking available?
WOMAN: Yes, we have complimentary valet parking for all our guests.
MAN: Excellent. Thank you so much for your help.
WOMAN: You're welcome! We look forward to seeing you on Tuesday at 9:30 AM.

**Q6:** What does the man NOT need to bring to the spa?
- 1. Swimsuit
- 2. Towels
- 3. His gift certificate
- 4. Dietary requirements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Swimsuit: The woman says "if you plan to use the pool, bring your swimsuit."
✅ 2. Towels: The woman states "We provide robes, slippers, towels, and all amenities."
❌ 3. His gift certificate: He already presented it but would need it for redemption on the day.
❌ 4. Dietary requirements: He verbally provided these, but they're not something to physically bring.

**Q7:** What special accommodations does the man require?
- 1. Vegetarian and gluten-free options
- 2. Special parking space
- 3. Additional treatment time
- 4. Specific massage therapist
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Vegetarian and gluten-free options: The man states "I'm gluten-free and vegetarian" when discussing dietary restrictions.
❌ 2. Special parking space: Regular valet parking is mentioned, not special parking.
❌ 3. Additional treatment time: No request for extra time is mentioned.
❌ 4. Specific massage therapist: No preference for a specific therapist is mentioned.

**Q8:** What service is included with the spa visit?
- 1. Free lunch
- 2. Valet parking
- 3. Extended thermal suite access
- 4. Personal training session
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Free lunch: Lunch is arranged but not mentioned as complimentary.
✅ 2. Valet parking: The woman states "we have complimentary valet parking for all our guests."
❌ 3. Extended thermal suite access: Regular access is included, but no extended access is mentioned.
❌ 4. Personal training session: No personal training is mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 353: Locked Bank Account | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a customer service representative at a bank, and the man is a customer who needs help with online banking.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm having trouble logging into my online banking account. I've tried several times, but it keeps saying my password is incorrect.
WOMAN: I understand, sir. I'll be happy to help you with that. First, could you please verify your identity? I'll need your account number and date of birth.
MAN: Sure, my account number is 45872301, and I was born on March 15, 1985.
WOMAN: Thank you for that information. I can see that your account has been temporarily locked due to multiple failed login attempts. This is a security measure we have in place to protect our customers.
MAN: Oh, I see. That explains why I couldn't get in. What should I do now?
WOMAN: I can help you reset your password right now. Would you like to proceed with that?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be great.

**Q1:** Why did the man contact customer service?
- 1. His account was locked
- 2. He forgot his account number
- 3. He couldn't log into his account
- 4. His mobile app wasn't working
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His account was locked: He didn't know his account was locked when he called; he discovered this during the conversation.
❌ 2. He forgot his account number: He was able to provide his account number when asked.
✅ 3. He couldn't log into his account: The man starts by saying 'I'm having trouble logging into my online banking account.'
❌ 4. His mobile app wasn't working: He mentions problems with online banking, not specifically the mobile app.

**Q2:** What caused the man's login problem?
- 1. A system maintenance issue
- 2. Multiple failed login attempts
- 3. An expired password
- 4. A technical glitch
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A system maintenance issue: The conversation doesn't mention any system maintenance as the cause.
✅ 2. Multiple failed login attempts: The woman clearly states 'I can see that your account has been temporarily locked due to multiple failed login attempts.'
❌ 3. An expired password: There's no mention of the password being expired in the conversation.
❌ 4. A technical glitch: No technical glitch is mentioned as the cause of the problem.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Alright, I'm going to send a verification code to your registered mobile number. Could you please confirm the last four digits of your phone number?
MAN: Yes, it should end in 4762.
WOMAN: Perfect. I've just sent the code. Could you please check your phone and read it to me?
MAN: Let me check... Yes, I got it. The code is 847593.
WOMAN: Thank you. Now, I'll guide you through creating a new password. It needs to be at least 8 characters long and include uppercase, lowercase letters, numbers, and a special character.
MAN: Okay, I think I have something in mind. Where do I enter it?
WOMAN: I'm sending you a secure link via email. You'll be able to enter your new password there.

**Q3:** What information does the new password need to include?
- 1. Only numbers and special characters
- 2. At least 12 characters
- 3. Multiple types of characters
- 4. Only letters and numbers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only numbers and special characters: The woman mentions more requirements than just these two types.
❌ 2. At least 12 characters: The woman says 'at least 8 characters long,' not 12.
✅ 3. Multiple types of characters: The woman states it needs 'uppercase, lowercase letters, numbers, and a special character.'
❌ 4. Only letters and numbers: This excludes the special character requirement that was mentioned.

**Q4:** How does the woman send the password reset link?
- 1. Through text message
- 2. Through email
- 3. Through postal mail
- 4. Through the mobile app
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Through text message: The verification code was sent via text, but not the reset link.
✅ 2. Through email: The woman says 'I'm sending you a secure link via email' and the man confirms receiving it in his email.
❌ 3. Through postal mail: This slow method is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Through the mobile app: The reset link is not sent through the app.

**Q5:** What happens after the verification code is sent?
- 1. The account is immediately unlocked
- 2. The man receives an automated call
- 3. The woman provides reset instructions
- 4. The system automatically resets
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The account is immediately unlocked: The account unlocking happens later in the process, not immediately after the code is sent.
❌ 2. The man receives an automated call: No automated call is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. The woman provides reset instructions: After the code is verified, the woman guides him through creating a new password with specific requirements.
❌ 4. The system automatically resets: The system doesn't automatically reset; the woman guides the man through the manual process.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I've received the email, but when I click the link, it says it's expired. Is that possible?
WOMAN: The security link is only valid for five minutes. Let me send you a new one. Could you check your email again?
MAN: Yes, got it this time. Okay, I've entered my new password. It says the change was successful.
WOMAN: Excellent! Just remember to wait about 15 minutes before trying to log in with your new password. This allows our system to fully process the change.
MAN: Great, thank you. One more thing - should I update the password on my banking app too?
WOMAN: No need - the new password will work for both online banking and the mobile app. Is there anything else I can help you with?
MAN: No, that's all. Thanks for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Have a great day!

**Q6:** Why did the first reset link fail?
- 1. It was sent to the wrong email
- 2. The man waited too long
- 3. The system was down
- 4. The email was blocked
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It was sent to the wrong email: There's no indication the email was sent to the wrong address.
✅ 2. The man waited too long: The woman explains 'The security link is only valid for five minutes' when the man says the link is expired.
❌ 3. The system was down: No system downtime is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The email was blocked: There's no mention of email blocking issues.

**Q7:** How long should the man wait before logging in?
- 1. 5 minutes
- 2. 10 minutes
- 3. 15 minutes
- 4. 30 minutes
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 5 minutes: This is how long the security link is valid, not the waiting time for login.
❌ 2. 10 minutes: This time period is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. 15 minutes: The woman specifically says 'Just remember to wait about 15 minutes before trying to log in with your new password.'
❌ 4. 30 minutes: This longer time period is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What is true about the new password?
- 1. It needs to be updated separately in the app
- 2. It works for all banking platforms
- 3. It expires after one month
- 4. It requires additional verification
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It needs to be updated separately in the app: The woman specifically says 'No need - the new password will work for both online banking and the mobile app.'
✅ 2. It works for all banking platforms: The woman confirms the new password works for both online banking and the mobile app.
❌ 3. It expires after one month: No expiration period is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. It requires additional verification: No additional verification is mentioned for using the new password.

## Practice 354: Apartment Internet Setup | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at an internet service provider office. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a customer requesting internet installation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'd like to set up internet service at my new apartment. I just moved in last week.
WOMAN: Welcome to the neighborhood! I'll be happy to help you with that. Do you know what speed of internet you're looking for?
MAN: Well, I work from home and do a lot of video calls. I also stream movies pretty often.
WOMAN: In that case, I'd recommend our fiber optic package with speeds up to 500 Mbps. It's perfect for remote work and streaming.
MAN: That sounds good. How much does that cost per month?
WOMAN: The package is $79.99 monthly, and there's a one-time installation fee of $50. However, if you sign up for a one-year contract, we'll waive the installation fee.
MAN: Oh, that's a good deal. And how soon can you install it?
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule... We have availability this Thursday morning or Friday afternoon.
MAN: Thursday would be perfect. What time exactly?

**Q1:** What is the main reason the man wants high-speed internet?
- 1. For gaming purposes
- 2. For work-related activities
- 3. For streaming movies
- 4. For general browsing
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. For gaming purposes: Gaming is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. For work-related activities: The man states 'I work from home and do a lot of video calls,' indicating work is his primary need.
❌ 3. For streaming movies: While he mentions streaming movies, he mentions work first, suggesting it's more important.
❌ 4. For general browsing: General browsing is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What incentive is offered to the customer?
- 1. A free router
- 2. A monthly discount
- 3. No installation fee with contract
- 4. Free technical support
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A free router: The router is not free; it costs $10 monthly to rent or $199 to purchase.
❌ 2. A monthly discount: No monthly discount is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. No installation fee with contract: The woman states 'if you sign up for a one-year contract, we'll waive the installation fee.'
❌ 4. Free technical support: While technical support is mentioned, it's presented as a standard service, not as a special incentive.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: We can schedule the technician to arrive between 9 AM and noon on Thursday. They'll call you 30 minutes before arriving.
MAN: That works for me. Do I need to be present during the entire installation?
WOMAN: Yes, someone over 18 needs to be home. The installation usually takes about two hours, depending on your apartment's setup.
MAN: No problem. Oh, and do I need to prepare anything before the technician arrives?
WOMAN: Just make sure there's clear access to the rooms where you want the internet connection, and ensure there's a power outlet nearby.
MAN: Got it. And what about the router? Is that included?
WOMAN: Yes, we provide a high-performance router as part of the package. There's a monthly rental fee of $10, or you can purchase it outright for $199.
MAN: I think I'll rent it for now. Can I change my mind later?
WOMAN: Absolutely! You can purchase it anytime.

**Q3:** What is required during installation?
- 1. A technician's phone call
- 2. An adult present
- 3. Special equipment
- 4. Multiple power outlets
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A technician's phone call: The technician will call 30 minutes before arriving, but this is not required during the actual installation.
❌ 2. An adult present: The woman specifically states 'Yes, someone over 18 needs to be home' when the man asks if he needs to be present.
✅ 3. Special equipment: The conversation doesn't mention any special equipment needed during installation.
❌ 4. Multiple power outlets: The woman only mentions ensuring 'there's a power outlet nearby,' not multiple outlets.

**Q4:** What option does the customer have regarding the router?
- 1. He must buy it outright
- 2. He can only rent it
- 3. He can choose between renting or buying
- 4. He must provide his own
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He must buy it outright: This is not the only option; he can also rent it.
❌ 2. He can only rent it: The conversation mentions both renting and buying options.
✅ 3. He can choose between renting or buying: The woman explains he can rent for $10 monthly or purchase for $199, and can 'purchase it anytime' if he initially chooses to rent.
❌ 4. He must provide his own: There's no mention of the customer needing to provide his own router.

**Q5:** How long is the installation expected to take?
- 1. 30 minutes
- 2. About two hours
- 3. Three hours
- 4. A full day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 30 minutes: This is how much notice the technician will give before arriving, not the installation time.
✅ 2. About two hours: The woman clearly states 'The installation usually takes about two hours, depending on your apartment's setup.'
❌ 3. Three hours: This duration is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A full day: The installation is not expected to take a full day according to the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: One more thing - what happens if I have technical issues after installation?
WOMAN: We provide 24/7 technical support. You can call us anytime, and we'll help troubleshoot over the phone. If needed, we'll send a technician at no extra cost.
MAN: That's good to know. And what about the billing cycle?
WOMAN: Your billing cycle will start on your installation date. We'll charge your card on the same date each month. Would you like to set up automatic payments?
MAN: Yes, that would be convenient. Here's my credit card.
WOMAN: Perfect. I'll just need you to sign these forms, and then we're all set for Thursday's installation.
MAN: Great! And I'll get an email confirmation?
WOMAN: Yes, you'll receive a confirmation email with your appointment details and account information within the next hour.
MAN: Perfect, thank you for your help!

**Q6:** What is included in the technical support?
- 1. Phone support only
- 2. Online chat support
- 3. Free technician visits if needed
- 4. Limited hours of support
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Phone support only: The support includes both phone support and technician visits if needed.
❌ 2. Online chat support: Chat support is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Free technician visits if needed: The woman states 'If needed, we'll send a technician at no extra cost.'
❌ 4. Limited hours of support: The woman specifically mentions '24/7 technical support,' not limited hours.

**Q7:** When will the customer's billing cycle begin?
- 1. Immediately
- 2. Next month
- 3. On the installation date
- 4. After the first payment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately: The billing doesn't start immediately after the conversation.
❌ 2. Next month: There's no mention of billing starting next month.
✅ 3. On the installation date: The woman clearly states 'Your billing cycle will start on your installation date.'
❌ 4. After the first payment: This is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What will happen after the conversation?
- 1. A technician will arrive
- 2. The customer will receive an email
- 3. The service will start
- 4. The payment will be processed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A technician will arrive: The technician is scheduled for Thursday, not immediately after the conversation.
✅ 2. The customer will receive an email: The woman states 'you'll receive a confirmation email with your appointment details and account information within the next hour.'
❌ 3. The service will start: The service will start after installation on Thursday, not immediately after the conversation.
❌ 4. The payment will be processed: While payment information is provided, there's no mention of immediate processing.

## Practice 355: Cancelled Flight Rebooking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at an airline customer service desk. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a passenger.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, I really need some help. My flight to Vancouver was just cancelled due to mechanical issues.
WOMAN: I'm sorry to hear that, sir. Let me help you with rebooking. May I see your boarding pass and ID, please?
MAN: Here they are. I really need to get there today. It's my sister's wedding tomorrow.
WOMAN: I understand your situation. Let me check what's available... I see we have a flight departing at 4:30 PM with one stopover in Calgary.
MAN: A stopover? My original flight was direct. How long is the layover?
WOMAN: The layover is about two hours. You'd arrive in Vancouver around 11:00 PM.
MAN: That's quite late, but I guess I don't have much choice. Are there any direct flights?
WOMAN: I'm checking... Unfortunately, all direct flights today are fully booked due to the cancellation.

**Q1:** Why does the man need to travel today?
- 1. He has a business meeting
- 2. He's attending a wedding
- 3. He's visiting family
- 4. He has a connecting flight
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has a business meeting: There is no mention of a business meeting in the conversation.
✅ 2. He's attending a wedding: The man specifically states 'I really need to get there today. It's my sister's wedding tomorrow.'
❌ 3. He's visiting family: While his sister is family, the specific reason for travel is to attend her wedding, not just a family visit.
❌ 4. He has a connecting flight: There is no mention of the man having a connecting flight beyond Vancouver.

**Q2:** What is the main issue with the alternative flight option?
- 1. It's more expensive
- 2. It has a layover
- 3. It's for the next day
- 4. It's overbooked
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's more expensive: There is no mention of the alternative flight being more expensive.
✅ 2. It has a layover: The man specifically expresses concern about this, saying 'A stopover? My original flight was direct' and asks about the length of the layover.
❌ 3. It's for the next day: The alternative flight is for the same day, departing at 4:30 PM.
❌ 4. It's overbooked: The direct flights are mentioned as being fully booked, not the alternative flight with the stopover.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Before I accept the flight with the stopover, could you tell me if there's any compensation for the inconvenience?
WOMAN: Yes, absolutely. Since this was a mechanical issue, we'll provide you with a meal voucher for $20 that you can use at any airport restaurant.
MAN: Just $20? This is really disrupting my travel plans.
WOMAN: I understand, sir. We can also offer you 5,000 bonus miles on your frequent flyer account.
MAN: Well, that's something at least. Oh, what about my checked baggage?
WOMAN: Don't worry about your luggage. We'll transfer it automatically to your new flight.
MAN: And will it make it through the connection in Calgary?
WOMAN: Yes, it will be checked through to your final destination in Vancouver.

**Q3:** What compensation is the airline offering?
- 1. A meal voucher only
- 2. Bonus miles only
- 3. A meal voucher and bonus miles
- 4. A free hotel stay
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A meal voucher only: The airline offers more than just a meal voucher.
❌ 2. Bonus miles only: The airline offers more than just bonus miles.
✅ 3. A meal voucher and bonus miles: The agent offers both 'a meal voucher for $20' and '5,000 bonus miles on your frequent flyer account.'
❌ 4. A free hotel stay: There is no mention of a hotel stay in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the airline promise about the luggage?
- 1. It will be delivered separately
- 2. The passenger must recheck it
- 3. It will be automatically transferred
- 4. It will arrive the next day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It will be delivered separately: The airline does not say the luggage will be delivered separately.
❌ 2. The passenger must recheck it: The agent specifically says they will transfer it automatically, so the passenger does not need to recheck it.
✅ 3. It will be automatically transferred: The agent states 'We'll transfer it automatically to your new flight' and confirms it will be 'checked through to your final destination.'
❌ 4. It will arrive the next day: There is no mention of the luggage arriving the next day.

**Q5:** Why is the man concerned about his baggage?
- 1. It might get lost during the connection
- 2. It's oversized
- 3. He needs to pay extra fees
- 4. It contains valuable items
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It might get lost during the connection: The man asks 'And will it make it through the connection in Calgary?' showing his concern about the baggage transfer during the connection.
❌ 2. It's oversized: There is no mention of the baggage being oversized.
❌ 3. He needs to pay extra fees: There is no mention of extra fees for baggage.
❌ 4. It contains valuable items: There is no mention of valuable items in the baggage.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: One more thing - I paid extra for a window seat on my original flight. Will I get the same on this new one?
WOMAN: Let me check your new seat assignment... I can offer you 14F, which is a window seat in the emergency exit row.
MAN: Emergency exit row? Don't you charge extra for those usually?
WOMAN: Yes, we do. We're upgrading you at no additional cost due to the inconvenience.
MAN: That's good to know. And about that meal voucher - where do I get it?
WOMAN: I'll print it right now along with your new boarding pass. Just present it at any restaurant in the terminal.
MAN: Okay, thanks. And what gate will the new flight depart from?
WOMAN: Your flight to Calgary will depart from Gate B15. Everything is printed on your new boarding pass.

**Q6:** What type of seat is the man offered on the new flight?
- 1. A middle seat
- 2. An aisle seat
- 3. A premium economy seat
- 4. An emergency exit window seat
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A middle seat: The conversation specifically mentions the man is offered seat 14F, which is a window seat, not a middle seat.
❌ 2. An aisle seat: The agent clearly states she's offering seat 14F, which is identified as a window seat, not an aisle seat.
❌ 3. A premium economy seat: There is no mention of premium economy in the conversation.
✅ 4. An emergency exit window seat: The agent specifically says 'I can offer you 14F, which is a window seat in the emergency exit row.'

**Q7:** What is unique about the new seat assignment?
- 1. It's in first class
- 2. It's a free upgrade
- 3. It's near the bathroom
- 4. It has extra legroom
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's in first class: There is no mention of first class in the conversation.
✅ 2. It's a free upgrade: The agent says 'We're upgrading you at no additional cost due to the inconvenience' when referring to the emergency exit row seat that usually costs extra.
❌ 3. It's near the bathroom: There is no mention of proximity to the bathroom in the conversation.
❌ 4. It has extra legroom: While emergency exit rows typically have extra legroom, this specific feature is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What will the agent provide to the passenger?
- 1. Only a new boarding pass
- 2. A boarding pass and meal voucher
- 3. A gate change notification
- 4. A luggage claim ticket
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only a new boarding pass: The agent will provide more than just a boarding pass.
✅ 2. A boarding pass and meal voucher: The agent states 'I'll print it [the meal voucher] right now along with your new boarding pass.'
❌ 3. A gate change notification: While the gate information is mentioned, it's printed on the boarding pass, not provided as a separate notification.
❌ 4. A luggage claim ticket: There is no mention of a luggage claim ticket being provided.

## Practice 356: Conference Room Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a company's reception desk. The woman is the receptionist, and the man is an employee.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there! I need to book a conference room for next week. Can you help me with that?
WOMAN: Of course! Let me check the availability. Do you have a specific date and time in mind?
MAN: Yes, I'm looking at Wednesday afternoon, probably around 2 PM. It's for a client presentation.
WOMAN: Okay, let me see what we have. The main conference room is already booked for that time, but the East Wing room is available. Would that work?
MAN: How many people can it accommodate? I'll have about 12 people attending.
WOMAN: The East Wing room can seat up to 15 people comfortably. It also has a projector and video conferencing equipment.
MAN: Perfect! I'll take that one then.
WOMAN: Great! How long do you need it for?
MAN: I think two hours should be enough.
WOMAN: Alright, I'll book it from 2 to 4 PM on Wednesday.

**Q1:** What is the man's primary request?
- 1. To schedule a client meeting
- 2. To reserve a conference room
- 3. To arrange for catering
- 4. To book video conferencing equipment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To schedule a client meeting: While the man mentions it's for a client presentation, this isn't his request to the receptionist.
✅ 2. To reserve a conference room: The man's first statement is "I need to book a conference room for next week."
❌ 3. To arrange for catering: This is a secondary request that comes later in the conversation.
❌ 4. To book video conferencing equipment: The man doesn't specifically request this equipment.

**Q2:** What was initially suggested to the man?
- 1. The Main Conference room
- 2. A room without equipment
- 3. The East Wing room
- 4. A smaller meeting space
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The Main Conference room: This was not initially suggested as it was already booked.
❌ 2. A room without equipment: The East Wing room had equipment (projector and video conferencing).
✅ 3. The East Wing room: The receptionist first suggests "the East Wing room is available. Would that work?"
❌ 4. A smaller meeting space: There's no mention of a smaller meeting space being suggested.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry to bother you again, but I forgot to ask about the equipment in the East Wing room.
WOMAN: No problem! What would you like to know?
MAN: Does it have a smart board? I'll need to make some real-time annotations during the presentation.
WOMAN: Actually, the East Wing room doesn't have a smart board. Only the Main Conference room has that feature.
MAN: Oh, that's going to be a problem. Is there any chance the Main Conference room might become available?
WOMAN: Let me check again... Actually, I see that the afternoon booking in the Main Conference room is only until 2:30. Would you be able to start your meeting at 3 PM instead?
MAN: Yes, that would work perfectly! Could you switch my booking to that room?
WOMAN: Of course! I'll make that change right now.

**Q3:** Why does the man change his room preference?
- 1. The first room was too small
- 2. He needs specific technology
- 3. The timing didn't work
- 4. The cost was too high
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The first room was too small: The East Wing room could accommodate his group size.
✅ 2. He needs specific technology: The man changes his preference because he needs a smart board, saying "I'll need to make some real-time annotations during the presentation."
❌ 3. The timing didn't work: The timing issue was with the Main Conference room, not the East Wing room.
❌ 4. The cost was too high: There's no mention of cost in the conversation.

**Q4:** What compromise is reached about the booking?
- 1. A different room is chosen
- 2. The meeting is shortened
- 3. The start time is changed
- 4. Extra equipment is added
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A different room is chosen: While rooms are discussed, the compromise is about timing, not the room itself.
❌ 2. The meeting is shortened: There's no indication the meeting duration was changed.
✅ 3. The start time is changed: The receptionist suggests starting at 3 PM instead of 2 PM, and the man agrees.
❌ 4. Extra equipment is added: No extra equipment is mentioned as being added.

**Q5:** What does the woman discover when checking again?
- 1. The Main Conference room becomes free earlier
- 2. The Main Conference room is partially available
- 3. A cancellation has occurred
- 4. Another room has opened up
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The Main Conference room becomes free earlier: This isn't stated in the conversation.
✅ 2. The Main Conference room is partially available: She discovers "the afternoon booking in the Main Conference room is only until 2:30."
❌ 3. A cancellation has occurred: There's no mention of any cancellation.
❌ 4. Another room has opened up: No other room is mentioned as becoming available.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I hate to be a bother, but there's one more thing about the conference room booking.
WOMAN: That's no problem at all. What can I help you with?
MAN: I just got word that we'll need catering for the meeting. Is that something you can arrange?
WOMAN: Yes, we work with several catering companies. How many people will need to be served?
MAN: It will be 12 people, and we'll need some light refreshments - maybe coffee, water, and some snacks?
WOMAN: I can arrange that. Our usual caterer offers a corporate package that includes beverages, pastries, and fruit platters. Would that work?
MAN: That sounds perfect. Can they deliver by 2:45 PM?
WOMAN: Yes, I'll make sure everything is set up before your meeting starts at 3 PM.
MAN: Great, thank you so much for all your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Let me know if you need anything else.

**Q6:** What additional service does the man request?
- 1. Technical support
- 2. Food and beverages
- 3. Extra seating
- 4. Room decoration
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Technical support: The man doesn't request technical support.
✅ 2. Food and beverages: The man says "I just got word that we'll need catering for the meeting" and specifies "coffee, water, and some snacks."
❌ 3. Extra seating: There's no mention of extra seating requests.
❌ 4. Room decoration: The conversation doesn't include any request for room decoration.

**Q7:** What type of catering package is suggested?
- 1. A full lunch menu
- 2. A corporate refreshment package
- 3. A breakfast package
- 4. A dinner buffet
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A full lunch menu: The conversation doesn't mention a full lunch menu.
✅ 2. A corporate refreshment package: The receptionist specifically mentions "Our usual caterer offers a corporate package that includes beverages, pastries, and fruit platters."
❌ 3. A breakfast package: There's no mention of a breakfast package in the conversation.
❌ 4. A dinner buffet: The conversation doesn't reference a dinner buffet.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the catering setup?
- 1. It will be done during the meeting
- 2. It will be ready 15 minutes before the meeting
- 3. The man will need to set it up
- 4. It will arrive after the meeting starts
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It will be done during the meeting: This contradicts the information provided.
✅ 2. It will be ready 15 minutes before the meeting: The woman says "I'll make sure everything is set up before your meeting starts at 3 PM" and the man requested delivery by 2:45 PM (15 minutes before).
❌ 3. The man will need to set it up: The receptionist says she'll make sure it's set up.
❌ 4. It will arrive after the meeting starts: This contradicts the agreed delivery time of 2:45 PM.

## Practice 357: Bicycle Tune-up Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a bicycle repair shop. The woman works at the repair shop, and the man is a customer with a bicycle problem.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm having some issues with my bike. The gears keep slipping when I change them.
WOMAN: Welcome to City Bikes! I can help you with that. How long have you been experiencing this problem?
MAN: It started about two weeks ago. At first, it was just occasional, but now it's happening almost every time I shift gears.
WOMAN: I see. This is actually a common issue. It could be related to your gear cable tension or derailleur adjustment. When was the last time you had your bike serviced?
MAN: To be honest, I've never had it serviced. I bought it new about eight months ago.
WOMAN: Ah, that explains it. We recommend getting a tune-up after the first six months of use. The cables stretch naturally during this period.
MAN: Oh, I had no idea. How long would a tune-up take?
WOMAN: Usually about two hours. We can check everything else while we're at it too.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the customer is experiencing?
- 1. The brakes are not working
- 2. The bike chain is broken
- 3. The gears are slipping
- 4. The wheels are damaged
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The brakes are not working: The customer doesn't mention that the brakes aren't working, only that they're squeaking.
❌ 2. The bike chain is broken: There's no mention of a broken chain in the conversation.
✅ 3. The gears are slipping: The man specifically states at the beginning, "I'm having some issues with my bike. The gears keep slipping when I change them."
❌ 4. The wheels are damaged: There's no mention of damaged wheels in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why did the problem likely occur?
- 1. The bike is too old
- 2. The customer hasn't serviced it since purchase
- 3. The customer damaged the bike
- 4. The bike was defective when bought
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The bike is too old: The bike is only 8 months old, not considered old for a bicycle.
✅ 2. The customer hasn't serviced it since purchase: The woman explains that bikes should be serviced after the first six months because "cables stretch naturally during this period," but the customer admits he's never had it serviced in the 8 months since purchase.
❌ 3. The customer damaged the bike: There's no suggestion that the customer damaged the bike.
❌ 4. The bike was defective when bought: There's no indication the bike was defective; the issues developed over time due to normal use without maintenance.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That sounds good. What exactly is included in the tune-up service?
WOMAN: We'll adjust your brakes, check and lubricate the chain, true the wheels if needed, and of course, fix that gear-shifting issue. We also do a safety check of all components.
MAN: Perfect. And how much would that cost?
WOMAN: The basic tune-up is $75. However, if we find any parts that need replacing, that would be extra. Would you like me to check our schedule?
MAN: Yes, please. I use my bike to commute to work, so I'm hoping to get it done soon.
WOMAN: Let's see... we're quite busy this week, but I can fit you in next Tuesday morning. Would that work?
MAN: Tuesday morning? Yes, that should be fine. What time do you open?
WOMAN: We open at 8 AM, and the service would take about two hours as I mentioned.

**Q3:** What service package does the woman suggest?
- 1. A complete bike replacement
- 2. A basic tune-up service
- 3. A gear-only repair
- 4. An emergency repair
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A complete bike replacement: There's no suggestion of replacing the entire bike.
✅ 2. A basic tune-up service: The woman specifically recommends a tune-up and describes what's included in this service.
❌ 3. A gear-only repair: While fixing the gear issue is part of the service, the woman suggests a comprehensive tune-up that includes multiple checks and adjustments.
❌ 4. An emergency repair: There's no mention of emergency repair services.

**Q4:** When is the earliest available appointment?
- 1. Later this week
- 2. This afternoon
- 3. Next Tuesday morning
- 4. Tomorrow morning
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Later this week: The woman says they're quite busy this week.
❌ 2. This afternoon: There's no mention of an afternoon appointment being available.
✅ 3. Next Tuesday morning: The woman states, "I can fit you in next Tuesday morning" and confirms they open at 8 AM.
❌ 4. Tomorrow morning: There's no mention of a next-day appointment being available.

**Q5:** How much will the customer likely pay?
- 1. Exactly $75
- 2. Less than $75
- 3. More than $75
- 4. The price wasn't discussed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Exactly $75: The woman states that the basic tune-up is $75, but also mentions "if we find any parts that need replacing, that would be extra."
❌ 2. Less than $75: There's no indication the price would be lower than the stated $75 basic tune-up cost.
✅ 3. More than $75: Since the bike hasn't been serviced in 8 months and has multiple issues (gear slipping and squeaky brakes), it's likely that parts may need replacing, which would cost extra beyond the $75 basic tune-up.
❌ 4. The price wasn't discussed: The price was clearly discussed - $75 for the basic tune-up plus extra for any replacement parts.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Great, I'll bring it in first thing Tuesday. Oh, one more question - do you have a secure place to store my bike accessories?
WOMAN: We recommend taking any removable items with you, like lights, water bottles, or bike computers. We're not responsible for those items.
MAN: That makes sense. I'll remove them before bringing it in. Do I need to clean the bike before bringing it in?
WOMAN: No need to clean it, but it would help if you could let us know about any specific issues you've noticed besides the gear shifting.
MAN: Well, the brakes have been squeaking a bit lately too.
WOMAN: We'll definitely check that out. Just fill out this service form with your contact details, and we'll see you on Tuesday at 8 AM.
MAN: Perfect, thank you for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Don't forget to bring your ID when you drop off the bike.

**Q6:** What does the shop recommend about bike accessories?
- 1. Leave them attached to the bike
- 2. Store them at the shop
- 3. Take them home
- 4. Lock them to the bike
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Leave them attached to the bike: The shop specifically recommends not leaving accessories on the bike.
❌ 2. Store them at the shop: The woman states they're not responsible for these items, indicating they don't offer storage for them.
✅ 3. Take them home: The woman clearly states, "We recommend taking any removable items with you, like lights, water bottles, or bike computers."
❌ 4. Lock them to the bike: There's no mention of locking accessories to the bike.

**Q7:** What additional problem does the customer mention?
- 1. The wheels are wobbly
- 2. The brakes are squeaking
- 3. The chain is rusty
- 4. The seat is loose
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The wheels are wobbly: The customer doesn't mention any issues with the wheels.
✅ 2. The brakes are squeaking: Near the end of the conversation, the man states, "Well, the brakes have been squeaking a bit lately too."
❌ 3. The chain is rusty: There's no mention of a rusty chain.
❌ 4. The seat is loose: There's no mention of issues with the seat.

**Q8:** What must the customer bring when dropping off the bike?
- 1. The original purchase receipt
- 2. The bike manual
- 3. Identification
- 4. Payment in advance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The original purchase receipt: There's no mention of needing to bring the purchase receipt.
❌ 2. The bike manual: There's no mention of needing to bring the bike manual.
✅ 3. Identification: At the end of the conversation, the woman specifically says, "Don't forget to bring your ID when you drop off the bike."
❌ 4. Payment in advance: There's no mention of needing to pay in advance.
