## Practice 358: Local Move Estimate | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a moving company office. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is inquiring about moving services.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Good morning! Welcome to Swift Moving Company. How can I assist you today?
MAN: Hi, I'm planning to move next month and wanted to get some information about your services and rates.
WOMAN: Of course! I'd be happy to help. Do you know the exact date you're planning to move?
MAN: Yes, I'm looking at July 15th. It would be a local move, from downtown to the suburbs.
WOMAN: Perfect. For local moves, we charge by the hour, plus a flat fee for the truck. How big is your current place?
MAN: It's a two-bedroom apartment on the third floor. There's no elevator, unfortunately.
WOMAN: I see. That's important to know. For a two-bedroom apartment, we typically recommend a three-person crew. The rate would be $150 per hour, plus $100 for the truck.
MAN: That sounds reasonable. How many hours do you think it might take?
WOMAN: Based on similar moves, I'd estimate 4-5 hours, depending on the amount of furniture and boxes.

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the man's visit?
- 1. To schedule a move immediately
- 2. To get information about moving services
- 3. To purchase packing supplies
- 4. To hire piano movers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To schedule a move immediately: This is incorrect. The man says he wants to think about it and call back tomorrow.
✅ 2. To get information about moving services: This is correct. The man's opening statement is "I'm planning to move next month and wanted to get some information about your services and rates."
❌ 3. To purchase packing supplies: This is incorrect. While packing supplies are discussed, this was not the main purpose of his visit.
❌ 4. To hire piano movers: This is incorrect. The piano was mentioned later in the conversation as an afterthought ("I just remembered I have a piano"), not as the main purpose.

**Q2:** What is the base cost for the moving service?
- 1. $$100 per hour
- 2. $$150 per hour plus $100
- 3. $$200 total
- 4. $$150 total
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $100 per hour: This is incorrect. The hourly rate mentioned is $150, not $100.
✅ 2. $150 per hour plus $100: This is correct. The woman states, "The rate would be $150 per hour, plus $100 for the truck."
❌ 3. $200 total: This is incorrect. This is the amount of the deposit required to secure a date, not the base cost of the service.
❌ 4. $150 total: This is incorrect. $150 is the hourly rate, not the total cost.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I see. And do you provide packing services as well?
WOMAN: Yes, we offer full packing services. We can pack everything for you the day before the move.
MAN: What about insurance coverage? I have some valuable items I'm concerned about.
WOMAN: We provide basic insurance coverage of $0.60 per pound per item. However, I recommend our premium coverage for valuable items.
MAN: Could you tell me more about the premium coverage?
WOMAN: The premium coverage is based on the declared value of your items. It's $10 per $1,000 of coverage.
MAN: That makes sense. And what about packing supplies? Do you sell those?
WOMAN: Yes, we have a full range of supplies - boxes, tape, bubble wrap, and wardrobe boxes. We can deliver them to your current address.
MAN: Great! That would be helpful. Could you give me a rough estimate for supplies?

**Q3:** What type of insurance does the company recommend?
- 1. Basic coverage only
- 2. No insurance coverage
- 3. Premium coverage for valuable items
- 4. Third-party insurance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Basic coverage only: This is incorrect. While the company does offer basic coverage, the woman specifically recommends premium coverage for valuable items.
❌ 2. No insurance coverage: This is incorrect. The company does offer insurance coverage options.
✅ 3. Premium coverage for valuable items: This is correct. When the man asks about insurance for valuable items, the woman responds, "I recommend our premium coverage for valuable items."
❌ 4. Third-party insurance: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention any third-party insurance options.

**Q4:** How much does the premium insurance coverage cost?
- 1. $$0.60 per pound
- 2. $$1,000 per item
- 3. $$10 per $1,000 of coverage
- 4. $$10 per item
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $0.60 per pound: This is incorrect. This is the rate for the basic insurance coverage, not the premium coverage.
❌ 2. $1,000 per item: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention this rate for insurance.
✅ 3. $10 per $1,000 of coverage: This is correct. The woman explains, "The premium coverage is based on the declared value of your items. It's $10 per $1,000 of coverage."
❌ 4. $10 per item: This is incorrect. The premium coverage is not charged per item but based on the declared value.

**Q5:** What service does the company offer regarding packing supplies?
- 1. They must be picked up at the store
- 2. They offer delivery to current address
- 3. They only sell boxes
- 4. They provide them for free
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They must be picked up at the store: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention this requirement.
✅ 2. They offer delivery to current address: This is correct. The woman states, "We can deliver them to your current address" when discussing packing supplies.
❌ 3. They only sell boxes: This is incorrect. The woman mentions they sell a "full range of supplies - boxes, tape, bubble wrap, and wardrobe boxes."
❌ 4. They provide them for free: This is incorrect. The conversation indicates there is a cost for supplies, with the woman estimating "$200-300 on supplies" for a two-bedroom apartment.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: For a two-bedroom apartment, most customers spend around $200-300 on supplies. Would you like me to create a detailed list?
MAN: Yes, please. And one more thing - I just remembered I have a piano. Do you handle those?
WOMAN: Yes, we do, but there's an additional fee for specialty items like pianos. It's $250 extra, and we'll send our piano specialist team.
MAN: That's fine. Better to have experts handling it. So, what's the next step to book the move?
WOMAN: I can create a reservation now if you'd like. We just need a $200 deposit to secure the date.
MAN: Could I think about it for a day and call back tomorrow?
WOMAN: Of course! Just keep in mind that July is a busy month, so I'd recommend booking soon to get your preferred date.
MAN: Thanks for all the information. I'll definitely call back tomorrow.

**Q6:** What is the additional cost for moving a piano?
- 1. $$200
- 2. $$250
- 3. $$300
- 4. $$150
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $200: This is incorrect. The woman specifically mentions that the additional fee for specialty items like pianos is $250, not $200.
✅ 2. $250: This is the correct amount. The woman states, "Yes, we do, but there's an additional fee for specialty items like pianos. It's $250 extra, and we'll send our piano specialist team."
❌ 3. $300: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention $300 as the fee for moving a piano.
❌ 4. $150: This is incorrect. The hourly rate for the moving crew is $150, but this is not the additional cost for moving a piano.

**Q7:** What does the company require to secure a moving date?
- 1. Full payment in advance
- 2. A $200 deposit
- 3. A signed contract
- 4. Credit card information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Full payment in advance: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention requiring full payment in advance.
✅ 2. A $200 deposit: This is correct. The woman states, "We just need a $200 deposit to secure the date."
❌ 3. A signed contract: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention a signed contract requirement.
❌ 4. Credit card information: This is incorrect. The conversation does not specifically mention requiring credit card information.

**Q8:** What is implied about booking in July?
- 1. It's cheaper than other months
- 2. The company offers special rates
- 3. Dates may be limited due to demand
- 4. The weather is better for moving
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's cheaper than other months: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention any price differences between months.
❌ 2. The company offers special rates: This is incorrect. No special rates for July are mentioned.
✅ 3. Dates may be limited due to demand: This is correct. The woman warns, "Just keep in mind that July is a busy month, so I'd recommend booking soon to get your preferred date," implying that availability may be limited.
❌ 4. The weather is better for moving: This is incorrect. The conversation does not mention weather conditions.

## Practice 359: Mobile Plan Upgrade | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a mobile phone store. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm interested in upgrading my phone plan. I've been with your company for about three years now.
WOMAN: Hello! I'd be happy to help you with that. Could I see your current phone and ID, please?
MAN: Sure, here they are. I'm currently on the basic plan with 4GB of data, but I keep running out halfway through the month.
WOMAN: I see. Looking at your usage history, you're consistently going over your data limit. We have several plans that might better suit your needs. Our most popular is the Premium Plan with 15GB of data.
MAN: That sounds like it might work. What's the monthly cost?
WOMAN: It's $65 per month, which includes unlimited calling and texting. Plus, right now we have a promotion where you get the first three months at half price.
MAN: That's interesting. Could you tell me about any other options?

**Q1:** What is the man's current data plan?
- 1. 2GB basic plan
- 2. 4GB basic plan
- 3. 8GB standard plan
- 4. 15GB premium plan
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 2GB basic plan: The man does not mention having a 2GB plan.
✅ 2. 4GB basic plan: The man clearly states "I'm currently on the basic plan with 4GB of data, but I keep running out halfway through the month."
❌ 3. 8GB standard plan: This is the plan the man decides to switch to (Standard Plus Plan), not his current plan.
❌ 4. 15GB premium plan: This is one of the options presented to the man, not his current plan.

**Q2:** What promotion is currently being offered on the Premium Plan?
- 1. Three months free service
- 2. Half price for six months
- 3. 50% off for three months
- 4. First month free
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Three months free service: The conversation mentions a promotion for the Premium Plan, but it's not free service.
❌ 2. Half price for six months: The promotion is for three months, not six months.
✅ 3. 50% off for three months: The woman specifically states "right now we have a promotion where you get the first three months at half price" for the Premium Plan.
❌ 4. First month free: The promotion is half price for three months, not one month free.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: We also have our Ultimate Plan with 25GB of data for $85 per month. It includes free streaming on most popular platforms.
MAN: That seems a bit excessive for my needs. What about something between my current plan and the Premium Plan?
WOMAN: Well, there's our Standard Plus Plan with 8GB of data for $45 per month. It might be perfect for you based on your average usage.
MAN: That sounds more reasonable. Does it include the same calling and texting features?
WOMAN: Yes, all our plans now come with unlimited calling and texting within North America. The main difference is just the data allowance.
MAN: And what happens if I go over the 8GB limit?
WOMAN: Your speed would be reduced, but you won't be charged extra. You can also add data anytime through our mobile app.

**Q3:** What is unique about the Ultimate Plan?
- 1. It has unlimited data
- 2. It includes free streaming
- 3. It offers international calling
- 4. It has premium customer support
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It has unlimited data: The Ultimate Plan has 25GB of data, not unlimited data.
✅ 2. It includes free streaming: The woman specifically mentions that the Ultimate Plan "includes free streaming on most popular platforms."
❌ 3. It offers international calling: International calling is not mentioned as a feature of the Ultimate Plan.
❌ 4. It has premium customer support: Premium customer support is not mentioned as a feature of any plan.

**Q4:** What happens when customers exceed their data limit on the Standard Plus Plan?
- 1. They are charged extra fees
- 2. Their service is suspended
- 3. Their speed is reduced
- 4. They receive a warning message
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are charged extra fees: The woman specifically states "you won't be charged extra" when going over the data limit.
❌ 2. Their service is suspended: There is no mention of service suspension for exceeding data limits.
✅ 3. Their speed is reduced: The woman clearly states "Your speed would be reduced, but you won't be charged extra" when the man asks what happens if he goes over the 8GB limit.
❌ 4. They receive a warning message: There is no mention of warning messages for exceeding data limits.

**Q5:** Which statement is true about all the plans mentioned?
- 1. They all include international calling
- 2. They all have unlimited data
- 3. They all offer North American calling
- 4. They all have the same monthly cost
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They all include international calling: International calling is not included in any of the base plans and requires an add-on.
❌ 2. They all have unlimited data: The plans have different data limits (4GB, 8GB, 15GB, 25GB).
✅ 3. They all offer North American calling: The woman states "all our plans now come with unlimited calling and texting within North America."
❌ 4. They all have the same monthly cost: The plans have different costs ($45 for Standard Plus, $65 for Premium, $85 for Ultimate).

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I think I'll go with the Standard Plus Plan. Oh, one more thing - what about international calling?
WOMAN: International calling isn't included, but we can add our Global Package for $10 monthly. It gives you reduced rates to most countries.
MAN: Actually, I mainly call my sister in the UK. Do you have any specific packages for that?
WOMAN: Yes! We have a UK-specific add-on for just $5 monthly. It includes 100 minutes of calling to the UK.
MAN: Perfect! I'll take the Standard Plus Plan with the UK calling add-on.
WOMAN: Great choice! I'll process that right now. The changes will take effect on your next billing cycle, which starts next week.
MAN: Sounds good. Thanks for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Don't hesitate to come back if you have any questions.

**Q6:** Why does the man ask about international calling?
- 1. He travels frequently
- 2. He has family in the UK
- 3. He runs an international business
- 4. He wants the Global Package
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He travels frequently: The man does not mention traveling as a reason for needing international calling.
✅ 2. He has family in the UK: The man specifically states "I mainly call my sister in the UK" when asking about international calling options.
❌ 3. He runs an international business: There is no mention of the man running a business.
❌ 4. He wants the Global Package: While the Global Package is mentioned, the man specifically asks about calling the UK, not about getting this package.

**Q7:** What is the cost difference between the Global Package and the UK-specific add-on?
- 1. $$5
- 2. $$10
- 3. $$15
- 4. $$20
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. $5: The Global Package costs $10 monthly, while the UK-specific add-on costs $5 monthly, making the difference $5.
❌ 2. $10: This is the cost of the Global Package, not the difference between the two options.
❌ 3. $15: This is not the correct difference between the two packages.
❌ 4. $20: This is not the correct difference between the two packages.

**Q8:** When will the changes to the man's plan take effect?
- 1. Immediately
- 2. Next month
- 3. Next week
- 4. In three months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately: The woman does not say the changes will take effect immediately.
❌ 2. Next month: The woman does not say the changes will take effect next month.
✅ 3. Next week: The woman specifically states "The changes will take effect on your next billing cycle, which starts next week."
❌ 4. In three months: The woman does not say the changes will take effect in three months.

## Practice 360: Children&#x27;s Musical Auditions | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two parents at a community center. They are discussing their children's upcoming theater auditions.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main concern of the woman?
- 1. Her son's lack of singing ability
- 2. Her son's young age
- 3. The length of the audition process
- 4. The competition for roles
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Her son's lack of singing ability: The woman mentions her son has been 'singing non-stop,' suggesting no concern about his ability.
✅ 2. Her son's young age: The woman explicitly states 'I'm a bit nervous though – he's only eight,' showing this is her main concern.
❌ 3. The length of the audition process: While she's surprised by the three-hour length, this comes up later and isn't presented as her main concern.
❌ 4. The competition for roles: The woman doesn't express any concern about competition for roles in the conversation.

**Q2:** What does the man suggest about the audition process?
- 1. Children must prepare multiple songs
- 2. Parents should stay to watch
- 3. It focuses mainly on individual talent
- 4. It emphasizes following directions and teamwork
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Children must prepare multiple songs: The man says they'll teach children a short song, and it helps if they bring one they know well, but multiple songs aren't required.
❌ 2. Parents should stay to watch: The man specifically says 'they prefer if we don't' stay and watch.
❌ 3. It focuses mainly on individual talent: The man emphasizes group activities and working with others rather than individual talent.
✅ 4. It emphasizes following directions and teamwork: The man explicitly states that 'The directors mainly want to see how well they follow instructions and work with others.'

**Q3:** Why are there two casts for the musical?
- 1. To allow more children to participate
- 2. To have backup performers
- 3. To perform on different days
- 4. To separate age groups
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To allow more children to participate: The man specifically says 'They're planning to have two different casts to give more kids a chance to perform.'
❌ 2. To have backup performers: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. To perform on different days: While this might be implied, it's not stated as the reason for having two casts.
❌ 4. To separate age groups: There is no mention of separating children by age in the conversation.

**Q4:** What do the parents decide to do during the audition?
- 1. Watch their children perform
- 2. Help with the registration process
- 3. Go to a nearby coffee shop
- 4. Assist the theater directors
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Watch their children perform: The man mentions that the directors prefer parents not to stay and watch.
❌ 2. Help with the registration process: There's no mention of parents helping with registration.
✅ 3. Go to a nearby coffee shop: The man suggests 'There's a coffee shop around the corner where most parents hang out. Would you like to join me there?' and the woman agrees.
❌ 4. Assist the theater directors: There's no mention of parents assisting the directors during the audition.

**Q5:** What inspired Tommy to try out for the musical?
- 1. His friends' involvement in theater
- 2. His sister's previous performances
- 3. A musical he saw downtown
- 4. His mother's encouragement
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His friends' involvement in theater: There is no mention of Tommy's friends being involved in theater.
❌ 2. His sister's previous performances: Tommy doesn't have a sister mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. A musical he saw downtown: The woman specifically says 'he's been singing non-stop since he saw that musical downtown last month.'
❌ 4. His mother's encouragement: The conversation doesn't indicate that the mother encouraged Tommy to try out.

## Practice 361: Security System Consultation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a home security company. The woman is a security consultant, and the man is a potential customer.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm interested in getting a home security system installed. Can you tell me about your services?
WOMAN: Of course! We offer various packages depending on your needs. Are you looking to secure a house or an apartment?
MAN: It's a two-story house. We just moved in last month, and there's been a few break-ins in the neighborhood recently.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. Our most popular package includes door sensors, window sensors, motion detectors, and 24/7 monitoring.
MAN: That sounds good. What about cameras?
WOMAN: We can definitely add those. We have both indoor and outdoor cameras with night vision capabilities. They connect to your smartphone so you can check your property anytime.
MAN: Perfect. Could someone come to my house to assess what I need?
WOMAN: Absolutely! We offer free consultations. When would you like to schedule it?

**Q1:** Why is the man interested in a security system?
- 1. He wants to monitor his children
- 2. There have been security incidents nearby
- 3. His insurance company requires it
- 4. His old system needs replacement
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He wants to monitor his children: There is no mention of children in the conversation.
✅ 2. There have been security incidents nearby: The man specifically states "there's been a few break-ins in the neighborhood recently."
❌ 3. His insurance company requires it: There is no mention of insurance requirements in the conversation.
❌ 4. His old system needs replacement: There is no mention of an existing system that needs replacement.

**Q2:** What feature of the cameras does the woman emphasize?
- 1. Their recording quality
- 2. Their easy installation
- 3. Their remote access capability
- 4. Their warranty coverage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Their recording quality: While night vision is mentioned, recording quality is not emphasized.
❌ 2. Their easy installation: Installation ease is not discussed regarding the cameras.
✅ 3. Their remote access capability: The woman emphasizes "They connect to your smartphone so you can check your property anytime."
❌ 4. Their warranty coverage: No warranty information is provided in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I was thinking about this weekend. Would Saturday morning work?
WOMAN: Let me check our schedule... I see we have an opening at 10 AM. Would that work for you?
MAN: Actually, that's perfect. Do I need to prepare anything for the consultation?
WOMAN: Just make sure someone over 18 is home to let our technician in. It's helpful if you can think about specific areas you're concerned about.
MAN: Got it. Oh, and I wanted to ask about the monthly monitoring fee.
WOMAN: The basic monitoring starts at $29.99 per month. That includes 24/7 professional monitoring and emergency dispatch. Adding cameras would be an extra $10 per camera monthly.
MAN: That seems reasonable. And what about installation costs?
WOMAN: The basic installation fee is $199, but we're running a promotion this month - free installation with a three-year contract.

**Q3:** What special offer is currently available?
- 1. Discounted monthly fees
- 2. Extra cameras at no cost
- 3. No installation charge with contract
- 4. Extended warranty period
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Discounted monthly fees: No discounts on monthly fees are mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Extra cameras at no cost: There is no mention of free cameras in the conversation.
✅ 3. No installation charge with contract: The woman states "The basic installation fee is $199, but we're running a promotion this month - free installation with a three-year contract."
❌ 4. Extended warranty period: No warranty or extended warranty is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What is the basic monthly monitoring fee?
- 1. $$29.99
- 2. $$39.99
- 3. $$10.00
- 4. $$199.00
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. $29.99: The woman clearly states "The basic monitoring starts at $29.99 per month."
❌ 2. $39.99: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. $10.00: This is mentioned as the additional cost per camera monthly, not the basic monitoring fee.
❌ 4. $199.00: This is mentioned as the basic installation fee, not the monthly monitoring fee.

**Q5:** What requirement is mentioned for the consultation?
- 1. Proof of ownership
- 2. Home insurance documents
- 3. An adult present
- 4. Payment deposit
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Proof of ownership: This is not mentioned as a requirement for the consultation.
❌ 2. Home insurance documents: No mention of insurance documents is made in the conversation.
✅ 3. An adult present: The woman specifically says "Just make sure someone over 18 is home to let our technician in."
❌ 4. Payment deposit: No payment deposit is mentioned as being required for the consultation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I have one more question about the cameras. How many would you recommend for a house my size?
WOMAN: For a two-story house, we typically recommend at least four outdoor cameras - one for each corner of the house. Then maybe 1-2 indoor cameras for main areas.
MAN: And these all record continuously?
WOMAN: They can, but most customers prefer motion-activated recording to save storage space. The footage is stored in the cloud for 30 days.
MAN: That makes sense. And if there's a power outage?
WOMAN: All our systems have battery backup that lasts up to 24 hours. The cellular backup ensures monitoring continues even if your internet goes down.
MAN: Perfect, that's exactly what I needed to know. I'll see your technician on Saturday then.
WOMAN: Great! You'll receive a confirmation email shortly. Have a great day!

**Q6:** How many outdoor cameras are suggested for the house?
- 1. Two
- 2. Three
- 3. Four
- 4. Six
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Two: This number is not suggested for outdoor cameras.
❌ 2. Three: This number is not suggested for outdoor cameras.
✅ 3. Four: The woman states "For a two-story house, we typically recommend at least four outdoor cameras - one for each corner of the house."
❌ 4. Six: This number is not suggested in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is mentioned about the camera recording system?
- 1. It only records at night
- 2. It records based on movement
- 3. It records in high definition only
- 4. It requires manual activation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It only records at night: The conversation mentions cameras have night vision capabilities, but doesn't say they only record at night.
✅ 2. It records based on movement: The woman states "most customers prefer motion-activated recording to save storage space."
❌ 3. It records in high definition only: There is no mention of recording quality or high definition in the conversation.
❌ 4. It requires manual activation: The conversation indicates the system can record continuously or be motion-activated, not that it requires manual activation.

**Q8:** What backup feature is NOT mentioned in the conversation?
- 1. Battery backup
- 2. Solar power backup
- 3. Cloud storage
- 4. Cellular backup
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Battery backup: This is mentioned when the woman says "All our systems have battery backup that lasts up to 24 hours."
✅ 2. Solar power backup: There is no mention of solar power backup anywhere in the conversation.
❌ 3. Cloud storage: The woman mentions "The footage is stored in the cloud for 30 days."
❌ 4. Cellular backup: This is mentioned when the woman says "The cellular backup ensures monitoring continues even if your internet goes down."

## Practice 363: Dinner Reservation Adjustment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a restaurant. The woman is the restaurant manager, and the man is a customer who wants to modify his reservation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I made a reservation for tonight under the name Thompson, but I need to make some changes.
WOMAN: Of course, let me pull up your reservation. Yes, I see it here - table for four at 7:30 PM.
MAN: That's right. Unfortunately, two of our friends can't make it anymore. Would it be possible to change it to a table for two?
WOMAN: Absolutely, that won't be a problem. Actually, this might work out better since we're quite busy tonight. Would you like to keep the same time?
MAN: Well, I was wondering if we could move it a bit earlier? Maybe 6:30?
WOMAN: Let me check our availability... Yes, we can accommodate that. I can give you a nice window table for two at 6:30.
MAN: That would be perfect, thank you! Just to confirm - it's now for two people at 6:30?
WOMAN: That's correct. Your reservation is updated, Mr. Thompson.

**Q1:** What is the main change the man wants to make to his reservation?
- 1. To change the date
- 2. To reduce the party size and time
- 3. To request a specific table
- 4. To add more guests
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To change the date: The man doesn't request to change the date of the reservation.
✅ 2. To reduce the party size and time: The man asks to change from a table for four to a table for two, and to move the time from 7:30 to 6:30.
❌ 3. To request a specific table: While he gets a window table, he doesn't specifically request this.
❌ 4. To add more guests: The opposite is true - he wants to reduce the number of guests from four to two.

**Q2:** Why does the woman say the change 'might work out better'?
- 1. They have more small tables
- 2. The restaurant is busy that night
- 3. It's easier to serve fewer people
- 4. The window tables are available
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They have more small tables: This reason isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. The restaurant is busy that night: The woman says "this might work out better since we're quite busy tonight" when the man asks to reduce the party size.
❌ 3. It's easier to serve fewer people: This isn't mentioned as a reason in the conversation.
❌ 4. The window tables are available: While a window table is offered later, this isn't given as the reason why the change works out better.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry to bother you again, but I forgot to ask - do you have a dress code?
WOMAN: Yes, we do maintain a business casual dress code. No shorts or flip-flops, and gentlemen are required to wear collared shirts.
MAN: Oh good, I should be fine then. I was also wondering about parking. Is there a lot nearby?
WOMAN: Yes, we have our own parking lot right behind the restaurant. It's complimentary for our guests. Just show your dining receipt to the attendant when you leave.
MAN: That's convenient. And one more thing - my wife has a gluten allergy. Do you have gluten-free options?
WOMAN: Absolutely! We have quite an extensive gluten-free menu. Our chef can also modify most dishes to accommodate allergies.
MAN: That's great news. She'll be relieved to hear that.

**Q3:** What must diners do to use the parking facility?
- 1. Pay an additional fee
- 2. Make a reservation in advance
- 3. Show their dining receipt
- 4. Register their vehicle
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Pay an additional fee: The woman states the parking is complimentary, so no fee is required.
❌ 2. Make a reservation in advance: There's no mention of needing to reserve parking in advance.
✅ 3. Show their dining receipt: The woman specifically says "Just show your dining receipt to the attendant when you leave."
❌ 4. Register their vehicle: There's no mention of vehicle registration in the conversation.

**Q4:** What concern does the man express about his wife?
- 1. She has dietary restrictions
- 2. She doesn't like formal dining
- 3. She is a vegetarian
- 4. She has multiple allergies
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She has dietary restrictions: The man specifically mentions "my wife has a gluten allergy" which is a dietary restriction.
❌ 2. She doesn't like formal dining: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. She is a vegetarian: The man doesn't mention his wife being vegetarian, only that she has a gluten allergy.
❌ 4. She has multiple allergies: The man only mentions one allergy (gluten), not multiple allergies.

**Q5:** What does the restaurant require from its customers?
- 1. Formal attire
- 2. Business formal dress
- 3. Business casual clothing
- 4. Smart casual wear
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Formal attire: The restaurant doesn't require formal attire.
❌ 2. Business formal dress: This level of formality is not mentioned.
✅ 3. Business casual clothing: The woman explicitly states "we do maintain a business casual dress code."
❌ 4. Smart casual wear: While similar to business casual, this specific term isn't used in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I hate to be a bother, but something else just came up. Would it be possible to push our reservation back to 7:00?
WOMAN: Let me check the availability again... I apologize, but we're fully booked from 7:00 to 8:30. We can still keep your 6:30 reservation, or I could move it to 8:45.
MAN: Oh, that's later than we'd like. I suppose we'll try to make it work at 6:30. Do you have a cancellation list in case something opens up around 7:00?
WOMAN: Yes, I can add you to our waiting list. If anything becomes available, I'll call you right away. What's the best number to reach you?
MAN: It's 555-0123. And just to double-check - you still have us down for two people at 6:30?
WOMAN: Yes, that's correct. I'll call you if an earlier time opens up.

**Q6:** Why can't the restaurant accommodate the man's latest request?
- 1. The restaurant is closing early
- 2. They are fully booked during that time
- 3. The chef is unavailable
- 4. They don't take late reservations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The restaurant is closing early: The conversation doesn't mention anything about closing early.
✅ 2. They are fully booked during that time: The woman says "I apologize, but we're fully booked from 7:00 to 8:30" when the man asks to push the reservation to 7:00.
❌ 3. The chef is unavailable: There's no mention of the chef being unavailable.
❌ 4. They don't take late reservations: The woman actually offers an 8:45 reservation as an alternative, showing they do take late reservations.

**Q7:** What solution does the woman offer?
- 1. A different table location
- 2. A discount for the inconvenience
- 3. To put him on a waiting list
- 4. To speak with the manager
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A different table location: While a window table is mentioned earlier, this isn't offered as a solution to the final scheduling problem.
❌ 2. A discount for the inconvenience: No discount is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. To put him on a waiting list: When the man can't get his preferred 7:00 time, the woman says "I can add you to our waiting list. If anything becomes available, I'll call you right away."
❌ 4. To speak with the manager: The woman is the manager, and she doesn't suggest speaking with anyone else.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will cancel his reservation
- 2. The man will dine at 6:30 unless contacted
- 3. The woman will find another restaurant
- 4. The man will change the date
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will cancel his reservation: There's no indication he plans to cancel.
✅ 2. The man will dine at 6:30 unless contacted: The man agrees to keep the 6:30 reservation while being on the waiting list for a 7:00 opening, so he'll dine at 6:30 unless the restaurant contacts him about an opening.
❌ 3. The woman will find another restaurant: There's no mention of the woman suggesting another restaurant.
❌ 4. The man will change the date: There's no indication he wants to change to a different day.

## Practice 364: Travel Insurance Documentation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at a travel insurance company, and the man is a customer calling about a claim.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about a travel insurance claim I submitted last week. I haven't heard anything back yet.
WOMAN: I can help you with that. Could you provide your claim number, please?
MAN: Yes, it's TC458921. I submitted it on the 15th.
WOMAN: Thank you. Let me pull that up... Yes, I can see your claim here. It's regarding the flight cancellation in Paris, correct?
MAN: That's right. Our return flight was cancelled due to a strike, and we had to book a hotel for two extra nights.
WOMAN: I see that in the system. However, I notice we're still missing some documentation. We need the original flight cancellation notice from the airline and receipts from your hotel stay.
MAN: Oh, I thought I included everything. I have those documents right here. Can I email them now?
WOMAN: Absolutely! Please send them to claims@travelcare.com with your claim number in the subject line.

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the man's call?
- 1. To file a new insurance claim
- 2. To check the status of his claim
- 3. To dispute a claim decision
- 4. To purchase travel insurance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To file a new insurance claim: The man has already submitted his claim the previous week.
✅ 2. To check the status of his claim: The man begins by saying "I'm calling about a travel insurance claim I submitted last week. I haven't heard anything back yet."
❌ 3. To dispute a claim decision: There's no mention of disputing a decision in the conversation.
❌ 4. To purchase travel insurance: The man already has insurance and is calling about an existing claim.

**Q2:** What does the woman request from the man?
- 1. A new claim form
- 2. His policy number
- 3. Additional documents
- 4. Payment information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A new claim form: The woman doesn't request a new claim form.
❌ 2. His policy number: She asks for his claim number, not policy number.
✅ 3. Additional documents: The woman says "We need the original flight cancellation notice from the airline and receipts from your hotel stay" and later asks for the taxi receipt.
❌ 4. Payment information: The woman doesn't request payment information.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, it's me again. I just sent those documents, but I realized I have a question about the hotel receipts.
WOMAN: Of course, what would you like to know?
MAN: Well, the hotel bill includes restaurant charges. Does the insurance cover meals as well?
WOMAN: Yes, your policy includes meal expenses during travel disruptions, up to $75 per person per day.
MAN: That's good to know. Also, we had to take a taxi to the new hotel since it was late at night. Is that covered?
WOMAN: Yes, reasonable transportation costs are included. Did you keep the taxi receipt?
MAN: I did, actually. Should I send that too?
WOMAN: Yes, please. The more documentation you provide, the faster we can process your claim.
MAN: Perfect, I'll scan and send it right away.
WOMAN: Great! Is there anything else you need clarification on?

**Q3:** What does the man learn about meal coverage?
- 1. It's not included in his policy
- 2. It has a daily limit per person
- 3. Only hotel restaurants are covered
- 4. He needs to file a separate claim
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's not included in his policy: The woman confirms meals are covered.
✅ 2. It has a daily limit per person: The woman states "your policy includes meal expenses during travel disruptions, up to $75 per person per day."
❌ 3. Only hotel restaurants are covered: There's no mention of this restriction in the conversation.
❌ 4. He needs to file a separate claim: There's no mention of needing a separate claim for meals.

**Q4:** What additional expense does the man ask about?
- 1. Room service charges
- 2. Taxi fare
- 3. Phone calls
- 4. Internet fees
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Room service charges: The man asks about restaurant charges on the hotel bill, not specifically room service.
✅ 2. Taxi fare: The man specifically asks "we had to take a taxi to the new hotel since it was late at night. Is that covered?"
❌ 3. Phone calls: There's no mention of phone call expenses in the conversation.
❌ 4. Internet fees: There's no mention of internet fees in the conversation.

**Q5:** What is implied about the man's documentation?
- 1. He is very organized
- 2. He lost some receipts
- 3. He submitted everything electronically
- 4. He needs to translate some documents
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He is very organized: The man mentions he has the required documents "right here" and also kept his taxi receipt, suggesting he's organized with his paperwork.
❌ 2. He lost some receipts: There's no mention of lost receipts in the conversation.
❌ 3. He submitted everything electronically: Initially he missed submitting some documents, so he didn't submit everything electronically at first.
❌ 4. He needs to translate some documents: There's no mention of translation requirements in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry to bother you again, but I just received an email about my claim being processed.
WOMAN: No problem at all. What's your concern?
MAN: Well, the email mentions something about a deductible, but I don't remember choosing one when I bought the policy.
WOMAN: Let me check your policy details... Ah, yes. All our comprehensive travel policies have a standard $100 deductible.
MAN: Oh, I see. So that means you'll subtract $100 from the total reimbursement?
WOMAN: That's correct. Based on the documents you've submitted, your total claim is $850, so you'll receive $750 after the deductible.
MAN: And how long until I receive the payment?
WOMAN: Once approved, it typically takes 5-7 business days for the payment to reach your account.
MAN: Perfect, thank you for explaining everything so clearly.

**Q6:** What surprises the man about his claim?
- 1. The processing time
- 2. The deductible requirement
- 3. The total amount
- 4. The payment method
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The processing time: The man doesn't express surprise about the processing time.
✅ 2. The deductible requirement: The man states "the email mentions something about a deductible, but I don't remember choosing one when I bought the policy," showing he was unaware of this requirement.
❌ 3. The total amount: The man doesn't express surprise about the total amount.
❌ 4. The payment method: Payment method is not discussed in the conversation.

**Q7:** How much will the man receive from his claim?
- 1. 850 dollars
- 2. 750 dollars
- 3. 100 dollars
- 4. 700 dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 850 dollars: This is the total claim amount before the deductible is applied.
✅ 2. 750 dollars: The woman states "your total claim is $850, so you'll receive $750 after the deductible."
❌ 3. 100 dollars: This is the amount of the deductible, not what the man will receive.
❌ 4. 700 dollars: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the insurance company's service?
- 1. They process claims quickly
- 2. They have strict documentation requirements
- 3. They are difficult to contact
- 4. They rarely approve full payments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They process claims quickly: The conversation indicates claims take 5-7 business days after approval, not necessarily quick processing.
✅ 2. They have strict documentation requirements: The woman repeatedly asks for specific documentation (flight cancellation notice, hotel receipts, taxi receipt) and mentions "the more documentation you provide, the faster we can process your claim."
❌ 3. They are difficult to contact: The conversation shows the company is responsive and helpful throughout multiple calls.
❌ 4. They rarely approve full payments: The conversation indicates they're processing the claim with just a standard deductible, not refusing full payment.

## Practice 365: Overheating Engine Assistance | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works for a roadside assistance service, and the man is a stranded motorist.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Roadside Assistance, how can I help you today?
MAN: Hi, my car just broke down on Highway 401, heading east. I'm pulled over on the shoulder, and I'm not sure what's wrong.
WOMAN: I understand, sir. Are you in a safe location?
MAN: Yes, I managed to get completely off the road. I'm about two kilometers past the Wellington exit.
WOMAN: Okay, good. Can you provide me with your membership number?
MAN: Yes, it's RAS-789456.
WOMAN: Thank you. I see you're Mr. Thompson. Can you tell me what happened before the car stopped?
MAN: Well, the engine started making this weird clicking sound, and then the temperature gauge went way up. There's also some steam coming from under the hood.
WOMAN: Alright, it sounds like your engine might be overheating. I'll dispatch a tow truck to your location right away. The estimated wait time is about 45 minutes.
MAN: That long? Is there any way to get someone here sooner?
WOMAN: I'm sorry, sir, but that's the fastest we can manage given current traffic conditions.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the man is experiencing?
- 1. A flat tire on the highway
- 2. An overheating engine
- 3. A dead battery
- 4. Brake failure
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A flat tire on the highway: The man never mentions a flat tire in the conversation.
✅ 2. An overheating engine: The woman says "it sounds like your engine might be overheating" after the man describes a clicking sound, temperature gauge going up, and steam coming from under the hood.
❌ 3. A dead battery: The man doesn't mention any issues with starting the car or battery problems.
❌ 4. Brake failure: There is no mention of brake problems in the conversation.

**Q2:** How does the woman initially respond to the man's request for faster service?
- 1. She offers to prioritize his request
- 2. She explains it's impossible due to traffic
- 3. She suggests an alternative solution
- 4. She agrees to find another driver
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She offers to prioritize his request: She doesn't offer to prioritize his request.
✅ 2. She explains it's impossible due to traffic: She says "I'm sorry, sir, but that's the fastest we can manage given current traffic conditions."
❌ 3. She suggests an alternative solution: She doesn't provide any alternatives.
❌ 4. She agrees to find another driver: There's no mention of finding another driver.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I called earlier about my broken-down car on Highway 401.
WOMAN: Yes, Mr. Thompson. The tow truck should be arriving shortly. Has anything changed with your situation?
MAN: Actually, yes. A police officer just stopped to check on me. He says I need to get the car moved soon because it's rush hour approaching.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. Let me contact our driver and get an update. Please hold... Okay, good news - our driver is just about 10 minutes away now. He's in a yellow tow truck with our company logo.
MAN: That's a relief. Should I stay in my car or wait outside?
WOMAN: For safety reasons, please remain in your vehicle with your seatbelt on. Keep your hazard lights on and your doors locked. The driver will call your cell phone when he arrives.
MAN: Okay, got it. And the tow truck will take me to the nearest service station?
WOMAN: Yes, unless you have a preferred mechanic you'd like to use.

**Q3:** Why does the man call back?
- 1. To complain about the wait time
- 2. To report a police warning
- 3. To change his destination
- 4. To cancel the service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To complain about the wait time: This isn't mentioned in his second call.
✅ 2. To report a police warning: The man says "A police officer just stopped to check on me. He says I need to get the car moved soon because it's rush hour approaching."
❌ 3. To change his destination: The man doesn't request a change of destination.
❌ 4. To cancel the service: The man doesn't try to cancel the service.

**Q4:** What instruction does the woman give about waiting?
- 1. Stand outside the vehicle
- 2. Wait in the car with seatbelt on
- 3. Walk to a safer location
- 4. Turn off all vehicle lights
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Stand outside the vehicle: The woman specifically advises against this.
✅ 2. Wait in the car with seatbelt on: The woman says "For safety reasons, please remain in your vehicle with your seatbelt on."
❌ 3. Walk to a safer location: The woman doesn't suggest walking anywhere.
❌ 4. Turn off all vehicle lights: The woman actually says to "Keep your hazard lights on."

**Q5:** How will the driver contact the customer?
- 1. Through the dispatch office
- 2. By honking when arriving
- 3. By cell phone
- 4. Through the police officer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Through the dispatch office: This method isn't mentioned.
❌ 2. By honking when arriving: There's no mention of honking.
✅ 3. By cell phone: The woman specifically says "The driver will call your cell phone when he arrives."
❌ 4. Through the police officer: There's no arrangement for contact through the police officer.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Mr. Thompson, I'm following up on your service call. Did our driver arrive?
MAN: Yes, he did, thank you. But I have a question about the service charge on my account.
WOMAN: Of course, I can help with that. What seems to be the concern?
MAN: Well, I was charged for a 20-kilometer tow, but the mechanic's shop was only about 15 kilometers away.
WOMAN: Let me check that for you... You're right, I apologize for the error. The system automatically calculated the longer route due to highway construction, but the driver took an alternate path.
MAN: So can I get a refund for the difference?
WOMAN: Absolutely. I'll adjust the charge right now. You should see a credit of $25 on your account within 24 hours.
MAN: Perfect, thank you. And just so you know, your driver was really professional and helpful.
WOMAN: I'm glad to hear that. Is there anything else you need assistance with today?
MAN: No, that's all. Thanks again for your help.

**Q6:** Why was there an overcharge on the service?
- 1. The driver took a longer route
- 2. The system calculated an incorrect distance
- 3. The rate was entered incorrectly
- 4. Extra services were added
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The driver took a longer route: Actually, the driver took a shorter route than calculated.
✅ 2. The system calculated an incorrect distance: The woman explains "The system automatically calculated the longer route due to highway construction, but the driver took an alternate path."
❌ 3. The rate was entered incorrectly: There's no mention of an incorrect rate entry.
❌ 4. Extra services were added: No extra services are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is the man's attitude toward the driver?
- 1. Dissatisfied with the service
- 2. Neutral about the experience
- 3. Appreciative of the service
- 4. Concerned about the driver's behavior
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Dissatisfied with the service: The man actually expresses positive feelings about the driver.
❌ 2. Neutral about the experience: The man is clearly positive, not neutral.
✅ 3. Appreciative of the service: The man specifically states "your driver was really professional and helpful," showing appreciation.
❌ 4. Concerned about the driver's behavior: The man expresses no concerns about the driver's behavior.

**Q8:** What will happen with the overcharge?
- 1. It will be refunded immediately
- 2. It will be credited within a day
- 3. It will be applied to next service
- 4. It will be reviewed by management
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It will be refunded immediately: The woman doesn't say it will be immediate.
✅ 2. It will be credited within a day: The woman specifically says "You should see a credit of $25 on your account within 24 hours."
❌ 3. It will be applied to next service: There's no mention of applying it to a future service.
❌ 4. It will be reviewed by management: The woman doesn't mention any management review process.

## Practice 366: Spanish Course Registration | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at an online language school's support center, and the man is trying to register for a course.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about registering for your online Spanish course. I saw it advertised on your website.
WOMAN: Hello! I'd be happy to help you with that. We have several Spanish courses available. Are you a complete beginner, or do you have some experience with Spanish?
MAN: Well, I studied it briefly in high school, but that was years ago. I've forgotten most of it.
WOMAN: In that case, I'd recommend our Spanish Fundamentals course. It's designed for beginners but also works well for people who need a refresher. The course runs for 12 weeks, with two live online sessions per week.
MAN: That sounds perfect. What are the class times?
WOMAN: We have morning sessions from 9:00 to 10:30 AM, or evening sessions from 6:00 to 7:30 PM, Eastern Time.
MAN: The evening sessions would work better for me. How much does it cost?

**Q1:** What is the man's main purpose for calling?
- 1. To schedule a trial class
- 2. To inquire about Spanish courses
- 3. To discuss payment options
- 4. To ask about teaching methods
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To schedule a trial class: While the man does schedule a trial class later in the conversation, this wasn't his initial purpose for calling.
✅ 2. To inquire about Spanish courses: This is the correct answer as the man begins by saying "I'm calling about registering for your online Spanish course. I saw it advertised on your website."
❌ 3. To discuss payment options: The man asks about the cost later, but this wasn't his main purpose for calling.
❌ 4. To ask about teaching methods: The man doesn't specifically call to ask about teaching methods.

**Q2:** Why does the woman recommend the Spanish Fundamentals course?
- 1. It's the cheapest option available
- 2. It's designed for advanced students
- 3. It suits both beginners and those needing review
- 4. It has the most flexible schedule
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's the cheapest option available: Price is not mentioned as a reason for the recommendation.
❌ 2. It's designed for advanced students: The course is not for advanced students.
✅ 3. It suits both beginners and those needing review: The woman says "It's designed for beginners but also works well for people who need a refresher."
❌ 4. It has the most flexible schedule: Schedule flexibility is not mentioned as a reason for the recommendation.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: The course fee is $599, which includes all learning materials and access to our online practice platform.
MAN: That's a bit more than I expected. Do you offer any discounts?
WOMAN: Yes, actually! We're currently running an early bird special. If you register at least two weeks before the course starts, you'll get a 15% discount.
MAN: When does the next course begin?
WOMAN: The next session starts on March 15th, so you still have time to qualify for the early bird discount if you register today.
MAN: That's good to know. I also saw something about a free trial class on your website. Is that still available?
WOMAN: Yes, absolutely! We offer a complimentary 30-minute trial class where you can meet the instructor and experience our teaching method.
MAN: Great! I'd like to try that first before committing to the full course.

**Q3:** What is true about the course fee?
- 1. It excludes learning materials
- 2. It includes online platform access
- 3. It can be paid in installments
- 4. It varies based on class time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It excludes learning materials: The woman states the fee "includes all learning materials."
✅ 2. It includes online platform access: The woman says the course fee "includes all learning materials and access to our online practice platform."
❌ 3. It can be paid in installments: Payment installments are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. It varies based on class time: There's no indication that morning and evening sessions have different prices.

**Q4:** What condition must be met to receive the discount?
- 1. Pay the full amount upfront
- 2. Register for morning sessions
- 3. Sign up two weeks before start date
- 4. Take the trial class first
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Pay the full amount upfront: This condition is not mentioned.
❌ 2. Register for morning sessions: The discount is not related to session time.
✅ 3. Sign up two weeks before start date: The woman says "If you register at least two weeks before the course starts, you'll get a 15% discount."
❌ 4. Take the trial class first: Taking a trial class is not mentioned as a condition for the discount.

**Q5:** What does the man decide to do?
- 1. Register immediately for the course
- 2. Request more information
- 3. Try a sample class first
- 4. Look for a cheaper alternative
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Register immediately for the course: The man doesn't decide to register immediately.
❌ 2. Request more information: While he does ask questions, his final decision is to try a trial class.
✅ 3. Try a sample class first: The man says "I'd like to try that first before committing to the full course" and schedules a trial class for Thursday.
❌ 4. Look for a cheaper alternative: The man doesn't indicate he'll look for a cheaper alternative.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: I can schedule your trial class for this week. Would you prefer Tuesday at 6:00 PM or Thursday at 7:00 PM?
MAN: Thursday would be better. But before we schedule it, I have one more question about the technology requirements.
WOMAN: Of course! You'll need a computer with a stable internet connection, a webcam, and a headset with a microphone. We use Zoom for our classes.
MAN: Perfect, I have all of that. Oh, and what happens if I miss a class?
WOMAN: Don't worry! All sessions are recorded and available for 48 hours afterward. Plus, you'll have access to our online practice materials to catch up.
MAN: That's really helpful. Okay, let's go ahead and schedule that trial class for Thursday.
WOMAN: Great! I'll just need your email address to send you the confirmation and meeting link.

**Q6:** What option is available if a student misses a class?
- 1. Attend another group's session
- 2. Get a private makeup lesson
- 3. Access the recorded session
- 4. Receive a partial refund
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend another group's session: This option is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Get a private makeup lesson: Private makeup lessons are not mentioned as an option.
✅ 3. Access the recorded session: The woman says "All sessions are recorded and available for 48 hours afterward."
❌ 4. Receive a partial refund: There's no mention of refunds for missed classes.

**Q7:** What is NOT mentioned as a technical requirement?
- 1. Webcam
- 2. Printer
- 3. Headset with microphone
- 4. Internet connection
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Webcam: The woman mentions "You'll need a computer with a stable internet connection, a webcam, and a headset with a microphone."
✅ 2. Printer: A printer is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation as a requirement.
❌ 3. Headset with microphone: The woman specifically mentions "a headset with a microphone" as a requirement.
❌ 4. Internet connection: The woman mentions "a stable internet connection" as a requirement.

**Q8:** What will happen next?
- 1. The man will make a payment
- 2. The woman will send class materials
- 3. The man will provide his email
- 4. The woman will call back later
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will make a payment: The man hasn't committed to making a payment yet, only to trying a trial class.
❌ 2. The woman will send class materials: There's no mention of sending class materials at this point.
✅ 3. The man will provide his email: At the end of the conversation, the woman says "I'll just need your email address to send you the confirmation and meeting link."
❌ 4. The woman will call back later: There's no mention of the woman calling back later.

## Practice 367: Basement Flooding Emergency | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a customer calling for emergency home repair service, and the man is a customer service representative.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Hello, I need urgent help! My basement is flooding right now!
MAN: I understand this is an emergency. Please stay calm. Can you tell me your address first?
WOMAN: Yes, it's 245 Oak Street, in Richmond.
MAN: Thank you. Can you describe what's happening and when you first noticed the flooding?
WOMAN: I just came home from work about 30 minutes ago and found water coming up through the basement floor drain. The water level is rising quickly!
MAN: Have you turned off the main water supply to your house?
WOMAN: No, I don't know where it is!
MAN: That's okay. Look for a large valve near your water heater or where the main water line enters your house. It's usually in the basement or crawl space.
WOMAN: I found it! Should I turn it clockwise?
MAN: Yes, turn it clockwise until it stops. This should help slow down the flooding.

**Q1:** Why does the woman call for help?
- 1. Her water heater is broken
- 2. There is a flood in her basement
- 3. She has a broken water pipe
- 4. Her sewer line is blocked
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Her water heater is broken: This isn't mentioned as the reason for her call.
✅ 2. There is a flood in her basement: The woman's first words are 'My basement is flooding right now!'
❌ 3. She has a broken water pipe: While this could cause flooding, it's not specifically mentioned as the issue.
❌ 4. Her sewer line is blocked: This is identified later as the cause, but not what she initially reports.

**Q2:** What does the man instruct the woman to do first?
- 1. Call a plumber
- 2. Remove valuable items
- 3. Turn off the main water valve
- 4. Use a wet vacuum
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Call a plumber: The man doesn't instruct her to call a plumber first; he's already handling that for her.
❌ 2. Remove valuable items: This comes after turning off the water valve.
✅ 3. Turn off the main water valve: The man first instructs her to find and turn off the main water valve clockwise to slow down the flooding.
❌ 4. Use a wet vacuum: The man mentions buckets later in the conversation, not as the first instruction.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Okay, I've turned off the water. What's next?
MAN: I'm dispatching our emergency plumber right now. They should arrive within the next hour. Meanwhile, can you tell me if you have any valuable items in the basement?
WOMAN: Yes, there are some boxes with family photos and my computer equipment!
MAN: If it's safe to do so, try to move those items to higher ground or upstairs. Do you have a wet vacuum or any buckets?
WOMAN: I have some buckets in the garage.
MAN: Good. Use them to start removing as much water as you can. This will help minimize damage until our team arrives.
WOMAN: The water seems to be coming from the main sewer line. Could this be a backup?
MAN: Yes, that's likely the case. Our plumber will bring specialized equipment to clear any blockages.
WOMAN: Thank goodness! How long did you say until they arrive?
MAN: They should be there within the hour. I've marked this as a priority emergency.

**Q3:** What immediate action does the man recommend for the woman's belongings?
- 1. Leave them where they are
- 2. Call insurance company
- 3. Take photos of damage
- 4. Move them to a higher location
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Leave them where they are: The man doesn't suggest leaving items in place.
❌ 2. Call insurance company: This isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Take photos of damage: This isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. Move them to a higher location: The man specifically says 'try to move those items to higher ground or upstairs' when she mentions valuable items.

**Q4:** What equipment does the woman have available?
- 1. A wet vacuum
- 2. Some buckets
- 3. A water pump
- 4. Nothing useful
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A wet vacuum: The man asks if she has a wet vacuum, but she doesn't confirm having one.
✅ 2. Some buckets: The woman specifically states 'I have some buckets in the garage.'
❌ 3. A water pump: This equipment isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Nothing useful: This is incorrect as she confirms having buckets.

**Q5:** How long will the woman have to wait for help?
- 1. Less than 30 minutes
- 2. More than 2 hours
- 3. Within an hour
- 4. The next morning
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Less than 30 minutes: The man doesn't promise help this quickly.
❌ 2. More than 2 hours: The man doesn't say it will take this long.
✅ 3. Within an hour: The man specifically says 'They should arrive within the next hour' and later confirms 'They should be there within the hour.'
❌ 4. The next morning: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: The plumber just left. I can't believe how quickly he fixed the problem!
MAN: I'm glad we could help. Did he explain what caused the backup?
WOMAN: Yes, he said there were tree roots growing into the sewer line. He recommended regular maintenance to prevent this from happening again.
MAN: That's right. Would you like me to schedule you for our annual maintenance program?
WOMAN: Yes, please! I never want to go through this again. What does the program include?
MAN: It includes camera inspection of your sewer lines, root treatment if needed, and preventive cleaning. The cost is $299 per year.
WOMAN: That sounds reasonable. Sign me up!
MAN: Great choice. I'll email you the details right away. Is there anything else you need help with?
WOMAN: No, that's all. Thank you so much for your quick response today!
MAN: You're welcome! Have a good evening.

**Q6:** What caused the flooding problem?
- 1. A broken water pipe
- 2. Heavy rainfall
- 3. Tree root intrusion
- 4. Faulty water heater
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A broken water pipe: This isn't identified as the cause in the conversation.
❌ 2. Heavy rainfall: This isn't mentioned as a cause in the conversation.
✅ 3. Tree root intrusion: The plumber explained that 'there were tree roots growing into the sewer line.'
❌ 4. Faulty water heater: This isn't mentioned as a cause in the conversation.

**Q7:** What does the maintenance program cost?
- 1. $$199 annually
- 2. $$299 annually
- 3. $$399 annually
- 4. $$499 annually
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $199 annually: This price isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. $299 annually: The man specifically states 'The cost is $299 per year.'
❌ 3. $399 annually: This price isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. $499 annually: This price isn't mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What will happen next?
- 1. The woman will receive an email
- 2. A technician will return
- 3. The woman will make a payment
- 4. They will schedule another call
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The woman will receive an email: The man says 'I'll email you the details right away' regarding the maintenance program.
❌ 2. A technician will return: There's no mention of a technician returning.
❌ 3. The woman will make a payment: While she agrees to the program, immediate payment isn't mentioned.
❌ 4. They will schedule another call: There's no mention of scheduling another call.

## Practice 368: Senior Care Consultation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman during a phone consultation. The woman works at a senior care service agency, and the man is inquiring about care options for his mother.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello, I'm calling about senior care services for my mother. She's been living alone, and I'm getting concerned about her well-being.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. I'd be happy to discuss our services. Could you tell me a bit more about your mother's current situation?
MAN: Well, she's 78 and generally in good health, but she's becoming forgetful. Last week, she left the stove on overnight, and I'm worried about her safety.
WOMAN: That must have been frightening. We offer various levels of care, from occasional check-ins to full-time assistance. Does your mother need help with daily activities?
MAN: She manages most things, but she sometimes forgets to take her medications, and I've noticed her house isn't as clean as it used to be.
WOMAN: I see. Based on what you're describing, our part-time care service might be a good fit. We could start with a few hours daily to help with medications, light housekeeping, and meal preparation.

**Q1:** What is the man's primary concern about his mother?
- 1. Her physical health
- 2. Her mobility issues
- 3. Her safety at home
- 4. Her social isolation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Her physical health: He mentions she's 'generally in good health.'
❌ 2. Her mobility issues: No mobility problems are mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Her safety at home: He specifically mentions being 'worried about her safety' after she left the stove on overnight.
❌ 4. Her social isolation: Social isolation isn't discussed as a concern.

**Q2:** What type of service does the woman recommend?
- 1. Full-time care
- 2. Part-time assistance
- 3. Weekly check-ins
- 4. Emergency response service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Full-time care: The woman mentions they offer full-time assistance but doesn't recommend it for this situation.
✅ 2. Part-time assistance: The woman specifically says 'our part-time care service might be a good fit' and suggests 'a few hours daily.'
❌ 3. Weekly check-ins: This isn't what the woman recommends based on the mother's needs.
❌ 4. Emergency response service: This type of service isn't mentioned or recommended in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That sounds promising. What about the caregivers? How do you select them?
WOMAN: All our caregivers undergo thorough background checks and receive specialized training in senior care. We also match caregivers based on personality and specific needs.
MAN: That's good to know. My mother can be quite particular about who she lets into her home. Would we be able to meet the caregiver beforehand?
WOMAN: Absolutely! We always arrange a meet-and-greet before starting services. This helps ensure your mother feels comfortable with the caregiver.
MAN: Perfect. And what happens if the caregiver is sick or unavailable?
WOMAN: We always have backup caregivers familiar with your mother's care plan. They can step in when needed, so there's no interruption in service.
MAN: That's reassuring. Could you tell me about the costs?

**Q3:** What policy does the company have regarding caregivers?
- 1. They only hire experienced seniors
- 2. They require family recommendations
- 3. They match based on compatibility
- 4. They rotate staff weekly
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only hire experienced seniors: The content doesn't mention hiring only experienced seniors.
❌ 2. They require family recommendations: There's no mention of requiring family recommendations in the conversation.
✅ 3. They match based on compatibility: The woman clearly states 'We also match caregivers based on personality and specific needs.'
❌ 4. They rotate staff weekly: The content doesn't mention any weekly rotation policy.

**Q4:** What happens when regular caregivers are unavailable?
- 1. Services are postponed
- 2. Family members must fill in
- 3. Backup caregivers are provided
- 4. Hours are rescheduled
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Services are postponed: The woman doesn't mention postponing services.
❌ 2. Family members must fill in: There's no mention of family members filling in.
✅ 3. Backup caregivers are provided: The woman states 'We always have backup caregivers familiar with your mother's care plan.'
❌ 4. Hours are rescheduled: Rescheduling isn't mentioned as the solution.

**Q5:** What aspect of the service does the man seem most interested in?
- 1. The quality of care
- 2. The scheduling flexibility
- 3. The caregiver selection process
- 4. The emergency procedures
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The quality of care: While important, he doesn't focus most on this aspect.
❌ 2. The scheduling flexibility: This isn't his main focus in the conversation.
✅ 3. The caregiver selection process: He asks detailed questions about caregiver selection, background checks, and meeting them beforehand.
❌ 4. The emergency procedures: He asks about backup caregivers but this isn't his primary interest.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Our basic part-time care package starts at $25 per hour, with a minimum of three hours per visit. We also offer weekly and monthly packages that can be more cost-effective.
MAN: I see. Does insurance cover any of these services?
WOMAN: Some long-term care insurance policies do cover our services. Do you know if your mother has such a policy?
MAN: Actually, I'm not sure. I'll need to check her insurance documents.
WOMAN: That's fine. When you find out, let me know, and I can help verify coverage. Would you like to schedule an initial assessment?
MAN: Yes, but I should discuss this with my mother first. Could I call you back next week?
WOMAN: Of course! Take your time. When you're ready, we can set up a home visit to create a personalized care plan.

**Q6:** What is the minimum cost requirement for the service?
- 1. $$25 per day
- 2. $$75 per visit
- 3. $$25 per visit
- 4. $$75 per hour
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $25 per day: The pricing is per hour, not per day.
✅ 2. $75 per visit: The woman states '$25 per hour, with a minimum of three hours per visit,' which equals $75 per visit.
❌ 3. $25 per visit: This would be just one hour, but there's a minimum of three hours per visit.
❌ 4. $75 per hour: The hourly rate is $25, not $75.

**Q7:** Why does the man want to call back later?
- 1. To check insurance coverage
- 2. To consult with his mother
- 3. To compare other services
- 4. To arrange payment details
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To check insurance coverage: While he mentions needing to check insurance, this isn't why he wants to call back.
✅ 2. To consult with his mother: The man clearly states 'I should discuss this with my mother first' as his reason for calling back.
❌ 3. To compare other services: There's no mention of comparing with other services.
❌ 4. To arrange payment details: Payment arrangements aren't mentioned as the reason for calling back.

**Q8:** What is the next step the woman suggests?
- 1. Signing a contract
- 2. Making a payment
- 3. Meeting the caregiver
- 4. Conducting a home assessment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Signing a contract: No contract signing is mentioned as the next step.
❌ 2. Making a payment: Payment isn't suggested as the immediate next step.
❌ 3. Meeting the caregiver: This comes after the assessment, not as the immediate next step.
✅ 4. Conducting a home assessment: The woman suggests scheduling 'an initial assessment' and mentions 'a home visit to create a personalized care plan.'
