## Practice 369: Apartment Heating Repair | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at a property maintenance hotline, and the man is a tenant calling about maintenance issues.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about some urgent maintenance issues in my apartment. The heating system stopped working last night.
WOMAN: I can help you with that. Could you provide your apartment number and building address?
MAN: Yes, it's apartment 304 at 1250 Cedar Street.
WOMAN: Thank you. I see you're in Building C. How long has the heating been out?
MAN: Since around midnight. I woke up freezing and noticed the radiator wasn't working.
WOMAN: I understand. That's definitely a priority issue. I'll send our maintenance team first thing tomorrow morning. They can be there between 8 and 10 AM. Will someone be home?
MAN: Yes, I work from home, so I'll be there.
WOMAN: Perfect. In the meantime, please use caution with any portable heaters, and make sure your windows are properly sealed.
MAN: Okay, thanks. I appreciate the quick response.

**Q1:** What is the initial problem the man reports?
- 1. A water leak in the kitchen
- 2. A broken radiator system
- 3. Non-functioning heating system
- 4. Problems with window seals
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A water leak in the kitchen: This is the second problem reported, not the initial one.
❌ 2. A broken radiator system: While related to heating, he doesn't specifically mention a broken radiator.
✅ 3. Non-functioning heating system: The man's first call states 'The heating system stopped working last night.'
❌ 4. Problems with window seals: Window seals are mentioned by the woman as advice, not as a reported problem.

**Q2:** What arrangement is made for the maintenance visit?
- 1. The team will come immediately
- 2. A visit between 8 and 10 AM next day
- 3. The man needs to schedule another time
- 4. An evening appointment is set
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The team will come immediately: The maintenance team is not scheduled to come immediately.
✅ 2. A visit between 8 and 10 AM next day: The woman says 'I'll send our maintenance team first thing tomorrow morning. They can be there between 8 and 10 AM.'
❌ 3. The man needs to schedule another time: The woman schedules the time directly, no rescheduling is needed.
❌ 4. An evening appointment is set: The appointment is set for morning, not evening.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, it's apartment 304 again. The maintenance team fixed the heating, but now I'm noticing a leak under my kitchen sink.
WOMAN: I'm sorry to hear that. When did you first notice the leak?
MAN: Just now when I went to get something from under the sink. The cabinet floor is quite wet.
WOMAN: Have you turned off the water valve under the sink?
MAN: No, I wasn't sure if I should touch it.
WOMAN: Yes, please turn it clockwise until it stops. That will prevent any further water damage. I'll send our plumber this afternoon. He can be there between 2 and 4 PM.
MAN: That works for me. Should I empty the cabinet?
WOMAN: Yes, please remove everything and put down some towels. The plumber will need clear access to work.
MAN: Got it. Thanks for your help.

**Q3:** What immediate action does the woman recommend for the new problem?
- 1. Call a plumber immediately
- 2. Turn off the water valve
- 3. Wait for maintenance team
- 4. Clean up the water only
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Call a plumber immediately: The woman arranges for the plumber herself, not asking the tenant to call one.
✅ 2. Turn off the water valve: The woman instructs 'please turn it clockwise until it stops' referring to the water valve.
❌ 3. Wait for maintenance team: She doesn't tell him to wait for maintenance without taking action.
❌ 4. Clean up the water only: While cleanup is mentioned, the primary immediate action is turning off the valve.

**Q4:** What preparation does the tenant need to make?
- 1. Move furniture away from the sink
- 2. Leave the apartment during repairs
- 3. Clear the cabinet and place towels
- 4. Document the water damage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Move furniture away from the sink: Moving furniture is not mentioned.
❌ 2. Leave the apartment during repairs: The tenant is not asked to leave during repairs.
✅ 3. Clear the cabinet and place towels: The woman specifically says 'Yes, please remove everything and put down some towels.'
❌ 4. Document the water damage: Documentation of damage is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** When will the plumber arrive to fix the leak?
- 1. In the morning
- 2. Between 2 and 4 PM
- 3. After business hours
- 4. Within the hour
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. In the morning: The woman specifically schedules the plumber for the afternoon, not morning.
✅ 2. Between 2 and 4 PM: The woman clearly states 'I'll send our plumber this afternoon. He can be there between 2 and 4 PM.'
❌ 3. After business hours: There is no mention of after business hours appointment in the conversation.
❌ 4. Within the hour: The woman schedules the appointment for later that day, not within the hour.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello again. The plumber came by, but he said we need a special part that he didn't have with him.
WOMAN: Yes, I just received his report. He needs to order a new valve assembly. It should arrive within two days.
MAN: Two days? That's a long time without being able to use the kitchen sink.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. Let me check if we can expedite the order... Good news! I found another supplier who can deliver it tomorrow.
MAN: That's much better. Will the plumber come back tomorrow then?
WOMAN: Yes, I'll schedule him for tomorrow afternoon. And because of the inconvenience, we'll give you a $50 credit on next month's rent.
MAN: Oh, I appreciate that. What time should I expect the plumber?
WOMAN: He'll be there between 1 and 3 PM. I'll send you a confirmation text.

**Q6:** How does the woman address the man's concern about the wait time?
- 1. She offers alternative accommodation
- 2. She finds a faster parts supplier
- 3. She suggests a temporary fix
- 4. She provides a different plumber
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She offers alternative accommodation: No alternative accommodation is mentioned.
✅ 2. She finds a faster parts supplier: The woman says 'I found another supplier who can deliver it tomorrow' after the man expresses concern about waiting two days.
❌ 3. She suggests a temporary fix: No temporary fix is suggested in the conversation.
❌ 4. She provides a different plumber: There's no mention of changing the plumber.

**Q7:** What compensation is offered to the tenant?
- 1. Free maintenance service
- 2. A new kitchen sink
- 3. A rent credit of $50
- 4. Priority maintenance status
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Free maintenance service: Maintenance service is provided but not mentioned as compensation.
❌ 2. A new kitchen sink: A new sink is not offered as compensation.
✅ 3. A rent credit of $50: The woman clearly states 'And because of the inconvenience, we'll give you a $50 credit on next month's rent.'
❌ 4. Priority maintenance status: No priority status is mentioned as compensation.

**Q8:** How will the tenant receive the final appointment details?
- 1. By email
- 2. By phone call
- 3. By text message
- 4. By written notice
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By email: Email is not mentioned as a communication method.
❌ 2. By phone call: There's no mention of a follow-up phone call.
✅ 3. By text message: The woman specifically states 'I'll send you a confirmation text.'
❌ 4. By written notice: No written notice is mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 370: Auto Insurance Update | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a car insurance company. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a customer calling about his policy.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my car insurance policy. I need to make some changes.
WOMAN: I'll be happy to help you with that. Can I have your policy number, please?
MAN: Yes, it's ACP-789456. I recently moved to a new address, and I also got a new job that's much closer to home.
WOMAN: Thank you for letting us know. Changes in address and commuting distance can affect your premium. Could you provide your new address?
MAN: Sure, it's 425 Maple Street, Unit 302, Vancouver, BC, V6B 2L4.
WOMAN: And how far is your new workplace from this address?
MAN: It's only about 5 kilometers now. I used to drive 25 kilometers each way.
WOMAN: That's quite a difference! This should actually help reduce your premium. Let me update that information for you.
MAN: That's great news! I was hoping it might cost less.

**Q1:** Why does the man contact the insurance company?
- 1. To report a car accident
- 2. To cancel his insurance policy
- 3. To make modifications to his policy
- 4. To complain about high rates
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To report a car accident: No accident is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. To cancel his insurance policy: He wants to keep his policy but make changes to it.
✅ 3. To make modifications to his policy: The man clearly states at the beginning that he needs to make some changes to his policy.
❌ 4. To complain about high rates: He's not complaining but rather updating his information and adding coverage.

**Q2:** How will the man's commuting distance affect his insurance?
- 1. It will increase his premium
- 2. It will reduce his premium
- 3. It will have no effect
- 4. It requires additional documentation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It will increase his premium: The shorter commute actually helps reduce costs, not increase them.
✅ 2. It will reduce his premium: The woman clearly states that the shorter commute (5km vs 25km) should help reduce his premium.
❌ 3. It will have no effect: The woman specifically mentions that commuting distance changes can affect the premium.
❌ 4. It requires additional documentation: No additional documentation is mentioned for the commuting distance change.

### Audio Transcript

WOMAN: It's documents@carinsure.com. Please include your policy number in the subject line.
MAN: Got it. And how much of a discount would that give me?
WOMAN: The security system could reduce your premium by up to 10%, depending on the type.

**Q3:** What does the man need to do regarding his security system?
- 1. Install it immediately
- 2. Get it certified
- 3. Send documentation by email
- 4. Pay an additional fee

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Install it immediately: The system is already installed according to the conversation.
❌ 2. Get it certified: The man already has certification papers from the installer.
✅ 3. Send documentation by email: The woman asks him to email the certification documents to documents@carinsure.com.
❌ 4. Pay an additional fee: No additional fee is mentioned for the security system.

**Q4:** What is the maximum discount offered for the security system?
- 1. 5%
- 2. 10%
- 3. 15%
- 4. 20%
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 5%: This is not the maximum discount mentioned.
✅ 2. 10%: The woman clearly states that the security system could reduce the premium by up to 10%.
❌ 3. 15%: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. 20%: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What would be the best way to describe the security system installed?
- 1. Basic alarm system
- 2. Tracking and anti-theft system
- 3. Camera surveillance system
- 4. Remote start system

✅ 2. Tracking and anti-theft system: The man specifically mentions it's a GPS tracking system with an engine immobilizer, which covers both tracking and anti-theft functions.

WOMAN: I can help you with that. Adding a new driver will affect your premium though. How long has she had her license?

❌ 3. Her clean driving record: As a new driver, she doesn't have an established driving record yet.
❌ 4. Her age: Age is not mentioned as a factor in the conversation.

**Q7:** How much will the monthly premium increase?

- 2. $$15
- 3. $$20
- 4. $$25

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $10: This is not the correct amount mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 3. $20: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. $25: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What is the most likely reason for the relatively small premium increase?

- 2. The combination of discounts and increases
- 3. A special promotion

## Practice 371: Piano Lesson Registration | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at an online music school's support desk, and the man is trying to register for online piano lessons.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm calling about registering for online piano lessons. I saw your ad online but I'm having trouble with the registration process.
WOMAN: Hello! I'd be happy to help you with that. Could you tell me where exactly you're stuck in the process?
MAN: Well, I created an account on your website, but when I try to book a trial lesson, it keeps saying there's an error with my time zone settings.
WOMAN: I see. That's actually a common issue. Let me check your account. Could you provide your email address?
MAN: Sure, it's michael.parker@email.com.
WOMAN: Perfect, I can see your account here. Ah, I see the problem. Your time zone is set to GMT, but you mentioned in your profile you're in Toronto. I'll change that to Eastern Time.
MAN: Oh, that makes sense. I was wondering why all the lesson times seemed odd.
WOMAN: There we go. I've fixed that for you. Now you should be able to see the correct available time slots for your area.

**Q1:** What is the main problem the man is experiencing?
- 1. He cannot create an account
- 2. His email address is invalid
- 3. The time zone settings are incorrect
- 4. The website is not loading properly
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He cannot create an account: He already created an account successfully.
❌ 2. His email address is invalid: His email works fine and the woman finds his account with it.
✅ 3. The time zone settings are incorrect: He says 'it keeps saying there's an error with my time zone settings' and the woman confirms this is the problem.
❌ 4. The website is not loading properly: The website loads fine, the issue is specifically with time zone settings.

**Q2:** What action does the customer service representative take?
- 1. Creates a new account for the man
- 2. Adjusts the time zone from GMT to Eastern Time
- 3. Sends him a new registration link
- 4. Schedules a trial lesson
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Creates a new account for the man: The man already had an account created, so no new account was needed.
✅ 2. Sends him a new registration link: No registration link was mentioned or sent during the conversation.
❌ 3. Adjusts the time zone from GMT to Eastern Time: The woman specifically says 'I'll change that to Eastern Time' after identifying the time zone was incorrectly set to GMT.
❌ 4. Schedules a trial lesson: No trial lesson was scheduled during this call, the man was just getting help with the registration process.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Thanks for fixing that. Now I can see the available slots, but I notice there are different types of trial lessons. Could you explain the difference?
WOMAN: Of course! We offer two types of trial lessons. The 30-minute basic trial is free and gives you a chance to meet the instructor and try out our online platform. The 60-minute comprehensive trial costs $25 and includes a skill assessment and personalized learning plan.
MAN: I see. And what about the regular lessons after the trial? What are the package options?
WOMAN: We have several packages: 4 lessons per month, 8 lessons per month, or 12 lessons per month. The more lessons you book, the lower the per-lesson cost. Would you like me to break down the pricing?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be helpful.
WOMAN: For 4 lessons, it's $60 per lesson. For 8 lessons, it's $50 per lesson, and for 12 lessons, it's $45 per lesson.

**Q3:** What is the cost difference between the trial lesson options?
- 1. The comprehensive trial costs $60 more
- 2. Both trials are free
- 3. The comprehensive trial costs $25 more
- 4. The basic trial costs $25 more
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The comprehensive trial costs $60 more: This is incorrect, the difference is only $25.
❌ 2. Both trials are free: This is wrong, only the basic trial is free.
✅ 3. The comprehensive trial costs $25 more: The basic trial is free and the comprehensive trial costs $25, so the difference is $25.
❌ 4. The basic trial costs $25 more: This is backwards, the basic trial is free, not $25.

**Q4:** Which package offers the best value per lesson?
- 1. 4 lessons per month
- 2. 8 lessons per month
- 3. 12 lessons per month
- 4. The trial lesson package
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 4 lessons per month: This costs $60 per lesson, which is the most expensive option.
❌ 2. 8 lessons per month: This costs $50 per lesson, which is mid-range pricing.
✅ 3. 12 lessons per month: This costs $45 per lesson, which is the lowest price per lesson, making it the best value.
❌ 4. The trial lesson package: Trial lessons are not regular lesson packages and don't represent ongoing value.

**Q5:** What is the cost per lesson for the 8-lesson package?
- 1. $$45
- 2. $$50
- 3. $$60
- 4. $$25
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $45: This is the cost per lesson for the 12-lesson package, not the 8-lesson package.
✅ 2. $50: The woman states 'For 8 lessons, it's $50 per lesson.'
❌ 3. $60: This is the cost per lesson for the 4-lesson package.
❌ 4. $25: This is the cost of the comprehensive trial lesson, not a regular lesson package.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I think I'll start with the free trial lesson and then decide on a package. But I have one more question about the technical requirements.
WOMAN: Sure, what would you like to know?
MAN: I have a digital piano with USB connectivity. Will that work with your online platform?
WOMAN: Yes, that's perfect! A digital piano with USB is ideal. You'll also need a computer with a webcam and stable internet connection. Have you tested your internet speed?
MAN: Not recently. How fast does it need to be?
WOMAN: We recommend at least 10 Mbps for the best experience. You can test your speed on our website. If you click on 'Technical Requirements' at the bottom of the page, there's a built-in speed test.
MAN: Great, I'll do that right now. Thanks for all your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! Let me know if you need anything else.

**Q6:** What equipment does the man already have?
- 1. A webcam and stable internet
- 2. A digital piano with USB connectivity
- 3. A computer with high-speed internet
- 4. A basic piano without connectivity
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A webcam and stable internet: The woman mentions these as requirements but doesn't confirm the man has them.
✅ 2. A digital piano with USB connectivity: The man specifically states 'I have a digital piano with USB connectivity.'
❌ 3. A computer with high-speed internet: While he likely has a computer, his internet speed hasn't been tested recently.
❌ 4. A basic piano without connectivity: He has a digital piano WITH USB connectivity, not without.

**Q7:** What is the minimum internet speed requirement?
- 1. 5 Mbps
- 2. 8 Mbps
- 3. 10 Mbps
- 4. 15 Mbps
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 5 Mbps: This is lower than what the woman stated as the requirement.
❌ 2. 8 Mbps: This is still lower than the stated requirement.
✅ 3. 10 Mbps: The woman clearly states 'We recommend at least 10 Mbps for the best experience.'
❌ 4. 15 Mbps: This is higher than the stated minimum requirement.

**Q8:** Where can the man test his internet speed?
- 1. On any speed test website
- 2. Through his email link
- 3. On the Technical Requirements page
- 4. By calling customer service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. On any speed test website: The woman gives a specific location, not just any website.
❌ 2. Through his email link: No email link was mentioned for speed testing.
✅ 3. On the Technical Requirements page: The woman says 'If you click on Technical Requirements at the bottom of the page, there's a built-in speed test.'
❌ 4. By calling customer service: The woman directs him to test it himself online, not by calling.

## Practice 372: Student Housing Application | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at the university housing office, and the man is a student seeking assistance with his housing application.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my student housing application for the fall semester. I submitted it last week but haven't heard anything back.
WOMAN: I can help you with that. Could you provide your student ID number?
MAN: Yes, it's 78542391.
WOMAN: Thank you. Let me check your application status... I can see your application here, but it appears to be incomplete. We're missing your proof of enrollment for the upcoming semester.
MAN: Oh, I thought I uploaded that. The system was having issues when I tried to submit it.
WOMAN: That happens sometimes. You can email the document to housing@university.edu or upload it directly through the student portal.
MAN: I'll try uploading it again through the portal. How long after I submit that document will I hear back about my application?
WOMAN: Once your application is complete, you should receive a response within 5-7 business days.
MAN: Perfect, thank you for checking that for me.

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the man's initial call?
- 1. To submit a new housing application
- 2. To inquire about the status of his application
- 3. To report a system error
- 4. To change his room preference
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To submit a new housing application: The man has already submitted his application, as he states "I submitted it last week."
✅ 2. To inquire about the status of his application: The man says "I'm calling about my student housing application for the fall semester. I submitted it last week but haven't heard anything back" which shows he's checking on its status.
❌ 3. To report a system error: While system issues are mentioned later, this wasn't the initial reason for his call.
❌ 4. To change his room preference: The room preference change comes later in the conversation, not during his initial call.

**Q2:** According to the conversation, what is preventing the application from being processed?
- 1. Missing student ID number
- 2. Incorrect contact information
- 3. Missing proof of enrollment
- 4. Expired application deadline
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Missing student ID number: The man provides his ID number (78542391) at the beginning of the conversation.
❌ 2. Incorrect contact information: There's no mention of incorrect contact information in the conversation.
✅ 3. Missing proof of enrollment: The woman specifically states "I can see your application here, but it appears to be incomplete. We're missing your proof of enrollment for the upcoming semester."
❌ 4. Expired application deadline: There's no mention of any expired deadline in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, it's me again. I just tried uploading my enrollment document, but I'm getting an error message.
WOMAN: Could you tell me what the error message says?
MAN: It says 'File format not supported.' I'm trying to upload a PDF file.
WOMAN: That's strange because PDF is definitely a supported format. Let me check the system status... Ah, I see the issue. Our portal is currently undergoing maintenance for the next hour.
MAN: Oh, that explains it. Should I try again later today?
WOMAN: Yes, please try again after 3 PM. If you're still having problems, you can always email the document to us.
MAN: Got it. By the way, do you know if there are still spots available in the North Campus residence?
WOMAN: Yes, there are still some rooms available, but they're filling up quickly. That's why it's important to complete your application as soon as possible.

**Q3:** Why can't the man upload his document immediately?
- 1. His PDF file is corrupted
- 2. The system is under maintenance
- 3. His student ID is invalid
- 4. The deadline has passed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His PDF file is corrupted: The file isn't corrupted; the man is able to identify it as a PDF.
✅ 2. The system is under maintenance: The woman explains "Our portal is currently undergoing maintenance for the next hour" which is preventing the upload.
❌ 3. His student ID is invalid: His student ID is valid and was used to look up his application.
❌ 4. The deadline has passed: There's no mention of a missed deadline in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the woman suggest about the North Campus residence?
- 1. It is already full
- 2. It requires immediate action
- 3. It has a waiting list
- 4. It is under renovation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It is already full: The woman confirms there are still rooms available.
✅ 2. It requires immediate action: The woman says "there are still some rooms available, but they're filling up quickly. That's why it's important to complete your application as soon as possible" suggesting urgency and immediate action.
❌ 3. It has a waiting list: A waiting list is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. It is under renovation: There's no mention of any renovation in the conversation.

**Q5:** What alternative solution does the woman offer for document submission?
- 1. Faxing the document
- 2. Mailing a hard copy
- 3. Sending it by email
- 4. Bringing it in person
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Faxing the document: Faxing is not mentioned as an option in the conversation.
❌ 2. Mailing a hard copy: Mailing documents is not mentioned as an option.
✅ 3. Sending it by email: The woman says "You can email the document to housing@university.edu or upload it directly through the student portal" and later reminds him "you can always email the document to us" as an alternative.
❌ 4. Bringing it in person: Bringing documents in person is not mentioned as an option.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello again. I successfully uploaded my document, but I just realized I made a mistake on my application.
WOMAN: What kind of mistake are we talking about?
MAN: I accidentally selected 'single room' as my preference, but I wanted to apply for a shared room to reduce costs.
WOMAN: No problem, I can help you modify that. Would you like me to make that change now?
MAN: Yes, please. Will this affect my place in the queue?
WOMAN: No, your original submission date will remain the same. I've updated your room preference to 'shared.' Is there anything else you'd like to change while we're here?
MAN: Actually, yes. Could you also update my meal plan preference to the basic plan instead of the premium one?
WOMAN: Done. I've made both changes. You'll receive a confirmation email shortly with your updated preferences.
MAN: Thank you so much for your help today.

**Q6:** What changes does the man request in Section 3?
- 1. Room type and move-in date
- 2. Room type and meal plan
- 3. Meal plan and payment method
- 4. Payment method and room location
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Room type and move-in date: While he does request a room type change, he doesn't mention anything about changing his move-in date.
✅ 2. Room type and meal plan: The man asks to change from a single room to a shared room, and then also asks to "update my meal plan preference to the basic plan instead of the premium one."
❌ 3. Meal plan and payment method: While he does request a meal plan change, there's no mention of changing payment methods.
❌ 4. Payment method and room location: Neither payment method nor room location changes are requested.

**Q7:** What concern does the man express about his modification request?
- 1. The cost difference
- 2. The waiting period
- 3. His position in the queue
- 4. The room availability
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The cost difference: The man doesn't express concern about cost differences when making modifications.
❌ 2. The waiting period: The man doesn't ask about how long the modifications will take.
✅ 3. His position in the queue: The man specifically asks "Will this affect my place in the queue?" when requesting to change his room preference.
❌ 4. The room availability: While room availability is mentioned elsewhere in the conversation, it's not his concern regarding the modification request.

**Q8:** What will happen next according to the conversation?
- 1. The man will need to pay additional fees
- 2. The woman will call him back
- 3. He will receive a confirmation email
- 4. He needs to submit more documents
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will need to pay additional fees: There's no mention of additional fees in the conversation.
❌ 2. The woman will call him back: The woman doesn't say she'll call him back.
✅ 3. He will receive a confirmation email: The woman states "You'll receive a confirmation email shortly with your updated preferences" after making the changes he requested.
❌ 4. He needs to submit more documents: His application is complete once he uploads the missing document, and no additional documents are mentioned.

## Practice 373: Backyard Landscaping Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a garden design consultant, and the man is a potential client calling about garden design services.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the man's call?
- 1. To purchase gardening supplies
- 2. To hire a maintenance service
- 3. To inquire about design services
- 4. To report a garden problem
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To purchase gardening supplies: The man isn't calling to buy supplies but to get professional design help.
❌ 2. To hire a maintenance service: He's not looking for maintenance but for initial garden design.
✅ 3. To inquire about design services: The man opens with 'I'm calling about your garden design consultation services' which clearly states his purpose.
❌ 4. To report a garden problem: He's not reporting a problem but seeking design services for his empty backyard.

**Q2:** What is the current state of the man's backyard?
- 1. Overgrown with weeds
- 2. Has only grass
- 3. Partially landscaped
- 4. Contains old garden features
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Overgrown with weeds: The man doesn't describe any weeds or overgrowth.
✅ 2. Has only grass: The man clearly states 'the backyard is completely empty. It's basically just a lawn right now.'
❌ 3. Partially landscaped: He describes it as completely empty, not partially landscaped.
❌ 4. Contains old garden features: He says it's completely empty with just lawn, no old garden features are mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That sounds good. I do have some specific requirements though. We have two dogs, and we'd like to make sure the garden is safe for them.
WOMAN: That's important information. We'll need to avoid toxic plants and create designated areas for your dogs. What breeds are they?
MAN: They're both Labradors, so pretty energetic. We'd like some space where they can run around.
WOMAN: I can definitely work that into the design. We could create a specific play area with durable grass or perhaps artificial turf. Would you also like some fencing recommendations?
MAN: Yes, that would be helpful. The current fence is quite old.
WOMAN: I can suggest some pet-friendly fencing options. Also, have you considered adding a water feature? Dogs often enjoy having a designated water play area.
MAN: That's an interesting idea. Would it be difficult to maintain?
WOMAN: Not with the right design. We can install a self-cleaning system that's quite manageable.

**Q3:** What specific requirement does the man mention?
- 1. Low-maintenance plants
- 2. Pet-friendly design
- 3. Water conservation
- 4. Shade features
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Low-maintenance plants: While he mentions wanting something 'low-maintenance but beautiful,' this isn't the specific requirement he emphasizes.
✅ 2. Pet-friendly design: The man specifically states 'We have two dogs, and we'd like to make sure the garden is safe for them' as a specific requirement.
❌ 3. Water conservation: No mention of water conservation is made in the conversation.
❌ 4. Shade features: Shade features are not specifically mentioned as a requirement.

**Q4:** What additional feature does the woman suggest?
- 1. A vegetable garden
- 2. A water feature
- 3. A storage shed
- 4. A seating area
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A vegetable garden: The woman never mentions or suggests a vegetable garden during the conversation.
✅ 2. A water feature: The woman specifically asks 'have you considered adding a water feature?' and suggests dogs often enjoy having a designated water play area.
❌ 3. A storage shed: No mention of a storage shed is made in the conversation.
❌ 4. A seating area: While this might be common in garden design, the woman doesn't specifically suggest a seating area in this conversation.

**Q5:** What issue with the property is mentioned?
- 1. Poor soil quality
- 2. Aging fence
- 3. Drainage problems
- 4. Lack of sunlight
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Poor soil quality: While soil conditions are mentioned as something to assess, poor soil quality isn't specifically identified as an issue.
✅ 2. Aging fence: The man clearly states 'The current fence is quite old' when discussing fencing recommendations.
❌ 3. Drainage problems: No drainage issues are mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Lack of sunlight: Sunlight is mentioned as a factor to assess, but lack of sunlight isn't identified as a problem.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: When would you be available for the consultation?
WOMAN: Let me check my calendar. I have openings next Tuesday morning or Thursday afternoon. Which would work better for you?
MAN: Thursday afternoon would be perfect. What time exactly?
WOMAN: I could come at 2:00 PM. The consultation usually takes about two hours. Would that work?
MAN: Yes, that's fine. Do you need our address?
WOMAN: Yes, please. And could you also email me any pictures you have of the current garden? That would help me prepare for the visit.
MAN: Sure, what's your email address?
WOMAN: It's sarah@gardendesign.com. Also, please make sure the gates are unlocked when I arrive, and it would be helpful if you could mark any underground utilities you're aware of.
MAN: Will do. Thanks for your help!

**Q6:** What does the woman request from the client?
- 1. A deposit payment
- 2. Garden photos
- 3. Previous design plans
- 4. Soil samples
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A deposit payment: No mention of a deposit is made in the conversation.
✅ 2. Garden photos: The woman specifically asks 'could you also email me any pictures you have of the current garden? That would help me prepare for the visit.'
❌ 3. Previous design plans: No request for previous design plans is mentioned.
❌ 4. Soil samples: While soil conditions will be assessed, she doesn't ask the client to provide soil samples.

**Q7:** What preparation is the client asked to make?
- 1. Remove existing plants
- 2. Mark underground utilities
- 3. Get permits
- 4. Clear the driveway
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Remove existing plants: The backyard is described as just lawn, and no plant removal is requested.
✅ 2. Mark underground utilities: The woman specifically says 'it would be helpful if you could mark any underground utilities you're aware of.'
❌ 3. Get permits: No mention of permits is made in the conversation.
❌ 4. Clear the driveway: No request to clear the driveway is mentioned.

**Q8:** How long will the consultation take?
- 1. One hour
- 2. Two hours
- 3. Three hours
- 4. Half a day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One hour: The woman clearly states the consultation takes about two hours, not one.
✅ 2. Two hours: The woman explicitly says 'I'll spend about two hours at your property' and later confirms 'The consultation usually takes about two hours.'
❌ 3. Three hours: The woman specifically mentions two hours, not three.
❌ 4. Half a day: The woman gives a specific timeframe of two hours, which is much less than half a day.

## Practice 374: Refrigerator Repair Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at an appliance repair service company, and the man is a customer calling about a broken refrigerator.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello, I'm calling about my refrigerator. It's making this strange humming noise, and it's not cooling properly.
WOMAN: I understand. I can help you with that. Could you tell me the make and model of your refrigerator?
MAN: It's a Samsung, model RF28. I bought it about three years ago.
WOMAN: Thank you. And when did you first notice these issues?
MAN: The humming started about a week ago, but the cooling problem just started yesterday. The temperature keeps rising even though it's set to the coldest setting.
WOMAN: I see. Have you checked if there's proper airflow around the fridge? Sometimes items placed too close to the vents can cause cooling issues.
MAN: Yes, I've already checked that. I even cleaned the coils at the back, but nothing changed.
WOMAN: Alright, it sounds like we'll need to send a technician to take a look. Let me check our availability.

**Q1:** What is the main issue with the refrigerator?
- 1. It's making loud clicking sounds
- 2. The freezer isn't working
- 3. It's making a humming noise and not cooling properly
- 4. The door isn't sealing correctly
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's making loud clicking sounds: The man mentions a humming noise, not clicking sounds.
❌ 2. The freezer isn't working: The man doesn't mention any specific freezer problems.
✅ 3. It's making a humming noise and not cooling properly: The man clearly states both issues - the strange humming noise and that it's not cooling properly.
❌ 4. The door isn't sealing correctly: There's no mention of door sealing problems in the conversation.

**Q2:** What has the customer already tried to fix the problem?
- 1. Adjusted the temperature settings
- 2. Cleaned the coils and checked airflow
- 3. Called a different repair service
- 4. Defrosted the freezer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Adjusted the temperature settings: He mentions it's set to the coldest setting but doesn't say he adjusted it as a fix.
✅ 2. Cleaned the coils and checked airflow: The man specifically says he checked airflow and cleaned the coils at the back.
❌ 3. Called a different repair service: There's no mention of calling other services.
❌ 4. Defrosted the freezer: Defrosting the freezer is not mentioned as something he tried.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: We have a few slots available this week. Would you prefer morning or afternoon?
MAN: Morning would be better. I work from home in the afternoons.
WOMAN: Perfect. We have an opening tomorrow at 9 AM or Thursday at 10 AM.
MAN: Tomorrow at 9 would be great. How long do you think it will take?
WOMAN: The diagnostic usually takes about an hour. Once the technician identifies the problem, they'll provide an estimate for the repairs.
MAN: And what about the service fee?
WOMAN: The diagnostic fee is $85, which will be applied toward any repairs if you choose to proceed with our service.
MAN: That sounds reasonable. Do I need to do anything to prepare for the visit?
WOMAN: Just make sure there's clear access to the refrigerator, and please remove any food items from the freezer as a precaution.

**Q3:** Why does the customer prefer a morning appointment?
- 1. He has meetings in the afternoon
- 2. He works from home in the afternoon
- 3. The repair service is cheaper in the morning
- 4. The technicians only work mornings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has meetings in the afternoon: He doesn't mention meetings specifically.
✅ 2. He works from home in the afternoon: The man clearly states he works from home in the afternoons.
❌ 3. The repair service is cheaper in the morning: No price difference is mentioned.
❌ 4. The technicians only work mornings: The woman offers both morning and afternoon slots.

**Q4:** What is the cost of the diagnostic service?
- 1. $$85, non-refundable
- 2. $$85, applied to repair cost if proceeding
- 3. Free with repair
- 4. $$85, plus tax
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $85, non-refundable: The woman says it will be applied toward repairs if he proceeds.
✅ 2. $85, applied to repair cost if proceeding: The woman clearly states the $85 diagnostic fee will be applied toward any repairs if he chooses to proceed.
❌ 3. Free with repair: The diagnostic has a separate $85 fee.
❌ 4. $85, plus tax: No mention of additional tax is made.

**Q5:** What preparation is required before the technician's visit?
- 1. Clean the refrigerator thoroughly
- 2. Remove all food items
- 3. Clear access and empty the freezer
- 4. Disconnect the refrigerator
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Clean the refrigerator thoroughly: General cleaning is not mentioned as a requirement.
❌ 2. Remove all food items: Only freezer items are mentioned, not all food.
✅ 3. Clear access and empty the freezer: The woman asks for clear access to the refrigerator and to remove food items from the freezer.
❌ 4. Disconnect the refrigerator: Disconnecting the appliance is not mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: One more question - do you offer any warranty on the repairs?
WOMAN: Yes, all our repairs come with a 90-day warranty on both parts and labor. If you experience any issues related to the repair within that time, we'll fix it at no additional cost.
MAN: That's good to know. And what forms of payment do you accept?
WOMAN: We accept all major credit cards, debit cards, and cash. Payment is due upon completion of the repair.
MAN: Perfect. Oh, I almost forgot - I live in apartment 3B. Should I notify the building management?
WOMAN: Yes, please do. They might need to add our technician to the visitor list. Also, make sure to provide your apartment number when the technician calls tomorrow morning.
MAN: Will do. Thank you for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome! We'll see you tomorrow at 9 AM.

**Q6:** What is covered under the warranty?
- 1. Parts only for 90 days
- 2. Labor only for 90 days
- 3. Both parts and labor for 90 days
- 4. One year full coverage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Parts only for 90 days: The warranty covers more than just parts.
❌ 2. Labor only for 90 days: The warranty covers more than just labor.
✅ 3. Both parts and labor for 90 days: The woman clearly states all repairs come with a 90-day warranty on both parts and labor.
❌ 4. One year full coverage: The warranty is for 90 days, not one year.

**Q7:** When is payment expected?
- 1. Before the service
- 2. Within 30 days
- 3. After the repair is complete
- 4. In monthly installments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Before the service: Payment is not required upfront.
❌ 2. Within 30 days: No mention of a 30-day payment period.
✅ 3. After the repair is complete: The woman states payment is due upon completion of the repair.
❌ 4. In monthly installments: No installment payment option is mentioned.

**Q8:** What additional step must the customer take?
- 1. Sign a service agreement
- 2. Inform building management
- 3. Purchase replacement parts
- 4. Schedule a follow-up visit
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Sign a service agreement: No service agreement is mentioned.
✅ 2. Inform building management: The woman specifically asks him to notify building management to add the technician to the visitor list.
❌ 3. Purchase replacement parts: No mention of purchasing parts beforehand.
❌ 4. Schedule a follow-up visit: No follow-up visit is mentioned as required.

## Practice 375: Virtual Conference Login | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a technical support specialist, and the man is having issues with a virtual event platform.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm having trouble accessing the virtual conference platform. I keep getting an error message when I try to log in.
WOMAN: I'll be happy to help you with that. Could you please provide me with your registration email?
MAN: Yes, it's michael.smith@email.com. I'm supposed to be presenting in less than an hour, and I'm getting really worried.
WOMAN: I understand your concern. Let me check your registration status... I can see that your account is active, but it looks like you haven't completed the two-step verification process.
MAN: Oh, I didn't realize I needed to do that. How do I complete it?
WOMAN: I'll send you a new verification link right now. Once you receive it, just click on it and follow the prompts to set up your authentication method.
MAN: Perfect, thank you. Should I receive the email right away?
WOMAN: Yes, it should arrive within the next minute or two. Let me know if you don't receive it.

**Q1:** Why is the man concerned?
- 1. He cannot find his presentation materials
- 2. He is running late for his presentation
- 3. He cannot access the conference platform
- 4. He forgot his presentation time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He cannot find his presentation materials: The man doesn't mention missing materials.
❌ 2. He is running late for his presentation: The man doesn't say he's late.
✅ 3. He cannot access the conference platform: The man states at the beginning, "I'm having trouble accessing the virtual conference platform. I keep getting an error message when I try to log in."
❌ 4. He forgot his presentation time: The man knows his presentation time, mentioning "I'm supposed to be presenting in less than an hour."

**Q2:** What solution does the woman offer?
- 1. Reset his password
- 2. Send a verification link
- 3. Reschedule his presentation
- 4. Contact technical support
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Reset his password: Password reset is not mentioned as a solution.
✅ 2. Send a verification link: The woman says "I'll send you a new verification link right now. Once you receive it, just click on it and follow the prompts to set up your authentication method."
❌ 3. Reschedule his presentation: Rescheduling is not suggested as a solution.
❌ 4. Contact technical support: The woman is already providing technical support, and doesn't suggest contacting anyone else.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm back. I've completed the verification, but now I'm having issues with my presentation slides.
WOMAN: What seems to be the problem with your slides?
MAN: Well, I uploaded them successfully, but when I try to do a test run, they're not advancing when I click the next button.
WOMAN: I see. Are you using the presentation controls at the bottom of your screen, or are you trying to use your keyboard?
MAN: I've tried both, but neither seems to work. The slides just stay stuck on the first page.
WOMAN: Okay, let's try refreshing your browser first. Sometimes that can resolve these issues. If that doesn't work, we might need to re-upload your presentation.
MAN: Alright, I'll try refreshing now... No, that didn't fix it.
WOMAN: In that case, let's try uploading your presentation again. I'll guide you through the process.

**Q3:** What is the main issue with the presentation?
- 1. The slides won't advance
- 2. The presentation won't upload
- 3. The slides are corrupted
- 4. The presentation won't download
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The slides won't advance: The man specifically states "I uploaded them successfully, but when I try to do a test run, they're not advancing when I click the next button."
❌ 2. The presentation won't upload: The man confirms he uploaded the slides successfully.
❌ 3. The slides are corrupted: There is no mention of the slides being corrupted.
❌ 4. The presentation won't download: Downloading the presentation is not mentioned as an issue.

**Q4:** What is the first solution the woman suggests?
- 1. Restart the computer
- 2. Clear browser cache
- 3. Refresh the browser
- 4. Update the software
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Restart the computer: The woman never suggests restarting the computer at any point in the conversation.
❌ 2. Clear browser cache: The woman does not mention clearing browser cache as a solution.
✅ 3. Refresh the browser: When the man has issues with his presentation slides not advancing, the woman first suggests "let's try refreshing your browser first. Sometimes that can resolve these issues."
❌ 4. Update the software: The woman does not suggest updating any software during the conversation.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the presentation platform?
- 1. It requires specific software
- 2. It has frequent technical issues
- 3. It has multiple control options
- 4. It is difficult to use
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It requires specific software: No specific software requirements are mentioned.
❌ 2. It has frequent technical issues: While the man experiences issues, there's no indication these are frequent or common problems.
✅ 3. It has multiple control options: The woman asks "Are you using the presentation controls at the bottom of your screen, or are you trying to use your keyboard?" indicating multiple ways to control the presentation.
❌ 4. It is difficult to use: While the man has some issues, there's no clear indication the platform itself is difficult to use overall.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Thank you for your help earlier. The presentation went well, but now I'm having trouble accessing the networking lounge.
WOMAN: I'm glad your presentation was successful. Regarding the networking lounge, what exactly happens when you try to access it?
MAN: The page loads, but I can't see any of the other attendees or join any conversations.
WOMAN: Let me check your permissions... Ah, I see the issue. Your browser's blocking the necessary permissions for audio and video.
MAN: Oh, how do I fix that?
WOMAN: Look for a small camera icon in your browser's address bar. Click on it and make sure both camera and microphone permissions are allowed.
MAN: I see it now. Yes, they were blocked. I've enabled them... Perfect! I can see everyone now.
WOMAN: Excellent! Is there anything else you need help with?

**Q6:** What prevented the man from using the networking lounge?
- 1. Poor internet connection
- 2. Browser permission settings
- 3. Incorrect login credentials
- 4. Software incompatibility
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Poor internet connection: Internet connection issues are not mentioned as the cause.
✅ 2. Browser permission settings: The woman identifies the issue by saying "your browser's blocking the necessary permissions for audio and video."
❌ 3. Incorrect login credentials: Login credentials are not mentioned as an issue for the networking lounge.
❌ 4. Software incompatibility: There is no mention of software incompatibility.

**Q7:** Where can the man find the solution to his problem?
- 1. In the system settings
- 2. In the browser's address bar
- 3. In the help menu
- 4. In the conference platform settings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. In the system settings: System settings are not mentioned as a location for any solution.
✅ 2. In the browser's address bar: For the networking lounge issue, the woman says "Look for a small camera icon in your browser's address bar. Click on it and make sure both camera and microphone permissions are allowed."
❌ 3. In the help menu: A help menu is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. In the conference platform settings: The platform settings are not specifically mentioned as a solution location.

**Q8:** What can be concluded about the man's experience?
- 1. He is familiar with virtual conferences
- 2. He needed multiple types of support
- 3. He was unable to present
- 4. He had connection problems
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is familiar with virtual conferences: The man seems unfamiliar with the platform, needing help with verification, presentation controls, and networking features.
✅ 2. He needed multiple types of support: Throughout the conversation, the man needed help with login verification, presentation slides, and networking lounge access - multiple different issues.
❌ 3. He was unable to present: The man confirms his presentation was successful when he says "Thank you for your help earlier. The presentation went well."
❌ 4. He had connection problems: While he had technical issues, specific connection problems are not mentioned.

## Practice 376: Grocery Delivery Issues | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at an online grocery delivery service customer support, and the man is a customer with a complaint.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my grocery delivery from yesterday. There seem to be several issues with my order.
WOMAN: I apologize for any inconvenience. Could you please provide your order number?
MAN: Yes, it's ORDER-458796.
WOMAN: Thank you. I can see your order here. What seems to be the problem?
MAN: Well, first of all, several items were missing. I ordered six apples, but only received three. The organic milk I ordered wasn't included at all.
WOMAN: I'm very sorry about that. Let me check the inventory records from yesterday... Yes, I can see that we were actually out of organic milk, but the system didn't update in time. As for the apples, that's definitely our error.
MAN: Also, the delivery was supposed to arrive between 2 and 4 PM, but it didn't come until almost 6.
WOMAN: I sincerely apologize for these issues. I'll be happy to process a refund for the missing items and add a $10 credit to your account for the late delivery.

**Q1:** What is the main issue with the man's order?
- 1. The delivery person was rude
- 2. Multiple items were incorrect
- 3. Several items were missing
- 4. The order was delivered to wrong address
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The delivery person was rude: The conversation doesn't mention any rudeness from the delivery person.
❌ 2. Multiple items were incorrect: The items weren't incorrect - they were missing or of poor quality.
✅ 3. Several items were missing: The man specifically mentions that he received only three apples instead of six, and the organic milk was completely missing.
❌ 4. The order was delivered to wrong address: The order was delivered late (at 6 PM instead of between 2-4 PM), but to the correct address.

**Q2:** What compensation does the woman initially offer?
- 1. A full refund of the order
- 2. A refund for missing items and a $10 credit
- 3. A 20% discount on next order
- 4. Free delivery for next month
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A full refund of the order: She only offers refunds for specific missing or poor quality items, not the entire order.
✅ 2. A refund for missing items and a $10 credit: The woman states, "I'll be happy to process a refund for the missing items and add a $10 credit to your account for the late delivery."
❌ 3. A 20% discount on next order: This is offered later in the conversation, not initially.
❌ 4. Free delivery for next month: Free delivery is not mentioned as compensation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Thank you, but there's one more issue. The bananas I received were already quite brown and overripe.
WOMAN: I understand your frustration. We have a fresh produce guarantee, so I'll refund those as well. Would you like me to add them to your next order?
MAN: Actually, I'm hesitant to order again after this experience. This was my first time using your service.
WOMAN: I completely understand your concern. Besides the refund and credit, I'd like to offer you 20% off your next two orders. We truly value our customers and want to make this right.
MAN: Well, that's generous of you. How long is that offer valid for?
WOMAN: The discount will be automatically applied to your next two orders within the next 30 days. You'll see it at checkout.
MAN: Okay, I might give it another try. Do you have any system in place to prevent these issues from happening again?
WOMAN: Yes, we've actually just implemented a new quality control process for our produce section.

**Q3:** Why is the man hesitant about ordering again?
- 1. The prices are too high
- 2. The website is difficult to use
- 3. This was his first order and it went poorly
- 4. He prefers shopping in person
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The prices are too high: The man never mentions pricing as an issue.
❌ 2. The website is difficult to use: There's no mention of website usability in the conversation.
✅ 3. This was his first order and it went poorly: The man explicitly states "I'm hesitant to order again after this experience. This was my first time using your service."
❌ 4. He prefers shopping in person: The man doesn't mention preferring in-person shopping.

**Q4:** What additional offer does the woman make?
- 1. Free delivery for a month
- 2. A premium membership trial
- 3. 20% off next two orders within 30 days
- 4. Double credit on future purchases
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Free delivery for a month: This isn't mentioned as an offer.
❌ 2. A premium membership trial: She describes premium membership but doesn't offer a trial.
✅ 3. 20% off next two orders within 30 days: The woman specifically states, "Besides the refund and credit, I'd like to offer you 20% off your next two orders" and confirms it's valid for 30 days.
❌ 4. Double credit on future purchases: No mention of double credits is made.

**Q5:** What improvement has the company recently made?
- 1. Hired new delivery staff
- 2. Implemented new quality control
- 3. Updated their ordering system
- 4. Changed their supplier
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Hired new delivery staff: While additional staff is mentioned, it's for quality inspection, not delivery.
✅ 2. Implemented new quality control: The woman specifically states, "we've actually just implemented a new quality control process for our produce section" and later mentions adding staff to inspect produce.
❌ 3. Updated their ordering system: While there was an inventory system issue, no update to the ordering system is mentioned.
❌ 4. Changed their supplier: No change in suppliers is mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: That's good to know. One last question - I noticed you have a premium membership option. What exactly are the benefits?
WOMAN: Our premium membership includes free delivery on all orders over $50, exclusive weekly deals, and priority delivery slots. It's $9.99 per month.
MAN: And what about the quality control you mentioned? Does that apply to all orders?
WOMAN: Yes, absolutely. All orders go through the same quality checks, regardless of membership status. We've added additional staff to inspect produce before it goes out for delivery.
MAN: That's reassuring. Could you email me the details about the premium membership?
WOMAN: Of course! I'll send that to the email address on your account right away. Is there anything else I can help you with today?
MAN: No, that's all. Thank you for addressing these issues.
WOMAN: You're welcome! You'll receive confirmation of the refund and credit within 24 hours.

**Q6:** What is NOT mentioned as a premium membership benefit?
- 1. Priority quality inspection
- 2. Free delivery over $50
- 3. Exclusive weekly deals
- 4. Priority delivery slots
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Priority quality inspection: This is never mentioned as a benefit. The woman specifically states that "all orders go through the same quality checks, regardless of membership status."
❌ 2. Free delivery over $50: This is explicitly mentioned as a premium benefit.
❌ 3. Exclusive weekly deals: This is explicitly mentioned as a premium benefit.
❌ 4. Priority delivery slots: This is explicitly mentioned as a premium benefit.

**Q7:** What will the man receive within 24 hours?
- 1. Premium membership details
- 2. A new delivery
- 3. Confirmation of refund and credit
- 4. Quality control report
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Premium membership details: The woman says she'll email these right away, not within 24 hours.
❌ 2. A new delivery: There's no mention of sending a new delivery.
✅ 3. Confirmation of refund and credit: The woman states at the end, "You'll receive confirmation of the refund and credit within 24 hours."
❌ 4. Quality control report: No quality control report is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the company's response to complaints?
- 1. They only compensate premium members
- 2. They are proactive in addressing issues
- 3. They rarely offer refunds
- 4. They ignore first-time customers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only compensate premium members: The man isn't a premium member, yet he receives compensation.
✅ 2. They are proactive in addressing issues: The woman immediately offers refunds, credits, and discounts, and mentions they've implemented new quality control processes.
❌ 3. They rarely offer refunds: The woman offers refunds multiple times without hesitation.
❌ 4. They ignore first-time customers: The woman is very attentive despite the man being a first-time customer.

## Practice 377: Wheelchair Rental Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a medical equipment rental company. The woman works at the rental company, and the man is calling about renting equipment.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Thank you for calling Medical Equipment Rentals. How may I assist you today?
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about renting a wheelchair. My mother is being discharged from the hospital tomorrow, and she'll need one for about six weeks while she recovers from hip surgery.
WOMAN: I can definitely help you with that. We have several wheelchair options available. Do you know if she needs a standard manual wheelchair or an electric one?
MAN: The doctor recommended a manual wheelchair. She'll have someone with her most of the time.
WOMAN: Perfect. Our standard manual wheelchairs rent for $45 per week, which includes delivery and pickup. We also require a $200 security deposit that's fully refundable when the wheelchair is returned in good condition.
MAN: That sounds reasonable. Do I need to provide any documentation?
WOMAN: Yes, we'll need a copy of your ID, proof of address, and the discharge papers from the hospital. You can email those to us.

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the man's call?
- 1. To inquire about medical equipment prices
- 2. To rent a wheelchair for his mother
- 3. To schedule a hospital discharge
- 4. To arrange for post-surgery care
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To inquire about medical equipment prices: While prices are discussed, this is not the primary purpose of the call.
✅ 2. To rent a wheelchair for his mother: The man states at the beginning, "I'm calling about renting a wheelchair. My mother is being discharged from the hospital tomorrow..."
❌ 3. To schedule a hospital discharge: The man mentions the discharge but is not calling to schedule it.
❌ 4. To arrange for post-surgery care: The man is arranging for equipment, not comprehensive care services.

**Q2:** How much deposit is required for the wheelchair?
- 1. $$45
- 2. $$100
- 3. $$200
- 4. $$300
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $45: This is the weekly rental cost for the wheelchair, not the deposit.
❌ 2. $100: This is the deposit amount for the walker, not the wheelchair.
✅ 3. $200: The woman states "We also require a $200 security deposit that's fully refundable when the wheelchair is returned in good condition."
❌ 4. $300: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Great, I can send those right away. By the way, do you also rent other equipment? The doctor mentioned she might need a walker later in her recovery.
WOMAN: Yes, we do! We actually have a recovery package that includes both a wheelchair and a walker. Many patients find it helpful during the transition period. The package costs $60 per week, which saves you about $15 compared to renting them separately.
MAN: That's good to know. Could you tell me more about the walker options?
WOMAN: We have standard walkers and rollator walkers with seats. The rollator is more expensive but provides better stability and has a built-in seat for resting.
MAN: I think the standard walker would be fine. How much is that on its own?
WOMAN: The standard walker rents for $30 per week, with a $100 deposit.

**Q3:** What type of package does the company offer?
- 1. A complete medical equipment set
- 2. A wheelchair and walker combination
- 3. A post-surgery recovery kit
- 4. A mobility assistance package
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A complete medical equipment set: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. A wheelchair and walker combination: The woman specifically mentions "a recovery package that includes both a wheelchair and a walker."
❌ 3. A post-surgery recovery kit: While the equipment is for recovery, this specific term is not used to describe their package.
❌ 4. A mobility assistance package: This term is not used in the conversation.

**Q4:** How much money would the customer save with the package deal?
- 1. $$10
- 2. $$15
- 3. $$30
- 4. $$45
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $10: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. $15: The woman states the package costs $60 per week, "which saves you about $15 compared to renting them separately."
❌ 3. $30: This amount is not mentioned as the savings amount.
❌ 4. $45: This amount is not mentioned as the savings amount.

**Q5:** What is the weekly rental cost for a standard walker?
- 1. $$15
- 2. $$30
- 3. $$45
- 4. $$60
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $15: This price is not mentioned in the conversation for any equipment.
✅ 2. $30: This is the correct weekly rental cost for a standard walker as stated by the woman: "The standard walker rents for $30 per week, with a $100 deposit."
❌ 3. $45: This is the weekly rental cost for a standard manual wheelchair, not a walker.
❌ 4. $60: This is the cost of the recovery package that includes both a wheelchair and a walker.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I think I'll start with just the wheelchair for now. Can we add the walker later if needed?
WOMAN: Absolutely! You can add equipment at any time during the rental period. Now, let me get your delivery information. What's the address?
MAN: It's 1234 Oak Street, Apartment 302.
WOMAN: Oh, I should mention that for apartment buildings, we need to know if there's an elevator or if it's a walk-up.
MAN: There's an elevator, thankfully. And there's a building manager on-site during business hours.
WOMAN: Perfect, that's helpful to know. For tomorrow's delivery, we have slots available at 10 AM, 2 PM, or 4 PM. Which would work best?
MAN: The 2 PM slot would be perfect. That's right after she's discharged.
WOMAN: Great, I'll schedule that for you.

**Q6:** What important information does the woman need about the delivery location?
- 1. The building manager's contact
- 2. The apartment building's access details
- 3. The street address
- 4. The discharge time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The building manager's contact: This information is not requested by the woman.
✅ 2. The apartment building's access details: The woman specifically asks "for apartment buildings, we need to know if there's an elevator or if it's a walk-up."
❌ 3. The street address: While the address is collected, the question asks about what additional important information is needed specifically about the apartment building.
❌ 4. The discharge time: This is related to scheduling the delivery time, not about the delivery location itself.

**Q7:** Why does the man choose the 2 PM delivery slot?
- 1. It's the only time available
- 2. It aligns with his work schedule
- 3. It matches the discharge timing
- 4. It's when the building manager is available
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's the only time available: The woman offers three time slots (10 AM, 2 PM, or 4 PM).
❌ 2. It aligns with his work schedule: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. It matches the discharge timing: The man states "The 2 PM slot would be perfect. That's right after she's discharged."
❌ 4. It's when the building manager is available: The man mentions the building manager is on-site during business hours, but this is not given as the reason for choosing 2 PM.

**Q8:** What can we conclude about the rental service?
- 1. They only deliver to private homes
- 2. They are flexible with equipment additions
- 3. They require full payment upfront
- 4. They only offer same-day delivery
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only deliver to private homes: They are delivering to an apartment building, so this is incorrect.
✅ 2. They are flexible with equipment additions: The woman states "You can add equipment at any time during the rental period" when the man asks if he can add the walker later.
❌ 3. They require full payment upfront: Payment terms are not discussed in the conversation.
❌ 4. They only offer same-day delivery: The conversation is about scheduling a delivery for the next day, not same-day.

## Practice 378: Wedding Photography Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman discussing wedding photography packages. The woman is a professional photographer, and the man is a potential client planning his wedding.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I saw your wedding photography portfolio online. My fiancÃ©e and I are getting married next summer, and we're looking for a photographer.
WOMAN: That's wonderful! Congratulations! When exactly is the wedding planned for?
MAN: Thank you! It's scheduled for July 15th next year. We're having both the ceremony and reception at Riverside Gardens.
WOMAN: Oh, I love that venue! I've shot several weddings there. The natural lighting is perfect, especially during summer afternoons. Do you have a specific style in mind?
MAN: Well, we really liked the candid shots in your portfolio. Those natural, unposed moments.
WOMAN: Those are my specialty! I believe the best wedding photos capture genuine emotions. Would you like to hear about our different packages?
MAN: Yes, please. We're trying to stay within a reasonable budget, but we definitely want quality photos.
WOMAN: Of course! Let me pull up our current packages for you.

**Q1:** What attracted the man to this photographer?
- 1. Her affordable rates
- 2. Her wedding venue knowledge
- 3. Her portfolio of candid shots
- 4. Her summer availability
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Her affordable rates: The man never mentions being attracted to the photographer's rates.
❌ 2. Her wedding venue knowledge: While the photographer knows the venue, this wasn't mentioned as what attracted him.
✅ 3. Her candid shots: The man specifically states "we really liked the candid shots in your portfolio. Those natural, unposed moments."
❌ 4. Her summer availability: The man doesn't mention being attracted to her availability.

**Q2:** What is the main concern for the couple?
- 1. Finding an available photographer
- 2. Getting quality photos within budget
- 3. The wedding venue location
- 4. The photography style
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Finding an available photographer: Availability isn't mentioned as a concern.
✅ 2. Getting quality photos within budget: The man states "We're trying to stay within a reasonable budget, but we definitely want quality photos."
❌ 3. The wedding venue location: The venue is already decided, not a concern.
❌ 4. The photography style: While they like candid shots, this isn't framed as a concern.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: So, our basic package starts at $2,500. It includes 6 hours of coverage, all edited digital images, and an online gallery.
MAN: That sounds good, but what about the premium package you mentioned on your website?
WOMAN: The premium package is $3,800. It includes 8 hours of coverage, an engagement shoot, all edited digital images, a wedding album, and two parent albums.
MAN: The engagement shoot would be nice. My fiancée mentioned wanting that. How far in advance should we book that?
WOMAN: I recommend doing engagement photos about 3-4 months before the wedding. That gives us plenty of time for editing and for you to use the photos for save-the-dates or wedding invitations.
MAN: That makes sense. And what about a second photographer? Is that included?
WOMAN: It's actually an add-on option for both packages. It's an additional $500.

**Q3:** What is the difference in coverage time between the packages?
- 1. 4 hours
- 2. 3 hours
- 3. 2 hours
- 4. 1 hour
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 4 hours: This is not the correct time difference.
❌ 2. 3 hours: This is not the correct time difference.
✅ 3. 2 hours: The basic package includes 6 hours of coverage, while the premium package includes 8 hours - a difference of 2 hours.
❌ 4. 1 hour: This is not the correct time difference.

**Q4:** Why does the photographer suggest doing engagement photos 3-4 months before the wedding?
- 1. To ensure good weather conditions
- 2. To practice with the couple
- 3. To allow time for editing and invitation use
- 4. To secure the booking early
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To ensure good weather conditions: Weather conditions aren't mentioned as a reason.
❌ 2. To practice with the couple: The photographer doesn't mention practice as a reason.
✅ 3. To allow time for editing and invitation use: The photographer says "That gives us plenty of time for editing and for you to use the photos for save-the-dates or wedding invitations."
❌ 4. To secure the booking early: Securing the booking isn't mentioned as a reason for the timing.

**Q5:** How much extra would a couple pay for both the premium package and a second photographer?
- 1. $$2,500
- 2. $$3,800
- 3. $$4,300
- 4. $$5,000
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $2,500: This is just the cost of the basic package.
❌ 2. $3,800: This is just the cost of the premium package.
✅ 3. $4,300: The premium package ($3,800) plus a second photographer ($500) equals $4,300.
❌ 4. $5,000: This doesn't match any combination of costs mentioned.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I've been thinking about the packages, and I think we'd like to go with the premium one. But I have a few more questions.
WOMAN: Of course! What would you like to know?
MAN: First, what happens if it rains? Riverside Gardens has an indoor space, but I'm worried about photo opportunities.
WOMAN: Don't worry! I always scout backup locations nearby. Plus, some of my favorite shots have been taken in the rain with clear umbrellas - they're quite romantic!
MAN: That's reassuring. And about the wedding album - how many photos does it typically include?
WOMAN: The premium album includes 50 pages, with about 75-100 photos. You'll get to select your favorites from the edited collection.
MAN: Perfect! One last thing - how do we secure the date?
WOMAN: I require a signed contract and a 30% deposit to hold your date. I can email those to you today.

**Q6:** What is the photographer's attitude toward rain on the wedding day?
- 1. Concerned about the outcome
- 2. Suggests rescheduling options
- 3. Positive and prepared
- 4. Prefers indoor venues only
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Concerned about the outcome: The photographer shows no concern about rain affecting outcomes.
❌ 2. Suggests rescheduling options: The photographer doesn't suggest rescheduling.
✅ 3. Positive and prepared: She says "Don't worry! I always scout backup locations nearby. Plus, some of my favorite shots have been taken in the rain with clear umbrellas - they're quite romantic!"
❌ 4. Prefers indoor venues only: She doesn't express a preference for indoor venues only.

**Q7:** What is required to book the photographer?
- 1. Full payment upfront
- 2. A 30% deposit and contract
- 3. A meeting at the venue
- 4. Selection of the album photos
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Full payment upfront: Only a deposit is required, not full payment.
✅ 2. A 30% deposit and contract: The photographer states "I require a signed contract and a 30% deposit to hold your date."
❌ 3. A meeting at the venue: A venue meeting isn't mentioned as a requirement.
❌ 4. Selection of the album photos: Album photo selection would happen after booking, not as a requirement to book.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the premium album?
- 1. It contains all photos taken
- 2. It has limited photo options
- 3. Clients can customize the selection
- 4. It only includes formal shots
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It contains all photos taken: The conversation indicates clients select from edited photos, not all photos.
❌ 2. It has limited photo options: There's no indication of limited options.
✅ 3. Clients can customize the selection: The photographer says "You'll get to select your favorites from the edited collection."
❌ 4. It only includes formal shots: There's no mention of only including formal shots.
