## Practice 379: Daycare Fever Notification | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at a childcare center, and the man is a parent calling about his child.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello, I'm calling about my daughter Emma. She's in the Sunshine Room. I got a message saying she has a fever?
WOMAN: Yes, Mr. Thompson. Emma's temperature was 101.2 degrees when we checked about 30 minutes ago. She's been complaining of a headache and hasn't eaten much during lunch.
MAN: Oh no, I'm so sorry to hear that. I'm currently in a meeting downtown. Is there any way you could give her some children's Tylenol? I believe I signed the medication authorization form.
WOMAN: Let me check her file... I'm sorry, but we don't have a current medication authorization form on file. The last one expired last month.
MAN: Right, I forgot to renew it. I'll try to get there as soon as possible, but it might take me about an hour with traffic.
WOMAN: No problem, we're keeping her comfortable in the quiet room. She's resting on a cot and we're monitoring her temperature.

**Q1:** What is the main reason for the man's call?
- 1. To get medical advice for his daughter
- 2. To inquire about his daughter's illness
- 3. To ask about medication administration
- 4. To update emergency contact information
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To get medical advice for his daughter: The man is not seeking medical advice, but responding to a message about his daughter's fever.
✅ 2. To inquire about his daughter's illness: The man starts the conversation with "I'm calling about my daughter Emma... I got a message saying she has a fever?" showing he's responding to a notification about her condition.
❌ 3. To ask about medication administration: The man specifically asks "Is there any way you could give her some children's Tylenol?" which indicates his main purpose is to request medication for his daughter.
❌ 4. To update emergency contact information: The man doesn't mention updating contact information until later in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why can't the daycare give Emma medication?
- 1. They don't have any medication available
- 2. The authorization form has expired
- 3. The father hasn't signed the form
- 4. It's against their policy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They don't have any medication available: The conversation doesn't mention whether medication is available or not.
✅ 2. The authorization form has expired: The woman specifically states "we don't have a current medication authorization form on file. The last one expired last month."
❌ 3. The father hasn't signed the form: The father mentions he signed a form but forgot to renew it, not that he never signed it.
❌ 4. It's against their policy: While they may have policies, the specific reason given is the expired authorization form.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm stuck in terrible traffic. How is Emma doing?
WOMAN: Her temperature has gone up slightly to 101.8. She's still resting, but she's becoming more uncomfortable. Would it be possible for someone else on your emergency contact list to pick her up?
MAN: Yes, actually. Her grandmother is listed. Could you try calling her?
WOMAN: Of course. Let me pull up Emma's file again... Is it Mrs. Sarah Thompson?
MAN: That's right. She should be home today. If you can't reach her home number, try her cell phone.
WOMAN: I'll do that right away. Should I call you back once I've spoken with her?
MAN: Yes, please. And could you also let me know if Emma's condition changes at all?
WOMAN: Absolutely. I'll call Mrs. Thompson now and get back to you shortly.

**Q3:** What has changed since the first conversation?
- 1. Emma's symptoms have improved
- 2. The father's location has changed
- 3. Emma's temperature has increased
- 4. The daycare's staff has changed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Emma's symptoms have improved: The conversation indicates her condition has worsened, not improved.
❌ 2. The father's location has changed: The father remains stuck in traffic throughout the conversations.
✅ 3. Emma's temperature has increased: The woman states "Her temperature has gone up slightly to 101.8" from the initial 101.2 degrees.
❌ 4. The daycare's staff has changed: There's no indication of staff changes in the conversation.

**Q4:** What solution does the woman suggest?
- 1. Calling a doctor
- 2. Contacting another family member
- 3. Waiting for the traffic to clear
- 4. Giving Emma medication
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Calling a doctor: The woman doesn't suggest calling a doctor.
✅ 2. Contacting another family member: When the father mentions being stuck in traffic, the woman asks "Would it be possible for someone else on your emergency contact list to pick her up?"
❌ 3. Waiting for the traffic to clear: The woman doesn't suggest waiting for traffic to clear.
❌ 4. Giving Emma medication: The woman explains they can't give medication due to the expired authorization form.

**Q5:** What does the father request?
- 1. To be updated about both his mother-in-law and Emma
- 2. To have someone else contact his mother-in-law
- 3. To receive directions to avoid traffic
- 4. To speak directly with his mother-in-law
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To be updated about both his mother-in-law and Emma: The father asks "Should I call you back once I've spoken with her?" and he responds "Yes, please. And could you also let me know if Emma's condition changes at all?"
❌ 2. To have someone else contact his mother-in-law: The father agrees to the daycare contacting the grandmother, but doesn't request it initially.
❌ 3. To receive directions to avoid traffic: The father mentions being stuck in traffic but doesn't ask for directions.
❌ 4. To speak directly with his mother-in-law: The father doesn't request to speak directly with his mother-in-law.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Mr. Thompson? I've reached your mother-in-law.
MAN: Oh, thank goodness. Is she able to pick up Emma?
WOMAN: Yes, she said she'll be here in about 20 minutes. She also mentioned she has some children's fever medication at home.
MAN: That's perfect. I'm still stuck in traffic, but I'll head straight home once I get out of this. Could you make sure Emma takes her backpack and lunch box?
WOMAN: Of course. We'll have everything ready when Mrs. Thompson arrives. Also, just to remind you, our policy requires that Emma stays home until she's fever-free for 24 hours.
MAN: Yes, I understand. I'll make arrangements to work from home tomorrow. Thank you so much for taking such good care of her.
WOMAN: You're welcome. We'll see you when Emma's feeling better.

**Q6:** What helpful information does the grandmother provide?
- 1. She has medical experience
- 2. She has fever medication
- 3. She lives close to the daycare
- 4. She can work from home
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has medical experience: There's no mention of the grandmother having medical experience.
✅ 2. She has fever medication: The woman tells the father "She also mentioned she has some children's fever medication at home."
❌ 3. She lives close to the daycare: While she can arrive in 20 minutes, there's no explicit statement about her living close.
❌ 4. She can work from home: There's no mention of the grandmother's work situation.

**Q7:** What does the man plan to do?
- 1. Meet his mother-in-law at home
- 2. Return to work tomorrow
- 3. Take Emma to the doctor
- 4. Work remotely the next day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Meet his mother-in-law at home: He says he'll head straight home but doesn't mention meeting his mother-in-law there.
❌ 2. Return to work tomorrow: He actually plans not to return to work.
❌ 3. Take Emma to the doctor: There's no mention of taking Emma to a doctor.
✅ 4. Work remotely the next day: The man states "I'll make arrangements to work from home tomorrow" after being reminded of the 24-hour fever-free policy.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the daycare's policy?
- 1. Parents must provide medication
- 2. Sick children need medical clearance
- 3. Children with fever must stay home
- 4. Emergency contacts must be local
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Parents must provide medication: While medication authorization is mentioned, there's no indication parents must provide it.
❌ 2. Sick children need medical clearance: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Children with fever must stay home: The woman explicitly states "our policy requires that Emma stays home until she's fever-free for 24 hours."
❌ 4. Emergency contacts must be local: While an emergency contact is called, there's no mention of a requirement for them to be local.

## Practice 380: Transit Pass Auto-Renewal | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman works at the transit authority call center, and the man is calling about his transit pass.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling about my monthly transit pass. It's supposed to auto-renew today, but I got an error message saying the payment didn't go through.
WOMAN: I can help you with that. Could you please provide your transit pass number?
MAN: Sure, it's TC-458921.
WOMAN: Thank you. I can see here that your credit card on file expired last month. That's why the auto-renewal failed.
MAN: Oh, right! I completely forgot about that. I got a new card but haven't updated the information.
WOMAN: No problem, I can help you update that now. Would you like to provide the new card details?
MAN: Actually, I'm driving right now. Would it be okay if I call back in about an hour?
WOMAN: Of course! Our customer service line is open until 8 PM today. Just call this same number when you're ready.

**Q1:** Why did the man initially contact customer service?
- 1. To update his payment information
- 2. To report a lost transit pass
- 3. Because of a failed auto-renewal
- 4. To cancel his monthly pass
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To update his payment information: While he did update his payment information, this wasn't his initial reason for calling.
❌ 2. To report a lost transit pass: There is no mention of a lost pass in the conversation.
✅ 3. Because of a failed auto-renewal: The man's first statement is "I'm calling about my monthly transit pass. It's supposed to auto-renew today, but I got an error message saying the payment didn't go through."
❌ 4. To cancel his monthly pass: The man never expressed any intention to cancel his pass.

**Q2:** Why couldn't the man complete the transaction during the first call?
- 1. The system was down
- 2. He was operating a vehicle
- 3. He didn't have his credit card
- 4. The office was closing
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The system was down: There is no mention of system issues in the conversation.
✅ 2. He was operating a vehicle: The man specifically states "Actually, I'm driving right now. Would it be okay if I call back in about an hour?"
❌ 3. He didn't have his credit card: While he needed to update his card information, he doesn't say he doesn't have his card with him.
❌ 4. The office was closing: The woman mentions they're open until 8 PM, so this wasn't the reason.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I called earlier about my transit pass auto-renewal issue. I'm ready to update my card information now.
WOMAN: Yes, I remember. I still have your file open. Could you please provide your new credit card number?
MAN: It's 4582-9921-3347-8854.
WOMAN: And the expiration date?
MAN: It expires in March 2025.
WOMAN: Perfect. Now, I just need the three-digit security code on the back.
MAN: It's 443.
WOMAN: Thank you. I've updated your payment information. Would you like me to process the renewal now?
MAN: Yes, please. How long will it take to activate?
WOMAN: The pass should be active within the next 30 minutes.

**Q3:** What is the expiration date of the man's new credit card?
- 1. March 2024
- 2. May 2025
- 3. March 2025
- 4. March 2023
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. March 2024: This date is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. May 2025: This date is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. March 2025: The man specifically states his card "expires in March 2025" when asked about the expiration date.
❌ 4. March 2023: This date is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** How long will it take for the pass to become active?
- 1. Immediately
- 2. Within 30 minutes
- 3. Within 24 hours
- 4. By the next business day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately: The woman does not say the pass will be active immediately.
✅ 2. Within 30 minutes: The woman specifically states "The pass should be active within the next 30 minutes."
❌ 3. Within 24 hours: This timeframe is longer than what was stated in the conversation.
❌ 4. By the next business day: This timeframe is longer than what was stated and "business day" terminology wasn't used.

**Q5:** What was the reason for the man's file still being open?
- 1. He had called earlier that day
- 2. He was a priority customer
- 3. His pass had expired
- 4. He had payment issues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He had called earlier that day: The woman specifically says "Yes, I remember. I still have your file open," indicating she kept his file open from his earlier call that same day.
❌ 2. He was a priority customer: There is no mention of the man being a priority customer in the conversation.
❌ 3. His pass had expired: The issue was with payment method expiration, not the pass itself expiring.
❌ 4. He had payment issues: While he did have payment issues, this wasn't the reason his file was still open - it was because he had called earlier.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry, one more thing before I go. I noticed the renewal fee is higher than last month.
WOMAN: Yes, there was a rate increase that went into effect this month. All pass holders were notified by email about three months ago.
MAN: I must have missed that email. How much is the increase?
WOMAN: The monthly pass increased from $95 to $105. However, we're offering a new annual pass option that could save you money.
MAN: Oh? How does that work?
WOMAN: If you pay for 12 months upfront, you get two months free. That works out to about $87.50 per month.
MAN: That sounds like a better deal. Can I switch to the annual pass instead?
WOMAN: Absolutely! Would you like me to process that change now?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be great.

**Q6:** How were customers informed about the rate increase?
- 1. By phone call
- 2. Through mail
- 3. By email
- 4. Through text message
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By phone call: The conversation doesn't mention phone calls being used to notify customers about the rate increase.
❌ 2. Through mail: There is no mention of physical mail being used for notifications.
✅ 3. By email: The woman specifically states "All pass holders were notified by email about three months ago" regarding the rate increase.
❌ 4. Through text message: Text messages are not mentioned as a notification method in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is the monthly cost savings with the annual pass option?
- 1. $$17.50
- 2. $$10.00
- 3. $$15.00
- 4. $$20.00
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. $17.50: The monthly pass costs $105, while the annual pass works out to $87.50 per month, resulting in savings of $17.50 per month.
❌ 2. $10.00: This is not the correct calculation based on the information provided.
❌ 3. $15.00: This is not the correct calculation based on the information provided.
❌ 4. $20.00: This is not the correct calculation based on the information provided.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the man's decision?
- 1. He was hesitant about the annual commitment
- 2. He preferred to keep his monthly pass
- 3. He chose the more economical option
- 4. He wanted to think about it more
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He was hesitant about the annual commitment: The man shows no hesitation about switching to the annual pass.
❌ 2. He preferred to keep his monthly pass: The man actually decides to switch from monthly to annual.
✅ 3. He chose the more economical option: When presented with the annual pass option that would save him money ($87.50 per month instead of $105), he says "That sounds like a better deal" and chooses to switch.
❌ 4. He wanted to think about it more: The man makes his decision immediately, saying "Yes, please. That would be great" when asked if he wants to process the change.

## Practice 381: Virtual Tour Troubleshooting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a virtual tour guide for an online art gallery, and the man is a visitor seeking assistance.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm having some trouble accessing the virtual tour of your modern art exhibition. Is someone available to help?
WOMAN: Hello! Yes, I'd be happy to assist you. Are you trying to access it through our website or the mobile app?
MAN: I'm on the website, but when I click the 'Start Virtual Tour' button, nothing happens. I've tried refreshing the page several times.
WOMAN: I see. First, let's make sure you're using a compatible browser. Which browser are you currently using?
MAN: I'm using Safari on my MacBook. Should I switch to something else?
WOMAN: Actually, our virtual tour works best with Chrome or Firefox. Safari sometimes has compatibility issues with our 3D rendering software.
MAN: Oh, that makes sense. I have Chrome installed, so I'll switch over to that.
WOMAN: Perfect! Once you've opened Chrome, clear your cache and try accessing the tour again. That should resolve the issue.

**Q1:** What is the main issue the man is experiencing?
- 1. The website is completely down
- 2. He cannot start the virtual tour
- 3. His internet connection is unstable
- 4. The exhibition is closed
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The website is completely down: The website is working, but the virtual tour feature isn't working for him.
✅ 2. He cannot start the virtual tour: The man specifically states "when I click the 'Start Virtual Tour' button, nothing happens."
❌ 3. His internet connection is unstable: Internet connection issues are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The exhibition is closed: There's no mention of the exhibition being closed.

**Q2:** What solution does the woman suggest?
- 1. Update the computer's operating system
- 2. Install a new browser
- 3. Switch to a different browser
- 4. Restart the computer
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Update the computer's operating system: This solution is not suggested in the conversation.
❌ 2. Install a new browser: The woman doesn't suggest installing a new browser, just using one he already has.
✅ 3. Switch to a different browser: The woman suggests "our virtual tour works best with Chrome or Firefox" and the man confirms he has Chrome installed and will switch to it.
❌ 4. Restart the computer: Restarting the computer is not suggested in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi again. I've switched to Chrome and can see the exhibition now, but I'm not sure how to navigate through the gallery.
WOMAN: Great that you can access it now! Navigation is pretty straightforward. Use your mouse to look around – left click and drag to rotate your view, and the scroll wheel to zoom in and out.
MAN: Got it. And how do I move between rooms?
WOMAN: You'll see blue arrows on the floor – just click those to move to different areas. There are also thumbnail previews of each room at the bottom of your screen for quick navigation.
MAN: Perfect, I see them now. One more thing – some artworks have a small icon next to them. What does that mean?
WOMAN: Those icons indicate additional content. Clicking them will open detailed information about the artwork, including the artist's biography and sometimes even video interviews.
MAN: That's fantastic! This is much more interactive than I expected.

**Q3:** What is NOT mentioned as a navigation feature?
- 1. Using arrow keys on keyboard
- 2. Clicking blue arrows on floor
- 3. Using mouse scroll wheel
- 4. Using thumbnail previews
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Using arrow keys on keyboard: This navigation method is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Clicking blue arrows on floor: The woman specifically mentions "You'll see blue arrows on the floor – just click those to move to different areas."
❌ 3. Using mouse scroll wheel: The woman explains "Use your mouse to look around – left click and drag to rotate your view, and the scroll wheel to zoom in and out."
❌ 4. Using thumbnail previews: The woman mentions "There are also thumbnail previews of each room at the bottom of your screen for quick navigation."

**Q4:** What happens when users click on icons next to artworks?
- 1. They can purchase the artwork
- 2. They can download images
- 3. They access additional information
- 4. They can leave comments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They can purchase the artwork: The woman mentions a separate shopping cart icon for purchases, not the information icons.
❌ 2. They can download images: Downloading images is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. They access additional information: The woman explains "Clicking them will open detailed information about the artwork, including the artist's biography and sometimes even video interviews."
❌ 4. They can leave comments: Leaving comments is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** Which statement best describes the virtual tour experience?
- 1. It's primarily focused on sales
- 2. It's more interactive than expected
- 3. It's difficult to navigate
- 4. It's limited in features
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's primarily focused on sales: While purchases are possible, the tour focuses on viewing art with interactive features.
✅ 2. It's more interactive than expected: The man explicitly states "That's fantastic! This is much more interactive than I expected."
❌ 3. It's difficult to navigate: After receiving instructions, the man understands how to navigate and says "Perfect, I see them now."
❌ 4. It's limited in features: The conversation reveals multiple features including 3D navigation, information about artworks, artist biographies, and videos, suggesting it's feature-rich.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Sorry to bother you again, but I'm really interested in one of the pieces I've seen. Is it possible to purchase artwork through the virtual tour?
WOMAN: Absolutely! Which piece caught your eye?
MAN: It's the large abstract painting in Gallery Three, titled 'Urban Dreams.' I'd love to know more about the pricing and availability.
WOMAN: Let me check that for you... Yes, 'Urban Dreams' is still available. You can click the shopping cart icon next to the artwork to see pricing and shipping information. We also offer a certificate of authenticity with each purchase.
MAN: That's perfect. And do you offer international shipping?
WOMAN: Yes, we ship worldwide. The shipping cost will be calculated based on your location during checkout. We also provide insurance for all artwork shipments.
MAN: Excellent! I'll definitely consider making a purchase. Thanks for all your help today.

**Q6:** What artwork interests the man?
- 1. A sculpture
- 2. A photograph
- 3. An abstract painting
- 4. A digital artwork
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A sculpture: The man doesn't mention being interested in a sculpture.
❌ 2. A photograph: The man doesn't mention being interested in a photograph.
✅ 3. An abstract painting: The man specifically states "It's the large abstract painting in Gallery Three, titled 'Urban Dreams.'"
❌ 4. A digital artwork: The man doesn't mention being interested in a digital artwork.

**Q7:** What is included with artwork purchases?
- 1. A frame
- 2. A certificate of authenticity
- 3. An artist signature
- 4. A warranty
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A frame: A frame is not mentioned as being included with purchases.
✅ 2. A certificate of authenticity: The woman specifically states "We also offer a certificate of authenticity with each purchase."
❌ 3. An artist signature: An artist signature is not mentioned as being included.
❌ 4. A warranty: A warranty is not mentioned; the woman mentions insurance for shipments, but not a warranty.

**Q8:** What can be inferred about the gallery's services?
- 1. They only sell local art
- 2. They offer limited shipping options
- 3. They provide comprehensive purchase support
- 4. They focus on digital art only
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only sell local art: The conversation mentions they ship worldwide, so they don't only sell local art.
❌ 2. They offer limited shipping options: The woman states they ship worldwide, indicating extensive shipping options.
✅ 3. They provide comprehensive purchase support: The conversation shows they offer pricing information, shipping worldwide, insurance for shipments, and certificates of authenticity - demonstrating comprehensive support.
❌ 4. They focus on digital art only: The man is interested in purchasing a physical painting, so they don't focus only on digital art.

## Practice 382: Yoga Schedule Switch | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a yoga studio. The woman works at the front desk, and the man is a student at the studio.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm calling about my yoga class schedule. I've been attending the morning flow class on Tuesdays and Thursdays.
WOMAN: Of course, let me pull up your account. Could you provide your name, please?
MAN: Yes, it's Michael Chen. I registered about two months ago.
WOMAN: Ah yes, I see your account here. You're enrolled in the 7 AM Vinyasa Flow with instructor Sarah.
MAN: That's right. The thing is, my work schedule is changing next month, and I won't be able to make it to the morning classes anymore. I was wondering if I could switch to evening classes?
WOMAN: We can definitely help you with that. We have several evening classes available. Would you like me to go through the options with you?
MAN: Yes, please. That would be really helpful.

**Q1:** Why is the man contacting the yoga studio?
- 1. To cancel his membership
- 2. To change his class schedule
- 3. To complain about an instructor
- 4. To register for new classes
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To cancel his membership: The man doesn't mention wanting to cancel his membership.
✅ 2. To change his class schedule: The man explicitly states "my work schedule is changing next month, and I won't be able to make it to the morning classes anymore. I was wondering if I could switch to evening classes?"
❌ 3. To complain about an instructor: The man doesn't express any complaints about instructors; in fact, he mentions liking Sarah's teaching style.
❌ 4. To register for new classes: He's not registering for new classes but rather changing his existing schedule.

**Q2:** What class has the man been attending?
- 1. Evening Flow with David
- 2. Morning Flow with Sarah
- 3. Afternoon Flow with Sarah
- 4. Evening Meditation with David
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Evening Flow with David: The man has not been attending this class; he's considering switching to it.
✅ 2. Morning Flow with Sarah: The woman confirms this is the class he's been attending when she says "You're enrolled in the 7 AM Vinyasa Flow with instructor Sarah."
❌ 3. Afternoon Flow with Sarah: There's no mention of an afternoon class with Sarah in the conversation.
❌ 4. Evening Meditation with David: David teaches an evening class, but it's not specifically a meditation class, and the man hasn't been attending it.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: So, we have two evening Vinyasa Flow classes. One is at 6 PM with instructor David, and another at 7:30 PM with Sarah, the same instructor you currently have.
MAN: Oh, that's great that Sarah teaches an evening class too! I really like her teaching style.
WOMAN: Yes, she's one of our most popular instructors. However, I should mention that her evening class is currently waitlisted.
MAN: Oh no, how long is the waitlist usually?
WOMAN: For the 7:30 PM class, it's typically about two to three weeks before a spot opens up.
MAN: Hmm, that's a bit long. What about David's 6 PM class? Is there space in that one?
WOMAN: Yes, there are several spots available in David's class. Would you like me to tell you more about his teaching style?

**Q3:** What is true about Sarah's evening class?
- 1. It's less popular than David's class
- 2. It's available immediately
- 3. It has a waiting period
- 4. It's at 6 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's less popular than David's class: The conversation suggests Sarah is very popular, with her evening class being waitlisted.
❌ 2. It's available immediately: The woman states it's waitlisted, not immediately available.
✅ 3. It has a waiting period: The woman explains "it's typically about two to three weeks before a spot opens up."
❌ 4. It's at 6 PM: Sarah's evening class is at 7:30 PM, not 6 PM.

**Q4:** How long would the man need to wait for Sarah's evening class?
- 1. One week
- 2. Two to three weeks
- 3. One month
- 4. No wait time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One week: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Two to three weeks: The woman specifically states "For the 7:30 PM class, it's typically about two to three weeks before a spot opens up."
❌ 3. One month: This timeframe isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. No wait time: The conversation clearly indicates there is a wait time for Sarah's evening class.

**Q5:** What is different about David's class time?
- 1. It has immediate availability
- 2. It's earlier in the morning
- 3. It's on different days
- 4. It's a longer class
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It has immediate availability: The woman states "there are several spots available in David's class" while Sarah's evening class is waitlisted.
❌ 2. It's earlier in the morning: David's class is at 6 PM, not in the morning.
❌ 3. It's on different days: The classes are still on Tuesdays and Thursdays, the same days as his current schedule.
❌ 4. It's a longer class: There's no mention of David's class being longer than other classes.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Yes, please. I'd like to know what to expect if I switch to his class.
WOMAN: David's style is similar to Sarah's, but he focuses more on strength building and holds poses a bit longer. He also incorporates more meditation at the beginning of class.
MAN: That actually sounds perfect for my practice. I think I'll switch to his class. Can we make that change now?
WOMAN: Absolutely! I can process that for you right away. Just to confirm, you want to switch to the Tuesday and Thursday 6 PM classes with David, starting next month?
MAN: Yes, that's correct. And my remaining classes from this month's package will transfer over, right?
WOMAN: Yes, your package will automatically transfer. I've made the change in our system. You're all set to start with David's class next month.
MAN: Perfect, thank you so much for your help!

**Q6:** How does David's teaching style differ from Sarah's?
- 1. He only focuses on meditation
- 2. He moves through poses more quickly
- 3. He holds poses for longer periods
- 4. He doesn't include strength training
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He only focuses on meditation: The woman mentions he incorporates more meditation, but doesn't say he only focuses on it.
❌ 2. He moves through poses more quickly: The conversation states he holds poses longer, not moves through them more quickly.
✅ 3. He holds poses for longer periods: The woman explains "he focuses more on strength building and holds poses a bit longer."
❌ 4. He doesn't include strength training: The conversation actually states he focuses more on strength building, not that he excludes it.

**Q7:** What concern does the man express about switching classes?
- 1. The teaching style
- 2. The remaining class credits
- 3. The class duration
- 4. The new instructor
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The teaching style: He asks about the teaching style but doesn't express concern about it.
✅ 2. The remaining class credits: The man asks "And my remaining classes from this month's package will transfer over, right?" showing this is his concern.
❌ 3. The class duration: There's no mention of the man being concerned about class duration.
❌ 4. The new instructor: He asks about David's teaching style out of interest, not concern.

**Q8:** When will the man start his new class schedule?
- 1. Immediately
- 2. Next week
- 3. The following month
- 4. In two months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately: The conversation indicates the change will happen next month, not immediately.
❌ 2. Next week: There's no mention of starting next week.
✅ 3. The following month: The woman confirms this when she says "Just to confirm, you want to switch to the Tuesday and Thursday 6 PM classes with David, starting next month?"
❌ 4. In two months: The conversation doesn't mention a two-month timeframe.

## Practice 383: Ski Equipment Rental | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a winter sports equipment rental shop. The woman works at the rental shop, and the man is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there! I'm planning to go skiing this weekend, and I need to rent some equipment.
WOMAN: Welcome! Are you looking for downhill or cross-country skiing equipment?
MAN: Downhill skiing. It's actually my first time, so I'm not really sure what I need.
WOMAN: No problem at all! We'll get you properly equipped. What's your height and weight? We'll need that to properly size your skis and boots.
MAN: I'm 5'10" and about 175 pounds.
WOMAN: Perfect. And do you know your shoe size?
MAN: Yes, I wear a size 10.
WOMAN: Great! For beginners, we recommend shorter skis that are easier to control. We also include poles and boots in our basic rental package. Would you like to add a helmet rental as well?
MAN: Yes, definitely. Safety first, right?
WOMAN: Absolutely! The helmet rental is an additional $10 per day. How many days will you need the equipment?

**Q1:** What type of skiing equipment is the man renting?
- 1. Cross-country skiing gear
- 2. Advanced downhill equipment
- 3. Basic downhill equipment
- 4. Snowboarding gear
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cross-country skiing gear: The man specifically asks for downhill skiing equipment, not cross-country.
❌ 2. Advanced downhill equipment: The woman mentions they'll recommend shorter skis for beginners, indicating he's getting beginner equipment, not advanced.
✅ 3. Basic downhill equipment: The woman states "The basic package with skis, boots, and poles is $45 per day" and the man confirms he's a first-time skier needing basic equipment.
❌ 4. Snowboarding gear: The conversation is specifically about skiing equipment, not snowboarding.

**Q2:** Which additional safety item does the man agree to rent?
- 1. Wrist guards
- 2. Helmet
- 3. Knee pads
- 4. Safety goggles
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Wrist guards: Wrist guards are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Helmet: The man responds "Yes, definitely" when asked if he wants to add a helmet rental.
❌ 3. Knee pads: Knee pads are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Safety goggles: Goggles are not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I'd like to rent it for two days, Saturday and Sunday.
WOMAN: Okay, let me check our availability... Yes, we have everything you need. The basic package with skis, boots, and poles is $45 per day, plus the helmet rental. That comes to $110 total for both days.
MAN: That sounds reasonable. Oh, wait - do I also need special clothing?
WOMAN: We do rent ski jackets and pants. They're $20 each per day.
MAN: I think I'll need both. I don't have any proper ski wear.
WOMAN: No problem. Let me grab those for you. What size do you usually wear?
MAN: Large for both, please.
WOMAN: Perfect. With the clothing, your total comes to $190 for the two days.
MAN: Great. Can I pick everything up on Saturday morning?
WOMAN: Of course! We open at 7 AM.

**Q3:** What is the cost of the basic equipment package per day?
- 1. $$55
- 2. $$110
- 3. $$45
- 4. $$95
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $55: This is not the stated price for the basic package.
❌ 2. $110: This is the total for two days including the helmet rental, not the daily rate for the basic package.
✅ 3. $45: The woman clearly states "The basic package with skis, boots, and poles is $45 per day."
❌ 4. $95: This amount is not mentioned as the cost of the basic package.

**Q4:** What additional items did the man decide to rent?
- 1. Just a ski jacket
- 2. Ski jacket and goggles
- 3. Ski pants and helmet
- 4. Ski jacket and pants
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Just a ski jacket: The man rents both a jacket and pants, not just a jacket.
❌ 2. Ski jacket and goggles: Goggles are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Ski pants and helmet: While he does rent these items, he also rents a ski jacket.
✅ 4. Ski jacket and pants: The man says "I think I'll need both" when the woman offers ski jackets and pants for rent.

**Q5:** When can the man collect his equipment?
- 1. Friday evening
- 2. Saturday at 7 AM
- 3. Sunday morning
- 4. Saturday afternoon
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Friday evening: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Saturday at 7 AM: The man asks if he can pick everything up on Saturday morning, and the woman responds "Of course! We open at 7 AM."
❌ 3. Sunday morning: While the rental is for Saturday and Sunday, the pickup is scheduled for Saturday morning.
❌ 4. Saturday afternoon: The man specifically asks about Saturday morning, not afternoon.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: One more question - do you offer any kind of insurance for the equipment?
WOMAN: Yes, we do recommend getting our damage protection plan. It's $15 per day and covers any accidental damage to the equipment.
MAN: That sounds like a good idea, especially since I'm a beginner. I'll add that too.
WOMAN: Smart choice. So with the insurance, your new total is $220. Would you like to pay now or when you pick up the equipment?
MAN: I'll pay now. Do you need a deposit or anything?
WOMAN: Yes, we'll need a valid credit card for the deposit, and I'll need to see your driver's license when you pick up the equipment.
MAN: Perfect. Here's my credit card.
WOMAN: Great! I'll get your receipt ready. Everything will be prepared for you on Saturday morning.
MAN: Thanks for all your help!

**Q6:** Why does the man add insurance to his rental?
- 1. It's mandatory for all rentals
- 2. Because he's inexperienced
- 3. It was on special offer
- 4. The woman insisted on it
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's mandatory for all rentals: The conversation indicates insurance is optional, not mandatory.
✅ 2. Because he's inexperienced: The man says "That sounds like a good idea, especially since I'm a beginner" when deciding to add insurance.
❌ 3. It was on special offer: No special offer for insurance is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The woman insisted on it: The woman recommends it but doesn't insist; she says "we do recommend getting our damage protection plan."

**Q7:** What is required for the deposit?
- 1. Cash only
- 2. Credit card and cash
- 3. Credit card and driver's license
- 4. Driver's license only
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cash only: Cash is not mentioned as a requirement for the deposit.
❌ 2. Credit card and cash: Cash is not mentioned as part of the deposit requirement.
✅ 3. Credit card and driver's license: The woman says "we'll need a valid credit card for the deposit, and I'll need to see your driver's license when you pick up the equipment."
❌ 4. Driver's license only: A credit card is also required, not just a driver's license.

**Q8:** What is the final total cost for the two-day rental?
- 1. $$190
- 2. $$110
- 3. $$220
- 4. $$250
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $190: This was the total before adding insurance ($110 for basic equipment + helmet + $80 for clothing).
❌ 2. $110: This was only the cost for the basic package and helmet for two days.
✅ 3. $220: The woman states "So with the insurance, your new total is $220" which is the final amount.
❌ 4. $250: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 384: Visa Application Delay | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a visa application center. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is inquiring about his visa application status.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Good morning. I'm calling about my visa application status. I submitted it about three weeks ago.
WOMAN: I'll be happy to help you check that. Could you please provide your application reference number?
MAN: Yes, it's VFS2024789.
WOMAN: Thank you. Let me pull up your information... I can see your application was received on January 15th. However, there seems to be an issue with one of your supporting documents.
MAN: Really? What's the problem?
WOMAN: The bank statement you provided is more than three months old. We need a recent statement from the past 90 days.
MAN: Oh, I didn't realize that. So what should I do now?
WOMAN: You'll need to submit an updated bank statement. You can upload it through our online portal using your reference number.
MAN: I see. And how long will it take to process after I submit the new statement?
WOMAN: Once we receive the updated document, it typically takes 5-7 business days for review.

**Q1:** What is the main issue with the man's application?
- 1. Missing passport information
- 2. Outdated bank statement
- 3. Incorrect reference number
- 4. Incomplete application form
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Missing passport information: The conversation doesn't mention any issues with passport information.
✅ 2. Outdated bank statement: The woman specifically states "The bank statement you provided is more than three months old. We need a recent statement from the past 90 days."
❌ 3. Incorrect reference number: The reference number works fine as the woman is able to access his information.
❌ 4. Incomplete application form: The issue is with supporting documents, not the application form itself.

**Q2:** How long will the review process take after submitting the new document?
- 1. 3 weeks
- 2. 90 days
- 3. 5-7 business days
- 4. 10 business days
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 3 weeks: This is how long ago the man initially submitted his application, not the review time.
❌ 2. 90 days: This refers to how recent the bank statement needs to be, not processing time.
✅ 3. 5-7 business days: The woman specifically states "Once we receive the updated document, it typically takes 5-7 business days for review."
❌ 4. 10 business days: This is the processing time for the final review stage, not for reviewing the new document.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi, I'm calling back about my visa application. I've uploaded the new bank statement as requested.
WOMAN: Let me check that for you. What's your reference number again?
MAN: It's VFS2024789.
WOMAN: Yes, I can see the new document was uploaded yesterday. However, I notice there's another detail we need to clarify. The employment letter you submitted doesn't include your current salary information.
MAN: But I thought the bank statement would be enough to show my financial status?
WOMAN: Actually, we need both. The employment letter verifies your current job status and income, while the bank statement shows your savings.
MAN: I understand now. Should I get a new letter from my employer then?
WOMAN: Yes, please make sure it includes your position, start date, and current salary.
MAN: Alright, I'll contact my HR department right away.

**Q3:** What additional document does the man need to provide?
- 1. Updated passport
- 2. Employment letter with salary details
- 3. Proof of residence
- 4. Educational certificates
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Updated passport: There's no mention of needing an updated passport.
✅ 2. Employment letter with salary details: The woman states "The employment letter you submitted doesn't include your current salary information" and advises him to get a new letter that "includes your position, start date, and current salary."
❌ 3. Proof of residence: This document is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Educational certificates: These are not mentioned as required documents.

**Q4:** Why does the woman say they need both bank statement and employment letter?
- 1. To verify identity and citizenship
- 2. To check work experience and skills
- 3. To confirm savings and current income
- 4. To validate address and employment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To verify identity and citizenship: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. To check work experience and skills: The woman doesn't mention needing to verify work experience or skills.
✅ 3. To confirm savings and current income: The woman explicitly states "The employment letter verifies your current job status and income, while the bank statement shows your savings."
❌ 4. To validate address and employment: While employment is part of it, address validation is not mentioned, and this option doesn't include the financial aspect.

**Q5:** What is the man's likely next step?
- 1. Contact his bank
- 2. Visit the visa center
- 3. Contact HR department
- 4. Submit his passport
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Contact his bank: He already has the updated bank statement.
❌ 2. Visit the visa center: He doesn't need to visit the center until his visa is approved.
✅ 3. Contact HR department: The man explicitly states "I'll contact my HR department right away" after learning he needs a new employment letter with salary details.
❌ 4. Submit his passport: He doesn't need to submit his passport at this stage; he'll need it when collecting the approved visa.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hello again. I've submitted both the updated bank statement and the new employment letter.
WOMAN: Perfect, let me check your file... Yes, I can see both documents have been received and verified. Your application is now complete and in the final review stage.
MAN: That's great news! How long should I expect to wait for the final decision?
WOMAN: The standard processing time is 10 business days from this point. Would you like to sign up for SMS notifications?
MAN: Yes, that would be helpful. My mobile number is 555-0123.
WOMAN: I've added that to your file. You'll receive a message when your application status changes.
MAN: Thank you. One last question - if my visa is approved, how do I collect it?
WOMAN: Once approved, you can pick it up at our center. Just bring your original passport and the reference number.

**Q6:** What service does the woman offer the man?
- 1. Express processing
- 2. SMS notification updates
- 3. Email updates
- 4. Home delivery service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Express processing: No mention of express processing is made in the conversation.
✅ 2. SMS notification updates: The woman specifically asks "Would you like to sign up for SMS notifications?" and then adds his mobile number to the file.
❌ 3. Email updates: Email updates are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Home delivery service: There is no mention of home delivery; the man is told he needs to pick up the visa at the center.

**Q7:** What is required to collect the visa if approved?
- 1. Only the reference number
- 2. Original passport and reference number
- 3. SMS notification and passport
- 4. Employment letter and passport
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Only the reference number: The reference number alone is not sufficient.
✅ 2. Original passport and reference number: The woman clearly states "Once approved, you can pick it up at our center. Just bring your original passport and the reference number."
❌ 3. SMS notification and passport: SMS notification is not required for collection, only for updates.
❌ 4. Employment letter and passport: The employment letter is needed for the application process, not for collecting the approved visa.

**Q8:** How long is the final review stage expected to take?
- 1. 5-7 business days
- 2. 10 business days
- 3. 90 days
- 4. 3 weeks
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 5-7 business days: This is how long it takes to review after submitting the updated bank statement, not the final review stage.
✅ 2. 10 business days: The woman clearly states "The standard processing time is 10 business days from this point" when referring to the final review stage.
❌ 3. 90 days: This is mentioned as the recency requirement for bank statements, not a processing time.
❌ 4. 3 weeks: This is how long ago the man initially submitted his application, not the processing time for the final review.

## Practice 385: Library Card Application | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a public library. The woman is a librarian, and the man is a customer who has recently moved to the area.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** Why can't the man get a library card immediately?
- 1. He doesn't have enough identification.
- 2. His driver's license has expired.
- 3. His identification shows his previous address.
- 4. The library's system is currently down.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He doesn't have enough identification: The man has identification (his driver's license), but the issue is with the address on it.
❌ 2. His driver's license has expired: There's no mention of his license being expired in the conversation.
✅ 3. His identification shows his previous address: The man states, "I have my driver's license, but it still has my old address on it. I only moved here last week," which is why he can't get a card immediately.
❌ 4. The library's system is currently down: There's no mention of any system issues in the conversation.

**Q2:** What can the man do at the library without a card?
- 1. Borrow up to three books
- 2. Use the computers and reading areas
- 3. Reserve a study room
- 4. Access the digital collection
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Borrow up to three books: The librarian specifically states "you do need a library card to borrow materials."
✅ 2. Use the computers and reading areas: The librarian says "you're welcome to use our computers and reading areas today" without needing a card.
❌ 3. Reserve a study room: The librarian mentions that "Library cardholders can book them for up to two hours per day," indicating a card is required.
❌ 4. Access the digital collection: The librarian states "With your library card, you'll have access to e-books, audiobooks..." indicating a card is needed.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q3:** How long does it take to get a library card once proper documentation is provided?
- 1. About five minutes
- 2. One business day
- 3. Up to two weeks
- 4. Three to five business days
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. About five minutes: The librarian explicitly states "Once you bring in your proof of address, we can issue your card right away. It only takes about five minutes."
❌ 2. One business day: This contradicts what the librarian said about issuing the card right away.
❌ 3. Up to two weeks: This is much longer than the stated five minutes and contradicts the information provided.
❌ 4. Three to five business days: This also contradicts the librarian's statement about issuing the card in about five minutes.

**Q4:** What is the maximum amount of time a study room can be booked per day?
- 1. One hour
- 2. Two hours
- 3. Three hours
- 4. Four hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One hour: This is incorrect based on the information provided.
✅ 2. Two hours: The librarian specifically states that "Library cardholders can book them for up to two hours per day."
❌ 3. Three hours: This exceeds the stated maximum time limit of two hours per day.
❌ 4. Four hours: This also exceeds the stated maximum time limit of two hours per day.

**Q5:** How much does color printing cost at the library?
- 1. 15 cents per page
- 2. 25 cents per page
- 3. 35 cents per page
- 4. 50 cents per page
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 15 cents per page: This is the cost for black and white printing, not color.
❌ 2. 25 cents per page: This is not mentioned as a printing cost in the conversation.
❌ 3. 35 cents per page: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. 50 cents per page: The librarian states "It's 15 cents per page for black and white, and 50 cents for color."

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What document did the man bring to prove his address?
- 1. A bank statement
- 2. A hydro bill
- 3. A lease agreement
- 4. A phone bill
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A bank statement: While the librarian mentioned this as an acceptable document, it's not what the man actually brought.
✅ 2. A hydro bill: The man specifically states "I received my hydro bill today, so I brought it in along with my ID."
❌ 3. A lease agreement: This document is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A phone bill: This document is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is the grace period for returning library items?
- 1. One day
- 2. Two days
- 3. Three days
- 4. Five days
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One day: This contradicts the information provided by the librarian.
✅ 2. Two days: The librarian explicitly states "But we do have a grace period of two days."
❌ 3. Three days: This contradicts the stated grace period of two days.
❌ 4. Five days: This contradicts the stated grace period of two days.

**Q8:** Why might the man use the interlibrary loan service?
- 1. To save money on late fees
- 2. To access digital resources
- 3. To find books on Canadian history
- 4. To reserve study rooms in advance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To save money on late fees: This is not mentioned as a reason for using interlibrary loans.
❌ 2. To access digital resources: Digital resources are available with a regular library card, not through interlibrary loans.
✅ 3. To find books on Canadian history: The man specifically states "I'm actually researching Canadian history for my course, so I might need to use that service."
❌ 4. To reserve study rooms in advance: Study rooms can be reserved directly at the library with a library card, not through interlibrary loans.

❌ 3. Notify the facility 24 hours in advance: This requirement is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Pay an additional fee: No fee for additional users is mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 387: Phone Battery Shutdown | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a cell phone repair shop. The woman is a customer, and the man is a technician.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: Hi there. I'm having some issues with my phone. It's been acting really strange lately.
MAN: Good morning. I'd be happy to take a look at it for you. What seems to be the problem?
WOMAN: Well, it keeps shutting down randomly, even when the battery is at, like, 50%. And sometimes it gets really hot for no reason.
MAN: I see. How long has this been happening?
WOMAN: Um, about two weeks now. At first, I thought maybe it was just a glitch, you know? But it's getting worse.
MAN: Could be a few different things. Let me take a quick look. Do you mind unlocking it for me?
WOMAN: Sure, here you go. I really need this fixed soon because I use it for work. Is there any chance you could look at it today?
MAN: Actually, we're pretty backed up right now. But let me check our schedule... Hmm, I might be able to squeeze it in later this afternoon if it's something simple.
WOMAN: That would be great. Do you have any idea what might be causing it?

**Q1:** What problems is the woman experiencing with her phone?
- 1. It won't charge properly and the screen is cracked.
- 2. It shuts down randomly and overheats.
- 3. The battery drains quickly and apps keep crashing.
- 4. The screen freezes and it makes strange noises.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It won't charge properly and the screen is cracked: Neither of these issues are mentioned.
✅ 2. It shuts down randomly and overheats: The woman specifically says "it keeps shutting down randomly" and "sometimes it gets really hot for no reason."
❌ 3. The battery drains quickly and apps keep crashing: App crashes are not mentioned, and while battery issues are implied, quick draining isn't specifically mentioned.
❌ 4. The screen freezes and it makes strange noises: Neither of these problems are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why does the woman need her phone fixed quickly?
- 1. She's going on vacation soon.
- 2. She needs it for her job.
- 3. She's expecting important calls.
- 4. She wants to sell it this week.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She's going on vacation soon: There's no mention of a vacation in the conversation.
✅ 2. She needs it for her job: She specifically says "I really need this fixed soon because I use it for work."
❌ 3. She's expecting important calls: There's no mention of expecting important calls.
❌ 4. She wants to sell it this week: There's no indication she plans to sell the phone.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: After taking a closer look, I think your battery is starting to fail. See how it's slightly swollen here?
WOMAN: Oh! I didn't even notice that. Is that dangerous?
MAN: It can be. A swollen battery can potentially cause damage to other components or even pose a safety risk. I'd definitely recommend replacing it as soon as possible.
WOMAN: Okay, that sounds serious. How much would a new battery cost?
MAN: For your model, a genuine replacement battery would be about $79 plus tax, and labor is $45.
WOMAN: Hmm, that's more than I expected. Do you have any, um, less expensive options?
MAN: We do offer third-party batteries for around $45, but honestly, I don't recommend them for your phone. They tend to degrade faster and sometimes cause compatibility issues.
WOMAN: I see. And how long would the repair take?
MAN: If we do it today, you could have it back by closing time at 6:00. Otherwise, our standard turnaround is 24 to 48 hours.

**Q3:** What does the man identify as the cause of the problem?
- 1. A software glitch
- 2. Water damage
- 3. A failing battery
- 4. A damaged charging port
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A software glitch: The technician never mentions a software issue as the cause.
❌ 2. Water damage: There is no mention of water damage in the conversation.
✅ 3. A failing battery: The man specifically says "I think your battery is starting to fail" and points out that it's swollen.
❌ 4. A damaged charging port: The charging port is never mentioned as an issue.

**Q4:** How much would the genuine battery replacement cost in total?
- 1. $$45
- 2. $$79
- 3. $$124
- 4. $$45 plus tax
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $45: This is only the labor cost, not the total.
❌ 2. $79: This is only the cost of the genuine battery, not including labor.
✅ 3. $124: The man states the genuine battery costs $79 plus tax, and labor is $45, which totals $124 (plus tax).
❌ 4. $45 plus tax: This is incorrect as it only accounts for labor, not the battery cost.

**Q5:** How does the woman feel about the repair cost?
- 1. Satisfied
- 2. Confused
- 3. Surprised by how expensive it is
- 4. Angry about being overcharged
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Satisfied: She doesn't express satisfaction with the price.
❌ 2. Confused: She understands the pricing but is surprised by it.
✅ 3. Surprised by how expensive it is: She says "Hmm, that's more than I expected" and asks about less expensive options.
❌ 4. Angry about being overcharged: She never expresses anger or suggests she's being overcharged.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He recommends using third-party batteries.
- 2. He advises against cheaper replacement options.
- 3. He offers a discount for same-day service.
- 4. He suggests buying a new phone instead.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He recommends using third-party batteries: He actually advises against them, saying "I don't recommend them for your phone."
✅ 2. He advises against cheaper replacement options: He specifically says he doesn't recommend third-party batteries as they "degrade faster and sometimes cause compatibility issues."
❌ 3. He offers a discount for same-day service: No discount is mentioned for same-day service.
❌ 4. He suggests buying a new phone instead: He never suggests buying a new phone.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She hasn't backed up her photos recently.
- 2. She refuses to provide her phone password.
- 3. She decides to buy a new phone instead.
- 4. She has had battery problems before.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She hasn't backed up her photos recently: She specifically says "I haven't backed up my photos in a while" when asking about data safety.
❌ 2. She refuses to provide her phone password: She agrees to write down her password when asked.
❌ 3. She decides to buy a new phone instead: She decides to repair her current phone, not buy a new one.
❌ 4. She has had battery problems before: There's no mention of previous battery issues in the conversation.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will leave and think about other options.
- 2. The man will start working on the phone immediately.
- 3. The woman will complete the paperwork and leave her phone for repair.
- 4. The man will offer an additional discount to secure the sale.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will leave and think about other options: The woman has already decided to go with the genuine battery.
❌ 2. The man will start working on the phone immediately: The man mentioned they need to fill out paperwork first.
✅ 3. The woman will complete the paperwork and leave her phone for repair: The man asks her to fill out a repair authorization form, and she agrees to the repair.
❌ 4. The man will offer an additional discount to secure the sale: There's no indication of any discount being offered.

## Practice 389: Clinic Registration Inquiry | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a medical clinic. The woman is the receptionist, and the man is a patient who has recently moved to the area.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** Why has the man come to the clinic?
- 1. To get a prescription refilled
- 2. To register as a new patient
- 3. To see a physiotherapist
- 4. To get a medical note for work
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To get a prescription refilled: While he mentions needing a prescription renewal, his primary purpose is to register as a new patient.
✅ 2. To register as a new patient: The man clearly states at the beginning, "I just moved to this neighborhood and I need to register with your clinic."
❌ 3. To see a physiotherapist: Physiotherapy is only mentioned later in the conversation, not as his reason for coming.
❌ 4. To get a medical note for work: There is no mention of needing a medical note for work.

**Q2:** What problem does the man encounter?
- 1. He forgot his health card
- 2. His insurance won't cover the visit
- 3. The clinic isn't accepting new patients
- 4. His medical records are still in Vancouver
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He forgot his health card: The man has his health card, as he responds "Yes, here it is" when asked for it.
❌ 2. His insurance won't cover the visit: The receptionist confirms his provincial health card covers the basic consultation.
✅ 3. The clinic isn't accepting new patients: The receptionist specifically states "it looks like we're not accepting new patients at the moment. All our doctors have full patient loads right now."
❌ 4. His medical records are still in Vancouver: There's no mention of his records being in Vancouver or this causing a problem.

**Q3:** What solution does the receptionist offer?
- 1. Referring him to another clinic
- 2. Putting him on a waiting list
- 3. Using the walk-in clinic service
- 4. Scheduling an emergency appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Referring him to another clinic: The receptionist never suggests sending him to a different clinic.
❌ 2. Putting him on a waiting list: There is no mention of a waiting list in the conversation.
✅ 3. Using the walk-in clinic service: The receptionist specifically says "While we can't register you as a regular patient right now, we do have a walk-in clinic that operates Monday through Friday from 9 to 4. You could see a doctor there for your immediate needs."
❌ 4. Scheduling an emergency appointment: The receptionist does not offer to schedule any kind of appointment.

### Audio Transcript
WOMAN: No appointment is necessary for the walk-in clinic. You just come in, sign up at the desk, and wait your turn. The wait times can vary depending on how busy we are that day.
MAN: I see. Um, what about my prescription? I only have about three days' worth of medication left. Would a doctor at the walk-in be able to renew it?
WOMAN: Yes, our walk-in doctors can certainly provide prescription renewals. You should bring your current medication bottle with you so the doctor can see exactly what you've been prescribed.
MAN: That's good to know. And, just to be clear, will my provincial health insurance cover the visit, or are there additional fees?
WOMAN: Your provincial health card covers the basic consultation. However, there might be fees for certain services like medical notes for work or school. Those typically cost around $20 to $30.
MAN: I understand. One more thing - my back pain sometimes makes it difficult to sit for long periods. Is there any way to get an estimate of wait times before coming in?

**Q3:** How many days of medication does the man have left?
- 1. Two days
- 2. Three days
- 3. Five days
- 4. Seven days
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Two days: The man does not say he has two days of medication left.
✅ 2. Three days: The man specifically states "I only have about three days' worth of medication left."
❌ 3. Five days: The man does not say he has five days of medication left.
❌ 4. Seven days: The man does not say he has seven days of medication left.

**Q4:** What does the man need to bring to get his prescription renewed?
- 1. A note from his previous doctor
- 2. His current medication bottle
- 3. His complete medical history
- 4. Proof of his recent move
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A note from his previous doctor: This is not mentioned as a requirement.
✅ 2. His current medication bottle: The receptionist specifically says "You should bring your current medication bottle with you so the doctor can see exactly what you've been prescribed."
❌ 3. His complete medical history: While bringing medical records is mentioned, it's not specifically required for the prescription renewal.
❌ 4. Proof of his recent move: There is no mention of needing to prove his recent move.

**Q5:** How does the man feel about waiting at the clinic?
- 1. Anxious about the potential cost
- 2. Concerned about sitting for long periods
- 3. Worried about missing work
- 4. Frustrated about the lack of appointments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Anxious about the potential cost: While the man asks about costs, he doesn't express anxiety about it.
✅ 2. Concerned about sitting for long periods: The man specifically says "my back pain sometimes makes it difficult to sit for long periods" when asking about wait times.
❌ 3. Worried about missing work: There is no mention of the man being concerned about work.
❌ 4. Frustrated about the lack of appointments: While he's initially concerned about not being able to register as a regular patient, he doesn't express frustration about waiting.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has recently moved from Vancouver
- 2. He prefers afternoon appointments
- 3. He regularly uses physiotherapy
- 4. He has been to this clinic before
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He has recently moved from Vancouver: The man states "I moved from Vancouver about two weeks ago."
❌ 2. He prefers afternoon appointments: There is no mention of him preferring afternoon appointments. In fact, he plans to come in the morning.
❌ 3. He regularly uses physiotherapy: While physiotherapy is mentioned, there's no indication he regularly uses it.
❌ 4. He has been to this clinic before: The conversation clearly indicates this is his first visit as he's trying to register as a new patient.

**Q7:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will call his previous doctor
- 2. The man will visit the clinic early tomorrow
- 3. The receptionist will check for cancellations
- 4. The man will book a physiotherapy appointment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2

## Practice 391: Hotel Room Downgrade | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a downtown hotel. The woman works at the front desk, and the man is a traveler who has just arrived.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** What problem does the man have with his reservation?
- 1. He was given the wrong room type.
- 2. His reservation was not in the system.
- 3. He was charged for an extra night.
- 4. The hotel overbooked his room.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He was given the wrong room type: The man states 'I requested a room with a king bed' but was booked into a room with a queen bed.
❌ 2. His reservation was not in the system: The woman confirms his reservation is in the system saying 'I see your reservation here.'
❌ 3. He was charged for an extra night: There's no mention of any billing issues in the conversation.
❌ 4. The hotel overbooked his room: While the hotel is full, his specific reservation wasn't overbooked; he just received a different bed type than requested.

**Q2:** When will the man be able to move to his preferred room?
- 1. Immediately upon check-in
- 2. The following morning
- 3. After the conference ends
- 4. On his final night
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Immediately upon check-in: The woman explains they're full due to a conference and can't provide a king room immediately.
✅ 2. The following morning: The woman says 'we do have a king room becoming available tomorrow' and that it would be ready by 11:00 a.m. (though it actually becomes available earlier at 10:30 a.m.).
❌ 3. After the conference ends: There's no mention of waiting until after the conference ends.
❌ 4. On his final night: This would defeat the purpose of changing rooms, and the conversation clearly indicates he'll move the next morning.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, I'm in Room 412, and I was wondering about the Wi-Fi. I can't seem to connect to it.
WOMAN: Oh, I'm sorry about that, Mr. Wilson. Our Wi-Fi should be working throughout the hotel. Did you try using the network name and password on your room key card holder?
MAN: Yes, I entered 'Lakeview_Guest' as the network and the password exactly as written, but it keeps saying 'authentication failed.'
WOMAN: That's strange. Let me check if there's a system issue... Actually, it looks like the system automatically generated a new password this morning. I apologize for the confusion. Let me write down the correct password for you.
MAN: Thank you. Also, I was wondering about breakfast. Is it included with my room?
WOMAN: Yes, breakfast is included. It's served in our restaurant on the main floor from 6:30 to 10:00 a.m. on weekdays and until 11:00 a.m. on weekends.
MAN: Perfect. And one more thing—do you have any recommendations for dinner tonight? I'm quite hungry after my flight.

**Q3:** Why can't the man connect to the Wi-Fi?
- 1. His device is incompatible with the hotel network.
- 2. The Wi-Fi system is currently down.
- 3. He's using outdated password information.
- 4. He's trying to connect to the wrong network.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His device is incompatible with the hotel network: There's no mention of device compatibility issues.
❌ 2. The Wi-Fi system is currently down: The woman checks and doesn't indicate any system outage.
✅ 3. He's using outdated password information: The woman explains that 'the system automatically generated a new password this morning' and he was using the old password printed on his key card holder.
❌ 4. He's trying to connect to the wrong network: He correctly identified the network as 'Lakeview_Guest' as confirmed by the woman.

**Q4:** What time does breakfast end on weekends?
- 1. 6:30 a.m.
- 2. 10:00 a.m.
- 3. 11:00 a.m.
- 4. 12:00 p.m.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 6:30 a.m.: This is actually when breakfast begins on weekdays, not when it ends on weekends.
❌ 2. 10:00 a.m.: This is when breakfast ends on weekdays, not weekends.
✅ 3. 11:00 a.m.: The woman clearly states that breakfast is served 'until 11:00 a.m. on weekends.'
❌ 4. 12:00 p.m.: This time is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation regarding breakfast hours.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** How does the man feel after his first night?
- 1. Completely satisfied
- 2. Frustrated about the noise
- 3. Disappointed with the bed
- 4. Angry about the service
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Completely satisfied: The man mentions being woken up by construction noise, indicating he wasn't completely satisfied.
✅ 2. Frustrated about the noise: He specifically mentions being 'woken up around 5:00 a.m. by some construction noise' and calls it 'unfortunate timing.'
❌ 3. Disappointed with the bed: He actually says 'The bed was comfortable,' indicating satisfaction with the bed itself.
❌ 4. Angry about the service: The man doesn't express anger about the service; he remains polite throughout the conversation.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She knew about the construction for weeks.
- 2. She offers solutions to the man's problems.
- 3. She refuses to help with the luggage.
- 4. She works in the hotel restaurant.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She knew about the construction for weeks: The woman states that the hotel was only notified about the construction 'yesterday afternoon.'
✅ 2. She offers solutions to the man's problems: Throughout the conversation, she offers solutions for all his issues - arranging a room change, providing the correct Wi-Fi password, offering luggage assistance, and giving him a room away from construction.
❌ 3. She refuses to help with the luggage: On the contrary, she offers assistance with his luggage, saying 'Would you like assistance with your luggage?'
❌ 4. She works in the hotel restaurant: The conversation indicates she works at the front desk, not in the restaurant.

**Q8:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is staying at the hotel for business purposes.
- 2. He frequently stays at this particular hotel.
- 3. He is upset about changing rooms.
- 4. He arrived at the hotel very late at night.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He is staying at the hotel for business purposes: He mentions having a meeting at noon, suggesting he's there for business.
❌ 2. He frequently stays at this particular hotel: There's no indication in the conversation that he's a repeat guest.
❌ 3. He is upset about changing rooms: He actually seems pleased about changing rooms, especially since the new room faces away from the construction.
❌ 4. He arrived at the hotel very late at night: While he does say 'Good evening' at check-in, there's no indication it was 'very late' at night.

**Q9:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will complain to the hotel manager.
- 2. The man will cancel the remainder of his stay.
- 3. The man will move to his new room with assistance.
- 4. The man will miss his scheduled meeting.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will complain to the hotel manager: There's no indication he's dissatisfied enough to escalate complaints.
❌ 2. The man will cancel the remainder of his stay: He seems satisfied with the solution of moving to a new room.
✅ 3. The man will move to his new room with assistance: The conversation ends with him accepting the offer of luggage assistance and saying he'll head up to pack his things.
❌ 4. The man will miss his scheduled meeting: He specifically mentions wanting to get settled before his noon meeting, and his room is ready early, so there's no reason to believe he'll miss it.
