## Practice 392: Bus Route Directions | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a bus stop. The woman is a bus driver, and the man is a passenger trying to get to his destination.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, is this the number 42 bus that goes downtown?
WOMAN: Yes, it is. I'm heading downtown in about five minutes. Are you going far?
MAN: I need to get to the Central Library on Maple Avenue. I have a job interview at 2:30, and I'm not really familiar with this part of the city.
WOMAN: The Central Library? Let me think... that's actually about two blocks past my regular stop. You'd need to transfer to the number 15 bus at Wilson Street.
MAN: Oh no, really? I was hoping to get there directly. How long would the transfer take?
WOMAN: Well, the 15 runs every 20 minutes, but there's actually another option. If you don't mind walking a bit, you could get off at Elm Street and walk about 5 minutes. It might be faster than waiting for the transfer.
MAN: That sounds much better. I don't mind walking at all. How will I know which stop is Elm Street?
WOMAN: Don't worry, I'll announce it. It's about a 15-minute ride from here.

**Q1:** Why is the man taking the bus?
- 1. To get to work
- 2. To attend a job interview
- 3. To visit the transit center
- 4. To explore the downtown area
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To get to work: He doesn't yet have the job; he's going to an interview.
✅ 2. To attend a job interview: He explicitly states 'I have a job interview at 2:30' at the Central Library.
❌ 3. To visit the transit center: The transit center is mentioned, but it's not his destination.
❌ 4. To explore the downtown area: He's going downtown but specifically for an interview, not exploration.

**Q2:** What does the woman suggest the man do to reach his destination?
- 1. Take a different bus route entirely
- 2. Transfer to the number 15 bus
- 3. Get off at Elm Street and walk
- 4. Take a taxi from Wilson Street
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Take a different bus route entirely: The woman doesn't suggest a completely different route.
❌ 2. Transfer to the number 15 bus: While she mentions this option first, she then offers a better alternative.
✅ 3. Get off at Elm Street and walk: The woman specifically suggests this as a faster option, saying 'If you don't mind walking a bit, you could get off at Elm Street and walk about 5 minutes. It might be faster than waiting for the transfer.'
❌ 4. Take a taxi from Wilson Street: Taking a taxi is never mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Thanks for letting me know about the Elm Street stop. Um, I just realized I don't have exact change for the fare. Do you accept credit cards?
WOMAN: Sorry, we don't take credit cards on the bus. The fare is $3.25. Do you have any coins at all?
MAN: I only have a $20 bill and about $2 in change. I should have planned better, but I was in such a rush this morning.
WOMAN: That's a common problem. We don't give change for bills, unfortunately. But you know what? There's a convenience store right across the street. You could quickly run in and break your $20.
MAN: Oh, that's a good idea. But will you wait? I'd hate to miss this bus and be late for my interview.
WOMAN: Well, I'm supposed to leave in two minutes according to the schedule, but I can wait an extra minute or two. Just be quick, okay?
MAN: Thank you so much! I really appreciate it. I'll run as fast as I can.

**Q3:** What problem does the man encounter?
- 1. He doesn't have enough money for the fare
- 2. He doesn't know which bus to take
- 3. He forgot the address of the library
- 4. He lost his credit card
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He doesn't have enough money for the fare: The man states 'I only have a $20 bill and about $2 in change' when the fare is $3.25, and buses don't give change for bills.
❌ 2. He doesn't know which bus to take: He knows he needs the number 42 bus; he asks to confirm this at the beginning.
❌ 3. He forgot the address of the library: He knows it's on Maple Avenue; there's no indication he forgot the address.
❌ 4. He lost his credit card: He has a credit card but can't use it for the fare; there's no mention of it being lost.

**Q4:** How much is the bus fare?
- 1. $$2.00
- 2. $$3.25
- 3. $$5.00
- 4. $$20.00
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $2.00: The man mentions having about $2 in change, but this is not the fare amount.
✅ 2. $3.25: The woman explicitly states 'The fare is $3.25' when discussing payment options.
❌ 3. $5.00: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. $20.00: The man has a $20 bill, but this is not the fare amount.

**Q5:** How does the woman feel about waiting for the man?
- 1. Annoyed but obligated
- 2. Completely unwilling
- 3. Willing to help within limits
- 4. Enthusiastic about breaking rules
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Annoyed but obligated: There's no indication she feels annoyed or obligated.
❌ 2. Completely unwilling: She agrees to wait, so this is incorrect.
✅ 3. Willing to help within limits: She says 'I can wait an extra minute or two. Just be quick, okay?' showing she's helpful but has time constraints.
❌ 4. Enthusiastic about breaking rules: While she waits past the scheduled departure, there's no enthusiasm about breaking rules.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has experience working in libraries
- 2. He is applying for an IT position
- 3. He already has a monthly transit pass
- 4. He lives in the downtown area
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has experience working in libraries: There's no mention of him having library work experience.
✅ 2. He is applying for an IT position: The man specifically states 'It's for an IT support position. They're upgrading their computer systems, and they need someone to help with the transition and provide ongoing support.'
❌ 3. He already has a monthly transit pass: He doesn't have a pass; the woman suggests he get one.
❌ 4. He lives in the downtown area: The conversation indicates he's not familiar with this part of the city.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She is new to driving this bus route
- 2. She is familiar with transit payment options
- 3. She works at the transit center part-time
- 4. She regularly shops at the convenience store
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is new to driving this bus route: Nothing suggests she's new to the route; she knows the stops well.
✅ 2. She is familiar with transit payment options: She demonstrates knowledge about fare payment, the no-change policy, and monthly transit passes, including where to buy them and their cost ($85).
❌ 3. She works at the transit center part-time: There's no mention of her working at the transit center.
❌ 4. She regularly shops at the convenience store: The conversation doesn't indicate she shops at the convenience store.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will miss his stop
- 2. The man will exit the bus at Elm Street
- 3. The woman will give the man directions to the transit center
- 4. The woman will offer to drive the man directly to his interview
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will miss his stop: The woman says she'll announce the stop, so this is unlikely.
✅ 2. The man will exit the bus at Elm Street: The conversation ends with the woman saying 'We're approaching Elm Street now, so get ready to exit,' indicating this is the immediate next action.
❌ 3. The woman will give the man directions to the transit center: She already mentioned it's next to the library, but this isn't the immediate next action.
❌ 4. The woman will offer to drive the man directly to his interview: This would be inappropriate and isn't suggested in the conversation.

## Practice 394: Delayed Train Departure | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a train station. The woman works at the information desk, and the man is a passenger who needs assistance.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, I'm trying to get to Vancouver, but I think I might have missed my train. It was supposed to leave at 2:15.
WOMAN: Let me check that for you. Do you have your ticket with you?
MAN: Yes, here it is. I got stuck in traffic on the way here, and then I couldn't find parking. I was supposed to be here 30 minutes early, but I only arrived about 10 minutes ago.
WOMAN: I see. Well, according to our system, that train has been delayed by about 25 minutes due to some track maintenance. So you're actually in luck!
MAN: Oh, that's a relief! So it hasn't left yet?
WOMAN: No, it's now scheduled to depart at 2:40 from Platform 3. You still have about 15 minutes to board.
MAN: That's great news! Um, could you tell me how to get to Platform 3 from here? This is my first time at this station.
WOMAN: Certainly. Just go down those stairs behind you, turn right at the bottom, and follow the signs. Platform 3 is at the far end.

**Q1:** Why is the man concerned at the beginning of the conversation?
- 1. He can't find his ticket.
- 2. He thinks he missed his train.
- 3. He is at the wrong station.
- 4. He forgot his luggage in the car.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He can't find his ticket: The man has his ticket and shows it to the woman when asked.
✅ 2. He thinks he missed his train: The man explicitly states "I'm trying to get to Vancouver, but I think I might have missed my train. It was supposed to leave at 2:15."
❌ 3. He is at the wrong station: There's no mention of him being at the wrong station.
❌ 4. He forgot his luggage in the car: There's no mention of forgotten luggage in the conversation.

**Q2:** What time is the train now scheduled to depart?
- 1. 2:15
- 2. 2:30
- 3. 2:40
- 4. 3:00
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 2:15: This was the original departure time, not the current scheduled time.
❌ 2. 2:30: This is when the woman recommends the man be back at Platform 3, not the departure time.
✅ 3. 2:40: The woman clearly states "it's now scheduled to depart at 2:40 from Platform 3."
❌ 4. 3:00: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is familiar with the train station.
- 2. He arrived 30 minutes before departure.
- 3. He is traveling to Vancouver for the first time.
- 4. He is unfamiliar with the train station layout.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is familiar with the train station: The man states "This is my first time at this station."
❌ 2. He arrived 30 minutes before departure: He says he "was supposed to be here 30 minutes early" but only arrived "about 10 minutes ago."
❌ 3. He is traveling to Vancouver for the first time: The conversation only indicates it's his first time at this station, not Vancouver.
✅ 4. He is unfamiliar with the train station layout: The man asks "could you tell me how to get to Platform 3 from here? This is my first time at this station."

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi again. Sorry to bother you, but I have another question. I'm getting a bit hungry, and I was wondering if there's time to grab something to eat before my train leaves?
WOMAN: Yes, there should be enough time. We have a few options here at the station. There's a coffee shop just past the ticket counters that has sandwiches and pastries. They're pretty quick.
MAN: That sounds good. And will they make an announcement when my train is ready to board?
WOMAN: Yes, they'll announce it about 10 minutes before departure. But just to be safe, I'd recommend being back at Platform 3 by 2:30.
MAN: Perfect. Oh, and one more thing – do you know if there's a dining car on the train to Vancouver?
WOMAN: Yes, there is. They offer light meals and beverages, but the selection is somewhat limited compared to what you can get here at the station.
MAN: I see. In that case, I'll definitely grab something now. Thanks for your help!
WOMAN: You're welcome. Enjoy your trip!

**Q3:** Why does the man return to the information desk in Section 2?
- 1. To ask about food options before his train leaves
- 2. To check if his train is still delayed
- 3. To find out where the restrooms are located
- 4. To report a problem with his ticket
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To ask about food options before his train leaves: He says "I'm getting a bit hungry, and I was wondering if there's time to grab something to eat before my train leaves?"
❌ 2. To check if his train is still delayed: This is not why he returns in the second section.
❌ 3. To find out where the restrooms are located: There's no mention of restrooms in the conversation.
❌ 4. To report a problem with his ticket: He doesn't mention any problems with his ticket.

**Q4:** What does the woman recommend the man do?
- 1. Wait at Platform 3 until the train arrives
- 2. Return to Platform 3 by 2:30
- 3. Buy food on the train instead of at the station
- 4. Listen carefully for all announcements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Wait at Platform 3 until the train arrives: She doesn't tell him to wait there the whole time.
✅ 2. Return to Platform 3 by 2:30: She specifically says "I'd recommend being back at Platform 3 by 2:30."
❌ 3. Buy food on the train instead of at the station: She mentions the train has food but says it's "somewhat limited compared to what you can get here at the station."
❌ 4. Listen carefully for all announcements: While she mentions announcements will be made, she doesn't specifically recommend listening for all of them.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What happened to the train to Vancouver?
- 1. It was canceled due to maintenance issues.
- 2. It left earlier than scheduled.
- 3. It was delayed again due to a mechanical problem.
- 4. It was rerouted to a different platform.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It was canceled due to maintenance issues: The train was delayed, not canceled.
❌ 2. It left earlier than scheduled: The train was delayed, not early.
✅ 3. It was delayed again due to a mechanical problem: The woman states "there's been another delay. There's an issue with the train that was supposed to take you to Vancouver."
❌ 4. It was rerouted to a different platform: There's no mention of the train being rerouted.

**Q7:** How does the man feel about the additional delay?
- 1. Relieved that he can get something to eat
- 2. Worried about missing his appointment
- 3. Angry at the train station staff
- 4. Indifferent because he has plenty of time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Relieved that he can get something to eat: He doesn't express relief about the delay.
✅ 2. Worried about missing his appointment: He says "That's frustrating. I'm supposed to meet someone in Vancouver at 6:00. Do you think I'll make it?" showing his concern.
❌ 3. Angry at the train station staff: He expresses frustration about the situation but not anger at the staff.
❌ 4. Indifferent because he has plenty of time: He's clearly concerned about the timing, saying "That's cutting it close."

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will find another way to get to Vancouver.
- 2. The woman will help the man contact someone in Vancouver.
- 3. The man will charge his phone at the station.
- 4. The train will depart earlier than expected.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will find another way to get to Vancouver: There's no indication he's considering alternative transportation.
✅ 2. The woman will help the man contact someone in Vancouver: The woman offers "Would you like me to help you call your contact in Vancouver" and the man responds "That would be really helpful, actually."
❌ 3. The man will charge his phone at the station: While his phone battery is low, there's no mention of charging it.
❌ 4. The train will depart earlier than expected: The train is already delayed and there's no suggestion it will leave early.

❌ 2. About an hour: The receptionist clearly states "I'd estimate about an hour wait" when the man asks about the waiting time.

❌ 4. He works at the pharmacy next door: There is no mention of the man working at the pharmacy; the pharmacy is just mentioned as being next door to the clinic.

## Practice 396: Transfer Student Curriculum Catch-Up | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a school open house. The woman is a teacher, and the man is a parent of a new student.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, are you Ms. Peterson? My son Jake's new English teacher?
WOMAN: Yes, that's me. It's nice to meet you, Mr...?
MAN: Williams. Tom Williams. Jake just transferred here last week, and he's been a bit anxious about catching up with the curriculum. He mentioned you're studying a novel that the class started a month ago.
WOMAN: That's right. We're halfway through "To Kill a Mockingbird." I understand it can be intimidating to jump in midway.
MAN: Exactly. Jake's always been a strong reader, but he's worried about the upcoming project. He wasn't very clear about the details when I asked him.
WOMAN: I completely understand. The major project is a character analysis presentation due in three weeks. I actually have a project outline I can give you tonight.
MAN: That would be extremely helpful. I'd like to help him get organized at home.
WOMAN: Of course. And please tell Jake he can come see me during lunch period if he has any questions. I'm usually in my classroom on Tuesdays and Thursdays.

**Q1:** Why is the man concerned about his son?
- 1. His son is failing English class.
- 2. His son is having trouble making friends.
- 3. His son is worried about catching up with coursework.
- 4. His son doesn't like the assigned novel.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His son is failing English class: There's no mention of Jake failing.
❌ 2. His son is having trouble making friends: While Jake is described as shy, making friends isn't mentioned as a concern.
✅ 3. His son is worried about catching up with coursework: The father explicitly states "he's been a bit anxious about catching up with the curriculum" and is "worried about the upcoming project."
❌ 4. His son doesn't like the assigned novel: There's no indication Jake dislikes the novel.

**Q2:** What is the upcoming assignment about?
- 1. Writing an essay about the novel
- 2. Creating a character analysis presentation
- 3. Completing a reading comprehension test
- 4. Designing a book cover for the novel
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Writing an essay about the novel: The conversation doesn't mention an essay assignment.
✅ 2. Creating a character analysis presentation: The teacher specifically states "The major project is a character analysis presentation due in three weeks."
❌ 3. Completing a reading comprehension test: No test is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Designing a book cover for the novel: No book cover design is mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is true about Jake?
- 1. He dislikes reading books.
- 2. He is outgoing and makes friends easily.
- 3. He is comfortable discussing literature at home.
- 4. He frequently visits the teacher during lunch.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He dislikes reading books: The father states "Jake's always been a strong reader" and mentions they "read together in the evenings."
❌ 2. He is outgoing and makes friends easily: The father says "Jake's always been a bit shy when it comes to approaching new people."
✅ 3. He is comfortable discussing literature at home: The father explicitly states "He actually loves discussing books at home."
❌ 4. He frequently visits the teacher during lunch: The teacher invites Jake to visit during lunch, but there's no indication he has done this yet.

**Q4:** What does the teacher plan to do for Jake?
- 1. Give him a different assignment
- 2. Allow him extra time to complete the project
- 3. Provide him with additional reading materials
- 4. Place him with helpful students for group work
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Give him a different assignment: She doesn't mention giving him a different assignment.
❌ 2. Allow him extra time to complete the project: No extension is mentioned.
❌ 3. Provide him with additional reading materials: She gives his father a project outline but doesn't mention additional reading materials.
✅ 4. Place him with helpful students for group work: She explicitly states "Since he's new, I wanted to place him with students who could help him catch up. I have a few reliable students in mind."

**Q5:** How does the teacher feel about Jake?
- 1. Concerned about his academic abilities
- 2. Impressed by his classroom participation
- 3. Hopeful about his potential contributions
- 4. Frustrated by his lack of preparation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Concerned about his academic abilities: She doesn't express concern about his abilities.
❌ 2. Impressed by his classroom participation: She actually notes that he's "quiet in class."
✅ 3. Hopeful about his potential contributions: She states "sometimes the quietest students have the most insightful observations when they feel comfortable enough to share."
❌ 4. Frustrated by his lack of preparation: She shows no frustration, only understanding of his situation.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** Where will the field trip take place?
- 1. A local library
- 2. A courthouse
- 3. A museum
- 4. A theater
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A local library: The field trip is not to a library.
✅ 2. A courthouse: The teacher specifically states "we're planning to visit the local courthouse next month."
❌ 3. A museum: No museum is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A theater: No theater is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She is new to the school.
- 2. She is concerned about Jake's performance.
- 3. She tracks student allergies for field trips.
- 4. She provides lunch during field trips.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is new to the school: Nothing indicates the teacher is new to the school.
❌ 2. She is concerned about Jake's performance: She shows understanding about his situation but doesn't express concern about his performance.
✅ 3. She tracks student allergies for field trips: She explicitly states "I make note of all allergies before field trips. I'll ensure the chaperones are aware."
❌ 4. She provides lunch during field trips: She clearly states "Students need to bring their own lunch that day."

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will help Jake finish reading the novel.
- 2. The teacher will change Jake's group assignment.
- 3. Jake will visit the teacher during lunch tomorrow.
- 4. The man will pay the field trip fee and return the form.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The man will help Jake finish reading the novel: The father agrees to help Jake read the novel when the teacher suggests it as "the first priority" - "I can definitely do that. We usually read together in the evenings anyway."
❌ 2. The teacher will change Jake's group assignment: There's no indication she plans to change any assignment.
❌ 3. Jake will visit the teacher during lunch tomorrow: While this is possible, the teacher mentioned she's available Tuesdays and Thursdays, but we don't know what day it currently is.
❌ 4. The man will pay the field trip fee and return the form: The permission forms haven't been distributed yet - the teacher says "I was planning to distribute those next week."

- 4. A racing bike
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A mountain bike: The man never mentions wanting a mountain bike, and the woman doesn't recommend this type.
❌ 2. A comfort cruiser: While the woman recommends either comfort cruisers or hybrid bikes, the man specifically chooses the hybrid when he says "the hybrid sounds good."
✅ 3. A hybrid bike: The man clearly states "the hybrid sounds good" after the woman describes the options, and proceeds to rent this type.
❌ 4. A racing bike: Racing bikes are never mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** How much is the security deposit for the bike rental?
- 1. $$45
- 2. $$60

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $45: This is the weekend rate for renting the hybrid bike, not the security deposit.
❌ 2. $60: This is the total cost for renting the bike for three days (weekend plus Monday), not the security deposit.
✅ 3. $100: The woman clearly states "we require a $100 security deposit, which you'll get back when you return the bike in good condition."

$4d

❌ 2. 8 kilometers: This distance is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. 10 kilometers: This distance is not mentioned in the conversation.

$4e

## Practice 398: Roommate Chore Split | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two roommates discussing household responsibilities.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What happened to Sarah?
- 1. She broke her leg in an accident.
- 2. She injured her ankle and needs to rest it.
- 3. She had surgery on her foot.
- 4. She twisted her knee while exercising.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She broke her leg in an accident: The conversation mentions a sprained ankle, not a broken leg.
✅ 2. She injured her ankle and needs to rest it: The man (Sarah) says "It's a bit better, thanks for asking. The doctor said it's just a minor sprain, but I should stay off it as much as possible for the next week or so."
❌ 2. She injured her ankle and needs to rest it: Sarah (the man in the conversation) mentions having a sprained ankle that needs rest for about a week.
❌ 3. She had surgery on her foot: There is no mention of any surgery in the conversation.
❌ 4. She twisted her knee while exercising: The injury is to the ankle, not the knee, and there's no mention of how it happened.

**Q2:** How do the roommates usually handle grocery shopping?
- 1. They take turns going to different stores.
- 2. They always go shopping together.
- 3. Sarah typically does most of the grocery shopping.
- 4. They order groceries online every week.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They take turns going to different stores: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. They always go shopping together: The conversation doesn't indicate they shop together.
✅ 3. Sarah typically does most of the grocery shopping: The man (Sarah) says "I normally go to Metro on Thursdays" and the woman acknowledges this by saying "I know you usually do most of that stuff" when referring to grocery shopping and cooking.
❌ 4. They order groceries online every week: Online grocery ordering is not mentioned as their usual practice.

**Q3:** What does Sarah suggest to help with cooking while she recovers?
- 1. ordering takeout for the entire week
- 2. asking friends to bring meals over
- 3. doing meal preparation while seated
- 4. eating only simple sandwiches and salads
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. ordering takeout for the entire week: Takeout is not mentioned as a solution.
❌ 2. asking friends to bring meals over: There is no mention of asking friends to help with meals.
✅ 3. doing meal preparation while seated: Sarah (the man) suggests "I can still help with meal planning and maybe do some prep work while sitting down. You know, chopping vegetables or mixing things. I just can't stand for long periods."
❌ 4. eating only simple sandwiches and salads: While they discuss simple meals, specifically sandwiches and salads are not mentioned.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about cooking?
- 1. She feels confident about trying new recipes.
- 2. She believes she needs to improve her skills.
- 3. She thinks cooking is too time-consuming.
- 4. She prefers to eat at restaurants instead.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She feels confident about trying new recipes: The woman expresses a lack of confidence in her cooking abilities.
✅ 2. She believes she needs to improve her skills: The woman admits "I'm not nearly as good in the kitchen as you are" and references a "disaster" when she tried to make pasta, indicating she thinks her cooking skills need improvement.
❌ 3. She thinks cooking is too time-consuming: Time constraints are not mentioned as a concern.
❌ 4. She prefers to eat at restaurants instead: There is no mention of restaurant preferences.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Take Sarah to see the doctor again.
- 2. Research meal delivery services and go shopping.
- 3. Look for a new grocery store closer to home.
- 4. Ask other friends for cooking advice.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Take Sarah to see the doctor again: There's no indication Sarah needs another doctor visit.
✅ 2. Research meal delivery services and go shopping: The woman explicitly states at the end: "I'll go grocery shopping tomorrow and look into that meal delivery service tonight."
❌ 3. Look for a new grocery store closer to home: Finding a closer store is not discussed.
❌ 4. Ask other friends for cooking advice: Seeking advice from other friends is not mentioned.

## Practice 399: Granville Market Outing | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends planning a weekend activity.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What time do the friends plan to meet on Saturday?
- 1. 8 AM when the market opens
- 2. 10 AM after having coffee
- 3. later in the day to avoid crowds
- 4. Friday evening to confirm details
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 8 AM when the market opens: The woman specifically says this is "a bit early for a Saturday" and suggests a later time.
✅ 2. 10 AM after having coffee: The woman suggests "How about we meet around 10? That way we can grab coffee first" and the man agrees this is "perfect."
❌ 3. later in the day to avoid crowds: They actually want to meet at 10 AM to "beat the worst crowds" not later in the day.
❌ 4. Friday evening to confirm details: The man says he'll text Friday evening to confirm details, but this is not when they plan to meet for the market visit.

**Q2:** Why does the woman suggest taking the SkyTrain?
- 1. It's faster than driving downtown.
- 2. The market is located at the train station.
- 3. They can avoid parking problems and carrying bags.
- 4. It's cheaper than taking a taxi.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's faster than driving downtown: The conversation doesn't mention speed comparison between driving and taking the SkyTrain.
❌ 2. The market is located at the train station: The woman says the station is a five-minute walk from the market, not at the market.
✅ 3. They can avoid parking problems and carrying bags: The woman specifically says "Parking downtown can be a nightmare" and mentions they "won't have to worry about finding parking or carrying heavy bags back to the car."
❌ 4. It's cheaper than taking a taxi: While they mention taking a cab as an option for the return journey, cost comparison isn't given as a reason for taking the SkyTrain.

**Q3:** What does the man hope to buy for his sister?
- 1. fresh vegetables and homemade jam
- 2. artisan bread from a local baker
- 3. handcrafted items like pottery or honey
- 4. jewelry from his neighbor's recommendation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. fresh vegetables and homemade jam: These are items the woman wants to buy for herself, not what the man wants for his sister.
❌ 2. artisan bread from a local baker: The man mentions wanting to buy sourdough bread for himself, not as a gift for his sister.
✅ 3. handcrafted items like pottery or honey: The man specifically says "A nice piece of pottery or maybe some local honey would be perfect" when discussing gift options for his sister.
❌ 4. jewelry from his neighbor's recommendation: While jewelry is mentioned as something available at the market, the man's neighbor only recommended the market's bread, not jewelry.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about her current grocery store?
- 1. The prices are too expensive for her budget.
- 2. The quality of fresh produce is poor.
- 3. The location is inconvenient to reach.
- 4. The selection of items is too limited.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The prices are too expensive for her budget: The woman doesn't mention anything about prices at her grocery store.
✅ 2. The quality of fresh produce is poor: The woman specifically states "My grocery store produce has been pretty disappointing lately" when explaining why she wants to get fresh vegetables at the market.
❌ 3. The location is inconvenient to reach: There's no mention of the grocery store's location or accessibility in the conversation.
❌ 4. The selection of items is too limited: The woman doesn't comment on the variety or selection of items at her grocery store.

**Q5:** What will most likely happen after they visit the market?
- 1. They will definitely have lunch at a café on Robson Street.
- 2. They will go shopping for more gifts downtown.
- 3. They will decide based on how they feel and what they've bought.
- 4. They will take a taxi home with their heavy purchases.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They will definitely have lunch at a café on Robson Street: The woman says "If we're having fun, we could grab lunch" at the café, not that they will definitely do this.
❌ 2. They will go shopping for more gifts downtown: There's no mention of additional shopping for gifts after the market visit.
✅ 3. They will decide based on how they feel and what they've bought: The woman explicitly says "Let's see how it goes!" and presents options based on whether they're having fun or are tired with many purchases.
❌ 4. They will take a taxi home with their heavy purchases: While taking a taxi is mentioned as a possibility ("if we buy a lot of stuff, we can always take a cab back"), it's not presented as their definite plan.

## Practice 400: Garden Plant Swap | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors discussing a community event.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the event being discussed?
- 1. To sell homegrown vegetables
- 2. To exchange garden items with others
- 3. To teach gardening techniques
- 4. To raise money for the community center
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To sell homegrown vegetables: The man explicitly states "There's no money involved, just straight swapping."
✅ 2. To exchange garden items with others: The man explains that people "bring your extra seedlings, seeds, or garden tools you don't need anymore, and you set up at a little table. Then everyone walks around and makes trades."
❌ 3. To teach gardening techniques: There is no mention of teaching or learning gardening techniques at the event.
❌ 4. To raise money for the community center: The event doesn't involve money, as explicitly stated in the conversation.

**Q2:** Why is the woman interested in attending the event?
- 1. She wants to meet new neighbors
- 2. She needs to buy gardening tools
- 3. She has excess plants she doesn't want to waste
- 4. She's writing an article about community events
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to meet new neighbors: The woman never mentions wanting to meet new neighbors as her reason for attending.
❌ 2. She needs to buy gardening tools: The woman doesn't mention needing gardening tools, and the event doesn't involve buying items with money.
✅ 3. She has excess plants she doesn't want to waste: The woman specifically states, "I'm thinking of going because I have so many extra tomato seedlings this year. My garden is completely full, and I hate to just throw them away."
❌ 4. She's writing an article about community events: There's no mention of the woman writing an article or being involved in journalism.

**Q3:** What problem did the man have with his garden last year?
- 1. His plants didn't grow well
- 2. He produced too many zucchinis
- 3. His garden was damaged by pests
- 4. He didn't have enough space
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His plants didn't grow well: The man indicates his plants grew very well, producing too much rather than too little.
✅ 2. He produced too many zucchinis: The man states, "Last year we couldn't keep up with all the zucchinis we harvested. We were practically begging neighbors to take them!"
❌ 3. His garden was damaged by pests: There is no mention of pests or garden damage in the conversation.
❌ 4. He didn't have enough space: While space might be implied as an issue, the explicit problem mentioned was overproduction of zucchinis, not lack of space.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about the man?
- 1. He is too busy to attend community events
- 2. He is not very knowledgeable about gardening
- 3. He is helpful and generous with his plants
- 4. He is trying to sell his extra seedlings
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is too busy to attend community events: The man is planning to attend the event, so this is incorrect.
❌ 2. He is not very knowledgeable about gardening: The man demonstrates knowledge about gardening by growing basil seedlings under grow lights and discussing his zucchini plants.
✅ 3. He is helpful and generous with his plants: The man offers to bring basil seedlings specifically for the woman when he learns she needs them, showing helpfulness and generosity.
❌ 4. He is trying to sell his extra seedlings: The man explains that the event involves no money, just trading, and he offers his seedlings freely to the woman.

**Q5:** What will the man and woman most likely do after the swap meet?
- 1. Visit each other's gardens
- 2. Go to a café together
- 3. Plant their new seedlings
- 4. Attend another community event
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit each other's gardens: There is no mention of them planning to visit each other's gardens.
✅ 2. Go to a café together: The woman suggests, "And maybe afterward we could grab coffee at that new place on Lougheed Highway?" and the man agrees, adding he wants to try their breakfast sandwiches.
❌ 3. Plant their new seedlings: While they will get new plants, there's no specific mention of planting them together after the event.
❌ 4. Attend another community event: There is no mention of another community event they plan to attend.

## Practice 401: Victoria Day Cottage | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends discussing a holiday cottage rental.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the friends planning?
- 1. A weekend trip to Victoria
- 2. A holiday weekend at a cottage
- 3. A camping trip in Algonquin Park
- 4. A day trip to Lake Muskoka
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A weekend trip to Victoria: They mention Victoria Day weekend, which is a Canadian holiday, not a trip to a place called Victoria.
✅ 2. A holiday weekend at a cottage: The entire conversation revolves around renting a cottage for the Victoria Day weekend, with specific dates mentioned (May 18th to 21st).
❌ 3. A camping trip in Algonquin Park: While they discuss a cottage near Algonquin Park, they're not planning a camping trip in the park itself.
❌ 4. A day trip to Lake Muskoka: They're planning a multi-day stay (the Victoria Day weekend), not just a day trip, and they ultimately choose the Algonquin cottage, not the one at Lake Muskoka.

**Q2:** What is the main drawback of the Algonquin cottage?
- 1. It doesn't allow pets
- 2. It's too expensive
- 3. It's not directly on the water
- 4. It doesn't have enough bedrooms
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It doesn't allow pets: The conversation specifically mentions that the Algonquin cottage allows dogs, which is seen as a positive feature.
❌ 2. It's too expensive: The Algonquin cottage is actually cheaper at $250 per night compared to the Muskoka cottage at $375 per night.
✅ 3. It's not directly on the water: The man points out that the Algonquin cottage is 'about a 20-minute walk to the lake' and he was 'hoping for something right on the water,' which is identified as the main drawback.
❌ 4. It doesn't have enough bedrooms: While the Algonquin cottage has fewer bedrooms than the Muskoka one, this is presented as a manageable issue since there's a pull-out couch available.

**Q3:** Why do the friends decide against the Muskoka cottage?
- 1. It has poor reviews
- 2. It's too far from activities
- 3. It doesn't have enough bedrooms
- 4. It costs significantly more
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It has poor reviews: There's no mention of poor reviews for the Muskoka cottage in the conversation.
❌ 2. It's too far from activities: The conversation doesn't discuss the distance to activities for either cottage.
❌ 3. It doesn't have enough bedrooms: The Muskoka cottage actually has more bedrooms (three) than the Algonquin cottage (two).
✅ 4. It costs significantly more: The Muskoka cottage costs $375 per night while the Algonquin cottage is only $250 per night. The man specifically mentions the 'price difference - that's over $300 saved for the weekend' as a key factor in their decision.

**Q4:** What does the woman think about the walk to the lake?
- 1. It's too long and inconvenient
- 2. It's through a scenic forest area
- 3. It's dangerous at night
- 4. It requires special hiking equipment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too long and inconvenient: The woman doesn't express that the walk is too long or inconvenient.
✅ 2. It's through a scenic forest area: The woman mentions that 'the reviews say the walking trail to the lake is really beautiful - goes through a pine forest,' indicating she views this positively.
❌ 3. It's dangerous at night: There's no mention of any danger or nighttime concerns about the walk in the conversation.
❌ 4. It requires special hiking equipment: The conversation doesn't mention any need for special equipment for the walk.

**Q5:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will look for more cottage options
- 2. The man will contact Dave and Jen about the plans
- 3. The woman will make the reservation for the Algonquin cottage
- 4. They will reconsider the Muskoka cottage option
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will look for more cottage options: They've already decided on the Algonquin cottage, so they won't be looking for more options.
❌ 2. The man will contact Dave and Jen about the plans: The woman specifically says 'I'll book it tonight and let Dave and Jen know,' indicating she'll be the one to contact them.
✅ 3. The woman will make the reservation for the Algonquin cottage: The woman clearly states 'I'll book it tonight' after they decide on the Algonquin cottage.
❌ 4. They will reconsider the Muskoka cottage option: They've already made their decision in favor of the Algonquin cottage, with the man saying 'I think that makes the most sense.'

## Practice 402: Building Amenities Advice | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors in an apartment building.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** How long has the woman lived in the building?
- 1. three years
- 2. ten months
- 3. since last weekend
- 4. since the building opened
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. three years: The woman explicitly states 'I've been here for three years' when offering restaurant recommendations.
❌ 2. ten months: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. since last weekend: This describes when the man moved in, not the woman.
❌ 4. since the building opened: There is no mention of when the building opened or that the woman has lived there since that time.

**Q2:** What does the man appreciate about the apartment building?
- 1. the quiet neighbors
- 2. the fast elevators
- 3. the proximity to public transportation
- 4. the size of his apartment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. the quiet neighbors: The man doesn't mention anything about quiet neighbors.
❌ 2. the fast elevators: The woman actually mentions that 'the elevator can be slow during rush hour.'
✅ 3. the proximity to public transportation: The man specifically says 'The location is perfect - just a ten-minute walk to Bloor Station,' showing he appreciates the proximity to public transit.
❌ 4. the size of his apartment: The man doesn't comment on the size of his apartment.

**Q3:** What advice does the woman give about the laundry room?
- 1. to avoid using it altogether
- 2. to use it on weekday evenings
- 3. to bring extra detergent
- 4. to report any broken machines
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. to avoid using it altogether: The woman doesn't suggest avoiding the laundry room completely.
✅ 2. to use it on weekday evenings: The woman specifically says 'I usually do mine on Tuesday or Wednesday nights when it's quieter' and advises against weekends.
❌ 3. to bring extra detergent: The woman advises to 'bring loonies' (Canadian one-dollar coins) because 'the change machine is perpetually out of order.'
❌ 4. to report any broken machines: The woman doesn't mention reporting broken machines.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about the man?
- 1. He seems disorganized and unprepared.
- 2. He might be a good addition to the building community.
- 3. He asks too many questions about the building.
- 4. He should have researched the area before moving in.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He seems disorganized and unprepared: The woman shows no signs of thinking this about the man.
✅ 2. He might be a good addition to the building community: The woman is friendly, invites him to community events, offers recommendations, and agrees to meet for coffee - all suggesting she sees him as a welcome addition to the community.
❌ 3. He asks too many questions about the building: The woman responds helpfully to all his questions without any sign of annoyance.
❌ 4. He should have researched the area before moving in: The woman never implies this; she's happy to provide information.

**Q5:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will attend the rooftop barbecue.
- 2. The woman will help the man unpack his boxes.
- 3. The man and woman will meet for coffee after he settles in.
- 4. The man will move to a different apartment building.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will attend the rooftop barbecue: While this might happen eventually, it's not the next immediate step mentioned.
❌ 2. The woman will help the man unpack his boxes: There's no indication the woman offered to help with unpacking.
✅ 3. The man and woman will meet for coffee after he settles in: The man specifically suggests 'Maybe once I'm properly unpacked, we could grab coffee sometime?' and the woman responds positively with 'That would be lovely!'
❌ 4. The man will move to a different apartment building: There's no indication the man is unhappy or planning to move again.

## Practice 403: Neighbour Furniture Sale | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors in an apartment building.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man moving out of his apartment?
- 1. His rent has increased too much.
- 2. He wants to be closer to his workplace.
- 3. He needs a larger apartment.
- 4. His landlord asked him to leave.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His rent has increased too much: The man doesn't mention rent increases as a reason for moving.
✅ 2. He wants to be closer to his workplace: The man specifically states he found a place closer to his new job downtown because 'The commute from Riverside has been killing me - almost an hour each way on the 401.'
❌ 3. He needs a larger apartment: The man actually mentions his new place is 'much smaller' than his current one.
❌ 4. His landlord asked him to leave: The man mentions his 'lease is up' but doesn't say anything about being asked to leave by the landlord.

**Q2:** What furniture items is the man selling?
- 1. A bed, a sofa, and a bookshelf.
- 2. A sectional sofa, a dining set, and a bookshelf.
- 3. Kitchen appliances, lamps, and a dining table.
- 4. A bookshelf, lamps, and a bed.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A bed, a sofa, and a bookshelf: The man doesn't mention selling his bed; in fact, he says 'I'm sleeping on the bed until the end.'
✅ 2. A sectional sofa, a dining set, and a bookshelf: The man specifically states 'I've got that sectional sofa, the dining table with four chairs, and my bookshelf that I need to get rid of.'
❌ 3. Kitchen appliances, lamps, and a dining table: While he mentions having 'kitchen stuff and lamps' to sell as well, these are additional items beyond the main furniture pieces he first mentions.
❌ 4. A bookshelf, lamps, and a bed: The man is not selling his bed, and while he is selling a bookshelf and lamps, this doesn't include the sectional sofa and dining set he mentioned.

**Q3:** How much is the man asking for the dining table and chairs?
- 1. $$75
- 2. $$95
- 3. $$120
- 4. $$150
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $75: This is the price the man is asking for the bookshelf, not the dining set.
❌ 2. $95: This price is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.
✅ 3. $120: The man clearly states 'I'm asking $120 for the table and chairs together.'
❌ 4. $150: This price is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the woman think about the furniture prices?
- 1. They are too expensive.
- 2. They are fair.
- 3. She wants to negotiate lower prices.
- 4. She needs to check with her sister first.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are too expensive: The woman doesn't express any concern about the prices being too high.
✅ 2. They are fair: When the man mentions the bookshelf price of $75, the woman responds with 'That sounds reasonable,' indicating she thinks the price is fair.
❌ 3. She wants to negotiate lower prices: There's no indication in the conversation that the woman wants to negotiate the prices down.
❌ 4. She needs to check with her sister first: While she's bringing her sister to look at the furniture, she already expresses that the price sounds reasonable without needing to consult her sister about it.

**Q5:** What will likely happen tomorrow evening?
- 1. The man will help the woman move the furniture.
- 2. The woman's sister will inspect the furniture.
- 3. The man will post the items on Kijiji.
- 4. The woman will help the man pack his belongings.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will help the woman move the furniture: There's no mention of moving furniture tomorrow evening.
✅ 2. The woman's sister will inspect the furniture: The woman asks 'would you mind if I brought my sister over to take a look? Maybe tomorrow evening around 7:00?' and the man agrees.
❌ 3. The man will post the items on Kijiji: The man mentions he 'was actually planning to post everything on Kijiji this weekend,' but the woman suggests her sister visit before he does that.
❌ 4. The woman will help the man pack his belongings: While the man mentions he'll 'be home packing anyway,' there's no indication the woman will be helping him pack.

## Practice 404: Dog Walking Confusion | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors about a dog walking schedule.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the man miss walking Baxter?
- 1. He was busy with his new work project.
- 2. He forgot about the schedule change.
- 3. He thought it was Sarah's day to walk the dog.
- 4. He had a meeting on Bloor Street.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He was busy with his new work project: While his work project is mentioned as the reason for changing the schedule, it's not why he missed walking Baxter.
✅ 2. He forgot about the schedule change: Mark explicitly says "I completely forgot about that conversation" referring to when they changed the schedule.
❌ 3. He thought it was Sarah's day to walk the dog: He thought he was supposed to walk Baxter on Tuesdays and Thursdays, not realizing the schedule had changed.
❌ 4. He had a meeting on Bloor Street: Sarah had the meeting on Bloor Street, not Mark.

**Q2:** How did Sarah find out that Baxter needed a walk?
- 1. Baxter called her on the phone.
- 2. Mark texted her about his mistake.
- 3. A neighbor contacted her about the dog.
- 4. She came home and found Baxter waiting.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Baxter called her on the phone: Dogs cannot make phone calls.
❌ 2. Mark texted her about his mistake: Mark didn't realize his mistake until Sarah mentioned it.
✅ 3. A neighbor contacted her about the dog: The woman (Sarah) says "Mrs. Peterson from across the street noticed him looking sad in the window and texted me."
❌ 4. She came home and found Baxter waiting: Sarah came home early because Mrs. Peterson texted her, not because she found Baxter waiting.

**Q3:** What solution do Mark and Sarah agree on to prevent future mix-ups?
- 1. Mark will set phone alerts and Sarah will make a chart.
- 2. They will return to their original schedule.
- 3. They will hire Mrs. Peterson to help with dog walking.
- 4. Mark will take full responsibility for the dog walking.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Mark will set phone alerts and Sarah will make a chart: Mark says "I could put it in my phone calendar with alerts" and Sarah responds "I'll make a little chart for the fridge too, just to be safe."
❌ 2. They will return to their original schedule: They don't mention returning to the original schedule.
❌ 3. They will hire Mrs. Peterson to help with dog walking: Mrs. Peterson only helped by notifying Sarah; there's no mention of hiring her.
❌ 4. Mark will take full responsibility for the dog walking: They continue to share the responsibility, not transfer it all to Mark.

**Q4:** Based on the conversation, how does Sarah feel about the situation?
- 1. She is angry that Mark was irresponsible.
- 2. She is worried about Baxter's health.
- 3. She is understanding about the mistake.
- 4. She is concerned about her meeting schedule.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is angry that Mark was irresponsible: Sarah doesn't express anger in the conversation.
❌ 2. She is worried about Baxter's health: Sarah mentions Baxter was "fine, just a bit restless" but shows no health concerns.
✅ 3. She is understanding about the mistake: Sarah says "No worries at all. These things happen" showing she's understanding about Mark's mistake.
❌ 4. She is concerned about her meeting schedule: While she mentions coming home early from a meeting, she doesn't express concern about her schedule.

**Q5:** What is likely to happen next Wednesday?
- 1. Sarah will take Baxter to the Don Valley trail.
- 2. Mark will take Baxter to a veterinary appointment.
- 3. They will have another discussion about the schedule.
- 4. Mrs. Peterson will help with walking Baxter.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Sarah will take Baxter to the Don Valley trail: The Don Valley trail is mentioned for the weekend, not Wednesday.
✅ 2. Mark will take Baxter to a veterinary appointment: Sarah reminds Mark "don't forget he has that vet appointment next Wednesday at 4:30 at Dr. Klein's office" and Mark confirms "I'll definitely be there to take him."
❌ 3. They will have another discussion about the schedule: There's no mention of planning another discussion.
❌ 4. Mrs. Peterson will help with walking Baxter: Mrs. Peterson only helped by notifying Sarah; there's no mention of her walking Baxter.


---

## Practice 405: Book Club Voting | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends who are members of a book club.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the two friends discussing?
- 1. planning a trip to Vancouver
- 2. selecting a book for their club
- 3. organizing a dinner party
- 4. reviewing a book they've read
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. planning a trip to Vancouver: They mention Vancouver only as the setting of one book and the man's past trip.
✅ 2. selecting a book for their club: The entire conversation revolves around choosing which book to vote for at their book club.
❌ 3. organizing a dinner party: They plan to have dinner before their meeting, but this is not the main topic.
❌ 4. reviewing a book they've read: They briefly mention a previous book but aren't reviewing it in detail.

**Q2:** Why was the man initially interested in the mystery novel?
- 1. He enjoys mystery novels more than other genres.
- 2. He has personal experience with the setting.
- 3. He heard positive reviews from colleagues.
- 4. He wants to learn about Vancouver's history.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He enjoys mystery novels more than other genres.: The man doesn't mention preferring mystery novels over other genres.
✅ 2. He has personal experience with the setting.: The man says "I walked through Stanley Park when I visited Vancouver last summer, so I thought it would be fun to read something set there."
❌ 3. He heard positive reviews from colleagues.: He doesn't mention any reviews about the mystery novel.
❌ 4. He wants to learn about Vancouver's history.: He doesn't express interest in learning about Vancouver's history.

**Q3:** What did the friends think about their previous book club selection?
- 1. It was too complex for group discussion.
- 2. It was entertaining but unrealistic.
- 3. It was important but emotionally difficult.
- 4. It was historically accurate but boring.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It was too complex for group discussion.: They don't mention the previous book being too complex.
❌ 2. It was entertaining but unrealistic.: They don't describe the previous book as entertaining or unrealistic.
✅ 3. It was important but emotionally difficult.: The woman says the climate change book "was important but pretty depressing" and the man adds "I couldn't sleep for days after reading those chapters about the melting ice caps."
❌ 4. It was historically accurate but boring.: They don't describe the previous book as historically accurate or boring.

**Q4:** How does the woman likely feel about science fiction books?
- 1. She strongly dislikes them.
- 2. She prefers them over all other genres.
- 3. She's concerned about their suitability for group discussion.
- 4. She finds them more educational than historical fiction.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She strongly dislikes them.: The woman doesn't express strong dislike for science fiction.
❌ 2. She prefers them over all other genres.: She initially favors historical fiction, not science fiction.
✅ 3. She's concerned about their suitability for group discussion.: She says "I'm not sure everyone in the group would enjoy something that complex" and mentions that the last science fiction book "split the group pretty evenly between those who loved it and those who couldn't finish it."
❌ 4. She finds them more educational than historical fiction.: She actually expresses appreciation for historical fiction's educational value, saying "I love learning about Canadian history through stories."

**Q5:** What will the friends probably do before their next book club meeting?
- 1. Visit Stanley Park together.
- 2. Have dinner at a Thai restaurant.
- 3. Read multiple books to compare them.
- 4. Meet at Melissa's house for coffee.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit Stanley Park together.: There's no mention of plans to visit Stanley Park.
✅ 2. Have dinner at a Thai restaurant.: The man suggests "Should we grab dinner beforehand? There's that new Thai place just down the street" and the woman responds "Perfect! Let's plan on meeting there around 6:00."
❌ 3. Read multiple books to compare them.: They're only voting for and planning to read one book.
❌ 4. Meet at Melissa's house for coffee.: They specifically mention the meeting is at The Coffee Bean, not at Melissa's house.

## Practice 406: Last-Minute Potluck Prep | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two colleagues at an office.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why are the speakers concerned at the beginning of the conversation?
- 1. They forgot to prepare food for an office event.
- 2. They missed an important meeting.
- 3. They need to reschedule their lunch plans.
- 4. They received a last-minute assignment.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. They forgot to prepare food for an office event: Both speakers express concern about forgetting the potluck and not having prepared anything to bring.
❌ 2. They missed an important meeting: There's no mention of missing a meeting.
❌ 3. They need to reschedule their lunch plans: The man mentions changing his lunch plans, but this isn't the main concern.
❌ 4. They received a last-minute assignment: There's no mention of a work assignment.

**Q2:** What was the man originally planning to do for lunch the next day?
- 1. Eat at his desk.
- 2. Visit a sandwich shop.
- 3. Join colleagues at a restaurant.
- 4. Bring food from home.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Eat at his desk: This isn't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Visit a sandwich shop: The man specifically says "I was planning to go to that new sandwich place on Bloor Street for lunch tomorrow."
❌ 3. Join colleagues at a restaurant: He mentions going to a sandwich place but doesn't say anything about going with colleagues.
❌ 4. Bring food from home: There's no mention of him planning to bring lunch from home.

**Q3:** What special consideration do they need to make when choosing food?
- 1. Vegetarian options must be available.
- 2. The food must be homemade.
- 3. Items should be nut-free.
- 4. Everything must be gluten-free.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Vegetarian options must be available: The conversation doesn't mention any requirement for vegetarian options.
❌ 2. The food must be homemade: The speakers are actually planning to buy food rather than make it themselves.
✅ 3. Items should be nut-free: The woman specifically mentions Jennifer from HR has a severe nut allergy, and they agree to avoid nuts, with the man saying "I'll make sure to ask the bakery for nut-free options."
❌ 4. Everything must be gluten-free: There is no mention of gluten allergies in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about the man's suggestion to buy desserts?
- 1. It's too expensive.
- 2. It's a convenient solution.
- 3. It's not appropriate for the event.
- 4. It's not enough food.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too expensive: The woman doesn't express any concern about cost.
✅ 2. It's a convenient solution: The woman responds "That's actually a brilliant idea. I was stressing about having to cook something tonight after my yoga class," showing she appreciates the convenience of the suggestion.
❌ 3. It's not appropriate for the event: On the contrary, she thinks it's a "brilliant idea."
❌ 4. It's not enough food: She doesn't express concern about quantity and even suggests bringing just drinks or chips herself.

**Q5:** What will the speakers likely do differently for the next office potluck?
- 1. Prepare homemade dishes instead of store-bought items.
- 2. Coordinate their food choices with other colleagues.
- 3. Set calendar reminders to avoid last-minute preparation.
- 4. Volunteer to organize the entire event themselves.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Prepare homemade dishes instead of store-bought items: They don't suggest making homemade dishes next time.
❌ 2. Coordinate their food choices with other colleagues: They don't mention coordinating with others for future events.
✅ 3. Set calendar reminders to avoid last-minute preparation: The woman specifically says "And next time, let's put a reminder in our calendars so we're not scrambling at the last minute."
❌ 4. Volunteer to organize the entire event themselves: There's no suggestion they want to organize the next potluck.

## Practice 407: Building Water Shutoff | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors in an apartment building.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main issue being discussed?
- 1. Noise complaints in the building
- 2. Upcoming water shutdown for maintenance
- 3. Problems with the building's Wi-Fi
- 4. Renovations in the lobby area
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Noise complaints in the building: While noise is briefly mentioned regarding past work, it's not the main topic.
✅ 2. Upcoming water shutdown for maintenance: The entire conversation revolves around the planned pipe replacement that will cause water to be shut off for three days.
❌ 3. Problems with the building's Wi-Fi: Wi-Fi is only mentioned in relation to the café, not the building.
❌ 4. Renovations in the lobby area: The lobby is only mentioned as where the notice was posted, not as being renovated.

**Q2:** Why is the woman concerned about the maintenance work?
- 1. She has guests coming to visit
- 2. She needs to do laundry next week
- 3. She works from home and needs water
- 4. She is worried about potential damage to her apartment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has guests coming to visit: No guests are mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. She needs to do laundry next week: While laundry is mentioned as something to avoid, this isn't her specific concern.
✅ 3. She works from home and needs water: The woman explicitly states, "I work from home, and I need to be able to use the bathroom and make coffee throughout the day."
❌ 4. She is worried about potential damage to her apartment: No concerns about damage to her apartment are mentioned.

**Q3:** What solution does the man suggest for the woman's problem?
- 1. Taking time off work
- 2. Working from a café
- 3. Staying with a friend
- 4. Complaining to management
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Taking time off work: The man never suggests the woman should take time off work.
❌ 2. Working from a café: The man specifically suggests "maybe working from a coffee shop those days" and mentions "that new place on Bloor Street" (the Maple Brew Café).
✅ 3. Staying with a friend: The man doesn't suggest the woman should stay with a friend during the maintenance period.
❌ 4. Complaining to management: While the woman mentions visiting the management office later, this is her idea, not the man's suggestion.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about the Maple Brew Café?
- 1. It's too expensive for regular visits
- 2. It might be a suitable temporary workspace
- 3. It's too far from her apartment
- 4. It will be too noisy for video calls
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too expensive for regular visits: No mention of the café's prices appears in the conversation.
✅ 2. It might be a suitable temporary workspace: The woman responds positively to the suggestion of working there, saying "I guess I could give it a try on Monday" after asking about its Wi-Fi reliability.
❌ 3. It's too far from her apartment: Distance to the café is not discussed in the conversation.
❌ 4. It will be too noisy for video calls: The man actually mentions it "wasn't too crowded," suggesting noise might not be an issue.

**Q5:** What will the woman likely do next?
- 1. Ask for compensation from the building management
- 2. Seek more information about the maintenance work
- 3. Help organize other affected residents
- 4. Look for a different apartment to rent
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Ask for compensation from the building management: There's no indication in the conversation that the woman plans to ask for compensation.
✅ 2. Seek more information about the maintenance work: The woman explicitly states, "I think I'll stop by the management office later today to see if they have any more details."
❌ 3. Help organize other affected residents: The woman doesn't mention organizing other residents in the conversation.
❌ 4. Look for a different apartment to rent: There's no indication the woman is considering moving to a different apartment.

## Practice 408: Library Return Favour | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends at a coffee shop.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why can't the woman return her books to the Central Library?
- 1. The library is closed for renovations.
- 2. She doesn't have time to go there.
- 3. The library has changed its location.
- 4. Her membership has expired.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The library is closed for renovations: The man explicitly states "I think it's closed for renovations until next week" and mentions there was a sign on the door about updating computer systems and reorganizing shelves.
❌ 2. She doesn't have time to go there: The conversation doesn't indicate she has time constraints, only that the library is closed.
❌ 3. The library has changed its location: There's no mention of the library changing location in the conversation.
❌ 4. Her membership has expired: There's no mention of any membership issues in the conversation.

**Q2:** What solution does the man initially suggest for returning the books?
- 1. Going to the Riverside branch instead.
- 2. Waiting until next week when the library reopens.
- 3. Using the drop box on the east side of the building.
- 4. Calling the library to request an extension.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Going to the Riverside branch instead: The man doesn't initially suggest going to Riverside; the woman mentions this as an alternative she was considering.
❌ 2. Waiting until next week when the library reopens: The man doesn't suggest waiting until reopening.
✅ 3. Using the drop box on the east side of the building: The man first mentions "they have one of those blue drop boxes on the east side of the building, near the parking lot on Maple Avenue" as a solution.
❌ 4. Calling the library to request an extension: There's no mention of calling for an extension in the conversation.

**Q3:** Why is the man going near the library later?
- 1. To meet another friend for coffee.
- 2. To buy groceries at a nearby market.
- 3. To check if the renovations are complete.
- 4. To apply for a job at the new coffee shop.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To meet another friend for coffee: There's no mention of the man meeting anyone else.
✅ 2. To buy groceries at a nearby market: The man says "I'm heading over to that area later today to pick up some groceries at Wilson's Market."
❌ 3. To check if the renovations are complete: The man knows the renovations won't be complete until next week.
❌ 4. To apply for a job at the new coffee shop: There's no mention of the man seeking employment at the coffee shop.

**Q4:** How does the woman probably feel about the man's offer to return her books?
- 1. Suspicious of his motives.
- 2. Worried he might forget.
- 3. Grateful for his help.
- 4. Embarrassed about imposing.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Suspicious of his motives: The woman shows no signs of suspicion in the conversation.
❌ 2. Worried he might forget: The woman doesn't express any concern about him forgetting.
✅ 3. Grateful for his help: The woman says "That would be amazing!" and "Thanks so much, Mark. You're a lifesaver," clearly showing gratitude.
❌ 4. Embarrassed about imposing: While she asks if it's too much trouble, she doesn't show embarrassment about accepting his help.

**Q5:** What will likely happen when the Central Library reopens?
- 1. The friends will meet at the new coffee shop there.
- 2. The woman will avoid going there due to higher fees.
- 3. The man will apply for a job at the library.
- 4. The drop box will be permanently removed.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The friends will meet at the new coffee shop there: The man mentions "they're adding a coffee shop to Central when they reopen" and the woman responds "Maybe next time we could meet there instead."
❌ 2. The woman will avoid going there due to higher fees: There's no mention of higher fees in the conversation.
❌ 3. The man will apply for a job at the library: There's no indication the man is seeking employment at the library.
❌ 4. The drop box will be permanently removed: There's no mention of removing the drop box; in fact, it's currently available during renovations.

## Practice 409: Maple Avenue Block Party | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors discussing a community event.

### Audio Transcript
$4b

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of the conversation?
- 1. To discuss volunteering for a neighborhood event
- 2. To complain about last year's block party
- 3. To invite each other to a community meeting
- 4. To plan a private gathering between neighbors
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To discuss volunteering for a neighborhood event: This is correct as both neighbors are discussing how they can volunteer for the upcoming block party, with the woman considering food coordination and the man considering setup and business donations.
❌ 2. To complain about last year's block party: While they briefly mention an issue with food organization last year, the conversation is primarily focused on volunteering for this year's event, not complaining.
❌ 3. To invite each other to a community meeting: They mention attending a planning meeting together at the end, but this is not the main purpose of their conversation.
❌ 4. To plan a private gathering between neighbors: They are discussing a community block party, not a private gathering.

**Q2:** What problem occurred at last year's event?
- 1. The sound system didn't work properly
- 2. There was an imbalance of food items
- 3. Not enough volunteers showed up
- 4. The setup team arrived late
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The sound system didn't work properly: There's no mention of problems with the sound system from last year.
✅ 2. There was an imbalance of food items: The woman specifically mentions "We ended up with fifteen pasta salads and no desserts!" showing there was poor food variety due to disorganization.
❌ 3. Not enough volunteers showed up: This issue is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The setup team arrived late: There's no mention of the setup team being late last year.

**Q3:** What two roles is the man considering volunteering for?
- 1. Food coordination and children's activities
- 2. Setup team and contacting businesses
- 3. Raffle organization and tent assembly
- 4. Sound system setup and food preparation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Food coordination and children's activities: The man doesn't mention wanting to coordinate food or children's activities.
✅ 2. Setup team and contacting businesses: The man specifically says "I was thinking of volunteering for the setup team" and later adds "I could probably help with that too" regarding contacting businesses for donations.
❌ 3. Raffle organization and tent assembly: While tent assembly might be part of setup, there's no specific mention of him organizing the raffle.
❌ 4. Sound system setup and food preparation: The man doesn't volunteer for either of these roles.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about Mrs. Chen?
- 1. She is too controlling of community events
- 2. She lives too far away to be effective
- 3. She is capable of managing large events
- 4. She should delegate more responsibilities
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is too controlling of community events: The woman doesn't express any negative opinions about Mrs. Chen's management style.
❌ 2. She lives too far away to be effective: The woman mentions Mrs. Chen lives in "the corner house," suggesting she lives nearby.
✅ 3. She is capable of managing large events: The woman says "She did such a great job with the winter festival," indicating she thinks Mrs. Chen is competent at organizing community events.
❌ 4. She should delegate more responsibilities: There's no suggestion that Mrs. Chen doesn't delegate enough.

**Q5:** What will the man and woman likely do next?
- 1. Visit the bakery on Queen Street together
- 2. Create a food signup sheet for the event
- 3. Email their volunteer preferences separately
- 4. Attend the planning meeting as a team
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit the bakery on Queen Street together: While the man mentions knowing the bakery owner, there's no plan for them to visit together.
❌ 2. Create a food signup sheet for the event: The woman may do this if she gets the food coordination role, but it's not their immediate next step.
✅ 3. Email their volunteer preferences separately: They explicitly agree to both reply to the email with their volunteer preferences - the woman for food coordination and the man for setup and business donations.
❌ 4. Attend the planning meeting as a team: While they plan to walk to the meeting together the following week, this isn't their immediate next action.
