## Practice 410: Pool Care Guidance | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a swimming pool maintenance staff member.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman visit Aqua Pool Services?
- 1. To purchase a new swimming pool.
- 2. To seek advice on pool maintenance.
- 3. To complain about poor water quality.
- 4. To apply for a job as a pool technician.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To purchase a new swimming pool: She mentions she already has a pool in her new home.
✅ 2. To seek advice on pool maintenance: She explicitly states "I just moved into a house with a swimming pool, and I have absolutely no idea how to maintain it properly. I was hoping you could give me some advice on a maintenance schedule."
❌ 3. To complain about poor water quality: She doesn't mention any issues with water quality.
❌ 4. To apply for a job as a pool technician: Nothing suggests she's looking for employment.

**Q2:** Why does the woman seem concerned about the testing schedule?
- 1. She thinks it will be too expensive.
- 2. She doesn't understand how to use the equipment.
- 3. She feels the frequency might be demanding.
- 4. She worries about chemical safety.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She thinks it will be too expensive: The woman doesn't mention cost concerns about testing.
❌ 2. She doesn't understand how to use the equipment: She asks if special equipment is needed but doesn't express confusion about using it.
✅ 3. She feels the frequency might be demanding: When told she needs to check chemical levels twice a week, she responds "Twice a week? That sounds like a lot," indicating concern about the frequency.
❌ 4. She worries about chemical safety: The woman doesn't express any concerns about chemical safety in the conversation.

**Q3:** What is included in the basic service package?
- 1. Daily skimming and weekly chemical testing.
- 2. Chemical adjustments, cleaning, and equipment checks.
- 3. Filter cleaning and seasonal opening services.
- 4. Monthly visits with chemical delivery.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Daily skimming and weekly chemical testing: The basic package includes weekly visits, not daily skimming.
✅ 2. Chemical adjustments, cleaning, and equipment checks: The man states "Our basic package includes weekly visits where we test and adjust chemicals, skim, vacuum, and check equipment."
❌ 3. Filter cleaning and seasonal opening services: These are specifically mentioned as part of the premium package, not the basic package.
❌ 4. Monthly visits with chemical delivery: The basic package includes weekly visits, not monthly, and there's no mention of chemical delivery.

**Q4:** What will most likely happen this Friday?
- 1. The woman will purchase a test kit.
- 2. A technician will visit the woman's home.
- 3. The woman will learn how to clean her pool.
- 4. The pool will be closed for the season.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will purchase a test kit: There's no mention of her planning to buy a test kit on Friday.
✅ 2. A technician will visit the woman's home: The woman agrees to sign up for the basic package starting "this Friday morning," so a technician will visit her home.
❌ 3. The woman will learn how to clean her pool: The conversation indicates she's hiring professionals rather than learning to do it herself.
❌ 4. The pool will be closed for the season: There's no mention of closing the pool, and the context suggests she's starting regular maintenance.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the woman's lifestyle?
- 1. She travels frequently for work.
- 2. She has a flexible work schedule.
- 3. She works remotely at least one day per week.
- 4. She prefers to do household maintenance herself.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She travels frequently for work: Nothing in the conversation suggests she travels for work.
❌ 2. She has a flexible work schedule: While she works from home on Fridays, this doesn't necessarily mean her schedule is flexible overall.
✅ 3. She works remotely at least one day per week: She explicitly states "I work from home on Fridays," confirming she works remotely at least one day per week.
❌ 4. She prefers to do household maintenance herself: She's hiring a pool service rather than maintaining the pool herself, contradicting this option.

**Q6:** Why did the woman choose Friday morning for pool service?
- 1. It's when pool service is least expensive.
- 2. She wants to observe the first maintenance session.
- 3. It's her day off from work.
- 4. She'll be at home working during that time.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's when pool service is least expensive: No mention of price differences based on service days.
❌ 2. She wants to observe the first maintenance session: She doesn't state this as her reason.
❌ 3. It's her day off from work: She specifically says she works from home on Fridays, not that it's her day off.
✅ 4. She'll be at home working during that time: The woman states "I work from home on Fridays, so that would be ideal if possible," indicating she chose Friday because she'll be home working.

## Practice 411: Library Book Club Options | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a library.

### Audio Transcript
$4b

**Q1:** Why does the man prefer the Saturday Morning Readers group?
- 1. Because he enjoys contemporary fiction.
- 2. Because it fits his work schedule.
- 3. Because it has fewer members.
- 4. Because the meetings are shorter.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Because he enjoys contemporary fiction.: The man mentions enjoying historical fiction and mystery novels, not contemporary fiction.
✅ 2. Because it fits his work schedule.: The man states, "I work full-time during weekdays, so I'd need something that meets in the evening or on weekends," making the Saturday morning group suitable for his schedule.
❌ 3. Because it has fewer members.: The number of members is not given as a reason for his preference.
❌ 4. Because the meetings are shorter.: The length of meetings is not mentioned as a factor in his decision.

**Q2:** What is the man most likely to do after this conversation?
- 1. Apply for a library card.
- 2. Join the Tuesday evening group.
- 3. Read the current book selection.
- 4. Wait until the next month to join.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Apply for a library card.: The man already has a library card as he states, "I already have a library card, so that's not a problem."
❌ 2. Join the Tuesday evening group.: The man shows interest in the Saturday Morning Readers group, not the Tuesday evening group.
✅ 3. Read the current book selection.: The man agrees to take a copy of "The Clockmaker's Daughter" when the woman offers it, saying "That would be perfect. I'll take a copy and fill out the registration form."
❌ 4. Wait until the next month to join.: The conversation indicates he plans to join now and read the current book for the upcoming May 14th meeting.

**Q3:** How are the books for the club selected?
- 1. The coordinator chooses them.
- 2. Members vote on them.
- 3. They follow a predetermined list.
- 4. They are based on library availability.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The coordinator chooses them.: The conversation doesn't indicate that the coordinator selects the books.
✅ 2. Members vote on them.: The woman clearly states, "The group votes on the next book at the end of each meeting."
❌ 3. They follow a predetermined list.: There's no mention of a predetermined reading list.
❌ 4. They are based on library availability.: While the library provides copies, the selection is based on member votes, not availability.

**Q4:** What requirement for joining might be challenging for some people?
- 1. Paying the membership fee.
- 2. Attending a minimum number of meetings.
- 3. Living within the library district.
- 4. Bringing themed refreshments.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Paying the membership fee.: The woman explicitly states, "There's no fee" for joining.
✅ 2. Attending a minimum number of meetings.: The woman mentions, "We also ask that members commit to attending at least six meetings throughout the year," which could be difficult for some people to commit to.
❌ 3. Living within the library district.: While having a library card is required, the woman mentions it's free if you live in the district, but doesn't make this a requirement.
❌ 4. Bringing themed refreshments.: The woman mentions they "sometimes have themed refreshments" but doesn't indicate members are required to bring them.

**Q5:** What does the woman suggest to keep the man informed about book selections?
- 1. He should check the library website.
- 2. He should call before each meeting.
- 3. She will add him to an email list.
- 4. He will receive text message updates.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He should check the library website.: The woman doesn't mention checking the website for updates.
❌ 2. He should call before each meeting.: The woman doesn't suggest calling for updates.
✅ 3. She will add him to an email list.: The woman specifically says, "If you give me your email address, I can add you to our mailing list so you'll get updates about upcoming selections."
❌ 4. He will receive text message updates.: Text message updates are not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q6:** Why does the woman ask about the man's reading preferences?
- 1. To recommend an appropriate book club.
- 2. To suggest books he might enjoy.
- 3. To determine if he's a serious reader.
- 4. To start a friendly conversation.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To recommend an appropriate book club.: After asking about his preferences, she immediately recommends the Saturday Morning Readers group based on his interest in historical fiction and schedule needs.
❌ 2. To suggest books he might enjoy.: She doesn't suggest individual books based on his preferences.
❌ 3. To determine if he's a serious reader.: There's no indication she's evaluating his seriousness as a reader.
❌ 4. To start a friendly conversation.: While friendly, her question has a clear purpose of matching him with the right book club.

## Practice 412: CityWheels Car Share | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about a car sharing program.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman interested in the car sharing program?
- 1. She wants to reduce her environmental impact.
- 2. She recently moved and wants to avoid car ownership costs.
- 3. Her car broke down and needs a temporary solution.
- 4. She needs a second vehicle for occasional use.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to reduce her environmental impact: Environmental concerns aren't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. She recently moved and wants to avoid car ownership costs: The woman states 'I just moved downtown and I don't want the expense of owning a car, but I still need to drive occasionally.'
❌ 3. Her car broke down and needs a temporary solution: A broken car isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. She needs a second vehicle for occasional use: There's no mention of her already owning a vehicle.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after the conversation?
- 1. Compare prices with other car sharing services.
- 2. Complete the registration process immediately.
- 3. Download the car sharing app first.
- 4. Calculate if she can afford the monthly payments.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Compare prices with other car sharing services: The woman shows no indication of wanting to compare prices with other services.
✅ 2. Complete the registration process immediately: The woman explicitly states 'I'd like to sign up today' and asks how long the registration process takes, indicating she plans to register immediately.
❌ 3. Download the car sharing app first: While the app is mentioned, there's no indication she plans to download it before registering.
❌ 4. Calculate if she can afford the monthly payments: She has already determined the plan fits her budget of under $200 per month.

**Q3:** How long has the woman been a licensed driver?
- 1. Less than five years
- 2. Approximately eight years
- 3. More than a decade
- 4. She didn't specify
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Less than five years: This contradicts what the woman stated.
✅ 2. Approximately eight years: The woman explicitly states 'I've had my license for about eight years now.'
❌ 3. More than a decade: This contradicts what the woman stated.
❌ 4. She didn't specify: She did specify the length of time in the conversation.

**Q4:** Why does the woman choose the premium membership plan?
- 1. It offers better vehicle selection.
- 2. It provides additional insurance coverage.
- 3. It will cost her less based on her expected usage.
- 4. It allows for longer rental periods.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It offers better vehicle selection: The conversation doesn't mention any difference in vehicle selection between plans.
❌ 2. It provides additional insurance coverage: The conversation states both plans include insurance.
✅ 3. It will cost her less based on her expected usage: The man calculates that with her expected 15 hours of usage per month, she would save about $23 with the premium plan ($151.49 vs $128.74).
❌ 4. It allows for longer rental periods: The conversation doesn't mention any difference in rental periods between plans.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the man gives about accessing the vehicles?
- 1. Members use a special card or app to unlock cars.
- 2. Members must pick up keys from a central office.
- 3. Members need to call ahead to have cars unlocked.
- 4. Members should arrive early to ensure availability.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Members use a special card or app to unlock cars: The man explains 'we'll mail you a membership card that unlocks the vehicles. You can also use the app on your phone.'
❌ 2. Members must pick up keys from a central office: The man states 'The keys are inside the car,' not at a central office.
❌ 3. Members need to call ahead to have cars unlocked: The conversation mentions reserving through the app or online, not calling ahead.
❌ 4. Members should arrive early to ensure availability: This advice is not given in the conversation.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention the quarter tank of gas?
- 1. To explain how much gas is included with each rental
- 2. To inform her about a potential fee for not refilling
- 3. To suggest she should always fill up before returning
- 4. To explain how the fuel payment system works
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To explain how much gas is included with each rental: The conversation doesn't suggest this is the amount included.
✅ 2. To inform her about a potential fee for not refilling: The man states 'if you leave it with less than a quarter tank of gas, there's a $15 fee.'
❌ 3. To suggest she should always fill up before returning: He doesn't suggest filling up completely, only ensuring it's not below a quarter tank.
❌ 4. To explain how the fuel payment system works: He's explaining a penalty, not the general payment system for fuel.

❌ 2. She gets a reduced monthly rate.: No reduced monthly rate is mentioned for signing a contract.
❌ 3. She receives priority technical support.: No mention of priority support is made in the conversation.
❌ 4. She gets upgraded to a faster speed.: No speed upgrade is mentioned as a benefit of signing the contract.

**Q6:** Why did the woman prefer Thursday for the installation?

❌ 4. Request a test drive of one of the available vehicles: Test drives aren't mentioned as part of the service process.

**Q3:** What is included in all membership plans?
- 1. Roadside assistance and car washes
- 2. Fuel, insurance, and maintenance
- 3. GPS navigation and premium sound systems
- 4. Unlimited mileage and toll payments
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Roadside assistance and car washes: These services aren't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Fuel, insurance, and maintenance: The woman specifically states "All plans include fuel, insurance, and maintenance" when explaining the membership tiers.
❌ 3. GPS navigation and premium sound systems: These features aren't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Unlimited mileage and toll payments: These benefits aren't mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** Why might the man occasionally need an SUV or van?
- 1. To transport large purchases
- 2. To drive in winter conditions
- 3. To take friends on road trips
- 4. To move to a new apartment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To transport large purchases: The man specifically mentions "I sometimes need to transport larger items from stores" when asking about vehicle types.
❌ 2. To drive in winter conditions: Weather conditions aren't mentioned as a reason for needing larger vehicles.
❌ 3. To take friends on road trips: Taking friends on trips isn't mentioned as a reason for needing larger vehicles.
❌ 4. To move to a new apartment: Moving apartments isn't mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What is the representative's main recommendation for the man?
- 1. He should consider the Premium plan for maximum savings.
- 2. The Basic plan would be most economical for his needs.
- 3. He should share a membership with family members.
- 4. The Standard plan offers the best balance of cost and flexibility.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He should consider the Premium plan for maximum savings: The representative doesn't recommend this plan.
✅ 2. The Basic plan would be most economical for his needs: The woman specifically states "Based on what you've described, the Basic plan would likely be most economical."
❌ 3. He should share a membership with family members: Sharing memberships isn't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The Standard plan offers the best balance of cost and flexibility: The representative only mentions this plan might be worth considering if his usage increases, not as her main recommendation.

**Q6:** Why does the woman mention the $25 application fee?
- 1. To discourage casual applicants
- 2. To explain what covers the driving record check
- 3. To suggest the man apply in person to avoid it
- 4. To compare it with competitors' higher fees
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To discourage casual applicants: This isn't mentioned as a purpose of the fee.
✅ 2. To explain what covers the driving record check: The woman states "There's a one-time $25 application fee to cover your driving record check."
❌ 3. To suggest the man apply in person to avoid it: She doesn't suggest applying in person would avoid the fee.
❌ 4. To compare it with competitors' higher fees: No comparison with competitors' fees is mentioned.

❌ 2. Focus protection on the detached garage: The consultant doesn't specifically focus on the garage in his recommendation.
✅ 3. Use the Premium Protection package: The consultant directly states "I'd recommend our Premium Protection package" based on the woman's description and concerns.
❌ 4. Add extra cameras to the back of the house: While the woman mentions concern about the back door, the consultant doesn't specifically recommend extra cameras there.

**Q6:** Why does the woman ask about the system's ease of use?
- 1. She has had trouble with technology before.
- 2. She wants her children to be able to use it.
- 3. She doesn't consider herself technically skilled.
- 4. She had difficulties with her previous security system.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has had trouble with technology before: The woman doesn't mention any past experiences with technology.

## Practice 416: CityRide Tracking Support | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a woman and a transit app customer service representative.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why has the woman been experiencing problems with the transit app?
- 1. She has been using an outdated version of the app.
- 2. Her phone's background refresh setting is disabled.
- 3. The bus routes she uses have outdated GPS units.
- 4. She hasn't enabled location services on her phone.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has been using an outdated version of the app: While she is using version 3.1 instead of 3.2, this isn't identified as the main cause of her tracking problems.
✅ 2. Her phone's background refresh setting is disabled: The customer service representative identifies this as the main issue, saying "When background refresh is disabled, the app can't update bus locations unless you have it open and actively using it. Enabling that feature should help significantly."
❌ 3. The bus routes she uses have outdated GPS units: The representative specifically states that routes 42 and 67 that she uses "have been fully upgraded already."
❌ 4. She hasn't enabled location services on her phone: While location services are mentioned, the woman doesn't confirm whether this is enabled or disabled, and the representative focuses on background refresh as the main issue.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Call back to report continuing issues.
- 2. Switch to a different transit app.
- 3. Update her app and change her settings.
- 4. Take different bus routes with better tracking.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Call back to report continuing issues: There's no indication she expects continued problems after making the suggested changes.
❌ 2. Switch to a different transit app: She seems satisfied with the solutions offered and doesn't mention considering alternatives.
✅ 3. Update her app and change her settings: She directly states "I'll definitely update the app and enable background refresh" and "I'll give these changes a try."
❌ 4. Take different bus routes with better tracking: The representative confirmed her regular routes already have upgraded GPS.

**Q3:** Which bus routes does the woman use most often?
- 1. Routes 24 and 76
- 2. Routes 42 and 67
- 3. Routes 32 and 76
- 4. Routes 24 and 67
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Routes 24 and 76: These routes are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Routes 42 and 67: The woman specifically states "I take routes 42 and 67 most frequently."
❌ 3. Routes 32 and 76: These routes are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Routes 24 and 67: This combines one correct and one incorrect route number.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the CityRide app development team?
- 1. They are slow to respond to technical problems.
- 2. They regularly incorporate user suggestions.
- 3. They prioritize new features over fixing bugs.
- 4. They have recently reduced their development staff.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are slow to respond to technical problems: The conversation indicates they are actively working on improvements and upgrades.
✅ 2. They regularly incorporate user suggestions: The representative states "we've added a few things based on user feedback" when describing the new features in version 3.2.
❌ 3. They prioritize new features over fixing bugs: They are addressing both - fixing GPS tracking issues while also adding new features.
❌ 4. They have recently reduced their development staff: There is no information in the conversation about staffing changes.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the customer service representative gives?
- 1. To try different bus routes with better GPS coverage.
- 2. To enable background refresh and update the app.
- 3. To report issues directly through the app interface.
- 4. To wait until all buses have upgraded GPS units.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To try different bus routes with better GPS coverage: The representative confirms her current routes already have upgraded GPS.
✅ 2. To enable background refresh and update the app: The representative specifically identifies these as the solutions to her problem, saying "Enabling that feature should help significantly" and mentioning the benefits of updating to version 3.2.
❌ 3. To report issues directly through the app interface: This is mentioned as a new feature but not as advice for solving her current problem.
❌ 4. To wait until all buses have upgraded GPS units: The representative confirms her routes already have upgraded GPS, so this isn't relevant advice.

**Q6:** Why does the woman mention waiting in the rain?
- 1. To explain why she needs the notification feature.
- 2. To illustrate how the app's inaccuracy affected her.
- 3. To ask about weather predictions in the app.
- 4. To complain about the bus shelter conditions.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To explain why she needs the notification feature: She mentions this before learning about the notification feature.
✅ 2. To illustrate how the app's inaccuracy affected her: She uses this specific example to show the real-world impact of the tracking problems, saying "Yesterday, I waited for 20 minutes in the rain because the app said the bus was just around the corner."
❌ 3. To ask about weather predictions in the app: Weather predictions are not discussed in the conversation.
❌ 4. To complain about the bus shelter conditions: Bus shelter conditions are not mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 2. About 50: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. About 75: The woman specifically states "We have about 75 vendors today" when the man asks about the market.
❌ 4. About 100: This number is too high and not mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 3. About 50%: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. About 75%: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 434: Camping Gear Advice | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a camping equipment store. The woman is a sales associate, and the man is a customer planning a camping trip.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, I'm looking for some camping equipment. I'm planning a trip with my family next weekend, and we're complete beginners.
WOMAN: Of course, I'd be happy to help. Is this your first time camping?
MAN: Yeah, we've never done it before. My kids have been begging to go, so I thought we'd try Algonquin Park. I'm a bit overwhelmed with all the gear we need though.
WOMAN: Don't worry, that's what I'm here for. How many people will be camping?
MAN: It's me, my wife, and our two children - they're 8 and 10.
WOMAN: Great. So you'll need a four-person tent at minimum. I'd actually recommend a six-person tent for a family of four, just to have extra space for your gear.
MAN: That makes sense. Um, what about sleeping bags? Do we need special ones?
WOMAN: For summer camping in Ontario, you'll want bags rated for about 5 degrees Celsius. The nights can get chilly even in July. We have some family-friendly options over here that won't break the bank.

**Q1:** Why is the man at the camping store?
- 1. To return camping equipment
- 2. To prepare for a family camping trip
- 3. To buy gifts for his children
- 4. To get advice for an experienced camper
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To return camping equipment: There's no mention of returning anything.
✅ 2. To prepare for a family camping trip: The man states 'I'm looking for some camping equipment. I'm planning a trip with my family next weekend.'
❌ 3. To buy gifts for his children: While he mentions his children, he's shopping for camping equipment, not gifts.
❌ 4. To get advice for an experienced camper: The man clearly states they are 'complete beginners.'

**Q2:** What tent size does the woman recommend?
- 1. A four-person tent
- 2. A two-person tent
- 3. A six-person tent
- 4. An eight-person tent
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A four-person tent: The woman mentions this as the minimum but not her recommendation.
❌ 2. A two-person tent: This is too small for a family of four and isn't mentioned.
✅ 3. A six-person tent: The woman specifically says 'I'd actually recommend a six-person tent for a family of four, just to have extra space for your gear.'
❌ 4. An eight-person tent: This size is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** How does the man feel about the camping trip?
- 1. Confident but concerned about costs
- 2. Reluctant but willing to try
- 3. Excited but overwhelmed by preparations
- 4. Experienced but worried about his children
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Confident but concerned about costs: While he does mention cost concerns later, he initially describes himself as 'overwhelmed' not confident.
❌ 2. Reluctant but willing to try: The man doesn't express reluctance; he initiated the trip because his kids wanted to go.
✅ 3. Excited but overwhelmed by preparations: The man states 'I'm a bit overwhelmed with all the gear we need' and is taking initiative to prepare for the trip his kids have been 'begging' for.
❌ 4. Experienced but worried about his children: The man clearly states they are 'complete beginners' and 'we've never done it before.'

### Audio Transcript
MAN: These tents are larger than I expected. Do you think I'll be able to set it up myself?
WOMAN: This model is actually quite straightforward. It uses a color-coded pole system, so you know exactly which pole goes where. Would you like me to show you how it works?
MAN: That would be great, thanks. Oh, and what about cooking? We can't exactly order takeout in the wilderness, right?
WOMAN: Definitely not! For a family of four, I'd recommend a two-burner propane stove like this one. It's simple to use and will let you cook two things at once.
MAN: Perfect. And, um, do we need special cookware?
WOMAN: You could use regular pots and pans, but camping cookware is designed to be lightweight and durable. This nesting set includes everything you'd need and packs up small.
MAN: That looks convenient. What about food storage? I've heard bears can be an issue.
WOMAN: At Algonquin, you'll definitely want proper food storage. These bear-resistant containers are your best bet. They're mandatory in many parks now.

**Q3:** What is true about the tent the woman shows?
- 1. It requires professional setup
- 2. It has a color-coded system
- 3. It's smaller than standard tents
- 4. It's difficult for beginners to use
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It requires professional setup: The woman specifically says the tent is 'quite straightforward' to set up, contradicting this option.
✅ 2. It has a color-coded system: The woman explains that the tent 'uses a color-coded pole system, so you know exactly which pole goes where.'
❌ 3. It's smaller than standard tents: The man actually comments that 'These tents are larger than I expected,' not smaller.
❌ 4. It's difficult for beginners to use: The woman describes it as 'straightforward' and offers to show how it works, indicating it's suitable for beginners.

**Q4:** What cooking equipment does the woman recommend?
- 1. A single-burner stove
- 2. A microwave oven
- 3. A two-burner propane stove
- 4. A portable barbecue grill
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A single-burner stove: This is not what the woman recommends.
❌ 2. A microwave oven: This is not mentioned and would be impractical for camping.
✅ 3. A two-burner propane stove: The woman specifically says 'For a family of four, I'd recommend a two-burner propane stove like this one.'
❌ 4. A portable barbecue grill: This is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** Why does the woman suggest bear-resistant containers?
- 1. They're optional accessories
- 2. They're required in many parks
- 3. They're cheaper than other options
- 4. They're part of the starter kit
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They're optional accessories: The woman indicates they are necessary, not optional.
✅ 2. They're required in many parks: The woman states 'They're mandatory in many parks now.'
❌ 3. They're cheaper than other options: No price comparison with alternatives is mentioned.
❌ 4. They're part of the starter kit: The woman specifically says they are 'sold separately' from the starter kit.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q6:** How much can the man save with the family starter kit?
- 1. 10%
- 2. 15%
- 3. 20%
- 4. 25%

## Practice 436: Desk Rearrangement Help | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two coworkers discussing furniture moving and taking a coffee break.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman need to rearrange her desk area?
- 1. To make room for a new filing cabinet
- 2. To accommodate a new monitor
- 3. To create a presentation space
- 4. To match Diane's setup from accounting
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To make room for a new filing cabinet: She's trying to move an existing filing cabinet, not make room for a new one.
✅ 2. To accommodate a new monitor: She explicitly states "I'm trying to create more space for the new monitor they're giving me next week."
❌ 3. To create a presentation space: There's no mention of needing space for presentations in her work area.
❌ 4. To match Diane's setup from accounting: Diane is only mentioned as someone who previously moved the same cabinet, not as someone whose setup the woman is trying to match.

**Q2:** When do they plan to move the filing cabinet?
- 1. After lunch
- 2. After the department meeting
- 3. In about ten minutes
- 4. The following week
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. After lunch: They plan to move it before lunch, as the woman mentions wanting to get it done before lunch.
❌ 2. After the department meeting: The meeting is at 3:30, but they plan to move the cabinet much earlier in the day.
✅ 3. In about ten minutes: The man says "Give me about ten minutes? Then we can tackle it together" and later "I'll meet you by your desk in five minutes."
❌ 4. The following week: There's no mention of postponing the move to the next week; they plan to do it that same day.

**Q3:** What do they plan to do after moving the furniture?
- 1. Attend a presentation
- 2. Go to a coffee shop
- 3. Finish some reports
- 4. Meet with Dave
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend a presentation: The presentation is at 2:00, but it's only the woman who has this, not both of them.
✅ 2. Go to a coffee shop: The woman specifically invites the man to grab coffee at the Maple Bean Café after they move the cabinet.
❌ 3. Finish some reports: The man plans to finish his reports before helping move the cabinet, not after.
❌ 4. Meet with Dave: They will attend a department meeting later, but Dave is only mentioned as saying how long the meeting will last, not as someone they plan to meet with directly.

**Q4:** What does the man probably think about the woman's request for help?
- 1. It's an inconvenience to his schedule
- 2. It's a welcome break from his reports
- 3. It's a reasonable request that deserves a reward
- 4. It's something he'd rather postpone until tomorrow
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's an inconvenience to his schedule: The man responds positively and offers to help after finishing his reports, not treating it as an inconvenience.
❌ 2. It's a welcome break from his reports: He doesn't indicate he wants a break from reports; he actually wants to finish them first.
✅ 3. It's a reasonable request that deserves a reward: He agrees to help and then suggests they deserve a treat (coffee) afterward, saying "We definitely deserve a treat after moving that cabinet."
❌ 4. It's something he'd rather postpone until tomorrow: He offers to help in about ten minutes, not postponing to the next day.

**Q5:** What will likely happen at 3:30 PM?
- 1. They will return from the coffee shop
- 2. The woman will give her presentation
- 3. They will attend a department meeting
- 4. They will finish moving the filing cabinet
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They will return from the coffee shop: There's no indication they'll be at the coffee shop until 3:30.
❌ 2. The woman will give her presentation: The woman's presentation is at 2:00, not 3:30.
✅ 3. They will attend a department meeting: The man specifically states "It's at 3:30 in the Wellington Room" when referring to the department meeting.
❌ 4. They will finish moving the filing cabinet: They plan to move the cabinet much earlier, before lunch and before the woman's 2:00 presentation.

## Practice 437: Riverside Garden Plot Rental | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about booking a community garden plot.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman want to rent a garden plot?
- 1. She wants to meet other gardeners in the neighborhood.
- 2. She misses growing her own vegetables.
- 3. She needs more outdoor space at her new home.
- 4. She wants to learn organic gardening methods.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to meet other gardeners in the neighborhood: While she might meet others, this isn't her stated reason.
✅ 2. She misses growing her own vegetables: The woman explicitly states, "I used to have a big vegetable garden at my old place, and I really miss growing my own food."
❌ 3. She needs more outdoor space at her new home: While she mentions moving, she doesn't cite lack of space as her reason.
❌ 4. She wants to learn organic gardening methods: She actually mentions she already prefers organic gardening methods.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Purchase seeds and starter plants.
- 2. Attend the seed exchange event.
- 3. Complete the registration and make a payment.
- 4. Participate in a community workday.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Purchase seeds and starter plants: While she'll need these eventually, her immediate next step would be registration.
❌ 2. Attend the seed exchange event: The seed exchange is in early April, not immediately.
✅ 3. Complete the registration and make a payment: The woman says, "I'll take a standard plot and pay by e-transfer today," indicating this is her immediate next action.
❌ 4. Participate in a community workday: Community workdays happen monthly, but she needs to register for a plot first.

**Q3:** How long does the garden season last?
- 1. Six months
- 2. Five months
- 3. Seven months
- 4. Four months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Six months: The conversation states the growing season runs from April 15th to October 31st, which is more than six months.
❌ 2. Five months: The growing season is longer than five months.
✅ 3. Seven months: The man specifically says the growing season runs from April 15th to October 31st, which is approximately seven months (mid-April through the end of October).
❌ 4. Four months: The growing season is much longer than four months.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's gardening experience?
- 1. She is a professional gardener.
- 2. She has never grown vegetables before.
- 3. She has experience with chemical fertilizers.
- 4. She has previous gardening knowledge.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is a professional gardener: There's no indication she gardens professionally.
❌ 2. She has never grown vegetables before: She mentions having a vegetable garden at her old place.
❌ 3. She has experience with chemical fertilizers: She actually states she prefers organic methods.
✅ 4. She has previous gardening knowledge: The woman mentions she "used to have a big vegetable garden" and states she prefers organic gardening methods, indicating prior experience.

**Q5:** What is the main requirement for plot holders at this garden?
- 1. They must attend all monthly workshops.
- 2. They must use organic gardening methods.
- 3. They must share their harvest with others.
- 4. They must provide their own gardening tools.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They must attend all monthly workshops: The man mentions workshops as an included benefit, not a requirement.
✅ 2. They must use organic gardening methods: The man clearly states, "We're an organic garden, so no chemical pesticides or fertilizers are allowed" as one of their guidelines.
❌ 3. They must share their harvest with others: There is no mention of sharing harvests in the conversation.
❌ 4. They must provide their own gardening tools: The man mentions that shared gardening tools are included with the plot rental.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention the orientation session?
- 1. To help the woman meet the participation requirement.
- 2. To ensure she understands the garden rules.
- 3. To provide her with more information about resources.
- 4. To teach her how to use the shared gardening tools.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To help the woman meet the participation requirement: The orientation is not mentioned as counting toward the required community workdays.
❌ 2. To ensure she understands the garden rules: While rules might be covered, this isn't the specific reason he gives.
✅ 3. To provide her with more information about resources: The man specifically recommends the orientation session "to learn more about our community and resources."
❌ 4. To teach her how to use the shared gardening tools: There's no mention of tool training at the orientation.

## Practice 438: Hard Drive Diagnostic | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a computer repair store. The woman works at the store, and the man is a customer with a laptop problem.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** What is the main problem with the man's laptop?
- 1. The screen is damaged.
- 2. It's running slowly and freezing.
- 3. The keyboard isn't working properly.
- 4. The battery won't hold a charge.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The screen is damaged.: No screen damage is mentioned.
✅ 2. It's running slowly and freezing.: The man specifically states "it's been running extremely slow, and sometimes it just freezes completely."
❌ 3. The keyboard isn't working properly.: No keyboard issues are mentioned.
❌ 4. The battery won't hold a charge.: No battery problems are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What does the woman suspect might be causing the clicking noise?
- 1. A loose fan component
- 2. A virus in the system
- 3. A hard drive issue
- 4. An overheating processor
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A loose fan component: This is not mentioned as a possible cause.
❌ 2. A virus in the system: No virus is mentioned in relation to the clicking noise.
✅ 3. A hard drive issue: The woman directly states "That clicking sound could indicate a hard drive issue."
❌ 4. An overheating processor: Overheating is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** How long would a hardware repair likely take?
- 1. About an hour
- 2. One business day
- 3. 2-3 business days
- 4. Up to a week
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. About an hour: This is the time for the diagnostic test, not the repair.
❌ 2. One business day: This timeframe is not mentioned for repairs.
✅ 3. 2-3 business days: The woman specifically states "If it's hardware-related, like that hard drive we mentioned, it could take 2-3 business days."
❌ 4. Up to a week: This longer timeframe is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** Why is the man concerned about repair time?
- 1. He's leaving town soon.
- 2. He has a presentation on Friday.
- 3. He needs to finish an assignment.
- 4. He works remotely from home.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He's leaving town soon.: The conversation doesn't mention the man leaving town.
✅ 2. He has a presentation on Friday.: The man specifically states "I really can't be without my computer for that long. I have a big presentation on Friday."
❌ 3. He needs to finish an assignment.: No assignment is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. He works remotely from home.: While he mentions needing the laptop for work, there's no indication he works from home.

**Q5:** How does the man feel about the loaner laptop option?
- 1. Disappointed
- 2. Indifferent
- 3. Relieved
- 4. Suspicious
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Disappointed: The man is not disappointed with the loaner option.
❌ 2. Indifferent: The man shows clear positive emotion about the option.
✅ 3. Relieved: The man explicitly states "That's actually a relief" when told about the loaner laptop option.
❌ 4. Suspicious: The man shows no signs of suspicion about the loaner laptops.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She offers free data backup services.
- 2. She can complete all repairs within a day.
- 3. She provides diagnostic services before repairs.
- 4. She specializes in software problems only.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She offers free data backup services.: The woman mentions a data backup service for $25, not free.
❌ 2. She can complete all repairs within a day.: She states that hardware repairs could take 2-3 business days.
✅ 3. She provides diagnostic services before repairs.: The woman clearly explains the diagnostic process that must be done before repairs, stating "We can run a diagnostic test to determine what's happening."
❌ 4. She specializes in software problems only.: She discusses both software and hardware repairs, showing no specialization in just software.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He already has an external hard drive.
- 2. He refuses to pay for diagnostic services.
- 3. He doesn't care about his data files.
- 4. He wants the cheapest repair option.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He already has an external hard drive.: The man states "I actually have an external hard drive at home" when discussing backup options.
❌ 2. He refuses to pay for diagnostic services.: He agrees to the diagnostic service and asks about total costs.
❌ 3. He doesn't care about his data files.: He shows concern about his files by asking about backup options.
❌ 4. He wants the cheapest repair option.: He doesn't inquire about the cheapest option, only about the total cost estimate.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will begin repairing the laptop immediately.
- 2. The man will purchase a new laptop instead.
- 3. The man will return with his external drive.
- 4. The woman will order replacement parts.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will begin repairing the laptop immediately.: The woman said they'll start the diagnostic after backing up files.
❌ 2. The man will purchase a new laptop instead.: There's no indication he plans to buy a new laptop.
✅ 3. The man will return with his external drive.: The man's final statement is "I'll be back in an hour with my external drive."
❌ 4. The woman will order replacement parts.: She needs to run diagnostics first before knowing what parts might be needed.

## Practice 443: Office Potluck Coordination | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two colleagues discussing a team potluck.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman hesitant about what to bring to the potluck?
- 1. She doesn't have time to prepare anything.
- 2. She's concerned about her cooking skills.
- 3. She doesn't want to spend money on ingredients.
- 4. She doesn't enjoy office social events.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She doesn't have time to prepare anything: Time constraints aren't mentioned as a concern in the conversation.
✅ 2. She's concerned about her cooking skills: The woman explicitly states, "I'd like to make something, but I'm not exactly a master chef" showing her lack of confidence in her cooking abilities.
❌ 3. She doesn't want to spend money on ingredients: There's no mention of cost or budget concerns in the conversation.
❌ 4. She doesn't enjoy office social events: While initially hesitant about what to bring, by the end she says "This potluck might actually be fun after all," suggesting she's warming up to the idea.

**Q2:** What influenced the man to change his potluck contribution?
- 1. The location of the potluck.
- 2. His busy meeting schedule.
- 3. Dietary restrictions of colleagues.
- 4. The woman's suggestion.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The location of the potluck: The change in location from break room to Maple conference room didn't influence his food choice.
❌ 2. His busy meeting schedule: While he mentions having meetings, this isn't given as a reason for changing his contribution.
✅ 3. Dietary restrictions of colleagues: After discussing that Sarah is celiac and the new accountant is vegan, the man reconsiders his butter tarts saying, "I was thinking about my mom's famous butter tarts, but now I'm reconsidering. Maybe I'll do a fruit platter... Simple but everyone can enjoy it."
❌ 4. The woman's suggestion: The woman didn't suggest he change his contribution; he made this decision after their discussion about dietary restrictions.

**Q3:** Where will the potluck be held?
- 1. In the break room.
- 2. In the kitchen area.
- 3. In the Maple conference room.
- 4. On Bloor Street.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. In the break room: The conversation specifically mentions they won't be using the break room.
❌ 2. In the kitchen area: The kitchen area is being renovated next week according to the conversation.
✅ 3. In the Maple conference room: The woman states, "Oh, and did you see they want us to set up in the Maple conference room instead of the break room? Apparently they're renovating the kitchen area next week."
❌ 4. On Bloor Street: Bloor Street is mentioned as the location of a shop where the man might buy a fruit platter, not where the potluck will be held.

**Q4:** What does the woman think about office potlucks?
- 1. She finds them stressful but necessary.
- 2. She's starting to feel more positive about them.
- 3. She prefers catered events instead.
- 4. She thinks they're a waste of work time.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She finds them stressful but necessary: While she shows some initial hesitation, she doesn't describe potlucks as necessary or particularly stressful.
✅ 2. She's starting to feel more positive about them: At the end of the conversation, she explicitly states, "This potluck might actually be fun after all," showing her attitude is becoming more positive.
❌ 3. She prefers catered events instead: There's no mention of catered events or her preference for them in the conversation.
❌ 4. She thinks they're a waste of work time: She never expresses this opinion; in fact, she becomes more enthusiastic about participating by the end.

**Q5:** What will the woman likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Buy store-bought cookies again.
- 2. Talk to Priya about bringing samosas.
- 3. Help the man prepare his mother's butter tarts.
- 4. Reschedule the client call for the man.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Buy store-bought cookies again: The woman specifically mentioned feeling embarrassed about bringing store-bought cookies last time and is planning to make a quinoa salad instead.
✅ 2. Talk to Priya about bringing samosas: At the end of the conversation, the woman says "I'll swing by her desk this afternoon" after the man suggests checking with Priya about her samosas.
❌ 3. Help the man prepare his mother's butter tarts: The man decided against making butter tarts and will bring a fruit platter instead.
❌ 4. Reschedule the client call for the man: There's no mention of the woman helping reschedule the man's call; she only confirms the potluck timing won't conflict with his call.

- 2. Complete his application for the Basic membership.

- 2. The driving record check.
- 3. Unlimited vehicle reservations.

❌ 3. Unlimited vehicle reservations: There's no mention of unlimited reservations being included.

**Q4:** Why might the man occasionally need a larger vehicle?

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. For family vacations outside the city: The man doesn't mention family vacations.
❌ 2. To transport his personal furniture: The man doesn't mention moving his own furniture.
✅ 3. To help friends with moving items: The man specifically states "occasionally I help friends move furniture" when explaining why he might need a larger vehicle.
❌ 4. For his part-time delivery job: There's no mention of the man having a delivery job.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the woman gives about using the vehicles?
- 1. Return the car to the same location you picked it up.
- 2. Make sure the fuel tank isn't nearly empty.
- 3. Clean the vehicle before returning it.

✅ 2. Make sure the fuel tank isn't nearly empty: The woman specifically says "We just ask that you leave the tank at least quarter full for the next member."
❌ 3. Clean the vehicle before returning it: Cleaning requirements aren't mentioned.
❌ 4. Book at least 24 hours in advance: The woman mentions you can book "up to 30 days in advance" but doesn't specify a minimum booking time.

**Q6:** Why does the man take out his driver's license at the end?

- 2. To show he's legally able to drive.
- 3. To begin the application process.
- 4. To verify his home address.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To check if it's still valid: The man doesn't express concern about his license's validity.

✅ 3. To begin the application process: The man says "I have my driver's license with me" right after agreeing to start the application process, indicating he's ready to provide it as part of the application.
❌ 4. To verify his home address: There's no mention of address verification in the conversation.

**Q7:** How does the man feel about the situation?
- 1. Frustrated that the woman has so many complaints
- 2. Apologetic about not informing her of the changes
- 3. Concerned that the app might be difficult to use
- 4. Annoyed that he has to help with the technology
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Frustrated that the woman has so many complaints: The man remains helpful throughout and shows no signs of frustration.
✅ 2. Apologetic about not informing her of the changes: The man specifically says, "I apologize for that. We recently upgraded the laundry card system, and I should have mentioned it during your move-in."
❌ 3. Concerned that the app might be difficult to use: He actually says it's "quite simple once you get the hang of it" and doesn't express concern about difficulty.
❌ 4. Annoyed that he has to help with the technology: He offers help willingly, saying "I'd be happy to help you set it up right now."

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will call a professional plumber.
- 2. The man will show the woman how to use the laundry app.
- 3. The woman will wait for maintenance to fix the washing machine.
- 4. The man will issue a new laundry card to the woman.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will call a professional plumber: The maintenance person has already fixed the sink issue.
✅ 2. The man will show the woman how to use the laundry app: At the end of the conversation, the man offers to help her set up the app right now, and she accepts his offer saying "That would be really helpful, thank you."
❌ 3. The woman will wait for maintenance to fix the washing machine: The washing machine isn't broken; the issue is with the payment system.
❌ 4. The man will issue a new laundry card to the woman: The new system doesn't use physical cards anymore, as the man explains they now use an app instead.

## Practice 448: Drive-in Theatre Outing | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends discussing plans for a weekend activity.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why are the friends excited about the Stardust Drive-In?
- 1. They helped renovate it.
- 2. It's showing movies they've never seen before.
- 3. It's a nostalgic place from their childhood.
- 4. They own shares in the business.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They helped renovate it.: The conversation mentions that "some local business owners bought it and fixed it up," not the friends themselves.
❌ 2. It's showing movies they've never seen before.: The movies mentioned (Jaws and Back to the Future) are described as "classic movies," suggesting they are familiar with them.
✅ 3. It's a nostalgic place from their childhood.: The man mentions "My parents used to take me there when I was a kid" and both agree it will be "such a nostalgic experience."
❌ 4. They own shares in the business.: There is no mention of the friends having any ownership in the drive-in business.

**Q2:** Why did they choose to go on Saturday instead of Friday?
- 1. They prefer Back to the Future over Jaws.
- 2. The weather is expected to be better.
- 3. The tickets are cheaper on Saturday.
- 4. They have other plans on Friday.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They prefer Back to the Future over Jaws.: While the woman does mention "Back to the Future is more my style anyway," this is stated after they've already decided on Saturday, not as the primary reason.
✅ 2. The weather is expected to be better.: The man specifically states "Saturday might be better weather-wise. The forecast mentioned rain on Friday" as the reason for preferring Saturday.
❌ 3. The tickets are cheaper on Saturday.: There is no mention of ticket prices in the conversation.
❌ 4. They have other plans on Friday.: Neither person mentions having other plans on Friday; in fact, the man says "I'm free both evenings."

**Q3:** How has the drive-in been modernized?
- 1. They've installed digital screens.
- 2. They've added reclining seats.
- 3. They use car radios instead of window speakers.
- 4. They've built a full restaurant.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They've installed digital screens.: There is no mention of digital screens in the conversation.
❌ 2. They've added reclining seats.: The conversation doesn't mention reclining seats at the drive-in.
✅ 3. They use car radios instead of window speakers.: The woman explicitly states "Now you tune your car radio to a specific station to get the audio" in contrast to the old speakers the man remembers.
❌ 4. They've built a full restaurant.: The woman mentions only "a small concession stand with popcorn and hot dogs," not a full restaurant.

**Q4:** What does the man probably think about the woman's car?
- 1. It would be uncomfortable for watching a movie.
- 2. It doesn't have a working radio.
- 3. It uses too much gas.
- 4. It's too new for a nostalgic experience.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It would be uncomfortable for watching a movie.: The woman specifically says "Mine's pretty small and the seats don't recline much" when discussing which car to take, indicating it would be uncomfortable for watching a movie.
❌ 2. It doesn't have a working radio.: There is no mention in the conversation about the woman's car having radio problems.
❌ 3. It uses too much gas.: The conversation doesn't mention anything about gas consumption of either car.
❌ 4. It's too new for a nostalgic experience.: There is no indication in the conversation that the woman's car is new or that this would affect the nostalgic experience.

**Q5:** What will the friends likely do before the movie starts?
- 1. Set up a picnic in the park.
- 2. Meet Chris and Jen at the drive-in.
- 3. Have dinner at a Thai restaurant.
- 4. Shop for snacks and blankets.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Set up a picnic in the park.: There is no mention of a picnic or going to a park before the movie.
❌ 2. Meet Chris and Jen at the drive-in.: While they plan to invite Chris and Jen, there's no specific mention of meeting them at the drive-in rather than joining them for the entire evening.
✅ 3. Have dinner at a Thai restaurant.: The man suggests "Want to grab dinner at that new Thai place on Queen Street beforehand?" and the woman agrees, calling it "Perfect!"
❌ 4. Shop for snacks and blankets.: While the man mentions bringing blankets and snacks, there's no indication they plan to shop for these items before the movie.

## Practice 450: Senior Library Volunteer Opportunities | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a woman and a library staff member about volunteering opportunities for seniors.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman interested in volunteering at the library?
- 1. She wants to continue her teaching career.
- 2. She recently stopped working and wants community involvement.
- 3. She needs to fulfill community service hours.
- 4. She wants to improve her own technology skills.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to continue her teaching career: The woman mentions she was a teacher, but doesn't say she wants to continue teaching.
✅ 2. She recently stopped working and wants community involvement: The woman explicitly states "I'm recently retired and would love to get involved in the community."
❌ 3. She needs to fulfill community service hours: There's no mention of required service hours in the conversation.
❌ 4. She wants to improve her own technology skills: She mentions being "fairly comfortable" with technology, not wanting to improve her skills.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Attend the volunteer orientation session.
- 2. Start reading with children immediately.
- 3. Complete the application and background check forms.
- 4. Reconsider which program fits her schedule better.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend the volunteer orientation session: This will happen, but not immediately next.
❌ 2. Start reading with children immediately: She needs to complete paperwork and orientation first.
✅ 3. Complete the application and background check forms: The man says "I have a volunteer application form you can complete now" and mentions giving her the background check form today.
❌ 4. Reconsider which program fits her schedule better: She's already decided on both programs and confirmed her schedule works.

**Q3:** When are the Technology Tutors sessions available?
- 1. Monday mornings from 10:00 to 11:00.
- 2. Tuesday afternoons at 2:00 p.m.
- 3. Wednesdays and Fridays between 9:00 and 12:00.
- 4. Every day of the week for three hours.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Monday mornings from 10:00 to 11:00: This is when Reading Buddies sessions take place.
❌ 2. Tuesday afternoons at 2:00 p.m.: This is when volunteer orientation sessions are held.
✅ 3. Wednesdays and Fridays between 9:00 and 12:00: The man specifically states Technology Tutors "can be scheduled on Wednesdays and Fridays between 9:00 and 12:00."
❌ 4. Every day of the week for three hours: The program requires three hours per week, but not every day.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's comfort level with technology?
- 1. She is an expert who can teach advanced computer skills.
- 2. She is confident enough to help others with basic functions.
- 3. She needs training before she can volunteer as a tutor.
- 4. She prefers working with children because technology intimidates her.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is an expert who can teach advanced computer skills: She describes herself as "fairly comfortable" not as an expert.
✅ 2. She is confident enough to help others with basic functions: She states "I'm fairly comfortable with my iPad and laptop" and the man confirms basic skills are sufficient.
❌ 3. She needs training before she can volunteer as a tutor: While training is provided, there's no indication she needs it before starting.
❌ 4. She prefers working with children because technology intimidates her: She expresses interest in both programs, and specifically asks about the Technology Tutors program.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the man gives about the Technology Tutors program?
- 1. Volunteers should have extensive technical knowledge.
- 2. The program requires more time commitment than Reading Buddies.
- 3. Basic technology skills are sufficient as training is provided.
- 4. Volunteers should be prepared to work with all age groups.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Volunteers should have extensive technical knowledge: The man actually says "You don't need to be an expert at all."
❌ 2. The program requires more time commitment than Reading Buddies: While this is true (3 hours vs 1 hour), it's not the main advice about the program itself.
✅ 3. Basic technology skills are sufficient as training is provided: The man states "Basic skills like email, internet browsing, and using social media are sufficient. We provide training for all volunteers."
❌ 4. Volunteers should be prepared to work with all age groups: The program is specifically for helping seniors, not all age groups.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention the police background check?
- 1. To discourage volunteers with criminal records.
- 2. Because the woman will be working with vulnerable populations.
- 3. To explain why there is a fee for volunteering.
- 4. Because it's required for all library employees.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To discourage volunteers with criminal records: This purpose isn't stated in the conversation.
✅ 2. Because the woman will be working with vulnerable populations: The man specifically says it's needed "since you'll be working with children and vulnerable adults."
❌ 3. To explain why there is a fee for volunteering: The man actually states "There's no cost for volunteers."
❌ 4. Because it's required for all library employees: The woman would be a volunteer, not an employee, and this reason isn't mentioned.
